Chapter 65: Heavenly Fire Appears

“Roar!” With a deafening roar, Gongwu Ape slowly turned around. This ape was over a hundred meters tall, with long, gray hair covering its entire body.

When its huge mouth opened and closed, two sharp fangs pointed diagonally to the sky. Its huge arms, when stretched out, were longer than its entire body. Its strong and powerful chest was as solid as rock.

Its huge, bell-like eyes coldly looked at the group of uninvited guests in front of it, but a very complex emotion flashed in its eyes. That complex emotion was well concealed, barely noticeable in the gray hue.

“Bold humans, how dare you trespass into the forbidden area of the Nine Guardians!” Gongwu Ape’s tyrannical voice, tinged with the awkwardness of not speaking for many years, exploded like thunder in everyone’s ears. Gongwu Yuan’s words confirmed that there were indeed nine ancient savage beasts in this place.

Zi Ying’s face turned deathly pale, and she subconsciously gripped the twin blades in her hands. After all, she was a woman, and her body couldn’t help but tremble. Le Shaolong and Xiao Yan remained calm.

Having made their choice, they would not back down until the truth was finally confirmed, and at least they would not back down because of Gong Wuyuan’s words.

“I just want to ask you, are you the ancient savage beast Gongwu Ape?” Instead of retreating, Le Shaolong stepped forward, staring at Gongwu Ape with no fear in his eyes. A single peak six-star magical beast was not enough to instill fear in everyone.

After all, their record against the Roaring Black Lord proved their strength, and besides, the Gongwu Ape in front of them was not as powerful as the Roaring Black Lord’s aura.

“You bunch of bastards, how dare you question the noble bloodline of this Gongwu Ape?” Upon hearing Le Shaolong’s words, Gongwu Ape seemed to be greatly insulted. He slammed his huge fist into the ground, and countless cracks immediately appeared on the mountaintop, winding like a spider web, all the way to everyone’s feet.

Strangely, although the Gongwu Ape appeared extremely angry, its giant, bell-like eyes spitting out fiery heat and its sharp fangs revealing a desire to tear everyone apart, it never attacked and did not display the attack instincts of a magical beast. This was quite contrary to common sense, causing Xiao Yan and Le Shaolong to slightly raise their tightly furrowed brows.

“As far as I know, the Gongwu Ape was born in the volcanic lava of the Western Regions in ancient times. It is naturally fond of heat, and its fur is all fiery red.” Having already suspected that there was something strange about this place, and now seeing the Gongwu Ape behaving abnormally, Le Shaolong was even more determined not to miss the opportunity to get to the bottom of things.

“Why is your fur dark gray, and why is this place so windy and shady?” Le Shaolong’s question was reasonable and without any faults. Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up as he stared intently at Gongwu Ape, anticipating Gongwu Ape’s next reaction.

As expected, a hint of panic flashed in Gongwu Yuan’s eyes, which was quickly replaced by tyranny. None of this escaped the eyes of Xiao Yan and Yue Shaolong.

“When the Gongwu Ape is enraged, it will pound its chest and roar, its eyes turning blood red. Why do you not match the legend? Or are you not the Gongwu Ape at all?”

Le Shaolong suddenly raised his aura and pressed on, not giving the Gongwu Ape any time to think. Just as Xiao Yan was praising Le Shaolong in his heart, Gong Wuyuan suddenly didn’t know how to answer Le Shaolong’s question, and dared not make a move easily, so he was stunned by the question. It turns out that this illusion was created by a powerful being who stood at the pinnacle of power back then, and it was a place specifically used to cultivate the three rare treasures obtained back then.

When that powerful being abandoned Gongwu Ape here, it was still immature and in a daze, not even knowing what it was. It lived a comfortable life here with some low-level magical beasts. As for why that powerful being never appeared again, it had no idea.

This place is located inside the illusion, guarded by many powerful magical beasts. Although the people in front of us are not of high level, they were able to break into this place, which means they must have considerable strength. The Gongwu Ape, which has just gained intelligence, has been staying in this comfortable environment.

Although it is at the peak of the sixth star like the Roaring Black Lord, it is neither as strong as the Roaring Black Lord nor has it ever really fought. Therefore, the more calm Xiao Yan and the others are, the more uncertain it feels and the less it dares to make a move. Little did it know that countless years had passed, and the outer perimeter was no longer the same as before.

The powerful magical beasts of the outer perimeter had all been killed by the eight-star Dou Di of the Heavenly Demon Phoenix Clan. It was only because the eight-star Dou Di of the Heavenly Demon Phoenix Clan was seriously injured at the time, and because there was a trace of the aura of that powerful being on the gate of light, that he did not dare to enter this place.

However, the three magical beast guards with the strength of six-star Dou Di left behind after his fall were all wiped out by the group of people in front of him. Fortunately, it didn’t know. If it had known all this, it probably wouldn’t have dared to show itself and would have stayed as far away as possible.

As for why it liked places with strong winds, it was because it actually just liked this completely different space below the mountain peak, and would occasionally come up to get some fresh air. But it didn’t need to explain to everyone, so it was stunned for a moment. When Gongwu Ape was stunned, Xiao Yan and the others were also very surprised.

How could a dignified ancient beast at the peak of the sixth star be stunned by their interrogation? It was probably the most absurd thing ever to have ever happened in the world. The silence did not last long.

As a peak six-star ape, Gongwu had its own inviolable dignity. The fact that it dared not make a move easily did not mean that it was afraid of the crowd. Speechless, the newly awakened creature suddenly found the people in front of it very annoying.

Shaking its huge head, it threw a punch directly at Le Shaolong, because he was the person it found most annoying. The force of the punch brought with it an incredibly hot temperature, causing the surrounding space to distort. Xiao Zhan took the initiative, his heavy punch solidifying into a tiger shadow, and a huge beam of light intercepted Gong Wu Yuan’s punch in mid-air.

Smoke and dust exploded in mid-air, creating a hazy scene. Xiao Zhan took three steps back. The scorching heat of the punch shocked him.

The residual heat caused a burnt smell to waft from the silk of Le Shaolong behind him. When his first attack failed, and seeing that his opponent was indeed formidable, Gongwu Ape was aroused by his bestial nature. His eyes blazed with fury, and his aura surged continuously.

His fighting spirit rolled out, burning as hot as fire, turning the light gray of the mountaintop into a dark shade. The entire mountaintop shook, and people could feel the endless, terrifying heat pressure. Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up, for he smelled the scent of Heavenly Fire!

Only Heavenly Fire or top-grade beast fire could produce such intense heat! This Gongwu Ape must be related to Heavenly Fire! It seems that choosing the Gongwu Ape was the right decision.

Now the only thing to worry about is the eight ancient beasts in the eight nearby mountains.

“Purple Shadow, keep an eye on the movements on the nearby hilltops. The rest of you, follow me and try to end this battle as quickly as possible!” Xiao Yan suppressed his excitement and calmly gave the order.

At the same time, he unleashed his heavy ruler, which was accompanied by dark blue heavenly fire, and cleaved with an unstoppable momentum, enveloping the Gongwu Ape.

As soon as Xiao Yan made his move, Gongwu Ape’s eyes suddenly changed. His gray eyes glared angrily at Xiao Yan’s heavy ruler. The gray color, which became even deeper with anger, resembled a pool of black water.

His gray fighting spirit actually formed an invisible but tangible flame around his body.

As soon as the gray flames appeared, a terrifying pressure shook the heavens and earth. The flames are hazy and murky, blending with the color of the rocky mountains and resonating with the forest. From the inside out, the gray flames change from dark to light and then back to dark, repeating endlessly, like the horizon where the sea and sky merge into one, indistinguishable in boundaries, yet forming a perfect cycle.

It was clearly a flame capable of burning everything, yet it somehow conveyed an endless cycle of life within it. How could this be? Because of the “Longevity Technique,” Jenny keenly sensed something incredible and frowned slightly.

Jenny was confused, but Xiao Yan was in a terrible state while he was in mid-air. He didn’t know what the gray flames were, but as soon as they appeared, they made the previously invincible Mixed Heavenly Flame shrink and become weak, its color also becoming extremely dim. It seemed that it was completely suppressed in front of the gray flames, which stirred up a huge wave in Xiao Yan’s heart.

The Chaos Sacred Flame, ranked eighteenth, is a mysterious flame that is said to have been born with the creation of the world. It is formed from the purest energy in the world and its power is even more astonishing after being fused with the Earth Explosion Heavenly Fire.

However, it is now being restrained at every turn by the gray flame, which is simply unbelievable. Why does this happen? Xiao Yan hurriedly circulated his Dou Qi to suppress the agitation of the Heavenly Fire.

After a moment’s thought, his eyes suddenly lit up, as if he had grasped a glimmer of understanding. Aside from its own kind, Tianhuo fears no powerful opponent. No matter how strong the opponent is, Tianhuo is fearless!

But why does his own mixed heavenly fire seem so afraid of the gray flames? There is only one explanation, and that is— This gray flame is a heavenly fire ranked above the Chaos Sacred Flame! Only this explanation can account for why his heavenly fire hesitated to advance!

Yes, that must be it! It’s like searching high and low without finding it, only to have it come to you effortlessly. Once he understood all this, Xiao Yan was overjoyed.

He gripped the heavy ruler tightly, channeled his fighting spirit into it, and slashed down with all his might. The powerful wind from the heavy ruler whistled as it came within a few feet of Gongwu Ape’s head. Gongwu Ape, however, smiled disdainfully, his gray flames forming armor as he withstood Xiao Yan’s attack.

Come to think of it, Xiao Yan is only a three-star Dou Di in the early stage, and the Heavenly Flame is lower than the Gray Flame. What does Gongwu Ape have to fear? The heavy, dark blue heavenly fire-laden shadows slammed into the head of the Gongwu Ape without any fancy moves.

The collision of the two peerless heavenly fires was like dry ice meeting a raging fire, creating ripples and the rising mist filled the entire mountaintop. With his fighting spirit inferior to his opponent’s and his heavenly fire weaker, there was no suspense. Xiao Yan was blasted away, and the heavenly fire protecting him scattered, almost collapsing completely.

Using the agility of his bone wings, Xiao Yan tumbled several times in the air before finally regaining his footing, but the desire in his eyes burned even brighter. Xiao Yan’s defeat startled Zhen Ni out of her confusion. Zhen Ni frowned and raised her eyebrows.

Green leaves flew out, like jade, shimmering with a crystal luster. Endless Dou Qi transformed into a sky full of green light, instantly engulfing the entire mountain peak, like a green cloud formed from countless stars falling down. The green leaves, like spinning blades, were incredibly sharp, cutting into the body beneath the gray flames.

Although they were annihilated in the collision with the heavenly fire, a few leaves still managed to tear through the fur of the Gongwu Ape. But strangely, what seeped out from the wound on Gongwu Ape wasn’t blood! Instead, it was wisps of gray mist!

This made everyone’s hearts tighten. This Gongwu Ape wasn’t made of flesh and blood? Then what exactly was it?

In the blink of an eye, after only a few exchanges, the Gongwu Ape was injured. This was a great humiliation for the Gongwu Ape. Although the injury was minor and had almost no impact, it was still a loss of face.

Thus, the Gongwu Ape was enraged! It spread its arms out in a fan shape and then closed them, like a mountain sinking down, engulfing everyone except Xiao Yan who was above its head. Gray flames swirled around the giant fist, burning fiercely.

The intense heat instantly scorched the rubble stirred up by the fist’s force, turning it ash in the wind. Before the fist even landed, Gongwu Ape circulated his fighting spirit, sending it from his dantian to his throat. He roared nine times in succession, and an invisible sound wave burst forth from his mouth, linking together to form a huge dark abyss that sealed off all of their escape routes.

The Gongwu Ape suddenly encountered a crisis; the gray flames were incredibly powerful, and their explosive force destroyed the towering mountains. As Le Shaolong had predicted, the Gongwu Ape was weaker than the Mountain-Breaking Beast and weaker than the Cloud-Swallowing Python.

However, no one expected that the Gongwu Ape would possess such terrifying fire power! Gongwu Ape taught everyone a simple lesson: never underestimate any opponent. Among the ancient beasts that can guard the nine peaks, some may be illusory or unreal, but even if they are illusory, at least on the surface, there won’t be such an obvious difference in strength.

Those that look weak may not actually be weak. Xiao Yan is currently in mid-air, while the others are lined up in a battle formation. Not far behind them is an endless cliff, and there is no way to avoid it.

There’s nowhere to hide, nowhere to dodge, so let’s fight. Xiao Zhan glanced at the crowd behind him, his eyes filled with arrogance. He was covered in golden armor, and the Howling Tiger appeared.

He squatted down, steadied his lower body, and instantly unleashed hundreds of punches. Countless tiger shadows flashed, appearing out of nothingness, changing in countless ways, without a fixed pattern, and met Gong Wu Yuan Ru Yuan’s attack. The mountain winds blew even more fiercely, and even the sky withered into dust amidst the swirling battle qi.

The powerful explosive force was like Pangu splitting the heavens and the earth. Although Xiao Zhan’s fist wind was strong, the flame power of Gongwu Ape was even more domineering and unparalleled, flowing endlessly and relentlessly. Xiao Zhan felt his body tremble, his bones cracking and popping, his flesh shaking constantly, and even his internal organs trembling repeatedly.

Retreating to deflect the force is probably the best option right now, but Xiao Zhan cannot retreat, nor can he afford to. Behind him are his comrades who share life and death; he has nowhere to retreat. Xiao Zhan has only one choice: to hold on, no matter what.

Xiao Zhan’s legs were completely embedded in the rocks, and he unleashed his full fighting spirit with both fists. His whole body shone like a golden sun, surrounded by golden light. The endless fighting spirit engulfed the mountain peak, and he withstood the heavy attack of Gongwu Ape.

As the smoke dissipated, Xiao Zhan had only taken three steps back, leaving shocking marks on the ground where his footprints had sunk deep. A trickle of blood escaped from the corner of Xiao Zhan’s mouth, but his face showed unwavering determination and strength. As always, he remained silent.

Xiao Zhan swallowed the sweet taste that rose to his throat, not giving the shocked Gongwu Ape another chance to attack. He channeled his energy into his legs, flexed his body, and charged forward like a god clad in golden armor, unleashing a storm of punches and kicks. The gray flames were too powerful.

The group had been only a few steps away from the cliff. If Gongwu Ape regained his senses, Xiaozhan was not confident that he could withstand the next round of attacks without dodging. Therefore, Xiaozhan endured the pain in his arms and took the initiative to buy time for the group.

Before advancing to the sixth-star Dou Di level, Xiao Zhan’s defense was insufficient to withstand the repeated attacks from a peak sixth-star magical beast. Now that he has advanced to the sixth-star Dou Di level, Xiao Zhan’s defense has greatly improved. He can now withstand the attacks of the Gongwu Ape, which buys time for everyone to make a move.

Even if the ancient beasts of the other eight mountains are real, they can still deliver their strongest attack before the other beasts can provide support, and try to take down the Gongwu Ape. A surge of excitement filled the crowd, and without any hesitation, they launched their fiercest attack on Gongwu Ape.

Le Shaolong took the initiative and immediately unleashed his full power, employing nine consecutive techniques: Thrust, Wind Thrust, Fierce Thrust, Transformation Thrust, Wandering Thrust, Rage Thrust, Seeking Thrust, Flash Thrust, and Extreme Thrust, creating a dazzling display of starlight. The Nine Dragon Thorns, swirling with wisps of black and red aura, pierced the sky and burst forth with endless starlight, like nine dragons converging.

It was the tenth move of the intermediate-level world-class combat skill “Nine Thorns Through the Sky”: One Line Breaks the Sky. A sliver of starlight pierced the sky, shattering all laws of the world. The mournful starlight relentlessly tore space apart with an unrepeatable speed.

When the beautiful starlight shines, it means that the dark clouds under the starry sky will disappear. Le Shaolong has never used this move before, not because the energy required to gather is unimaginably large, but because this move exists entirely for the purpose of attack, abandoning defense and focusing solely on attacking without any dodging. This strike will surely be earth-shattering!

This strike is a do-or-die situation! Fast! So fast!

Unavoidably fast! That was the only feeling Gongwu Ape had. Apart from instinctively crossing his arms, Gongwu Ape had no time to react.

Just as Gongwu Yuan raised his arms, at a crucial moment, a whirlwind that had been brewing for a long time rushed in and wrapped around Gongwu Yuan’s arms. With Gongwu Ape’s strength, he could easily destroy the whirlwind, but his movements inevitably slowed down. In that brief moment of hesitation, “A Line to Break the Sky” ripped through Gongwu Ape’s chest!

The hole pierced by Le Shaolong in Gongwu Ape’s chest let out a hazy light from the sky. The nearby flesh, tendons, and bones were shattered, and gray smoke burst forth. The enormous impact shook Gongwu Ape’s feet off the ground, and his body immediately lost its balance.

Without giving Gongwu Ape a chance to catch his breath, Xiaozhan unleashed a punch that seemed to send golden waves crashing across the sky. Gongwu Ape barely managed to raise his fist to block, but his body was shoved into the cliff behind him by the successive blows. Endless rocks collapsed, burying Gongwu Ape.

If this were enough to kill a six-star peak ancient beast, it would be far too unrealistic. Nan Erming understood perfectly well. His hand seals changed rapidly, and the two green giant pythons merged together like two dragons, strangling towards the collapsed rock.

Xiao Yan steadied himself, his pupils gleaming with golden light, his soul power surging, and the Nine Netherworld Yellow Springs that he unleashed stretched across the sky. A ten-thousand-foot-tall illusory giant strode across the river, and “Yellow Springs Wrath” followed the giant python. With a slight hesitation on her face, Zhenni raised an eyebrow and glanced at the eight nearby mountains.

Her expression hardened and she swallowed the “Hun Tian Dan” with a flick of her slender hand. Runes shone, and her life force rapidly gathered the power of heaven and earth. One after another, twelve elven warriors emerged from the sky.

This was Zhenni’s ultimate move to deal with the Roaring Black Lord. Gongwu Ape was not as strong as Roaring Black Lord, and Zhenni did not intend to use this ultimate move.

However, there were too many unknowns in the eight mountains nearby, and a decisive battle was the best option. This was Zhenni’s last resort. The twelve elven warriors drained all the surrounding energy, transforming their essence into the power of their spears.

The void trembled, and the spears flashed through the air, reappearing the next moment already embedded in the pile of rocks. The rocks shattered, turning into dust that fell to the ground, becoming nothing more than a pile of gray sand. Through repeated life-and-death trials, the team’s tacit understanding had become so seamless that they could coordinate perfectly without needing to say a word, and their timing of successive attacks had reached its peak.

The spear vanished, and the enormous wheel of life reappeared in the sky. The turning of the years propelled the vicissitudes of history, and the radiant glow made even the blazing sun pale in comparison. The battle went unexpectedly smoothly.

Even the Roaring Black Lord had met his end, and the Wheel of Life was now spinning uncontrollably. Everything was proceeding as everyone had hoped. At this time, the eight nearby mountains remained completely still, and things seemed to be going very well.

Even a six-star peak ancient beast with nascent intelligence, possessing heavenly fire, is no match for the current group of people combined with the heaven-defying elixir “Hun Tian Dan”. For a creature of flesh and blood, facing a well-coordinated team, top-notch fighting skills, and heaven-defying elixirs, even if the Roaring Black Venerable were to be reborn, he could not change his predetermined fate. Therefore, when a huge figure struggled out from where the rocks collapsed, the crowd did not react much, as if they were watching a dying life.

The wind was always strong on the mountaintop, but it was even stronger now. The gale whipped up dark clouds that obscured the sun, and the clear sky suddenly turned dark. The convergence of light and darkness came so quickly and so incredibly, just as unbelievably as when everyone saw Gongwu Ape shake his fighting spirit and scatter all the dust.

Aside from its disheveled fur and slightly disheveled appearance, the Gongwu Ape remained as always, its massive body standing motionless, with no other colors on the gray flames dancing around its body. Nan Erming’s giant python vanished without a trace, not even staining the silk of the Gongwu Ape with a hint of green. The Gongwu Ape’s enormous eyes blazed with savage and furious flames, but Xiao Yan’s “Yellow Spring Heavenly Wrath” was as light as a breeze, causing it not even the slightest stun.

Gray flames burned on the ravaged part of its chest, and vigorous life force surged forth like a vast ocean. The wound was healing rapidly at a visible speed, and wisps of starlight constantly seeped out from the wound and spread out of its body. The powerful life force caused everyone’s expression to change drastically.

This was all so illogical, far beyond everyone’s expectations, and completely overturned everyone’s thinking. Everyone knows the power of the fighting techniques; the fact that only rubble and ruins remain on half of the mountaintop is proof enough. No one would be so narcissistic as to think that the Gongwu Ape in front of them was just having a last burst of energy before his death, putting on a desperate struggle.

The decadent aura that had just emerged from the rubble was quickly stabilizing and then intensifying, which everyone could clearly feel. But judging from the Gongwu Ape’s appearance, it’s clear it’s completely unharmed. Even though it was injured, it has almost fully recovered.

How could its regenerative ability be so powerful? Moreover, the poison gas and soul power seem to have a direct immunity to the Gongwu Ape. Are ancient beasts really this formidable?

Everyone was completely unable to accept this fact. The shock was far from over. The Wheel of Life, which carried everyone’s greatest hopes, slowly turned, and the ear-piercing friction sound resounded through the sky.

However, the markings on the Wheel of Life that represented lifespan did not change at all. Then, the Wheel of Life gradually became illusory and disappeared into the air, as if it had never existed. Yet, Gongwu Ape did not even lose a single eyebrow.

The towering tree on the mountaintop still hadn’t collapsed, revealing its massive root system. Its ancient branches stretched out like a coiled dragon. Xiao Yan was suspended in mid-air, yet he was like a puppet in a shadow play, frozen in place, completely motionless.

His enormous bone wings, like kites stuck in the tree trunk, trembled slightly. Immune to poison gas? Unaffected by the power of the soul?

Even the Judgment of Life has no effect whatsoever? That’s impossible! If that’s the case, then it can no longer be considered a living being; it should be called a god.

If it truly is a god, even just a peak six-star god, I believe that all of us combined wouldn’t be enough to suppress it with a single finger. Therefore, Xiao Yan did not believe it. Xiao Yan’s gaze inadvertently swept across the ground.

The chaotic traces of the fight, mixed with sand, gravel, broken rocks, sword marks, and scars, proved that the battle had indeed taken place and that everyone was not dreaming. But something still felt missing. Xiao Yan looked up at the crowd who were still staring blankly, not yet able to react.

Seeing the drops of blood that had spilled from the corner of Xiao Zhan’s mouth when he had withstood the Gongwu Ape’s attack, a sudden thought struck him, and all his thoughts became clear. The battle had raged on, yet not a single drop of blood remained on the ground, nor did its wounds bleed. It was as unbelievable as a gentle breeze brushing across water without creating a ripple.

The only explanation was— “It is neither made of flesh and blood nor is it a true Gongwu Ape!” A flash of lightning appeared in Xiao Yan’s eyes, and he shouted out sharply.

“Perhaps, it is heavenly fire!” Xiao Yan pondered for a moment, then added a guess that was no less shocking than a bolt from the blue, leaving everyone stunned and speechless for a long time.

As soon as he finished speaking, seemingly to prove his conjecture, a silver thread flashed, and Xiao Yan’s figure disappeared. It was Xiao Yan who flapped his bone wings and unleashed “Three Thousand Thunderclaps”.

The next moment, Xiao Yan’s figure swept past Gongwu Ape. He bent his waist, leaned back, and turned to the side. At the same time, Xiao Yan spread his bone wings.

The sharp edges were no less than those of divine weapons. They made a series of clanging sounds, avoiding Gongwu Ape’s heavy arm. With a slight tremor, he cut countless tiny wounds.

The wound was cut open, and wisps of gray air flowed out. Upon closer inspection, everyone found that it was similar to gray flames, and the wound healed completely in the blink of an eye.

“It really is heavenly fire! And it’s heavenly fire with extremely strong regenerative abilities!” Xiao Yan rushed back to stand behind Xiao Zhan, overjoyed.

All his previous confusion turned into happiness, and everyone’s eyes couldn’t help but gleam with delight. In the world of martial arts, speed is the only way to break through defenses. With the help of the Bone Wings, Xiao Yan’s speed is incredibly fast.

From the moment his figure disappears to the moment the Bone Wings inflict damage on the Gongwu Ape, and then to the moment his figure slowly solidifies in the group, it is all over in the blink of an eye. Now, Xiao Yan has the speed to fight against a high-level Dou Di. Although he dares not say he will win, he at least has the ability to protect himself.

If he obtains the Heavenly Flame in front of him, who knows how much more powerful he will become. Upon hearing the words “Heavenly Fire” and Xiao Yan’s claim that he was Heavenly Fire, the Gongwu Ape, which had just crawled out of the rubble, paused for a moment. A rare look of confusion appeared in its eyes, which were filled with bloodshot veins due to extreme anger.

Gradually, seemingly stimulated by the words “heavenly fire,” its long-buried memories slowly opened the floodgates, and it finally remembered. I vaguely remember that countless years ago, there was an ancient beast called the Phantom Beast. This beast was extremely powerful.

Legend has it that when the Phantom Beast was born, strange phenomena appeared in the sky, the blue sea surged, and a bright moon slowly rose, casting down holy silver light. The holy moon rises once every nine days, and with each rise, the beast transforms into a different form. After eighty-one days and nine transformations, it can finally take its final form.

From then on, the beast can freely transform into one of the nine magical beasts and gain some of the magical beast’s abilities. Unfortunately, the mythical beasts were almost entirely wiped out in the ancient cataclysm, ultimately disappearing into the vast river of history.

However, one mythical beast was favored by the heavens and received a great opportunity. Although it could not escape the calamity of its demise, after its fall, its inner core fire, through a series of coincidences and the passage of time, became spiritualized and transformed into “Spiritual Core Fire.” Although its flames are not as powerful as other heavenly fires, it possesses extremely rapid recovery and healing abilities, ranking sixteenth on the list of heavenly fires.

It is said to also have a beneficial effect on alchemy, but whether this is true or not remains unknown. Gongwu Ape is indeed the “Spiritual Flame”!

However, its intelligence is still developing, and the phantom beast suffered severe soul trauma before its death, resulting in an incomplete inheritance. Therefore, despite having nine different magical beast forms, it is often confused about its identity. Now, Xiao Yan’s words, “It really is Heavenly Flame,” have awakened its memories and confirmed its identity.

Back when it was still immature, that powerful being held it in the palm of his hand and said the same thing. As everyone suddenly realized the truth, the “Spiritual Fire” also became clear, and the situation suddenly became delicate. In the distance, white clouds drifted against the blue sky, creating an atmosphere of tranquility and ethereal beauty.

The mountain scenery blended seamlessly with the landscape, a scene as picturesque as a poem.

However, the “Spiritual Fire” was experiencing a completely different mood. Its heart was in turmoil. Now that it knew its own identity and the strength of its opponent, it had not only failed to gain any advantage after only a few exchanges, but had also ended up battered and bruised.

Although the powerful regenerative ability of the Heavenly Fire had prevented it from suffering significant damage, the opponent’s strength was undeniable. The “Spiritual Fire’s” expression became extremely solemn. On Xiao Yan’s side, everyone was also uneasy.

After going through countless hardships, the revelation of the “Spiritual Flame’s” identity was as exciting as the first rain of spring, the first cry of a baby, or the first ray of dawn before sunrise. It seemed that all their efforts were about to be rewarded.

However, the “Spiritual Flame’s” inexhaustible life-restoring ability and its immunity to poison gas, soul power, and even the Wheel of Life made everyone feel extremely troubled. This was equivalent to losing almost half of their abilities, greatly weakening their combat power. What to do?

How to deal with this heavenly fire that we absolutely must obtain?

At this moment, the mountaintop, affected by the battle, looked as if it had been cleaved by an ancient battle axe. After the dust settled, broken walls and ruins remained, and the green mountains collapsed, leaving behind a scene of utter devastation. Looking around at the eight surrounding mountain peaks, although there was no movement at all, the group could not be sure whether the eight ancient beasts at the peak of the sixth star were watching them covetously.

It felt as if eight heavy mountains were pressing down on their hearts, making it difficult to relax. The tranquility on the mountaintop did not last long. The silence between the two sides was broken by the powerful punch of “Spiritual Fire” with the force of wind and thunder.

The humiliation he had suffered earlier made “Spiritual Fire” furious. The wind from his punch shattered the mountain peaks, just like the ebb and flow of the tide, blocking everyone’s escape routes. Despite some reservations, the battle was still fought.

The Golden Howling Tiger of the Sky broke through the air and pressed down, growing larger and larger. With each inch it pressed down, the space trembled. The powerful pressure even shook the mountains as it fought against the “Spiritual Fire”.

Both “Spiritual Fire” and Xiao Zhan follow a fierce and powerful approach. Their fists ripped through the sky like brilliant comets crashing into the earth, causing mountains and rivers to lose their color and the sky to shake. Neither would give way to the other.

“Spiritual Fire” relies on its powerful regenerative ability, making it virtually immortal; while Xiao Zhan relies on its extremely strong body, as if protected by heavy armor, making it difficult for ordinary attacks to penetrate its defenses. Xiao Yan also made a move. Since the soul attack was ineffective, the Heavenly Flame’s grade was suppressed, and the fighting techniques would be ineffective without the Heavenly Flame’s power, he would use the Bone Wings.

Xiao Yan had long wanted to see just how powerful the Bone Wings were. The bone wings vibrated rapidly, waving like a dream, creating a dazzling display of light that formed a curtain of light that slashed diagonally at the “Spiritual Fire.” Wherever the light curtain reached, space shattered.

It was both dreamlike and incredibly sharp. Even the faint glow emanating from the bone wings could not withstand the weight of the high mountains and cliffs. The light, using the mountaintop as a plane, arbitrarily drew countless crisscrossing lines on a chessboard, unfathomable and producing a series of resounding clangs.

Faced with extreme speed and sharpness, the enormous body of the “Spiritual Flame” became the perfect target.

In the blink of an eye, it was covered in countless wounds, and the gray flames dimmed. Jenny pointed out with one finger that the sky was a seamless blend of sea and sky, like a clear blue mirror, with a hazy, chaotic atmosphere. A green shoot emerged from the blue waves, growing rapidly in the wind.

Its tender leaves were covered with dewdrops, full of vitality, and it radiated thousands of rays of light and auspicious colors. The green light, of unknown origin, instantly transformed into towering vines upon its appearance. The azure waves began to wither rapidly, their nutrients vanishing in an instant.

Yet, the vines seemed far from satisfied, with endless green leaves enveloping the “Spiritual Flame.” The powerful energy contained within the gray flames continuously poured into the green vines. With a single move, Nan Erming shattered the sky, revealing a peerless weapon in his hand.

It rapidly approached with billowing green mist, surging in all directions like a tidal wave. The purple figure was faint and hazy, and she swung her twin blades eighty-one times. Lightning in the clear sky gathered and finally merged into a bolt of lightning that pierced the sky, as if it would destroy the world.

The storm seemed like a gentle breeze sweeping across the mountains, but the surrounding space was already subtly boiling. The wind elements in the air became almost violent, merging with the battle qi at an extremely fast speed, like a surging ocean. A tornado was about to arrive, and the world changed color.

Le Shaolong’s dagger lurked in the shadows, ready to strike a fatal blow at any moment. The unknown movements of the eight high mountains and the eight powerful and monstrous ancient beasts are like a shadow that follows closely in the night but can never be shaken off. This makes everyone have some guesses but can never get confirmation.

Therefore, they use their ultimate moves as soon as they make a move, without holding back, and dare not hold back. The scene of the gathering of battle qi was extremely shocking. Although it was a six-star peak magical beast like the Roaring Black Lord, the “Spiritual Fire” did not acquire any heaven-defying battle skills due to its nascent intelligence and the defects in its inheritance.

It also lacked combat experience, which plunged the “Spiritual Fire” into a deep crisis. Jenny’s green vines were full of life, and the green petals bloomed in the wind, emitting a refreshing fragrance. It seemed like a poem or a painting, but in the eyes of the “Spiritual Flame”, it was no less than a deadly temptation - the energy of the gray flames flowed into the petals, and the petals bloomed more profusely and beautifully.

The bone wings were strangely agile, a peerless weapon was half-hidden in the space, a lightning tornado was about to arrive, and the powerful killing intent of the blade and spikes was spreading in the dark… All of these indicated the danger of the “Spiritual Fire”. This situation is dangerous and seems unsolvable.

Because the green vines were thousands of feet away, they were difficult to approach due to Xiao Zhan’s obstruction, unless one possessed extraordinary speed; Xiao Yan’s bone wings were both fast and ruthless, sharp as divine weapons, impossible to dodge unless one possessed area control abilities; at the same time, Nan Erming and Zi Ying’s spears and lightning bolts came like thunderbolts, impossible to defend against unless one possessed formidable defense; the storm’s tornadoes were overwhelming, the only attack that “Spiritual Flame” was confident it could withstand, because although the wind was fierce, its speed was not fast, allowing for ample preparation…

But all of this combined makes the final whirlwind the greatest threat, like a final, ruthless blow to a dying person. With the outcome already decided, although the “Spiritual Fire” possesses extremely strong regenerative abilities, it seems that it will be severely damaged this time! The Spiritual Flame had no idea what the others were thinking.

Although it felt a little guilty, it was not flustered at all. It seemed that in its eyes, none of these threats were threats. Just as the blade was about to strike, “Spiritual Flame” let out a soft sigh and made a series of unexpected yet incredibly shocking moves…

This is the true power of the “Spiritual Flame”—very strong, extremely strong, at least much stronger than before. But Xiao Yan and the others smiled, a smile of relief and reassurance

Chapter 66: Fierce Battle Against Heavenly Fire

In the moment of crisis, the “Spiritual Flame” appeared to have a gleaming gray light in its eyes, seemingly possessing a demonic nature. Flames swirled around its body, and terrifying battle aura condensed. Its body twisted and turned amidst the rising flames, swiftly transforming into a gigantic monster covered in scales, with only one mouth above its face, and the rest of its features as flat as a gray ground.

Look up at the sky and roar! The “Spiritual Fire” has actually transformed into the Sky-Gazing Roar! The Sky-Looking Roar opened its mouth and roared angrily, unleashing invisible sound waves that pierced through the air.

Xiao Yan’s bone wings suddenly felt like they were stuck in mud, and his originally extremely smooth movements suddenly became clumsy. Taking advantage of the opportunity, Wangtianhou changed its form again, and a golden raven covered in golden flames replaced its previous huge body. The “Spiritual Flame” has transformed into a golden crow!

The golden crow, like a blazing ray of light, dodged Xiao Yan’s bone wings with a swift movement.

In the blink of an eye, it appeared on Zhen Ni’s green vines, destroying them instantly. Its speed was no less than that of a nine-tailed fox; it seemed to be the legendary Heaven-Burning Golden Crow. At this very moment, Zi Ying and Nan Erming’s attacks had already reached the “Spiritual Flame Pill,” their weapons imbued with lightning and strange power, emanating chaotic light.

Once again, the “Spiritual Flame” was enveloped in gray flames. Just as the spear and lightning were about to strike, a giant python emerged, its enormous body coiling around the entire mountain wall. Its forked tongue flicked slightly, and its entire body resembled gray rock, impervious to swords and blades.

The Sky-Devouring Python! The “Spiritual Flame” transformed from a golden raven into a Sky-Devouring Python! The moment the python’s body appeared, lightning struck, the spear arrived, and the storm’s tornado followed, slamming heavily into the python’s body.

No sooner said than done, in the blink of an eye, the “Spiritual Fire” transformed in succession into a Sky-Gazing Roar, a Golden Crow, and a Cloud-Swallowing Python. Everyone was stunned on the spot, their mouths agape, forgetting to brush the dust off their faces, forgetting the golden color of the bright sunlight, and ignoring the melodious sound of the mountain spring… Until the dust settled, the Cloud-Swallowing Python transformed back into the Martial Ape.

Meanwhile, the eight nearby peaks, which everyone had been concerned about, remained silent.

At this moment, everyone finally understood that the so-called nine savage beasts of the nine mountains were actually all incarnations of this Heavenly Fire, and it was all a mystery created by it! It must have been because it saw that someone had actually broken through the outer edge of the illusion and entered here, and knew that the person must be very powerful and not to be trifled with. Having no practical combat experience, it was actually very afraid.

So it used the soul detection power of the Sky-Gazing Roar and the extreme power of the Burning Heaven Golden Crow to create an illusion before everyone explored the Nine Mountains, wanting everyone to know the difficulty and retreat. Thinking of this, everyone felt a bit amused and exasperated. Only Tianhuo, who was just beginning to develop intelligence and was as ignorant as a child, could do such an incredible thing.

Everyone laughed, laughed heartily, and laughed so hard that it sent chills down the spine of “Spiritual Fire”. When everything quieted down, everyone joined hands and surrounded it. With no more worries, their confidence soared, and their cold eyes revealed a hint of anger at being toyed with by the “Spiritual Fire”.

And so, another fierce battle broke out on the side of the mountain… At the center of the battlefield, figures flashed, weapons flew, and fighting spirit surged like a vast ocean, withering nearby forests, destroying nearby rocks, blocking out the blazing sun, and severing the waterfalls cascading down the mountains. His body embodies nine beasts, each possessing its own abilities, which greatly enhances the power of the “Spiritual Fire.”

His strength rivals that of the Roaring Black Lord, and his every move exudes grandeur, with powerful battle aura suppressing everything below. The advantages that can be improved are obvious, but the disadvantages are even more obvious! The lack of experience in combat, the absence of life-and-death training, and the interruption of the inheritance of the heaven-defying fighting skills caused “Spiritual Fire” to be full of flaws under the tacit attack of the group.

In a life-and-death battle, flaws mean fatality. Before long, “Spiritual Fire” was covered in wounds. Excessive wounds would lead to blood loss and weakness for any magical beast, but it wasn’t a big problem for the “Spiritual Fire.”

Even its weakness was greatly reduced. The “Spiritual Fire’s” recovery ability was so strong that Xiao Yan and the others couldn’t help but click their tongues. This also meant that this was going to be a protracted battle, a contest of who would give up first.

In the morning, we watch the water flow eastward; in the evening, we watch the sun set in the west. The water flows eastward, and the leaves fall one after another. Time passes quietly but swiftly.

The battle between the people and the “Spiritual Fire” continues. The aftershocks of the battle have destroyed this space beyond recognition. More than half of the nine mountains that once stood have collapsed, and the forests and rocks are all broken.

From fierce battles accompanied by shouts of exhaustion to now making moves in silence, the momentum is no longer as great as before. No matter who has been fighting for so long, they will inevitably become physically and mentally exhausted. This was a battle that “Spiritual Flame” had never experienced before, and a battle that Xiao Yan and his companions had never encountered before.

It was a battle of willpower and physical strength, and even more so a battle that, in the end, no longer concerned with fighting techniques or ultimate moves, but only with attrition. Licking his slightly dry lips, Xiao Yan used his heavy ruler to block the terrifying claw attack of the “Spiritual Fire,” then turned and slashed diagonally with his bone wings, carving a foot-deep wound into the “Spiritual Fire.” Wisps of gray smoke rose, but the wound quickly healed as before.

Xiao Yan sighed inwardly and repeated the action he had just performed. Although practice makes perfect, his limbs were still numb. His gaze toward the “Spiritual Flame” was as sullen as that of a lonely woman confined to her boudoir.

Looking around, he saw that everyone else was not much better off than him. The expressions on their faces were like lines carved on a plaster statue, devoid of life and variation. They were just numbly and indifferently repeating the fighting techniques over and over again.

Everyone felt extremely frustrated, while “Spiritual Flame” was suffering terribly. Although the people attacking him were not high-level and of average strength, their teamwork was extremely smooth, and their combined power was extraordinary. No matter how he transformed into the nine beasts, he could not break through their encirclement.

The “Spiritual Flame” is a wondrous object born from heaven and earth. As long as heaven and earth exist, the flame will never be extinguished.

However, the “Spiritual Flame” never expected that after several days of continuous fighting, it was already exhausted, while its opponent was not only not worn down, but was still full of energy. Although its expression was dull and the battle was tedious, that was a matter of emotion and had nothing to do with strength. All of this stemmed from those wretched pills.

Not only did they have an incredibly fast recovery effect, but people also consumed them in huge quantities, as if they could never finish them. The “Spiritual Pill Fire” was so impressed that its eyelids twitched. In all its countless years, it had never seen anyone squander pills like that.

So “Spiritual Elixir Fire” really wanted to curse. Therefore, the “Spiritual Elixir Fire” was left speechless with despair. So what if I curse?

So what if I want to cry? I still have to keep going! This is a war of attrition, without bloodshed or excitement.

Although it is not very dangerous and is even quite tedious, it is still a tough war of attrition. It tests the team’s resources, the sustainability of the “Spiritual Fire,” the reserves of pills for Xiao Yan and his group, and their perseverance and endurance! After several more days of tug-of-war and attrition, even the mighty “Spiritual Fire” was starting to feel the strain.

The fatigue of the “Spiritual Fire” caught everyone’s attention, and their eyes lit up. Although the battles over several consecutive days were tedious, they significantly improved everyone’s skills. At least their understanding and proficiency in combat techniques were greatly enhanced.

While they couldn’t control their skills at will, they could at least use them with ease. Jenny made the first move, like a fairy standing on a cloud.

Suddenly, two blazing lights shot out from her watery eyes, and green leaves formed by the green light surrounded her, as if they were one. Jenny raised her jade hand and pointed at the “Spiritual Flame”. Wherever her finger pointed, the powerful battle qi seemed to solidify into a substance, piercing through space.

Countless green leaves, with a sigh of withering, left Jenny’s delicate body and flew to the side of the “Spiritual Flame” in an instant, fluttering like autumn. Autumn leaves always turn yellow, losing their vitality and becoming tinged with vicissitudes. The green leaves surrounding the “Spiritual Flame” are the same.

Each green leaf drifts past the gray flames and gently withers, but instead of turning yellow, they take on a thick gray hue, as if the color of this season is inherently gray.

数不尽的叶子在飘扬，在枯萎，显得那么自然随意，没有恐怖的能量波动，没有惊天动地的声响，一切都平淡如水，似有一片流云轻轻飘过，然后化成了飞灰，又像是雪花在消融，眨眼便消失不见。 随着大量叶子的消融，“通灵丹火”身上的灰色火焰以肉眼可见的度快稀薄起来，似乎是被碧叶带走了无限的生机，而这生机就是灰色火焰的能量。 “吼！” 一声穿云裂石的怒吼响起，澎湃的灵魂之力顿时切断了甄妮与碧叶的联系，“通灵丹火”脸色苍白，身躯一阵黯淡，脸上写满了惊恐与不安。 “长生决”实在太强大了，甄妮施展的碧叶就如春至花开，秋至叶落一样的正常，正常得让人无法提起警惕，正常到待“通灵丹火”现时，灰色火焰已经失去了大量的能量。 灰色火焰已经不复之前的威势，连番苦战与多日的消耗终于让“通灵丹火”精疲力竭，它浑身是伤，颜色从夜色的深邃变成了黎明的淡灰。 契机来了！ 这一刻，“通灵丹火”感到了危机，而众人看到了契机！ 啸战面无表情，在虚空中一踏步，黄金啸天虎如大山一般压了过来，重拳连连出击，不让“通灵丹火”有任何喘息的机会。 虎影极快，瞬息千万重，契机面前，啸战拼命了！ 他知道，“通灵天火”可化身九兽，身兼度、灵魂、力量、防御几大优势，只要自己一个大意，随时有可能生意外，现在已经到了收网的时候，无论如何，都要保证没有意外，保证萧炎收服天火。 金虎啸天，如银河坠落，似星域枯寂，庞大的压力让人有窒息的感觉，啸战与“通灵丹火”的对抗，已经从处于下风到相持平衡再到如今隐隐占了上风。 “通灵丹火”急了，它化身吞云蟒，巨尾横扫，以摧枯拉朽之势甩开啸战，同时心里烦躁不安起来。它对眼前的局势也看得很清楚，这样下去，结局很明显，它迟早会被耗死，心里顿生撤退之意，但往哪里逃呢？当年那位强大存在将此地作为培养之地，岂是能逃得出去的？ “不管如何，先回自己居住的空间，借助那里的火热天地之气快恢复了能量再说。”“通灵丹火”心思急转，打定主意。 心思方定，空中星辰闪耀，如混沌爆裂，势不可挡。 乐少龙心思何等缜密，一看“通灵丹火”拼命的架势，就看出它一定欲有所图，当即双刺出阵阵铿锵之音，刺上小龙盘旋，杀气腾腾地阻断了“通灵丹火”的去路。 苍穹之上，暴风急钻，风柱擎天，斗气流转，隆隆声铺天盖地，像是汪洋在怒卷，又如惊雷响彻九天十地。 风暴的斗技也及时而至，封住了“通灵丹火”的上空。 “通灵丹火”已生退意，不想过多纠缠，而今上下左右均被封锁，顿时怒不可遏，它怒吼一声，化身一头形似飞马，但肋无骨翅，头上双角似龙弯曲，四蹄踏雪的蛮兽，身上阴阳二气迷蒙，散着强大的威压，正是“穷奇”。 “穷奇”，乃远古蛮兽中的佼佼者，无限接近远古神兽，虽然只是“通灵丹火”所化，仅有其部分威能，但也不同凡响。 “穷奇”四蹄踏空，头顶左角乌光如测，右角白光烁烁，那是黑与白的对立，也是生与死的光华，黑白交相辉映间，血淋淋的意境如森罗地狱般迎面扑来，瞬间粉碎了乐少龙与风暴的攻势。 没有片刻的停留，“通灵丹火”火焰一卷，一只身有九尾的狐狸闪现，九尾灿灿生辉，在虚空中烙印出一个巨大的符文。 符文不明其义，高约两米左右，绚烂夺目，若璀璨的宝石凝聚而成，在阳光的照射下，隐隐在远方投射出一个黯淡的符文。 随着九尾狐的身影进入符文，原本如宝石般璀璨的符文急变暗，然后消失无踪，而远处的那个黯淡符文却亮如星辰，光芒四射，九尾狐的身影已然出现在了远方。 好快的度！好神秘的符文！ 只几个起落间，远处的山峦已近在眼前，那棵参天古树虽然饱经斗气摧残，断成几截，但根系依在，依然纵深不知几许，根系之下，自己居住多年的那方灼热空间触手可及，“通灵丹火”的眸子越来越亮，似乎看到了希望，恢复能量的希望。 在绝望的时刻看到了希望，那感觉，总是无比美好的，“通灵丹火”翻腾的灰色火焰因为激动而呈现不同灰调的急变化。 如横空而过的彩虹，天空中一个亮丽的符文再度闪起，一股难以说清的韵味引动着天地能量。 只需要一次，再只需一次横渡虚空，“通灵丹火”就能回到自己的空间，那里是火焰的天堂，是它唯一的希望。 理想总是丰满的，但现实总是很骨感。一支锐利的骨矛带着一股难明的意境横贯在“通灵丹火”的前方，穿透了上方闪烁的符文，符文渐渐迷蒙起来，如梦似幻，给人不太真实的感觉，然后消散于空中。 远处的南尔明，脸色如骨矛一样灰白，正露出一丝蔑笑看着“通灵丹火”。 从“通灵丹火”眼眸中透露出对前方的渴望那刻开始，南尔明就已经在准备了，他计算准了方位，然后将酝酿已久的斗气化为致命一击，在“通灵丹火”的惊愕中彻底泯灭了它的希望。 “佩服，南尔明，你是怎么现的？”萧炎笑问道，骨翅一振，迅追了上来。 “我站的方向刚好离这边比较近。”南尔明顿了顿，看着“通灵丹火”的身形叹了口气，似乎很是无奈，“我还没见过有哪个白痴连逃跑的路线都是那么笔直，不做任何掩饰的。” 闻听南尔明此话，“通灵丹火”九尾一甩，刚想准备再次循走的身形一僵，转过头怨恨地盯着南尔明，眸子中熊熊燃烧的火焰似乎要择人而噬。 从懵懂开启灵智以来，“通灵丹火”就是这个地区的最强者，虽不至于横行无忌，肆意妄为，但虎躯一震，万兽皆伏的自豪感还是潜伏在它每一丝丝中，如今被逼无奈想逃回住处补充能量已是憋闷，竟然还被一个五星斗帝骂为白痴，这简直就是奇耻大辱！ 是可忍，孰不可忍！怒吼中，“通灵丹火”化身破山战兽，巨脚踩裂了峰峦，巨掌拍碎了片片虚空，带着滔天怒焰罩向南尔明，誓要将南尔明毁于手下。 “见过容易被激将的，没见过这么容易就上当的。”南尔明身处半空，绿裳随风，略显苍白的脸英俊非凡，摇头不已。 巨掌离头顶眼看已不过几米之遥，南尔明却不闪不避，淡然以对。 而“通灵丹火”却心里一愣，南尔明的话在其心中掀起了惊慌之意，随后急蔓延了全身，看着逼近过来的萧炎众人，感受着自己身上能量的快流失，“通灵丹火”立刻有种上当的感觉。 众人逼近，分处几个方位，再次对“通灵丹火”形成了包围。 “通灵丹火”极不甘心，匆忙收回擎天巨掌，恨恨地瞪了一眼看似没有任何动作但手印却在衣袖中急变的南尔明，火焰翻腾中化身成一个有着两个狰狞脑袋，肋生双翅的魔兽，身形虚幻中一化为九，欲要冲出包围圈。 此兽不知为何名，不知其渊源来历，竟然可以一分为九，空灵而又变化莫测，不见形迹，难辨真假，众人一时竟然无法拦截。 Sunlight cast faint shadows under the nine menacing figures.

Just when everyone was at a loss and unable to mount an effective interception, the purple-faced girl’s expression turned solemn. She pointed her sword to the sky, its edge flashing, and lightning gathered. Zi Ying unleashed her ultimate technique – “A Thousand Years of Empty Space”.

Lightning struck from the clouds, traversing the vast sky.

At this moment, all the auspicious clouds blurred, the surroundings were hazy, and the scenery was indistinct. Endless fog enveloped the lightning, and a chilling killing intent swept over the nine figures formed by the “Spiritual Fire”. The lightning blocked out the small space, making the place seem ethereal and Zen-like.

The endless mysteries made everything formless, and the eight figures became illusory, so ethereal and unreal. Only one figure was unaffected and remained as solid as ever, rushing forward. The phrase “a thousand years of emptiness” has such a wonderful use!

It is no exaggeration to describe it as miraculous! Completely unaware that its whereabouts had been discovered, the “Spiritual Flame” was feeling quite smug as it was about to break free of the encirclement, only to find everyone shaking their heads at it and then raising their weapons.

“The battle can finally be over.” Jenny murmured, a hint of relaxation appearing on her tired face, her eyes full of charm. Jenny’s murmur reached the ears of “Spiritual Flame,” which felt as if struck by lightning. It sensed something was wrong.

Although it didn’t know how the people had discovered its true form, it clearly knew the answer from the way they looked at it—its whereabouts had been exposed. The “Spiritual Flame” changed its expression drastically, and in a rapid transformation, enormous creatures with nine horns on their heads, nine spikes on their backs, and nine teeth in their mouths materialized. The name of this beast was unknown.

During the transformation of the “Spiritual Fire,” everyone thought that its power was roughly the same as that of the other eight beasts, so they did not pay much attention to it.

However, once the beast solidified and appeared, its pressure was as terrifying as the underworld, making people feel as if they were facing an abyss or falling into hell. Their whole bodies were ice-cold, and cold sweat poured down their faces. Endless killing intent permeated the air.

Before the attack, the ancient beast’s form fully materialized. Its body, hundreds of meters tall, was pitch black and lusterless. Its cold eyes swept over the crowd without any emotion, as if nothing in this world mattered to it.

As the blood-red sunset cast a somber glow, the endless rubble and ruins accentuated the dark figures, creating an extremely oppressive atmosphere. Who would have thought that Heavenly Fire had such a trump card! Everyone was shocked, but they immediately and in perfect unison created some distance between themselves, staring intently at the savage beast before them.

Almost simultaneously, they were all thinking the same thing—if it had such a powerful magical beast avatar, why hadn’t it transformed into this beast before? The price of transforming into this beast must have been exorbitant, and it was very likely that it wouldn’t be able to maintain it for long. In fact, this beast is named “Zhentian” and has the bloodline of a king among ancient savage beasts.

It can destroy the world with every move it makes.

However, to transform into this beast, one needs far too much energy, an unimaginable amount, because this beast is very powerful—the stronger it is, the more difficult it is to transform. If it weren’t for the current dire circumstances, “Spiritual Fire” would not have wanted to transform into this beast. As everyone guessed, transforming into “Zhentian” would have been too costly for it, given its current energy deficiency, and it also lacked the energy to sustain itself for too long.

Inside the dantian of “Zhentian”, there is a miniature version of “Tongling Danhuo” (Spiritual Flame). The gray flame has become almost transparent, as illusory as a candle flickering in the wind, struggling to stay afloat. Once “Heaven-Shaking” was unleashed, “Spiritual Flame” had no intention of escaping anymore.

Because it had already consumed too much energy, returning to the fiery space would be of little use unless it had enough time to recover. Otherwise, it would be far from replenishing the previous losses.

However, “Spiritual Flame” knew very well that it no longer had that chance, because this time, when it transformed into “Heaven-Shaking,” it only had three moves of power, and those three moves were enough to decide the fate of the world! Therefore, this transformation became its final battle, a final battle it had no choice but to fight!

“Zhentian” raised his hand, his giant palm expanding continuously, passing over mountains and clouds, as if entering the extreme void. Only a profound stillness could be seen in his palm, as if a world was evolving. Then, he gently patted down.

As the giant hand slammed down, the sky suddenly changed, the heavy clouds turned into dust, and everything under the hand showed a vibrant green before quickly withering and dying, as if all life had been extinguished. The giant hand acted arbitrarily, seemingly casual yet domineering, as if it had experienced the transition from brilliance to desolation, from light to darkness, carrying the weight of history, and creating an unparalleled momentum of evolution from prosperity to decline. A gigantic hand, like a cloud covering the sky, pressed down!

The Howling Tiger, with its ten-thousand-foot-tall body, is but a corner of the dark clouds; the myriad stars are no match for the deep darkness of the cloud-covered mountains; the power of thunder and lightning, capable of splitting the heavens and earth, is but a drop in the ocean compared to the boundless clouds; the raging storm, as towering as waves, is still no match for the surging waves of the cloud sea.

The overwhelming clouds crushed the Golden Howling Tiger with devastating force, obscured the boundless starlight, shattered the lightning, and obliterated the storm! Until Jenny made her move, countless emerald vines and leaves emerged following an inexplicable trajectory, forming complex and profound runes of order that rose to the sky. The fighting spirit surged with restraint and weight, without a sound or ripples, only an inexplicable rule of heaven and earth flowing through it, appearing extremely mysterious.

It guided the overwhelming dark clouds from leaves to roots to the earth, and all things were like the beginning of Tai Chi, born in the heavens and returning to the earth. The solid rock beneath their feet seemed to have suffered a cataclysmic disaster, a once-in-a-millennium event. The powerful battle aura instantly destroyed the rocks, shattered the sand and gravel, turning them into swirling dust that layered upon layer, transforming the surrounding area into a desert.

It must be said that the predecessors who created the “Longevity Technique” were incredibly skilled. They used heaven and earth as their source, battle qi as their axis, transformed Tai Chi, and used the weak to overcome the strong. Their methods were extremely extraordinary.

Of course, this was also because the giant hand had consumed a lot of fighting spirit in the previous battle with everyone, otherwise it would not have been so easily destroyed by Zhen Ni. Nevertheless, the power of the “Longevity Technique” was still evident to everyone.

Without a moment’s pause, without the slightest pity, the giant hand reappeared, brimming with fighting spirit, as if it had traveled back in time to ten thousand years ago, generating an inexplicable power that constructed a huge net in the void, descending from the sky as if to capture all living beings under heaven. Wow! Absolutely amazing!

At this moment, everyone’s thoughts reached an astonishing consensus. Without time to hesitate, Xiao Zhan and Zhen Ni flipped their hands, and the “Hun Tian Dan” reappeared. They swallowed it, and their aura immediately surged.

Previously, Zhenni took the “Hun Tian Dan” a second time because Xiao Zhan was there to protect her, so even in her weakened state, she wasn’t in mortal danger. Now, having taken the pill at the same time as Xiao Zhan, once the effects wear off and he weakens, everyone will be doomed. They don’t know that the “Tong Ling Dan Huo” only has three moves of power; they only know that this “Zhen Tian” can’t last long, and they must defeat him before the effects wear off!

The two sides racing against time became even more tense. The giant hand covered an even wider area, encompassing almost the entire mountain peak. Everything under its palm withered, the grass and trees died, and turned into mud and sand.

This place has existed for millions of years, and now, in the blink of an eye, it has gone from a place of vibrant prosperity to a place of utter desolation. The change has happened so quickly that it evokes endless感慨 (feelings of deep emotion). With a deafening roar, the golden Howling Tiger’s body collided with the giant paw, unleashing a thunderous explosion that pierced everyone’s eardrums.

Endless waves of light rippled outwards, creating cracks wherever they passed, which spread for miles, weaving into a giant net. Then, the ground was pulverized amidst the thunderous roar. Two of the former nine mountains were destroyed, leaving only a desert.

The aftershocks were so terrifying, and the battles between the two sides were even more brutal. The tens of thousands of feet long body of the war tiger that Xiao Zhan had transformed into suddenly cracked, and then the cracks rapidly expanded, covering his entire body, like white bones that had been dried for tens of thousands of years. With a “crack”, it turned into fragments all over the sky.

Xiao Zhan spat out blood, his body was thrown into the air, and his protective armor became extremely dull before disappearing without a trace. After repelling Xiao Zhan, the giant hand became ethereal, no longer solid. The descending battle aura thinned considerably, and a storm arose.

The staff was waved, gusts of wind swept through, Le Shaolong and Zi Ying spun their twin blades, lightning and starlight swept across the heavens and earth, and Nan Erming’s giant python lashed its tail, splitting mountains and shattering rocks… Finally, the second strike of “Thunderclap” vanished into thin air and ceased to exist. The wind was gentle and the clouds were light.

The sky was still the same sky, the clouds were still the same clouds, and the wind was still the same wind. Only the desert on the ground and the mottled bloodstains sprayed out by Xiao Zhan testified to the power of those two palms. Whether the storm will pass and peace prevails, no one knows.

The crowd silently looks up, gazing at the sky, at the “earth-shaking” storm. After a moment of silence, Xiao Yan flipped his hand and distributed several “Hun Tian Dan” pills to everyone. No one expected “Zhentian” to be so powerful.

Seeing Xiaozhan injured, a dangerous shadow began to slowly spread in everyone’s hearts, and unease was growing. They had no choice but to fight desperately. The Spirit Fire was also extremely frustrated.

The Heaven-Shaking Technique was a double-edged sword. Although it was very powerful, it would leave the user vulnerable after three moves. The Spirit Fire would never dare to use it unless it was a matter of life and death.

Now that two moves had passed, apart from Xiao Zhan, who was pale and had blood still on his lips, the others were still alive and kicking. It really couldn’t guarantee whether the third move would be effective. As everyone silently watched “Zhentian,” “Zhentian” also silently gazed at them, his eyes no longer holding the cold indifference of someone looking down upon the world, but instead filled with complex emotions.

Time flowed by, the wind whipped up a cloud of fine sand, and the scene became awkward. After a moment of silence, Jenny stood up, seemingly floating on a cloud, her entire being ethereal and distant, like a drifting cloud or a gentle breeze, her mind and body serene and detached. Time was running out.

There was still a chance before the effects of the drug wore off, but once they wore off, they might all be wiped out. Although she was deeply afraid of the power of “Zhentian”, Jenny was not going to wait any longer. Once Jenny made her move, the others naturally wouldn’t lag behind.

Xiao Zhan stood up; his injuries had almost fully recovered after a short period of rest. His golden armor once again covered him, gleaming with a metallic sheen. His strong muscles were as hard as gray-white rocks, and his joints vibrated with the sound of metal clashing.

Tens of thousands of feet of tiger shadows materialized once more. The tiger shadow did not appear as ostentatiously as before. Instead, it contracted rapidly.

With each meter it contracted, the tiger’s body became more solid.

In the blink of an eye, the tiger shadow, which had been 100,000 feet long, solidified to 1,000 meters. Its immense pressure was almost tangible, like the resurrection of an ancient behemoth king that had crossed the starry sky and was covered in the dust of history. Every black stripe on its body was clearly visible.

As its sharp claws extended and retracted, the space resembled a plowed field, with furrows revealing the darkness of the starry sky. After several bloody battles, Xiao Zhan was deeply moved. Walking between life and death always makes it easier to understand things that cannot be understood in ordinary times.

The precise control of energy is far more powerful than a bluff. Now, Xiao Zhan is dressed in golden clothes and armor, his whole body is free from dust and vulgarity. He has lost some of his arrogance but gained more restraint, and looks very otherworldly.

The golden Roaring Tiger, about a thousand meters tall, stood proudly to the sky behind Xiao Zhan, its aura even more terrifying than the 100,000-foot tiger shadow. With a casual wave of its claws, the massive boulder on the distant mountain instantly turned into dust, which scattered and fell down. Seeing everyone make the first move, “Zhentian’s” expression changed drastically.

His eyes shifted unpredictably before finally turning into a fierce look of suicidal intent. He raised his giant hand again, stirring up the wind and clouds. Just now, Xiao Yan had been lost in thought.

Now that the giant palm was supporting the sky, Xiao Yan finally moved. He spread his bone wings, as agile as the wind, leaving a trail of afterimages with each step, and instantly arrived in front of the storm. Xiao Yan gathered his energy in his dantian, stood on his toes, tensed his calves, lowered his waist and twisted his body.

His bone wings spread out, sharp as blades, and he began to spin in place. His body spun faster and faster, creating gusts of air, like a shooting star, moving with extreme speed, leaving only countless afterimages. In the end, even the afterimages were swept into the whirlwind.

All his movements were as smooth as flowing water, very natural, like a gazelle hanging its horns without leaving a trace.

In the blink of an eye, Xiao Yan’s figure had disappeared, leaving only a vortex of heaven and earth in his place.

“Storm, unleash the power of a tornado!” Xiao Yan’s voice rang out from within the vortex, urgent and leaving no room for argument. Without the slightest hesitation, he pointed with the Storm Staff, and a cyan tornado came from the horizon, merging into Xiao Yan’s vortex.

Centered on Xiao Yan, with the hurricane as its guard, it pierced through the heavens and earth with extraordinary momentum.

“Purple Shadow, lightning merges!”

“Young Dragon, merge the starlight!”

“Nan Erming, a giant python!” Xiao Yan issued one command after another. Although everyone had some doubts, they did not hesitate at all and poured all their strength into the vortex.

As everyone poured their battle qi into it, the vortex grew larger and larger, drawing in the raging energy of heaven and earth. The powerful energy subtly transformed the area around Xiao Yan into a vacuum. Amidst the rising winds and swirling clouds, Xiao Yan soared into the sky, a massive tornado seemingly pouring down from the heavens directly into the underworld.

Xiao Yan’s figure was hidden within the vortex, the edges of the high-spinning whirlwind faintly brushing against the incredibly sharp light blades of his bone wings, easily slicing space into countless threads. Lightning danced within the whirlwind, starlight flickered on the lightning, and a giant python with an unfathomable length coiled around the whirlwind, its powerful energy causing the sky to change color. Xiao Yan actually wanted to use the speed advantage of his bone wings to form a vortex and merge the energy of four five-star Dou Di!

A mere three-star Dou Di actually wants to bear the energy of four five-star Dou Di? That idea is simply insane! If the four energies are not controlled properly, even by the slightest error, the powerful energy collision can instantly annihilate everything!

That’s incredibly bold! That’s too dangerous! That’s absolutely insane!

When everyone understood Xiao Yan’s intention, their faces turned pale instantly.

However, the energies had already converged, and the powerful battle qi storm prevented anyone from getting close. Like an arrow released from a bow, there was no room for retreat. Everyone’s hearts jumped into their throats, their hearts pounding as if they were about to burst out of their chests.

Their eyes were fixed on Xiao Yan’s direction. If they saw even the slightest sign of something amiss, they would risk their lives to rush in and save Xiao Yan, even if it meant being shattered to pieces. Xiao Yan was caught in the vortex, his face deathly pale.

Cold sweat kept seeping out from the center of the wind, and his soaked black clothes clung tightly to his body. Xiao Yan has never lacked courage. Whether before becoming a Dou Zhe or after becoming a Dou Di, bloodshed accompanied his growth, but Xiao Yan was never reckless.

He knew in his heart that under the shadow of the giant hand and in the face of immense life-or-death pressure, he had to have a trump card that could turn the tide, otherwise the whole team would be wiped out! But everyone has already used up all their tricks and ultimate moves, what can they do? He didn’t know how much longer “Zhentian” could hold out, but he knew that the effects of his “Huntian Pill” were running out.

If he couldn’t defeat “Zhentian” within the effective period, the consequences would be unimaginable! He has to find a way. A bold idea popped into his head: if he could control it well, he was 70% sure he would succeed!

With the life-and-death trials they had endured over the past few days, and the tacit understanding that they could grasp each other’s thoughts with just a glance, he believed that everyone could control the precision of their battle qi output. With his powerful soul force at the late Emperor Realm, he was also confident in controlling the energy of four five-star Dou Di. If successful, he could turn the tide!

Therefore, in this critical moment, Xiao Yan made this gamble!
