Chapter 67: Obtaining Heavenly Fire

Xiao Yan divided his soul power into countless threads, permeating them into the vortex. Following the pathways of the four energies, he painstakingly controlled them amidst the extreme storm, ensuring they followed his every step. Even a piece of paper can easily cut through a body, let alone the powerful energy of four five-star Dou Di within the extreme vortex.

Each time Xiao Yan’s soul power was instantly destroyed by the violent force, it was stubbornly rewoven back together by Xiao Yan, in an endless cycle. The rampant backlash from the energy collisions made Xiao Yan’s sea of soul feel like it was being pierced by a thousand needles and gnawed at by ten thousand ants. Indescribable pain assaulted Xiao Yan’s limits wave after wave.

Large beads of sweat slid down Xiao Yan’s cheeks, and blood seeped from every pore of his body, staining his carefree demeanor red. The vortex in mid-air possessed unimaginably powerful energy, capable of turning clouds into rain with a flick of the wrist. Moreover, in addition to being terrifying, it also had a hint of violent instability.

With the precise control ability of an alchemist, Xiao Yan finally managed to stabilize the several energies through countless attempts. The vortex was still condensing, and the unstable energy gradually calmed down under Xiao Yan’s control. The changes in the world caused by Xiao Yan alarmed countless people, and naturally could not escape the eyes of the “Spiritual Flame”.

The more the originally unstable energy in the vortex calmed down, the more the aura it emitted chilled the “Spiritual Flame”! This was naturally not what the “Spiritual Flame” wanted to see. A deep sense of apprehension flashed in the eyes of the “Spiritual Flame.”

At the same time, it also realized that now was the best time to kill Xiao Yan. As long as the few energies that were not yet fully balanced were destroyed and their power was unleashed, Xiao Yan would surely die without leaving a trace! The giant hand slightly changed direction, striking Xiao Yan with the power to annihilate stars.

Seeing the giant palm strike towards Xiao Yan, Xiao Zhan’s expression changed, and his eyes became sharp. He flicked his sleeves, took a big step forward, and his tiger claws moved with the wind, shooting out rays of light that stretched across the sky to block the giant palm from advancing. Claws and giant hands collided hundreds of times in the air, producing a continuous series of resounding clangs, and the intertwined energy converged into a screen of light.

The shrunken Golden Howling Tiger was even more powerful. Its body, enveloped in golden light, was incredibly lifelike. The enormous “King” character on its forehead shone so brightly that even the stars paled in comparison.

With each swing of its powerful muscles, the colorful black and gold stripes left a trail of beauty in the air. The void was shattered again and again under the tearing of its claws, and the violent energy, like the howling tiger’s unstoppable momentum, continued to rise.

However, the combined power of the ancient savage beast king and the Spirit-Connecting Pill Fire was far more than just the sum of its parts, far exceeding Xiao Zhan’s expectations. Although the Golden Howling Tiger was unstoppable, its giant paw was fierce and heavy, like a mountain crashing down. The terrifying shockwave caused even the thick clouds to shrink back.

The sky was temporarily cloudless, and the light barrier that Xiao Zhan had erected could not stop it at all. It shattered on the spot, turning into streaks of light that disappeared into the air. Without any pause, the giant hand rapidly enlarged, gathering thousands of layers of battle qi, encompassing nebulae within it, like a dome pressing down, instantly plunging the sky into darkness, as if night had fallen.

The black night sky trembled, crushing the Golden Howling Tiger. Its body could move mountains and seas, shatter gold and shatter rocks, but it could not withstand the power of the heavens and the sun and moon. The might of the heavens was immense; the Golden Howling Tiger was slowly swallowed by the night, its brilliant golden light fading, before it was reduced to fragments and instantly obliterated into the darkness.

The collapse of Xiaotianhu had clearly caused some damage to Xiaozhan. He groaned, and a trickle of blood seeped from the corner of his mouth, sliding down his resolute cheek and leaving a bloodstain on his golden armor. But his eyes shone even brighter, and a resolute glint flashed in his black pupils.

Xiao Zhan advanced instead of retreating, puffing out his chest, his entire body covered in armor, and threw a punch straight at the enemy. It was simple and direct, but his fighting spirit was overwhelming, and the air was compressed into a sharp sound. With a will that could shatter the heavens and a will that could break the sky, Xiao Zhan used his chest to provide Xiao Yan with a solid shield.

The straightforward intent of the fist was more powerful than any other form of technique. Xiao Zhan’s punch was incredibly heavy, carrying an irresistible force that seemed to reach the heavens and plunge into hell. It blasted a huge hole in the giant palm that covered the sky, revealing the blue sky behind it.

Sunlight shone directly in through the hole, illuminating a glimmer of hope on everyone’s faces. The giant palm was filled with swirling black mist, like an unstable space. Its color had darkened considerably, but it was still incredibly powerful.

A large amount of gray mist continuously flowed into the giant palm, as if winds and clouds from all directions were gathering, and it slammed into Xiao Zhan’s heavy fist.

“Snap!” A deep sound rang out, and the terrifying power emanating from the giant palm and Xiao Zhan’s heavy fist was awe-inspiring. The rolling black mist was dispersed, and amidst the rampant battle qi, the golden armor covering Xiao Zhan’s fist extended countless spider webs before being shattered and splitting into countless points of light.

The cracks spread upwards, covering the entire armor, like a freezing winter day when a cold front swept in overnight, and thin ice covered the lake surface, freezing the fragile life force beneath the surface, making life so powerless.

“Click” “Click” “Click”… Immediately afterwards, a series of sounds rang out. At first, the order of the shattering was still distinguishable, but in the end, it became so dense that they were indistinguishable.

From the arms to the shoulders and then to the chest, Xiao Zhan’s golden armor was completely destroyed, turning into countless streams of light that quickly disappeared into the air. His armor shattered, and even his undergarments turned to dust. A huge black palm print was deeply dented in Xiao Zhan’s chest.

A mouthful of blood stained the sky red, and his massive body fell helplessly to the ground. The giant hand paused slightly before continuing its descent. The heavy, dark mass still pressed down on the sky, but its descent slowed considerably, clearly indicating that it had expended a great deal of energy in its battle with Xiao Zhan.

Xiao Zhan’s body plummeted into the air, his blood splattering in a broken raindrop pattern. Just as the giant hand was about to catch up with Xiao Zhan, the dark aura was about to devour everything in its path. With no chance to resist, Xiao Zhan stared silently and coldly at the dark clouds that covered the sky, his remaining fighting spirit gathering in his right arm, preparing for the final blow.

Xiao Zhan shielded everyone from the wind and rain with his own body, so how could everyone let Xiao Zhan be put in a life-or-death situation? Lightning flashed across the sky, and a figure caught Xiao Zhan. The purple figure slid down with a twist, narrowly avoiding the giant hand.

Seeing Xiao Zhan, whose breathing was still unstable despite taking the pill, a feeling of anger spread through everyone’s hearts. Their cold eyes stared at the giant hand, as if they wanted to pierce through it and shoot into the heart of the “Spiritual Fire” to freeze everything.

“When the red spider lilies bloom, the starry sky vanishes.” Jenny, who had always been as gentle as a water lily, slowly opened her mouth, but her eyes became even colder. As her hand seals changed, runes shone all over her body, and her fighting spirit surged out, forming a flower seal in front of her chest.

“A brilliant and eloquent remark!” Jenny’s voice fell softly, carrying with it the anger over Xiao Zhan’s injuries. Instantly, beneath the giant palm, above the sky, a flower of unparalleled purity bloomed, as bright as white jade, sparkling and shimmering, swirling in the sky, flawless and immaculate, like a ball of pure white divine light.

The giant hand slashed down like a cliff, pressing down as if a piece of the sky had fallen; while the “other shore flower” bloomed, its silver light dazzling, petals fluttering up and swirling down, indestructible, magnificent yet concealing boundless killing intent. The “Red Spider Lily” is one of the four ancient celestial flowers in legend. It is said that the heavens descend upon this flower to solemnly preach the Dharma.

Those who see it can sever their evil karma. It has unparalleled beauty, but the flower and the leaf never meet, just like a destined missed connection. This makes people feel desolate and regretful despite its sacredness.

Perhaps the red spider lily is too beautiful, and the way of Heaven is to “take from the surplus and give to the deficiency,” so Heaven deprives the flower and leaf of the chance to stay together, allowing them to know each other but never to meet. Behind the flawless beauty lies an incomparably tragic sorrow. Legend has it that the red spider lily only blooms on the banks of the Sanzu River in the underworld, serving as a guide to the other side.

The red spider lily carpets the path to hell, and when it blooms in its most beautiful glory, the soul forgets everything from its previous life, falls into the cursed cycle of reincarnation, and leaves everything behind on the other side. The blooming of flowers signifies reincarnation.

The giant hand, as large as a mountain, seems to be returning to the state before it transformed into a mountain; the giant hand, as large as a cloud, seems to be returning to the state before it transformed into a cloud; the giant hand, as large as the sky, seems to be returning to the state before it transformed into the sky. The gray flames churned erratically, the mist was about to dissipate, and the giant hand became indistinctly chaotic on the horizon, like willow catkins dancing in the air, showing faint signs of disappearing into the sky.

All of this feels like time has turned back! Seeing this, the “Spiritual Flame” was terrified. The already dim gray flames rose again as if swept by a strong wind, and it did its best to channel all its energy into the giant palm.

Although the gray flames were almost transparent and illusory, far from their former power, they were still the sixteenth ranked Heavenly Flame of the Dou Di Continent, and should not be underestimated. The giant hand slowly stabilized. Flowers bloomed everywhere, filling the sky, locked in a stalemate with the giant hand.

Gray nightmares and white brilliance swirled and intertwined, like the boundary between hell and heaven. But the curse of fate for the red spider lily means it cannot maintain its brilliance for long. In less than a moment, the purity of its petals fades away, leaving only a desolate vigil like that of the other shore.

Its white, jade-like exterior can no longer conceal its bleak soul, and it exudes an aura of death. The giant hand, aided by the heavenly fire, regained its power. Although it was not as glorious as before, it still possessed the power to shatter stars and destroy the moon.

It fell from the sky, and endless fighting spirit and moonlight covered the flowers of the other shore. Though the flowers are beautiful and the cycle of reincarnation is strong, things that are too beautiful touch the limits of heaven and earth and are always too short-lived, like the ephemeral beauty of the night-blooming cereus, as the red spider lily slowly disappears into the horizon. As the spider lily withered, Jenny felt as if struck by lightning.

Her brows furrowed, and a mouthful of blood welled up in her throat. After forcibly suppressing it, Jenny’s face turned deathly pale, and her body swayed precariously, as if she had suffered a significant backlash. The giant palm destroyed the Howling Tiger and the Other Shore Flower, but the palm lines also became dim and lifeless.

The clouds collapsed inch by inch, and the dark light disappeared. It had little power left, but it still landed with the determination of a desperate gamble. This palm strike was like a life-or-death move in a game of chess, with black and white pieces locked in a battle!

One move decides the outcome; it’s a gamble on the fate of both sides!

At this moment, as the shadow of death loomed, Xiao Yan, despite paying a considerable price, finally gained complete control of the vortex of heaven and earth. With his strength as a three-star Dou Di in the early stage, he could not possibly control such powerful Dou Qi energy. Therefore, Xiao Yan had already consumed the “Hun Tian Dan”.

Even with his strength increasing dramatically, Xiao Yan still appeared extremely strained under the centrifugal force of the vortex of heaven and earth. His whole body was trembling slightly, and his forehead was covered with bulging veins like earthworms. Blood beads seeped from the pores of his body.

From a distance, he looked like a ferocious and terrifying demon crawling out of a pool of blood. This was only because everyone understood Xiao Yan’s strength and tacitly controlled their output of Dou Qi, keeping all the energy at a balanced critical point. Otherwise, Xiao Yan would have been destroyed by this violent energy long ago.

The giant hand slammed down in an instant, colliding with the vortex. The entire space was like a calm lake tossed with a boulder, creating a thousand waves. The clouds exploded into countless dust particles, boiling together to form a thick fog; the gale swept back, raging aimlessly through the fog; the ground, where the powerful energy wave struck, became eternal ruins; life, except for Xiao Yan and his companions, was utterly still…

Dust filled the air, swirling everywhere. Battle energy cleaved the earth into countless furrows. Zi Ying and Le Shaolong brandished their weapons, unleashing countless bursts of starlight and lightning that intertwined to form a protective shield, shielding everyone inside.

The powerful recoil caused their hands, gripping their weapons, to tremble. Le Shaolong and Zi Ying retreated repeatedly, yet they remained in front of everyone, ensuring that not a single ounce of energy could harm the others.

Meanwhile, Bao Feng and Nan Erming carried the injured Zhen Ni and Xiao Zhan on their backs, hiding behind the two. They stopped in a depression and looked up at Xiao Yan, who was fighting in mid-air, with complicated expressions of admiration, praise, and emotion. From the day they entered the illusionary realm, Xiao Yan, who was initially thought to be under everyone’s protection, completely overturned everyone’s perception.

Although he only possessed the strength of a three-star Dou Di in its early stages, he relied on his powerful Emperor-level soul force and his unparalleled control over the Heavenly Flame to dominate the outcome of many battles, even saving everyone from crisis. Xiao Yan proved his importance to the team with absolute facts, and time and again, everyone truly recognized his leadership position from the bottom of their hearts. In mid-air, the battle was fierce.

A giant palm descended, like mercury pouring down, heavy as ten thousand pounds, seemingly controlling the endless mountains and rivers, like a cage of heaven and earth scattering deadly light, locking onto Xiao Yan. The palm wind intertwined with the vortex, and the wind generated by the rapidly spinning bone wings in the vortex swept across the sky like streaks of lightning, condensing into countless sword beams that slashed and cleaved. When they collided with the giant palm formed by the energy, they produced a sharp metallic tremor.

The extreme speed transformed the intangible into the tangible, and the energy fluctuations that emanated from it made people’s hearts tremble. The void collapsed amidst the intense collision, and space itself distorted.

The vortex struck the giant palm, its fighting spirit flashing and its momentum overwhelming, connecting the entire void as if a sea of stars had fallen, giving people a suffocating feeling; the giant palm, however, was pitch black, like dark clouds pressing down, like a black mountain pressing down, and despite its fierce battle with Xiao and later with Zhen Ni, the giant palm still showed no signs of exhaustion. The entire sky darkened completely, with churning dark clouds pressing down and streaks of purple lightning flashing.

The battle between the two sides had reached a fever pitch. The energy at the center of their fighting spirit could easily annihilate anything. This terrifying scene was chilling, and the terrifying fluctuations directly shook people to their very bones, causing them to tremble involuntarily.

Xiao Yan, who was in the center of the vortex, had his clothes soaked in blood, which flowed down his body.

However, before it could leave his body, it was pulled into the vortex by the air currents, making the entire vortex faintly tinged with dark red blood. The smell of blood wafted in the air, making people nauseous. The power of the rampaging energy has far exceeded Xiao Yan’s expectations and is slowly getting out of control.

If this continues, even if he can hold out until the giant hand is destroyed, Xiao Yan will have almost no chance of survival under the interaction of the two energies. Xiao Yan possessed remarkable courage, but that didn’t mean he would recklessly throw his life away. With a thought in mind, he resolutely made a surprising move, just as he had planned.

Following the direction of the vortex, Xiao Yan quickly retracted his bone wings, enveloping his body within them. The crystal-clear bone wings isolated him from the powerful airflow, making him appear as if he were covered in crystal armor, incredibly hard and indestructible. Xiao Yan let out a long breath, his face regaining a slight rosy hue.

He then circulated his Dou Qi throughout his body to relieve some of the pressure. His brows furrowed as he gazed outside, his lips pressed into a straight line. Time was running out, and the thunderous sounds outside were approaching a frenzy.

Everyone’s hearts were in their throats. Even with eyes as sharp as Zi Ying’s, they couldn’t see clearly what was happening at the energy center. All they could see was a hazy gray, and they couldn’t guess what was going on.

But the barely suppressed energy seemed like a giant hand tightly gripping everyone’s hearts. Now that Zhenni and Xiaozhan are injured and need care, Le Shaolong arranged for Fengbao and Nan Erming to stay behind, and he and Ziying rushed into the storm of fighting spirit, wanting to share Xiao Yan’s burden.

However, their bodies could not even travel a thousand feet before they were bounced back like flies hitting a glass wall. Undeterred, Le Shaolong and Zi Ying charged again, but once more they returned empty-handed. The energy of the two sides fighting was terrifyingly powerful, making it impossible for them to break through!

He tried hundreds of times, blood spurting from his mouth as he crashed into the ground, his eyes filled with the despair of someone in darkness who could see no light, but the result remained unchanged; he still couldn’t break through. Le Shaolong and Zi Ying fell helplessly, their eyes filled with anxiety yet also a glimmer of hope. This glimmer of hope stemmed from their faith in Xiao Yan, a faith that Xiao Yan had proven with countless results.

In fact, Xiao Yan has not disappointed them, at least not so far.

At this moment, the blood-red setting sun had dimmed to the point of being almost invisible, as if bearing endless longing as it witnessed the destruction of life in this space. After Xiao Yan retracted his bone wings, Heavenly Flame protected his body. The azure flames, though restrained, contained immense power, continuously mitigating the impact of the energy striking his body.

Swallowing the mouthful of blood rising in his throat, Xiao Yan turned to look at the setting sun, a resolute glint in his eyes. He leaped up, unfurled his bone wings once more, and using the speed of his rotation, charged towards the very top of the vortex. Blood Sun has completely disappeared, sinking into the mountain peak.

All things wither and decay. Space collapses in large swathes. The vortex continues to shrink under the pressure of the giant hand.

The space within the vortex becomes smaller and smaller, but the energy becomes more solid and terrifying when compressed in a smaller volume. Once compressed to a certain degree and reaching the critical point, it will unleash a world-destroying power. With the aid of the bone wings and the heavenly fire, Xiao Yan passed through layers of air currents and saw a glimmer of light at the top of the vortex.

Where there is light, there is an exit; this might be the only possible way out of the vortex. As he got closer to the light, Xiao Yan felt the pulling force below increasing, like a vacuum black hole with amplified suction, causing his speed to drop abruptly. His body was as heavy as a mountain, his legs felt like they were filled with mercury, and every muscle and cell in his body was being sent downward signals.

His bone wings could no longer maintain their initial speed under the pull of countless air currents, and even the flames of the heavenly fire were reversed under the enormous suction. Looking up from below, it was as if Xiao Yan was not flying up, but flying down from above. Although he was extremely anxious, Xiao Yan remained calm and composed.

He knew very well that the vortex’s energy had completely spiraled out of control and was about to explode. If he couldn’t leave now, he would really perish here. Unfortunately, things in this world often go wrong, like “when it rains, it pours” or “when the boat is slow, it gets caught in a headwind.”

In such a critical moment, Xiao Yan learned what it meant for “misfortunes never come singly”!

Just then, the effects of the “Hun Tian Dan” wore off! Waves of weakness kept washing over me, and my muscles, which had been tense for so long, seemed to want to stretch out and relax. My eyelids grew heavy without my noticing, and I kept wanting to close them and fall asleep.

At this moment, if Xiao Yan pauses or slackens even slightly, he will face utter destruction! Having emerged from a sea of blood and bones, Xiao Yan’s countless life-or-death trials made him even more composed in the most critical moments. He took a deep breath, clenched his fists, his nails digging into his palms, and bit down hard on his lips.

The intense pain from these two points jolted Xiao Yan awake, instantly invigorating him. At the same time, Xiao Yan’s fighting spirit surged, and the Heavenly Fire protected his body. With all his might, he pushed off with both feet and leaped forward like an arrow, passing the exit at the top of the vortex and the sliver of light.

The azure blue above the black mist came into view, and it was so pleasing to the eye. Before he could even take a comfortable breath of fresh air or stretch his aching muscles, Xiao Yan turned around, and in a flash, a fiery lotus appeared in his palm, radiating a chilling energy.

“It’s time to end this.” Xiao Yan shook his head, which had become weak and dizzy from using the Fire Lotus technique, and forced himself to stay alert. His eyes, sharp as swords, were fixed on the vortex below.

“go!” With a flick of his finger, Xiao Yan sent a dark cyan fire lotus streaking across the void, flying towards the center of the vortex and disappearing instantly.

As soon as Huo Lian made her move, Xiao Yan immediately spread his bone wings and retreated quickly with rapid flapping. His figure was so fast that it was like a dream, leaving only a blur. Xiao Yan was able to sense the danger, and the “Spiritual Flame,” as a peak six-star magical beast, was also aware of it.

Just before the Fire Lotus arrived, the “Spiritual Flame” sensed the power of the collision between the two energies, which was beyond its capacity to withstand. Its expression changed drastically, and its eyes revealed a terrifying look of extreme fear. No longer caring about its extremely weak body, it turned around and transformed into a nine-tailed fox.

Its claws moved powerfully, and the brand appeared. The traces of the runes became brighter and brighter. In just a moment longer, the “Spiritual Flame” could escape the danger zone.

If it weren’t for Xiao Yan’s breathtakingly beautiful fire lotus at the end, the “Spiritual Fire” could have truly come true. Unfortunately, there are no “what ifs” in the world. Xiao Yan’s foresight and precise timing completely extinguished any hope of the “Spiritual Fire”.

Just as the rune lit up its final stroke, the fire lotus bloomed at the center of the vortex. The few strands of Dou Qi that Xiao Yan had barely managed to maintain completely broke through the critical point. Dou Qi of different attributes were instantly detonated, and the power of their mutual fusion or repulsion was completely beyond everyone’s expectations.

The void was distorted and collapsed continuously. In an instant, there was only eternal darkness and boundless silence where the vortex was located. Finally, the eternal darkness reached its limit and shattered with a loud bang.

When the void shatters, annihilation ensues! With a deafening roar, a powerful energy wave spread out, and a dazzling light shot straight into the sky, illuminating all directions. The aftershocks swept across the sky and down to the ground, crushing the heavens and rumbling as they came.

Xiao Yan was already tens of thousands of feet in the sky, but his speed of retreat could not outpace the energy fluctuations. In a moment of crisis, Xiao Yan closed his bone wings, and azure heavenly flames swirled around his body, bracing against the remaining force of the storm. Faced with the storm, Xiao Yan was like a small boat in a raging flood, tossing and sinking in the giant waves, only to reappear again and again.

With blood gushing out all the way, he retreated tens of thousands of feet before finally stabilizing himself, but his whole body ached terribly, and deep, bone-revealing marks from the storm were etched into his skin. Fortunately, with the powerful protection of the Heavenly Fire and the indestructible Bone Wings, Xiao Yan was safe and sound!

Meanwhile, in a depression in the ground, Le Shaolong and his group, though far from the battlefield, were still affected. This was because the explosive Dou Qi energy was simply too strong. The power of the “Spiritual Fire” combined with the Dou Qi of the four Dou Di and Xiao Yan’s mixed Heavenly Fire created a chaotic and overwhelming energy field, multiplying its power beyond description.

The situation was critical. Le Shaolong, Zi Ying, Nan Erming and Feng Bao attacked at the same time. Their fighting spirit swept across the front, and as their weapons flashed, the protective shield they built was filled with a shocking sense of power.

The energy storm broke through three shields before disappearing without a trace. Given the distance and the immense power of the attack, wouldn’t Xiao Yan be in grave danger? Upon realizing this, Le Shaolong’s expression changed drastically.

He leaped into the air, his soul power spreading out to search for Xiao Yan’s location. After settling Zhenni and Xiaozhan in, Ziying and Fengbao left Nan Erming to watch over them. They also took to the air, searching for Xiao Yan while keeping watch on the “Spiritual Flame.”

Their calls for Xiao Yan lingered in the air for a long time. The energy burst forth quickly and vanished just as fast.

In the blink of an eye, the thick fog had completely disappeared, leaving only a thin mist like smoke, lingering on the ground that had turned into sand, creating a rather indescribable charm. The crowd’s gazes lingered high above, and as they looked at that tiny black dot, smiles appeared on their lips, and their hearts finally settled. Beneath the vast sky, a black figure stood suspended in mid-air.

Although his clothes were tattered, they could not conceal his gentle and agile demeanor. He looked at the crowd with a pale face and a smile on his face. Xiao Yan slowly unfolded his bone wings, like flowing clouds or a gentle breeze, giving people a sense of ethereal beauty.

At this moment, he looked at everyone, and everything was understood without words. Xiao Yan approached the crowd weakly. Although he had a smile on his face, he could not hide his exhaustion and weakness.

“Young Master Xiao, what’s wrong? Are you injured?” Zi Ying asked anxiously.

“Hehe, it’s nothing, it’s just the weakened state of the Hun Tian Dan.”

“Phew!” Zi Ying breathed a sigh of relief and patted her chest a few times.

“That scared me to death! I’m glad you’re alright.”

“We’re all fine. Zhenni and Xiaozhan are being taken care of by Nan Erming. Young Master Xiao, we searched this place earlier but couldn’t find the Heavenly Fire.

Could it be that the power was too strong and destroyed it?” Zi Ying raised a question.

At this moment, she had a beautiful face, her complexion was slightly pale, and she looked like a pure white lotus flower.

“The Heavenly Fire is indestructible. As long as Heaven and Earth exist, the Heavenly Fire will never be extinguished and will endure forever.” Le Shaolong slowly shook his head, his eyes shining brightly, his gray robes fluttering as he stood there gazing into the distance.

“However, when I searched this space just now, I did not find any related life fluctuations.” Everyone nodded in agreement, saying nothing more, but their eyes inevitably held a deep sense of confusion.

“You judge based on life fluctuations?” Xiao Yan was taken aback, then spoke slowly, casually pointing to a place in the sky where gray mist was drifting. A faint shadow was extremely close to the mist.

It was almost night, and the sky was gray. The shadow had no life fluctuations, and even its aura was extremely weak upon careful sensing.

“This is Heavenly Fire?” As Xiao Yan pointed, the storm’s finger trembled slightly, unable to believe its eyes.

“Yes, I possess the Heavenly Flame, which makes me quite sensitive to this, so it’s easy for me to detect it.” Xiao Yan spoke, his entire being appearing ethereal, his black robes fluttering. He seemed to be in a meditative state, clearly carefully sensing something.

“It was already exhausted from being hit by the previous storm, so it couldn’t withstand such terrifying energy. It was probably stunted and its mind was probably obliterated. It’s in a dazed state now.”

“Your mind has been erased and you are extremely weak, yet you still treat it as your peak and try to sense the fluctuations of life, so it is inevitable that you will miss something.” Xiao Yan was extremely familiar with the Heavenly Flame. He analyzed the reasons and explained them to everyone in detail.

As a result, he finally felt relieved, and the look of exhaustion on his face became even more pronounced. Upon hearing this, everyone suddenly realized what was happening, and their gazes toward the “Spiritual Flame” were filled with complex emotions. After all, it was a heavenly flame, one of the three wonders of the Dou Di Continent, and its newly formed consciousness had been quickly suppressed, which inevitably made people sigh with emotion.

“Regardless, this battle has finally come to an end.” Le Shaolong withdrew his gaze and looked at Xiao Yan.

“First of all, congratulations to Young Master Xiao for getting his wish and obtaining the Heavenly Flame. Your strength can now reach another level.” Everyone stepped forward to congratulate Xiao Yan, their previous gloom and unhappiness vanishing, their genuine joy overflowing.

The journey has yielded an incredible harvest: first, the Spirit Seal Moss; then, two excellent weapons; next, the Bone Wings; and now, the Heavenly Fire. If the Spirit Seal were to be obtained as well, the journey would be nothing short of perfect. A fiery glint flashed in Xiao Yan’s eyes, and his face lit up with joy.

He had already carefully scanned the “Spirit Communication Pill Fire” with his soul power and confirmed that it was no longer a threat and was in a weakened state. This was a godsend for Xiao Yan, so a smile immediately bloomed on his face.

“The most urgent task is for everyone to recover from their injuries and regain their fighting spirit. Shaolong and Ziying are only lightly injured, so they will serve as guards.” Xiao Yan, with his meticulous mind, did not lose his composure despite his joy, and gave the order.

After glancing once more at the silent Heavenly Flame in the distance, Xiao Yan suppressed his urge to immediately subdue it, slowly exhaled a breath of stale air, and sat down cross-legged.

At this moment, the side effects of the “Hun Tian Dan” were still lingering. As Xiao Yan relaxed, he suddenly felt dizzy and his body desperately wanted to stretch out completely. Unable to resist glancing back at the Heavenly Flame one last time, Xiao Yan sighed.

The Heavenly Flame was so close, practically within reach at his speed, but his current physical condition was far from the best time to subdue and devour it. Xiao Yan sighed helplessly, adjusted his mindset, took a pill, and sat cross-legged to recover.

After receiving their orders, Le Shaolong and Zi Ying gathered everyone around Xiao Yan in a circle so that they could respond as quickly as possible should any unexpected events occur…As the moon set and the sun rose, and the first rays of dawn kissed the horizon, Xiao Yan exhaled the last breath of stale air and stood up. His figure was upright, and the dust on his clothes could not conceal his dashing demeanor.

“Young Master Xiao, are you fully recovered?” Le Shaolong and the others had already recovered from their injuries and stood by. After all, the injuries suffered by the others were not serious, while Zhen Ni and Xiao Zhan, although seriously injured, had taken the medicine before Xiao Yan and were naturally recovering faster than Xiao Yan.

Xiao Yan nodded, feeling no abnormality in the power emanating from his body.

“How did Young Master Xiao come up with that move? It terrified us.”

At this moment, everyone surrounded Xiao Yan, their eyes filled with admiration. Although Xiao Yan’s move was the key to turning the tide of the battle and even played a decisive role in deciding the outcome, everyone was well aware of the great risks involved. Xiao Yan was practically gambling with his life.

“It’s nothing. I just came up with this idea in a moment of inspiration, so I wanted to give it a try. I’m sorry to have worried everyone.” Xiao Yan smiled, but his smile still revealed a hint of lingering fear after surviving a close call.

It had been far too risky. Even after taking the “Hun Tian Dan” and greatly increasing his strength, he almost lost control of the energy in the vortex and destroyed himself within it. With a flick of his sleeve, Xiao Yan brushed the dust off his body and looked at the leaping gray flames.

Unlike Xiao Yan and the others who had top-grade pills to restore their “Spiritual Flame,” it remained illusory and unstable, having not recovered much and appearing somewhat dim and lackluster. Upon reaching the “Spiritual Flame,” Xiao Yan stood in mid-air, his black robes fluttering, his hands trembling slightly with excitement as he concealed them within his sleeves. The prospect of acquiring the third Heavenly Flame after the two Heavenly Flames, and one of even higher grade than the Chaos Sacred Flame, filled Xiao Yan with ecstatic joy.

With perfect understanding, everyone stood around Xiao Yan, forming a circle, ready to protect him from the Heavenly Flame.

“Let’s begin now. Taking advantage of the Heavenly Fire’s weakness is the best opportunity.” Zhenni had fully recovered, her complexion radiant, like a thin cloud veiling the bright moon, like a gentle breeze caressing jade flowers, her eyes like water, and she smiled softly, genuinely happy for Xiao Yan.

Xiao Yan stretched out his hand, and dark cyan heavenly fire surged forth, testing the reaction of the “Spiritual Pill Fire.” The gray flame swayed slightly, and the flame leaped up a few feet with difficulty before shrinking back to its original size, appearing somewhat listless.

However, the inherent nobility in Xiao Yan’s heart still caused the dark cyan heavenly fire in his hand to shrink slightly. Xiao Yan summoned the “Spiritual Flame,” but instead of sitting down to subdue and devour it, he looked toward the mountain where the Martial Ape had been and slowly said, “Perhaps there is a place more suitable for me to subdue and devour this Heavenly Flame.”

Upon hearing Xiao Yan’s words, everyone immediately recalled the previous behavior of the “Spiritual Flame” repeatedly fleeing towards the mountain peak, and their eyes lit up as they stared in the direction of the mountain peak. Without saying anything more, Xiao Yan stood up, a silver light flashing beneath his feet, and his figure had already reached the ruined mountaintop. The towering tree had collapsed, its scattered branches and leaves lying on the ground, stuck together with the dust and sand, no longer possessing its former grandeur.

Due to the excessive force of the fall, a section of the gray giant tree with its tightly rooted roots was forcibly pulled out of the soil, revealing a dark cave below. Gusts of hot wind rushed out of the cave, the scorching air baking the sand near the cave entrance until it turned slightly yellow, carrying the slight scorching of the tree roots, which faintly entered Xiao Yan’s nose
