Chapter 68: Devouring the Spirit-Connecting Pill Flame

“I thought it would take a lot of effort to find the place where the Heavenly Fire lingers, but I didn’t expect to find it so easily because of the impact of the battle just now.” Xiao Yan could no longer hide the joy in his eyes as he felt the faint residual aura of the Spirit-Connecting Pill Fire coming from the bottom of the cave.

“Young Master Xiao, are you sure you’re here?” The others followed, and Xiao Zhan stepped forward, seemingly casually, yet perfectly positioned himself diagonally in front of Xiao Yan. After all, the cave had not yet been explored, and Xiao Zhan’s position was just right to prevent any unforeseen events.

Xiao Yan could easily see through Xiao Zhan’s thoughts. A warmth spread through Xiao Yan’s heart, and his grateful gaze fell upon Xiao Zhan. He nodded slightly, which was an affirmation of Xiao Zhan’s intentions and a confirmation that he would subdue the Devouring Heavenly Flame here.

Upon confirmation, Xiao Zhan enveloped his fist in golden light and slammed it down with a powerful punch. Everyone naturally knew that this was not the real entrance, otherwise it wouldn’t have appeared at the base of the giant tree.

However, since they all led to the same place, it didn’t matter which way they entered. The heavy punch resounded like thunder, blasting a gap large enough for two people to pass through the hard floor of the cave entrance. Scalding heat rushed in, making even breathing feel fiery hot, pouring down the throat and into the lungs, as if trying to evaporate all the blood in the body.

Jenny’s sleeve fluttered slightly, and her fighting spirit surged forth, blocking the surging heat. Through the dim light, everyone peered into the cave below. The cave is not very deep, but it is quite spacious, with only one large hall.

It seems to have been carved out from a single piece of bluish-gray rock, giving it a natural and ancient feel. As if intimidated by the lingering aura of the heavenly fire below, despite Jenny’s ability to insulate against most of the heat, the air around everyone still became restless and turbulent. The resulting hot wind blew everyone’s clothes tightly against their bodies, making Jenny’s charming figure appear even more graceful.

Her long hair cascaded down her full breasts, and her pear-shaped face was rosy and exuded a silent allure. A casual glance from Xiao Yan suddenly ignited a surge of desire within him. By the time he realized it, his face was flushed.

Fortunately, everyone was focused on what was happening below and didn’t notice Xiao Yan’s change. While marveling at Zhen Ni’s beauty, Xiao Yan quickly suppressed his burgeoning thoughts, glancing around shyly. Seeing that no one was paying attention, he hurriedly followed everyone’s gaze and looked down.

Perhaps due to the intense heat, wisps of mist rose from below, gradually thickening from the surrounding area to the center, finally condensing in the very center of the stone chamber, so thick it seemed to churn endlessly like liquid, as if that were the source of the heat. Xiao Yan’s soul power swept around the stone chamber several times in an instant, but he found nothing unusual. He really hadn’t expected that such a powerful heavenly fire resided in such a simple stone chamber.

“There should be no danger down there. Since you don’t have the protection of the Heavenly Fire, you’ll inevitably suffer from heat poisoning if you go down. It’s better to stay up here.”

Xiao Yan’s words were true. Although Xiao Yan’s strength was far inferior to the others, with the Heavenly Flame protecting him, even the hottest places had little effect on him, and might even have helped his cultivation. After arranging for everyone to rest and guard the area, Xiao Yan nodded gently to Zhen Ni’s worried eyes to reassure her, and then lightly leaped into the stone chamber.

The air currents created by Xiao Yan’s landing stirred up the fog that filled the room. The scorching air currents instantly engulfed Xiao Yan in the fog. Xiao Yan suddenly felt as if night had fallen, his vision became extremely limited, and the temperature around him rose sharply, becoming scalding hot.

With the Heavenly Fire protecting him, Xiao Yan was not afraid of the high temperature, but it would take time for his eyesight to adjust. After a moment, Xiao Yan’s vision gradually cleared. He looked around and saw a dark cave on the right side of the stone chamber, winding upwards at an unknown distance.

“This is probably where the Heavenly Flame usually enters and exits,” Xiao Yan guessed. Xiao Yan slowly withdrew his gaze and looked at the center of the stone chamber. There was a stone pool in the center, where black and red magma was boiling and churning like boiling water, splashing into the dark stone chamber from time to time, like red diamonds set in the night sky, or like stars scattered across the sky and then falling to the ground.

“No wonder it desperately wanted to come here when it was exhausted. This place is actually connected to the fire at the earth’s core, and the power of the flames is extremely rich. It is indeed an excellent place for the Heavenly Fire to recover.” Xiao Yan said softly, finally confirming his judgment on the motive of the “Spiritual Pill Fire” and couldn’t help but sigh.

Such a place, teeming with the power of flames, could be considered the best place to subdue the Devouring Heavenly Flame. Xiao Yan’s eyebrows lifted slightly, a hint of joy in them.

Suddenly, his hand trembled, and the flame of the “Spiritual Fire” began to flicker. The earth fire seemed to sense the call of the heavenly fire, continuously pouring into the “Spiritual Fire.” The “Spiritual Fire” immediately felt like it had been parched for a long time and began to greedily absorb the energy of the earth fire, while also showing a faint resistance to the dark blue heavenly fire that enveloped it.

Xiao Yan frowned slightly, gently channeled his Dou Qi, and poured it into the dark cyan Heavenly Flame in his hand. The dark cyan Heavenly Flame immediately surged, severing the connection between the Earth Fire and the “Spiritual Pill Fire”. The “spiritual fire” immediately shrank after its source of heat was cut off.

“It seems we need to hurry up and subdue it, otherwise once it has a chance to recover, it will never be docile again.” Xiao Yan didn’t delay any longer. He quickly sat cross-legged near the central magma, regulated his breathing and circulated his qi, slowly expelling the turbid qi from his body and adjusting himself to his optimal state.

Glancing at the still-dormant “Spiritual Flame” in his hand, Xiao Yan, despite suppressing his excitement, couldn’t help but swallow hard. The third Heavenly Flame, and such a powerful one at that! Once subdued and absorbed, both his strength and the tempering of his body would be greatly enhanced.

How could Xiao Yan not be overjoyed! With a single thought, Xiao Yan unleashed a surge of dark cyan heavenly fire that enveloped his entire body, flowing ceaselessly through his muscles and meridians, forming a thick protective layer. He then clenched his fists, unleashing tremendous power.

Nodding in satisfaction, Xiao Yan’s eyes were clear as water. He opened his mouth and swallowed the “Spiritual Fire”. Although the “Spiritual Flame” was unstable and extremely weak, its instincts remained.

The powerful flames surged forth like flowing water, becoming fierce and sharp. A powerful aura, like a volcanic eruption, surged upwards, rushing straight towards Xiao Yan’s entire body, seemingly intending to burn Xiao Yan’s body to ashes. Once the “Spiritual Fire” exploded, its sixteenth-ranked pressure immediately spread out, and the Heavenly Fire within Xiao Yan’s body felt slightly sluggish, as if a huge rock was blocking the abyss as it flowed.

The originally surging waves of Heavenly Fire were immediately weakened considerably. The gray flames surged like a vast ocean, scorching every inch of Xiao Yan’s meridians and bones, then roasting and ravaging every cell within him. The excruciating pain seemed to penetrate to the very marrow of his bones, causing Xiao Yan to break out in a cold sweat that soaked through his clothes, clinging tightly to his body.

His face was deathly pale, veins bulging like the gnarled roots of an ancient tree, his fingernails piercing the flesh of his palms and digging into his meridians. The fact that the already extremely weakened Heavenly Fire still possessed such power was completely beyond Xiao Yan’s expectations. If it were at its peak, wouldn’t that be…?

Xiao Yan dared not think any further. Clenching his teeth, Xiao Yan struggled to raise his head, his resolute gaze sharpening like a drawn sword, his imposing aura and soaring fighting spirit filling the sky. Having journeyed from Dou Zhe, Xiao Yan had absorbed countless strange flames, enduring every excruciating pain, every burning agony, every brush with death, and he would overcome it all.

This time would be no exception! Xiao Yan’s gaze slowly shifted from resolute to determined.

“Resolute” represents an unwavering determination. Xiao Yan bit his lip, and a salty taste immediately spread down his throat, turning into a sharp pain that jolted his nerves and instantly focused his mind. Before he could catch his breath, Xiao Yan clenched his fists, mobilized all his fighting spirit, and poured it into the dark blue heavenly fire.

With the help of Dou Qi, the dark blue Heavenly Flame that had retreated from Xiao Yan’s body suddenly brightened up and burned fiercely on Xiao Yan’s body, reflecting a blue glow in the dark stone chamber. It quickly overcame its fear of the “Spiritual Flame” and released powerful energy. The dark blue heavenly fire leaped and blazed intensely, instantly surpassing the temperature of the earth fire in the stone chamber.

With a flick of his finger, Xiao Yan caused the boiling earth fire in the magma to continuously form a pillar of fire that enveloped him, as if he were wearing a colorful and scorching suit of fire armor. The heavenly fire ignited the earthly fire, causing it to swarm around the body. The combined power of the two created an intense heat that ignited the floor of the stone chamber.

In the blink of an eye, the floor was scorched, and the charred surface of the blue stone floor turned into a shiny porcelain-like surface. Terrifying flames swirled around Xiao Yan’s body, coiling like dragons and snakes, distorting the void. If it weren’t for the protection of the Heavenly Fire, or if Xiao Yan hadn’t mastered the Burning Technique, he would probably have turned into wisps of black smoke and vanished into the world in no time under this power.

However, the two Heavenly Flames that Xiao Yan possessed were among the top-ranked Heavenly Flames on the Dou Di Continent, and their power was quite extraordinary. Facing the violent flames that enveloped him, Xiao Yan’s eyes showed joy rather than worry. The dark cyan heavenly fire was extremely domineering and powerful, exuding a terrifying aura that made people tremble.

The earth fire power could not penetrate it at all. On the contrary, under the tempering of the heavenly fire, the violent flame power was constantly transformed into pure energy and injected into Xiao Yan’s body and the heavenly fire. The dark blue heavenly fire burned brighter and brighter, and Xiao Yan’s eyes also grew brighter.

Ever since he found this place, he had made a plan in his mind. He wanted to use the power of the earth fire to temper his body and strengthen the heavenly fire, as a great help in subduing and devouring the “Spiritual Pill Fire”. The “Spiritual Flame” never dreamed that the Earth Fire Land on which it relied for growth and recovery would now become the place where Xiao Yan devoured it.

Of course, this is inseparable from Xiao Yan’s meticulousness. Xiao Yan’s ability to get to where he is today is naturally beyond the reach of ordinary people in terms of insight and mental strength. The movements of the “Spiritual Flame” when it escaped were too obvious.

After careful analysis, Xiao Yan easily found this place and used his familiarity with flames over the years to control the earth fire to help him.

At this moment, the “Spiritual Flame” was surging throughout Xiao Yan’s body, with the gray flames fighting against the dark blue heavenly fire. Occasionally, a wisp of flame would seep out and touch Xiao Yan’s meridians and bones, causing a sizzling sound like boiling oil being poured onto a cold block of ice. The excruciating pain was so intense that even the resolute Xiao Yan couldn’t help but groan.

Xiao Yan’s hand seals changed rapidly, and the dark cyan heavenly fire suddenly became violent, frantically absorbing the energy of the earth fire. The surging energy flowed like rivers flowing into the sea, flowing into Xiao Yan’s body along with the heavenly fire, causing his aura to surge at a visible rate. Without a moment’s hesitation, Xiao Yan took a deep breath, and the dark cyan heavenly fire surrounding his body, carrying the power of earth fire, suddenly retracted inward.

The power of the flames flowed through every pore of his body to his muscles, then surged to his bones and entered his meridians. At the same time, the heavenly fire that was slowly circulating in his dantian began to stir, and the scorching energy responded from the inside out, attacking from both inside and outside.

Xiao Yan took a slight breath, and without pausing, the soul power of the late Emperor Realm in his sea of consciousness surged up like a surging wave, roaring out of his sea of consciousness, and then splitting into countless streams, each controlling the Heavenly Fire flowing through every part of his body. The power of the soul shifted from fierce to gentle, from fast to slow, from dynamic to static in the blink of an eye.

Guided by the power of the soul, the dark cyan heavenly fire retaliated, splitting the gray flames scattered throughout the meridians at an extremely fast speed, and annihilating them one by one. Instinctively sensing something was wrong, the gray flames of the “Spiritual Fire” transformed into streaks of gray light, rushing forward madly. The intense heat scorched and blurred the Dou Qi in his meridians, creating a hazy, primordial chaos.

He could vaguely hear the flames’ low roars and howls, as if they were somewhat afraid and unwilling to accept Xiao Yan’s resistance. Despite the excruciating pain, Xiao Yan continued to urge on the Heavenly Fire, accelerating the conversion of Earthly Fire energy to fight against the gray flames. The two colors of energy meet and refuse to give way, their scorching energy colliding and flowing with a hazy current, like mortal enemies vying for territory.

Within Xiao Yan’s body, the dark cyan Heavenly Flame and the “Spiritual Pill Flame” engaged in a game of chess, with the meridians as the chessboard, the Heavenly Flame as the chess pieces, and the soul power as the chess players. The battle was sharp and left no room for compromise, and in the end, it could only end with one side surrendering. The gray flames were powerful but had lost their intelligence; they were fierce but currently in a weakened state.

Xiao Yan’s dark cyan heavenly fire, though of slightly lower grade, was a fusion of two heavenly fires, making it exceptionally powerful. With the assistance of his soul power and earth fire energy, the gray flames scattered in various places gradually became chaotic, like pieces on a chessboard, unable to find their place. It was as if a red-hot iron sword had been plunged into ice water, melting a large area and nearly being annihilated.

Overjoyed at the success of his move, Xiao Yan pressed his advantage, his Heavenly Fire falling swiftly like a chess piece, each move resounding with every strike. He relentlessly pursued the remaining pieces, and the gray flames in his meridians, unable to withstand the onslaught, were instantly subdued, melting away like snowflakes and completely merging into Xiao Yan’s meridians, flowing towards his dantian. The stone chamber remained as dark as night.

The firelight reflected from the churning lava highlighted Xiao Yan’s pale face, which lacked color. Cold sweat streamed down his resolute cheeks, but his lips were bitten until they bled, the bright red color almost dripping down, like flames burning. After the scattered gray flames were subdued, they traveled along the meridians in Xiao Yan’s body.

The meridians, which had been scorched and damaged like withered grass in autumn wind and withered trees in winter frost, seemed to be nourished by the gentle rain after the spring breeze, and then began to repair themselves at an extremely fast speed. A smile appeared on Xiao Yan’s lips. Although the smile was distorted by the pain, the joy was still very evident.

If even a small portion of the scattered gray flames was so powerful, wouldn’t he achieve immortality after completely subduing and devouring them? Suppressing his excitement, Xiao Yan mobilized all his Dou Qi and Heavenly Fire to approach the “Spiritual Pill Fire,” scattering green pieces across the chessboard, leaving only one spot for the gray pieces to fight like trapped beasts. Seemingly sensing something extremely wrong, the “Spiritual Flame” transformed into a dazzling gray light, attempting to break free.

The scorching temperature caused Xiao Yan’s blood to thicken, and the vitality of his internal organs was rapidly draining away. Xiao Yan suppressed the sweet taste rising in his throat. With a thought, the dark cyan heavenly fire immediately enveloped the gray flames.

He unleashed his fighting spirit, soul power, and earth fire energy without restraint, completely cutting off any possibility of the “Spiritual Pill Fire” escaping. The two flames collided, producing a hissing sound of incompatibility, which resounded like ten thousand drums within Xiao Yan’s body.

The clash between the two great heavenly fires was sometimes like a thousand arrows piercing the heart, causing excruciating pain; sometimes like a whirlwind sweeping away clouds, the passing wind like falling knives, cutting everything in its path; sometimes like a mountain of knives and a sea of fire, the heat soaring to the heavens, burning everything in its path… Sweat turned into blood, and Xiao Yan’s body trembled incessantly.

His black clothes turned a dark purple from being soaked in blood, and the veins on his neck were clearly visible, like vines convulsing. Under the firelight, Xiao Yan looked like a demon crawling out of hell, extremely terrifying. Time flowed by like water, and Xiao Yan gradually calmed down, standing motionless like a stone statue, merging with the earth fire, blending with the night, and coexisting with the heavenly fire.

His mind was at peace, as if he were one with heaven and earth and all things. His soul power took shape, like a gentle breeze, feeling the pulse of the heavenly fire, and gradually saw the dawn of subjugation. The already weakened “Spiritual Fire” within the body had been resisting repeatedly, and its aura was extremely feeble.

Under the devouring of the dark blue heavenly fire, its energy continued to be lost, and its volume became smaller and smaller, shrinking into a ball in an attempt to put up a last-ditch resistance. Xiao Yan raised his head, his eyes filled with increasing joy. He took a deep breath and summoned the Heavenly Fire to pounce on the “Spirit Communication Pill Fire” with lightning speed.

The “Spirit Communication Pill Fire’s” attempts to resist were futile. It merely struggled for a moment before the gray flames flickered and dimmed, completely subdued. Looking inward at the “Spiritual Flame” suspended within his body, he saw that it was pure and without a trace of impurity, crystal clear like a piece of gray amber.

Endless vitality emanated from the rising gray flames, as if the flames were breathing with boundless energy. Xiao Yan’s tightly pursed lips tilted slightly with excitement, and his knuckles, clenched tightly with excitement, appeared somewhat blue. At the same time, the gray flames rippled, and a large amount of clear information appeared in Xiao Yan’s mind.

Spiritual Flame, formed from the flames of ancient mythical beasts through the long passage of time, often manifests in the form of various ancient savage beasts. While its power is not exceptionally strong, it is extremely protective of its master and possesses a remarkable ability to restore life force. It can quickly heal severe injuries and has a remarkable effect on nourishing elixirs during alchemy…

After briefly glancing at the information about the “Spiritual Flame,” Xiao Yan smiled. The things that had previously puzzled him suddenly became clear. Recalling the scene of the “Spiritual Flame” transforming into nine beasts to frighten everyone before it arrived to search the nine mountains, Xiao Yan’s smile widened.

“Kid, not bad.” A soft voice interrupted Xiao Yan’s thoughts. Who else could it be but Master Zhan? Xiao Yan looked up at Elder Zhan, who was floating in the dark stone chamber, and complained, “Your appearance was perfectly timed.

You didn’t even offer to help when we were fighting the Heavenly Flame.” Old Master Zhan smiled and stroked his beard, his white robes fluttering as if he could ride the wind and depart at any moment. His elegance and grace remained as always.

He slowly spoke, “How can one grow without experiencing hardship? How can one become an eagle without weathering storms?”

“Tch! How dare you make me, a Dou Di at the early stage of three stars, take such a huge risk to fight for the final blow?” Xiao Yan felt speechless, utterly speechless.

“This…this is indeed a bit dangerous, but facing death can stimulate one’s potential more quickly. At least you’re standing here safely now, aren’t you?” A hint of embarrassment flashed across Zhan Lao’s face as he glanced at Xiao Yan and quickly changed the subject: “Kid, you’re quite lucky.

This is the sixteenth ranked Heavenly Flame in the Dou Di Continent, containing boundless vitality.” Even though he knew Elder Zhan was changing the subject, the phrase “the sixteenth ranked Heavenly Flame” still drew Xiao Yan’s attention back to the Heavenly Flame. Xiao Yan only knew that the “Spiritual Pill Flame” was stronger than his own Mixed Heavenly Flame and its ranking was definitely above the Chaos Sacred Flame, but he didn’t really know the exact difference.

“The sixteenth?” Xiao Yan wanted to make sure he hadn’t misheard. Old Zhan’s white beard moved without wind, and he nodded with a smile.

He believed that Xiao Yan should already know the other information about the “Spiritual Flame” after subduing the Heavenly Flame. Eager to examine the “Spiritual Flame” within his body, Xiao Yan moved his mind, and the “Spiritual Flame” moved at his will, slowly traveling towards his dantian. Wherever it passed, the gray flames spread boundless vitality, quickly repairing the injuries to his body caused by the previous battle.

Like the most effective healing medicine in the world, gray flames spread from the center of the Heavenly Fire Source, slowly engulfing his entire body. His withered meridians were like parched land receiving sweet rain, or like grasslands after a great fire receiving several gentle spring rains, bursting forth with boundless vitality. In just a moment, Xiao Yan’s injuries were completely healed, and his body was filled with an explosive power.

When he clenched his fist, a slight sonic boom could be heard in the air.

“Although it’s only ranked two places higher than the Chaos Sacred Flame, its recovery ability is incredibly strong, and its power is extraordinary. It can even be said to be unique among heavenly flames. Haha, with it, wouldn’t I have the same recovery ability as the Gongwu Ape before?

Haha, what a wonderful thing!” Xiao Yan was overjoyed. Looking up at Elder Zhan, Xiao Yan smiled slightly, a hint of eagerness in his smile.

Looking at Xiao Yan’s burning gaze, Elder Zhan looked up at the top of the stone chamber, his deep eyes seemingly piercing through the thick gray stone layers. The night through the stone chamber entrance was not dark, but rather hazy, as if moonlight was shining down and letting in a sliver of light. Xiao Zhan’s focused attention and Le Shaolong’s vigilant stance with his blade earned a slight nod from Elder Zhan.

Jenny appeared beside the entrance, her hazy figure gazing downwards, seemingly worried about Xiao Yan’s safety. Under the hazy moonlight, Jenny Tseng seemed to emerge from the water. In the misty air, her fair body was graceful and alluring, with a slender waist and straight legs that could be vaguely seen.

Her swaying figure was extremely seductive.

“Such charm and allure, she can truly be called a rare beauty! Young man, you’re quite lucky with women!” Even Zhan Lao, who had lived for countless years and seen through the ups and downs of life, couldn’t help but sigh in his heart.

Slowly withdrawing his gaze, knowing that with everyone protecting him, Xiao Yan was naturally out of danger, Elder Zhan said quietly, “Look how anxious you are, kid. Go ahead, your strength will increase after absorbing the Heavenly Flame, which will be of great help to your future path.” Before he could finish speaking, Elder Zhan’s body became ethereal.

He glanced at Zhen Ni at the cave entrance, uttered his last words, and then vanished without a trace.

“Hey kid, don’t keep the beauty waiting too long, haha…” Xiao Yan’s body suddenly stiffened. The image of Zhen Ni appeared in his mind, and he felt a sweet warmth in his heart, but his face showed an extremely helpless expression.

At this moment, important matters must be taken care of; this is not the time for sentimentality. As Elder Zhan said, devouring the Heavenly Flame is related to the improvement of Xiao Yan’s strength, and it is the most important thing for Xiao Yan Xiao Yan calmed his fluctuating emotions, focusing his gaze on his nose, then his mouth, and finally his heart, gradually becoming one with his surroundings as he observed his internal body. After the “Spiritual Flame” was subdued, it had healed all of Xiao Yan’s injuries.

Xiao Yan carefully examined his body for a long time and felt that his spiritual sense was sharp and his life essence was strong, completely at its peak. It was time to devour the “Spiritual Flame”. With his mind at ease, Xiao Yan directed the “Spiritual Flame” to slowly move into his dantian.

Within his dantian, the gray flame, pure as jade and radiating light, hovered quietly, completely unaware of the approaching danger. Within his dantian, a vast and boundless world stretched out before him. Xiao Yan urged his dark cyan heavenly fire to slowly approach the “Spiritual Flame,” as naturally as jade seeking to approach a precious stone, without the slightest anxiety or unease.

The gray flames remained still, and the atmosphere was very peaceful. Facing the sixteenth-ranked Heavenly Flame, Xiao Yan dared not be careless in the slightest. His powerful soul force, like the moon’s reflection on water, urged his fighting spirit to gather into a river, flowing into his dantian and continuously injecting the dark cyan Heavenly Flame.

The dark cyan heavenly fire was surrounded by fighting spirit, rising like mist, making the dark cyan color even more dazzling. As the flames swayed, they trembled like flawless jade, bringing with them a green glow. Powerful energy fluctuated endlessly within the dantian.

Seemingly sensing a hint of unease, the gray flames of the “Spiritual Fire” gradually intensified, rippling around him like waves, and like wisps of smoke, preventing the approaching battle qi.

“Truly worthy of being ranked sixteenth among the Heavenly Flames, its self-defense awareness is so strong.” Xiao Yan sighed, maintaining the infusion of Dou Qi into the dark cyan Heavenly Flame without making any hasty moves, until the dark cyan Heavenly Flame transformed into pure green, the flames glowing with a faint, hazy light like a lotus flower. The dark cyan heavenly fire had reached its peak, but Xiao Yan still did not make any rash moves.

His fighting spirit was as gentle as a warm spring breeze, gently probing the “spiritual flame” time and time again, tender and without malice, like a gentle caress. This made the “spiritual flame” feel very comfortable, as if everything was normal and there was no danger. The gray flame gradually calmed down again.

Just as the “Spiritual Flame” relaxed its guard, Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up with joy. He uttered the word “swallow,” and the dark cyan heavenly fire suddenly became violent, instantly engulfing the “Spiritual Flame.” Flames danced within his dantian, and gray mist continued to gather.

Sensing danger, the gray flames suddenly became incredibly hot. Even though they were enveloped by dark blue heavenly fire, waves of heat still radiated outwards, scorching his dantian like a steamer placed over a raging fire. Heavenly fire surged, enveloping the gray flames.

Xiao Yan embraced it with his heart and guided it with the power of his soul, concentrating his mind highly. The dark blue heavenly fire burned like a dark blue sun, constantly devouring the gray flames. Heavenly fire burned fiercely, its flames roaring and clashing, producing a resounding clang as it clashed with the other side.

It transformed the formless into the tangible, almost tangible, and its violent energy seemed intent on burning away the nine heavens and turning Xiao Yan to ashes. Xiao Yan frowned, his skin was a terrifying crimson, as if it were about to bleed, and hot air was constantly emanating from his pores. The heat mixed with the fog from the underground fire outside, so thick it was impossible to disperse, enveloping the entire stone chamber like a deep abyss of mist.

The fog also carried rolling heat, scorching the entire blue stone chamber and Xiao Yan’s body and soul. My lips were cracked, my eyes were unfocused, and I was losing a lot of moisture. The waves of pain from the flames felt like 36,000 silver needles piercing every cell in my body.

The burning pain made me want to scream and go crazy. My skin turned ashen and wrinkled like a withered tree in just a few seconds, losing its original luster and elasticity. Xiao Yan never imagined that devouring the Heavenly Flame would be so difficult.

The gray flames surged and crashed within his dantian, occasionally breaking through the encirclement of the dark blue Heavenly Flame with their intense heat. The heat that seeped out clung tightly to the inner membrane of his dantian, leaving countless marks on it like a red-hot iron, and the smell of burning was foul. Xiao Yan gritted his teeth, and the dark blue sea of fire rolled up boundless waves, blocking the gray flames and concealing them inside.

A massive amount of earth fire energy, tempered by heavenly fire, continuously gathered in his dantian, suppressing the gray flames and preventing them from leaking out. After a struggle, with the combined assistance of Xiao Yan’s powerful soul force, fighting spirit, and earth fire, the gray flames were completely enveloped in dark cyan flames and burned fiercely, reducing the scope of the confrontation. Xiao Yan gathered all his fighting spirit and merged it into the dark cyan heavenly fire, constantly absorbing the energy of the gray flames.

The gray energy’s resistance would occasionally surge into towering flames, scorching Xiao Yan’s internal organs and causing him unbearable pain. Time passed swiftly amidst the agonizing torment, slipping through fingers like water, making it difficult to discern the days, months, and hours. The powerful life-restoring ability of the “Spiritual Fire” indefinitely prolonged this battle of heavenly fire.

Having absorbed an unknown amount of fire energy, Xiao Yan transformed the energy of the gray flames into his own and then used it to suppress the “Spiritual Pill Fire.” His days were spent in this repeated torment of absorption, transformation, suppression, and devouring. Finally, one day, the gray mist began to thin.

Xiao Yan let out a long breath. The prolonged burning had made his throat dry and sore, making even swallowing difficult. He licked his cracked lips, rubbed his dry eyelids, and tugged at his clothes, which were already stuck to his body with blood.

Xiao Yan sighed and forced himself to continue devouring the food. Time flew by, and another month passed. The gray flames finally dimmed, hanging there quietly like a piece of gray jade, radiating intense energy.

The “Spiritual Flame” was finally completely consumed! Before Xiao Yan could even rejoice, he immediately sensed the changes within himself. His essence, energy, and spirit were elevated, his battle qi surged, and gray energy erupted from the center of the flames, flowing through his meridians and into his entire body.

Wherever it passed, his meridians were rapidly repaired, his bones were reconnected, and his skin was reborn, regaining its vitality. His previously scattered eyes, like sharp swords piercing the sky, emitted a light that was almost tangible. As if a silver needle had pierced a balloon, or a boulder had smashed through ice, Xiao Yan felt as if his internal fighting spirit had found a breakthrough.

His three-star initial stage, which had been nearing a bottleneck after absorbing the Earth Explosion Heavenly Fire but had been stalled for a long time, was easily broken through. Endless energy of heaven and earth passed through the entrance of the stone chamber, driving the earth fire power in the stone pool, and frantically poured into Xiao Yan’s body, cleansing all the tendons and meridians in his body, strengthening all his muscles. His skin even shone with a jade-like luster under the firelight.

His fighting spirit surged and became powerful in his dantian, surrounded by chaotic mist, mysterious and hazy. Xiao Yan truly felt the immense power. Finally reaching the mid-stage of the three-star Dou Di realm, Xiao Yan’s eyes burned with the fervor of the midday sun.

He felt the terrifying Dou Qi within his body surging like a mighty river, and the powerful Dou Qi fluctuations with every move he made seemed capable of piercing the sky, sinking the earth, and drying up great rivers. With a long roar to the sky, Xiao Yan felt extremely comfortable.

However, to his surprise, the “Spiritual Flame” helped him break through the threshold of the early stage of the three-star level and reach the middle stage of the three-star level. It did not stop there. A continuous stream of energy was still gushing out from his dantian, seemingly without end.

Energy flows from the dantian, through the entire body, and back to the dantian, circulating endlessly. With each cycle, Xiao Yan’s fighting spirit becomes more solid, his meridians expand, and every inch of his skin and every cell frantically absorbs this powerful energy. The “Spiritual Flame” kept spinning, and endless gray energy, like a tsunami, layered upon layer, quickly filled Xiao Yan’s meridians and all the spaces within his body.

The energy seemed dissatisfied with the narrow confines of his body and wanted to break free from all constraints. The raging energy surged forth like a wild horse, its fury churning and roaring, its power, combined with its fighting spirit, aimed to tear apart the gray bottleneck. Xiao Yan’s aura began to solidify and continued to rise.

His internal fighting spirit surged and turbulent, merging with enormous energy, and surged towards the late stage of the three-star realm like mercury spilling onto the ground, creating a vast ocean-like wave that seemed like a sharp sword piercing the sky. Wave after wave of attacks came crashing down. Xiao Yan’s expression was solemn, and his Dou Qi appeared endlessly, like thousands of snow blades dancing, trying to crush all obstacles.

Powerful energies intertwined, galloping and advancing like dazzling lightning. The late-stage three-star resistance could no longer hold out, and a silver snake-like rift was cleaved open. The energy accumulated within finally found an outlet, instantly breaking through the dam.

Like a raging river bursting its banks, Xiao Yan’s breakthrough caused a change in the world. A torrent of energy surged forth, churning and intertwining in the sky, creating countless waves that rushed towards the stone chamber. The stone chamber had a narrow entrance, and the massive gathering of energy from heaven and earth formed a vortex that instantly filled the chamber.

The vortex brought with it endless gales that swept up the magma in the subterranean fire. The thick white energy, mixed with sparks, shimmered in the stone chamber like the reflections on the scales of a white dragon. The energy was extremely powerful, rushing towards Xiao Yan, who was sitting cross-legged in the center of the stone chamber with his eyes open.

Xiao Yan slowly raised his head, his eyes like sharp swords and his heart like a firm stone, accepting all the blessings bestowed upon him by heaven and earth. Energy enveloped Xiao Yan, merging into his body like pure white snow in spring. Endless white energy entered Xiao Yan’s body, crashing against every meridian like a tsunami.

His entire body’s meridians and bones vibrated, and all the outer membranes that were blocking the expansion of the meridians were almost destroyed and then shattered. The powerful flow of heaven and earth energy destroyed all obstacles, and nothing could stop it. The meridians quickly expanded and then stabilized, and the Dou Qi that was like a small stream became as wide as a small river.

Every bone is being tempered, becoming more and more crystalline like jade, more and more hard like divine iron; the flow of heaven and earth passing through it even produces a metallic tremor; every muscle is trembling slightly, the increased resilience allowing the muscles to stretch and contract with a sense of immense power; every cell is cheering, desperately absorbing the energy bestowed by heaven and earth…

Feeling every subtle change within his body, it was as if he could raise his hand and cause wind and thunder to fill the sky, clouds to rise and fall, and the earth to tremble. This power intoxicated Xiao Yan This sentence is filled with joy, and of course, a touch of shyness. Xiao Yan looked at Zhen Ni’s face, which was slightly thinner, and naturally understood that she had been worried about him these past few days.

He felt a warm current flowing through his heart and was about to say something.

“Raising a cup to invite the bright moon, we become three with our shadows.” The storm stood against the wind, and a sigh broke the world of Xiao Yan and Zhen Ni.

“We have been waiting for Young Master Xiao for so long, only to find him alone with his group. I wonder if we should just go with the wind and ask the moon?” Zi Ying looked at the two of them with a half-smile, her bright eyes sparkling.

“The scenery here is excellent, but it seems a bit too lively…” Xiao Zhan’s words were left unfinished, as if there was a deeper meaning behind them. Looking back at his brothers, Xiao Yan’s face flushed red, and Zhen Ni’s face turned even redder.

When love is at its peak, it’s like being intoxicated by wine. The sweet nothings between men and women often make them forget everything else in the world. Even though Xiao Yan and Zhen Ni are both Dou Di, they are not immune to this.

“Cough cough.” Xiao Yan coughed a few times to cover up his embarrassment and slowly came back to his senses. Looking at the exhaustion that could not be hidden beneath the tall figures of the crowd, he knew that it was due to the worry and concern that had been on their minds for so many days.

Xiao Yan felt very guilty and clasped his hands in a fist and said, “Thank you all for your hard work.”

“Young Master Xiao, you’re wrong to say that. This is what we should do.” Le Shaolong looked very serious, but anyone could tell that he was holding back his laughter.

“That’s right. If we’re talking about hard work, none of us work as hard as Sister Zhenni. She has to look down at the ground at least several times a day,” Ziying echoed firmly, her eyes blinking as she spoke.

“Yes, yes, Mr. Xiao, you should say ‘thank you for your hard work’ to the young lady separately,” Nan Erming even chimed in.

“Uh…” Xiao Yan was speechless and could only scratch his head and chuckle sheepishly.

“Alright, haven’t you all been guessing every day how much Young Master Xiao has gained this time? Now that he’s out, why don’t you hurry up and ask him to demonstrate it to us?” Jenny said with a hint of coquettishness, as if trying to cover up her embarrassment. Upon hearing this, everyone knew that Zhenni was changing the subject, but they immediately stopped teasing Xiao Yan and Zhenni, and their eyes lit up.

Xiao Yan had been in seclusion for dozens of days. Flames rose and thick fog rolled in the stone chamber. The people guarding the entrance could not see the scene clearly, even though they were close, and could not guess what was inside.

They were already getting impatient to know what Xiao Yan had gained. Seeing the expectant expressions on everyone’s faces, Xiao Yan stretched out his hand and with a thought, cyan-gray heavenly fire surged from his palm, covering his entire body. From a distance, it looked as if he were wearing a layer of warm jade.

The heavenly fire drifted freely, like a spring breeze, but when it fell into the perception of everyone’s divine senses, it was like a cold wind sweeping away the remaining snow, with endless killing intent. The bluish-gray flames roared fiercely, like a burning nebula, and terrifying shockwaves emanated from Xiao Yan’s body, threatening to destroy everything around him. Before everyone could recover from their shock, a heavy ruler as warm as jade appeared in Xiao Yan’s hand.

An even more terrifying energy fluctuation emanated from it, and the burning flames covering it were even more frightening, with an aura of destruction sweeping across the sky.

“This is…” Everyone was so surprised they couldn’t contain themselves.

“This is the Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler, which has advanced once again after fusing with the Heavenly Flame.” Xiao Yan’s explanation was spoken softly, yet it seemed to explode like a thunderclap! A weapon that can be upgraded using Heavenly Fire?

This… was unheard of to everyone, but seeing Xiao Yan’s matter-of-fact expression, they were speechless. Although everyone has witnessed too many amazing things from Xiao Yan, whether it’s the divine improvement in Dou Qi strength, the rapid advancement in alchemy, or even the top-grade medicine formula that caused a stir on the Dou Di Continent, a weapon that can theoretically evolve infinitely still makes everyone drool.

This is the only choice that can accompany you for a lifetime. After a long while, everyone slowly came to their senses.

“By the way, besides the Heavenly Fire and this top-grade heavy ruler, did you advance in your cultivation, Young Master Xiao?” Zi Ying recalled the energy fluctuations from before, but hesitated, wondering if it was the Heavenly Fire that had enhanced her aura, and asked uncertainly. Xiao Yan nodded, and the bluish-gray heavenly fire completely retracted into his body.

Then, the aura of a late-stage three-star Dou Di was fully unleashed, once again challenging everyone’s psychological endurance.

“Late stage of three stars? Leaping two levels in a row…” A collective gasp filled the air.

It wasn’t Xiao Yan’s strength that shocked everyone, but rather the speed of his advancement that gave them an unbelievable shock.

“Young Master Xiao, is your advancement speed even human?” Zi Ying’s cherry-like mouth could hardly close. Faced with the astonished gazes of the crowd, Xiao Yan scratched the back of his head somewhat embarrassedly and explained, “I had already reached the bottleneck of the early three-star stage after absorbing the Earth Explosion Heavenly Flame.

Now, with the help of the Spirit Connection Pill Flame, I broke through in one fell swoop, so…” After hearing Xiao Yan’s explanation, everyone’s mental state eased a little, and they quietly breathed a sigh of relief and nodded slightly.

“Is this heavenly fire called the Spirit-Communicating Heavenly Fire?”

“Um.” Then Xiao Yan gave everyone a general overview of the information about the Spiritual Flame.

“What?!” Everyone’s eyes lit up again.

“Heavenly Fire, ranked sixteenth, and with such a strong recovery ability? Young Master Xiao, aren’t you going to become an invincible monster? Goodness, this is incredible, Young Master Xiao, you’ve really reaped a great harvest on this illusionary journey.”

A wave of joy immediately enveloped everyone present, and even the scattered pebbles and sand on the ground became pleasing to the eye, while the hot wind rising from the scorching ground felt warm.

“Life is like this, at this moment, let us raise a toast.” Xiao Zhan’s hearty laughter, accompanied by a sigh of regret, fell into the empty wind, resonating with everyone.

“Take a look at this.” Nan Erming turned his storage ring upside down and actually found several porcelain bottles.

As soon as the caps were lifted, a rich aroma of wine tantalized everyone’s taste buds.

“It’s actually top-grade bamboo leaf green! Where did you get this from, kid?” Xiao Zhan’s surprise was no less than when he saw Xiao Yan’s bluish-gray heavenly fire.

Nan Erming’s extremely handsome face remained expressionless, but a hint of embarrassment was faintly visible. He glanced at Xiao Zhan with a resentful look, “Being alone inevitably brings moments of loneliness. Sometimes, when you’re all alone, how can you do without this drink?

It’s just that there’s not much wine left. This is my best-kept secret; I really can’t bear to take it out normally.” As Nan Erming said this, a trace of heartache flashed across his face.

“I never expected our Nan Erming to be such a sentimental person,” Storm remarked with a touch of emotion.

“Storm, you’re being too euphemistic. To put it bluntly, you’re just a repressed pervert.” Xiao Zhan wasn’t going to miss the chance to tease Nan Erming, whose appearance had always made everyone a little jealous.

“Say that again!” Nan Erming’s face instantly turned icy, even the occasional passing wind seemed to carry a chill, but he managed to suppress a genuine smile from the corners of his mouth that were twitching.

“Haha, for the sake of these few bottles of wine, just pretend I didn’t say anything.” Xiao Zhan snatched a few bottles of Zhuyeqing wine and stopped arguing with Nan Erming.

“Actually, if Nan Erming didn’t hold back his gentle smile, I think countless women on the Dou Di Continent would be captivated by him, and he wouldn’t be all alone,” Zhen Ni said softly, subtly saving face for Nan Erming. Everyone nodded, their eyes glued to Nan Erming’s handsome face, a face that would make even girls jealous. They stared intently, making Nan Erming look embarrassed…

A bonfire roared into the sky in the open space, its flickering flames mingling with the rich aroma of alcohol, accompanied by the hearty and sincere laughter of the crowd, making this moonlit night, with its few stars, all the more beautiful… The next morning, after the alcohol had worn off and everyone had sobered up, they stood under the vast sky, looking at each other in bewilderment. All their unspoken words finally culminated in a single sentence.

How do we get out? Facing everyone’s gaze, Xiao Yan sighed softly. This place was the territory of the “Spiritual Flame,” and since he had absorbed it, everyone naturally assumed that Xiao Yan was the most likely person to know the exit.

With a slight breath, without explanation, Xiao Yan fell into deep thought. He had explored this place with his soul power and found nothing suspicious. So there was only one possibility: the exit was located where the “Spiritual Fire” was, in other words, inside the stone chamber.

Previously, when Xiao Yan used the power of the earth fire to subdue the “Spiritual Pill Fire”, his soul power inadvertently followed the power of the earth fire to explore downwards. There seems to be an empty space below, but he did not pay much attention to it at the time. It seems that the exit is very likely there.

With this thought in mind, Xiao Yan waved for everyone to follow, and with a flash, he entered the stone chamber again. The magma in the central stone pool had dried up, its color had darkened, and it had lost its vitality. Xiao Yan’s soul power surged out, covering the magma, and explored downwards along the cracks within it.

The power of the soul extended for only a few miles before sinking into a fork in the earth’s fire source. Below was an extremely empty space, seemingly another world.

“This should be it.” Gathering the power of flames, Xiao Yan wielded the Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler repeatedly, smashing through the solidified magma with overwhelming force, carving out a passage. From the depths of the passage, a sliver of light shone through…

After passing through the not-so-long passage, the scene before them suddenly opened up. This is a completely different world. Beneath their feet, beneath the white clouds, floated a thick layer of viscous yellow mist.

The mist stretched across the sky without end, like the Yellow Springs River beneath the nine netherworlds, the boundary between heaven and hell separated by just one river. Above the Yellow Springs, as everyone sees it, there is a calm and peaceful world, with clear blue skies, blue seas, and sunshine. Beneath the Yellow Springs lies an endless desolation, with swirling yellow sand obscuring the sky and blocking out the sunlight.

Sunlight here is a luxury, and the few withered trees that occasionally emerge after the sandstorm are fossils from countless years ago. The crowd looked up and saw endless skeletons. The wind would scatter the bones when it rose and reveal them when it passed.

The occasional pieces of broken armor and spears looked very old and did not resemble clothing from this era at all.

“Could this place be another ruin from before the ancient catastrophe? Under the same sky, separated by a thin line, the difference is like heaven and earth. Should we call it magical or terrifying?”

Xiao Yan’s face was full of disbelief. Although they lived in different eras, they were in the same place. Xiao Yan seemed to have traveled through time, seeing the figures of past battle flags waving and iron horses galloping, and witnessing the historical evolution of prosperity fading and corpses littering the ground.

The ancient catastrophe was an enigmatic era, a time spanning both light and darkness, leaving behind countless miracles and perplexing events, making it truly unimaginable what happened during its rise and fall. Despite their feelings of awe and wonder, the group did not impulsively descend immediately. Instead, they flew around the sky for a while, becoming increasingly awestruck, and their expressions became more and more varied.

This place is tens of thousands of miles in circumference, far smaller than the outer edge of the illusionary realm and the space where the Heavenly Fire is located.

However, this place is circular, and when viewed from high above, it resembles an inverted bowl. Under the endless spread of yellow sand, the vague view gives people a feeling of boundlessness. Moreover, everyone discovered that this vast place had no buildings, no mountains, no flowing water, no life; yellow sand and withered bones were the only main features.

Finally, everyone’s gaze fell upon the only thing that caught their eye. It could be said to be the main theme of this space, or the most shocking thing here. Its appearance in everyone’s sight left them somewhat bewildered and dizzy.

At the horizon, in the region where the yellow sand runs rampant, a throne of bones stands tall, barely visible! Its height of several hundred meters is quite impressive, but the distance makes it seem like a tiny speck in the eyes of others. Nevertheless, it is still so conspicuous, as if the yellow sand and withered bones cannot cover up its former glory.

The entire throne of bones appeared in the boundless yellow sand, its flawless white color untouched by even a speck of dust. As everyone stood there in a daze, they suddenly froze in shock. Right before everyone’s eyes, the Bone Throne vanished silently, disappearing into thin air as if it had never existed.

Once the throne of bones disappeared, the space returned to its dull and monotonous state, with dust billowing everywhere and nothing else to attract attention.

“It seems that what we are looking for should be at the Bone Throne.” Jenny raised her eyebrows slightly and gazed into the distance. Xiao Yan stared at the spot where the White Bone Throne had disappeared for a long time, then slowly nodded.

Having gathered a considerable amount of Spirit Imprint Moss on the outskirts of the Illusionary Realm, there is no doubt that Spirit Imprints must exist within the Illusionary Realm! Although it is not yet certain whether they are here, the Three Wonders are rare treasures, naturally protected by the grace of heaven and earth. They could never be found in a place where yellow sand is everywhere, otherwise they would be as common as radishes and cabbages, and there would be no sense of their nobility.

If they are here, the only place that could be the White Bone Throne is there.

“Let’s go down and take a look.” Xiao Yan flapped his bone wings and disappeared into the sky, passing through the viscous liquid that resembled the Yellow Springs River, leaving only a lingering shadow standing beside the group. Everyone used their movement techniques to follow closely behind Xiao Yan.

As soon as everyone stepped into the “River of the Underworld,” the pressure suddenly increased, and they immediately sensed something was wrong. The arrival of life seemed to activate the “River of the Underworld.” The Underworld, which cuts off all life, is extremely sensitive to the smell of life.

The “River of the Underworld” is like a giant python, slowly awakening from its thousand-year slumber. A powerful pressure shook the ten directions, and the sky and the earth trembled. A murderous aura enveloped the entire sky and the surrounding area.

Exuding an ancient and desolate aura, the “River of the Underworld” surged across the sky with a deafening roar, as vast as a tsunami, emitting violent and terrifying fluctuations. Liquid-like yellow gas surged in the void, sweeping in with a thirst for flesh and life, engulfing everyone inside. The “River of the Underworld” squeezed every inch of everyone’s skin and every strand of their body, instantly filling every gap where the air and the human body came into contact.

The rumbling sound made everyone’s ears ring and their souls tremble, and their consciousness became hazy. The group quickly realized that their soul power could not penetrate very far into the “River of the Underworld.” They even seemed to be deprived of their ability to sense the energy of heaven and earth in the air.

Here, they were like ordinary people who could not see, possessing great cultivation but unable to use it. Xiao Zhan unleashed a barrage of powerful punches, their divine radiance dazzling, as if cast from gold. He unleashed immense power, but tragically, his battle aura vanished without a trace, as if injected into a massive vortex.

The “River of the Underworld” swallowed everything like a giant ocean eye. Xiao Yan, possessing the strongest soul power, was the first to notice this state. He opened his mouth to warn everyone, but unfortunately, without air as a medium, he could only open his mouth and not speak.

In desperation, his bone wings vibrated rapidly, their blades sharp as knives, shooting out beams of white light like swords that shattered the surrounding river water into large swaths of yellow mist.

However, it was like trying to cut water with a knife; the “River of the Underworld” continued to rush forward. Amidst the churning yellow mist, the deafening roar sent shivers down one’s spine. For a moment, everyone was gripped by panic, an instinctive reaction to the unknown.

Fortunately, the fear only lasted for a moment. The group, seasoned by countless battles and honed by life-or-death experiences, quickly recovered. They clearly remembered that when they explored the area below from above, the “River of the Underworld” was not deep.

Although they were now deeply trapped within it, the intense malevolent aura chilled them to the bone and created an extremely terrifying sense of oppression.

However, they did not sense any danger or encounter any substantial attacks. Perhaps the most direct and effective way to escape would be to channel their fighting spirit into their feet and plummet straight down. The “River of the Underworld” is actually a natural chasm created by heaven and earth here, blocking sunlight and life, thus creating the lifeless environment with yellow sand everywhere below.

Besides its isolating function, the “River of the Underworld” is also a natural maze. People inside cannot sense anything, cannot see anything, and are cut off from the body’s contact with external energy. The energy consumed inside cannot be replenished, and the longer one stays, the weaker one becomes.

In this environment, people inevitably panic, lose their composure, and become disoriented, eventually becoming trapped in the “River of the Underworld,” exhausting their energy and turning into yellow mist. This formation is not poisonous, but it is extremely strange. Once you see through it, it is incredibly simple.

It tests your ability to uphold your true self and maintain clarity of mind. If you lose your true self, you will lose yourself and eventually fall into its trap and perish here. For those with impure intentions, this formation is an insurmountable chasm; for those with pure hearts, it is as simple as a blank sheet of paper.

Therefore, this formation is difficult, yet not insurmountable. Xiao Yan and his companions are upright and fearless, unafraid of life or death, so how could they possibly lose themselves? Therefore, this formation is not difficult for them.

Xiao Yan was the first to appear below the “River of the Underworld,” looking at the surging currents of the “River of the Underworld” with slight worry, until he saw everyone else appear one after another after a while before he breathed a sigh of relief. Looking back at the “River of the Underworld” above, the extremely eerie feeling still lingered in everyone’s hearts, causing a faint sense of unease.

Such an incredible natural barrier made people marvel at the wonders of nature, and the place where the three wonders were located was indeed extraordinary. Standing amidst the yellow sand, Xiao Yan and his companions finally felt more clearly that below the “River of the Underworld,” the wind swept across the entire space, stirring up sand and rocks, violently ravaging this land. The terrifying fluctuations made the sky tremble, and even the speed of Le Shaolong and Zi Ying was significantly affected here.

As they marched toward the Bone Throne, their cloaks billowing, their eyes darted around, wary of this unknown world. In the distance, a high mountain made of yellow sand was flattened in an instant by the raging wind. The wind, carrying a sea of yellow sand, instantly filled another low-lying area.

Moving mountains and filling seas was as easy as snapping a finger here. The power of nature was casually unleashed, like a thousand horses galloping. The expressions on everyone’s faces grew increasingly solemn.

Yellow sand raged endlessly, and the sky began to darken as if it were the end of the world, with a desert storm imminent. The howling wind tore through the sky, and the yellow mist was filled with a sultry, fishy smell. As the group walked along the long desert road, they witnessed the storm hurling a rock tens of meters high into the air, instantly tearing a huge skeleton into countless fragments.

The wind was terrifyingly powerful; ancient trees and boulders were no more vulnerable than a sheep in the storm. This was the power of heaven and earth; destruction and burial were the constant melody here. But no one knew where this boundless storm came from or when it began.

Fortunately, Storm was in the group. With Storm’s affinity for wind, he was in his element here. Although he could not control the power of heaven and earth, it was not too difficult to judge the direction of the storm.

The group bypassed the tornado storm no less than three times and slowly approached the Bone Throne. As they traveled, they found the place extremely strange. They encountered no obstacles or magical beasts, only the might of heaven and earth following them all the way.

Gazing at the distant, faintly reappearing throne of bones, everyone felt utterly incredulous, as if in a dream, too unreal. Taking a deep breath of the air mixed with yellow sand, his chest instantly filled with hot, dry heat, Xiao Yan shook his head to make sure he wasn’t dreaming. Xiao Yan licked his dry lips and looked up at the gray sky.

He hadn’t encountered the danger he had imagined, but he still felt uneasy.

“Everyone, be on your best.” Xiao Yan said, glancing around at his surroundings. The group nodded to Xiao Yan and proceeded cautiously. No one spoke; they all gripped their weapons, their hearts filled with trepidation.

The road leading to the Bone Throne was winding and undulating, with hills of varying heights scattered along its sides. The terrain gradually leveled out, the swirling yellow sand subsided, and the weather was actually much better than before. Nothing seemed amiss, but Xiao Yan still felt an inexplicable unease in his heart.

As they traveled onward, the wind and sand would occasionally whip up the yellow sand that had been buried for countless years, revealing faintly visible skeletons beneath. The wind grew stronger, and the yellow sand swirled and drifted to both sides, exposing even more bones on the ground. Unlike the withered bones they had seen along the way, these bones exuded an eerie aura.

Judging from the skeletons, it can be seen that these skeletons must have been extremely powerful warriors in life. Perhaps in the past, battle flags fluttered here, and fierce battles were fought. Perhaps the ground here was red and stained with blood.

But all of this has been buried under the yellow sand and buried in history. The setting sun slants westward along the ridgeline of the desert, casting a dark, deep red glow over the earth, reminiscent of the ashen gray of blood seeping into the sand years ago; the shifting sand beneath his feet is scalding hot, as if it were the temperature of blood spilled in the past. Xiao Yan raises his head, gazing at the vast expanse in the distance, deeply feeling how insignificant he is within it.

“History is always too heavy. We don’t know what happened here before, but the desert sunset, the yellow sand and white bones, this gray color always makes people feel particularly sad.” Zhenni stood next to Xiao Yan, reached out and brushed her messy hair, and sighed. Xiao Yan nodded, still remaining silent, his unease growing stronger.

The wind grew stronger, like a sharp claw, peeling away layer after layer of the desert; more yellow sand was swept up, and waves of heat rose from the desert, like a hazy sand fog, making it difficult to even breathe. The orange-red light of the setting sun blended into the hazy mist, making the sky even darker. A patch of black appeared on the horizon, and night was about to fall.

Under the dim light, the yellow sand took on a dark red tinge, and a nauseating fishy smell wafted in the air, as if it were the stench of decay that had lingered for millennia. As far as the eye could see, the skeleton stretched along the undulating sand dunes, winding like waves in the sea towards the throne of bones.

“If I remember correctly, when we looked down from the sky at the location of the Bone Throne, it was a place where sandstorms raged like towering waves.” Le Shaolong spoke slowly, his brows furrowed.

“But when we got here, everything was different. This place has become the quietest place, at least for now.” Le Shaolong’s words made everyone’s hearts sink.

Recalling what they had seen before, everyone’s hearts pounded violently with unease. Apart from the sound of the wind, the surroundings were eerily quiet, with only the pounding of their hearts echoing across the desert littered with bones. The wind whipped up a section of white bone from a forearm.

The withered fingers greedily reached forward, the pale knuckles accompanied by the wailing of the yellow sand, like the howling of a night ghost. Less than three inches from Xiao Yan’s chest, it was gently shaken by his protective fighting spirit and fragilely broke into several pieces, falling onto the desert. The five fingers trembled slightly, seemingly unwilling to accept defeat.

The whole scene was eerily unsettling. Zi Ying gripped Xiao Zhan’s arm tightly, her nails digging deep into his skin. Her delicate face was deathly pale, and her expressive eyes trembled with fear.

No matter how strong Zi Ying was, she was still a girl, and instinctively felt disgust and terror towards such things. Jenny also felt the temperature drop suddenly, and a chill spread through her heart. Although she didn’t act like Ziying on the surface, she still subconsciously hugged her shoulders tightly.

The night breeze was cool, and everyone’s bodies stiffened slightly as they all looked at Xiao Yan, awaiting his next instructions.

“The road we must take must be taken.” Xiao Yan gazed for a long time, his tall figure standing straight like a javelin in the haze, or like a black reef, unmoved by any storm, inspiring confidence in everyone. After saying that, Xiao Yan drew out the Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler, and the blazing flames became a guiding light in the misty desert.

They traveled on in silence, with only the pale gray skyfire flickering eerily. As night deepened, the vast desert stretched out before them, devoid of human habitation. The group slowly made their way through several uneven sand dunes, where they saw a few dilapidated walls, weathered by wind and sand, standing forlornly against the backdrop of the desolate desert.

Under the cover of night, phosphorescent lights flickered. After passing several sand dunes, a path gleaming with white light appeared before them. The path wound its way forward, vaguely pointing towards the throne of bones.

The surrounding emptiness and sand dunes were shrouded in a hazy mist, seemingly elusive and unfathomable.

“How could this be?” Everyone gripped their weapons tightly, watching as everything around them silently disappeared, leaving only a narrow path in front of them. Yet, they hadn’t sensed anything amiss.

The situation was eerily unsettling.

“Stay true to yourself and keep going.” Xiao Yan bit his lip. His soul power seemed to be blocked, and his search range was extremely limited, yielding him no results.

“The answer will always appear, maybe it’s just ahead.” Xiao Yan said, stepping onto the long, winding path ahead. The path was shrouded in a white light and a thick layer of mist, which blocked the exploration of the power of the soul. From a distance, it had a dreamlike beauty.

But as soon as she stepped on it, the purple figure jumped back with a “whoosh” and clutched her chest. Her curves rose and fell like waves, still in shock. Upon closer inspection, everyone was shocked to discover that the path was actually made up of countless white skeletons, densely packed and deathly pale, with phosphorescent glows floating above them.

From the white mist formed by the rising resentment, one could vaguely hear unwilling roars. Countless howls disturbed everyone’s minds, as if countless ghosts and demons were waiting to devour them. No wonder Zi Ying acted so strangely.

Even Zhen Ni clung tightly to Xiao Yan’s arm, her beautiful body trembling slightly, her lips turning purple from biting her teeth, and her eyes, which were like a pool of clear water, were faintly rippling with unease. Xiao Zhan’s protective shield was already in place, and the Golden Howling Tiger, like a golden sun, shone brightly, protecting everyone completely.

But something even stranger happened: the drifting phosphorescence and the wailing eerie sounds ignored Xiao Zhan’s protective shield and easily penetrated it, surrounding everyone and giving off a sense of unease. Storm stretched out his hands, his fighting spirit protecting his body, and gently touched the eerie phosphorescence. To his surprise, he found that his hands felt no discomfort at all, as if it was all just to disturb everyone’s minds.

Xiao Yan’s earlier words about “staying true to oneself” were indeed prescient. Just as he cast an admiring glance at Xiao Yan, the storm revealed its troubles. Endless phosphorescence and intermittent wailing enveloped everyone.

Although they had their battle qi protecting them, they could not stop the invisible sound waves from disturbing their minds. In just a short while, everyone’s faces gradually turned pale, and their expressions became somewhat dazed. It seemed as if someone was constantly telling them about its misfortune and loneliness, causing them to lose their minds and become confused.

In such a state of unease, a single stray arrow could plunge everyone into utter ruin. Thinking of this, Storm broke out in a cold sweat. His staff emitted a soft light, but he helplessly lowered it, for he realized that even the wind could not stop this damned mess.

To be selected for elite training by a powerful merchant alliance with influence across the land, one must not only be loyal but also stand out among their peers. Even before Storm’s staff was raised, everyone reacted and launched their attacks. From afar, the multicolored battle aura bloomed brilliantly amidst the white mist.

It must be said that everyone reacted very quickly, and the panic only lasted for a moment before they regained their composure.

However, it must also be said that the result was very frustrating. No matter the starlight and lightning or the wind and fog, they could not get rid of the eerie feeling of the fog clinging to their bones. Just when everyone was at a loss, a bluish-gray light shot out from the sky.

Phosphorescent light and wailing sounds, as if they were afraid they couldn’t avoid it, retreated in all directions like surging waves, circling around the group three feet away, seemingly extremely unwilling. Above everyone’s heads spun a bluish-gray fire lotus, its color like warm jade—it was Xiao Yan’s hybrid heavenly fire. The fire lotus wasn’t large, but with each rotation it emitted an extremely fiery light, sacred and inviolable.

The heavenly fire was righteous and the mist was sinister, which was exactly what they needed to counteract each other, and everyone finally breathed a sigh of relief. With the aid of the heavenly fire, the group walked on the uneven path of bones. Each step they took produced a crisp cracking sound, accompanied by a hazy mist and a nauseating stench that wafted through the air.

An indescribable sense of suffocation slowly accumulated in their hearts
