Chapter 69

The unknown land trembled slightly, and the bloodstains on Xiao Yan’s body dispersed with the wind, his withered body regaining its vitality. Bathed in the energy of heaven and earth, Xiao Yan’s skin became crystalline, his black hair fluttering, light and ethereal.

“Finally, I’ve reached the late stage of the three-star level!” Xiao Yan clenched his fist, his heart filled with endless emotions about the journey he had taken. Although Xiao Yan’s advancement speed is already considered heaven-defying, his talent and fortuitous encounters have allowed him to reach the late-stage strength of a three-star Dou Di in just a few years, a level that would normally take ordinary people thousands of years to achieve.

However, everyone he is currently in contact with is a top figure in the Dou Di Continent, and he also shoulders the heavy responsibility of revitalizing the Xiao Clan. This invisible pressure constantly reminds Xiao Yan that his current strength is far from enough. Regardless, advancing to the late stage of a three-star Dou Di still brought Xiao Yan and the others a big step closer.

With excitement in his heart, Xiao Yan stared at the gradually dissipating energy of heaven and earth and slowly returned to reality. Suspended inside the stone chamber, Xiao Yan looked at the churning magma beneath his feet. Due to the absorption of the earth fire power, its color had become extremely dim.

Sensing the rich fire element and residual heaven and earth energy that had not yet been fully absorbed within the stone chamber, he changed his hand seals, and the three heavenly fires within his body began to rotate rapidly. With his soul as the guide and his battle qi as the aid, the three heavenly fires permeated and merged with each other. Taking advantage of the lingering energy of heaven and earth and the power of the earth fire in the stone chamber, Xiao Yan seized every opportunity.

The fusion of the three heavenly fires, like swirling clouds, was distinct yet gradually blended, each imbued with its own color. Having just advanced to the late stage of the three-star level, his battle qi surged and flowed continuously. Even the sense of touch of his soul power seemed to have become more acute.

With the experience of the Burning Technique and the previous fusion with Heavenly Fire, and with the help of the energy in the stone chamber, this fusion was much easier. As time passed, the colors of the three types of celestial fire gradually shifted from the boundary between light and dark clouds to a seamless blend of sea and sky, with occasional variations in shade but harmonious coexistence, gradually merging together.

As the surrounding energy gradually dissipated, the source of the earth fire completely lost its vitality, turning into a pool of semi-solid, dark red solid. The sparks in the air also disappeared without a trace. The stone chamber dimmed as if the heavens were sighing.

The fusion of the three heavenly fires had exhausted the last bit of essence in the stone chamber.

At this moment, Xiao Yan’s eyes were as bright as stars as he looked inward at the flame within his body. The fusion of dark cyan and gray heavenly fire eventually caused the quietly burning flame to gradually fade in color, turning into bluish-gray, rising gently like a misty, transparent veil, as if woven from countless gray and silver threads, beautiful as a dream.

Xiao Yan flicked his finger, and the bluish-gray Fusion Heavenly Flame appeared before him, its light filling the entire stone chamber like a deep, thick bluish-gray fog, like flowing liquid, gradually thickening and obscuring Xiao Yan and the distant entrance, turning the entire stone chamber into a hazy dream. Beautiful, very beautiful. Even Xiao Yan couldn’t help but marvel at how beautiful the bluish-gray hue could be, a beauty that seemed serene and untouched by worldly strife.

However, Xiao Yan had to admit that the grayish-blue flames were terrifyingly powerful! Wherever the flames passed, the bluestone ground that had once held the “Spiritual Fire” for countless years without damage was now emitting wisps of blue smoke and spreading with countless cracks. Even the nearly dried-up magma where he stood was rising with suffocating hot smoke under the scorching temperature.

Despite its immense power, the bluish-gray heavenly fire now carried a hint of attachment and reluctance, enveloping Xiao Yan like a thin veil, making him appear ethereal and carefree. The void around him trembled slightly under the scorching flames, occasionally cracking open with tiny fissures, seemingly unable to withstand the terrifying power of the heavenly fire and instinctively showing fear. Feeling the Heavenly Flame at his fingertips, Xiao Yan’s smile blossomed like a flower.

He slowly withdrew the Heavenly Flame, took a deep breath, and with a thought, the Heavenly Flame Eternal Ruler appeared in his hand. The bluish-gray flames shot out from his finger and landed in the large ruler. At first, they flowed like a trickle, but then they surged like a mighty river, quickly spreading throughout the entire heavy ruler.

The bluish-gray flames gradually intensified, enveloping the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler. The burning bluish-gray flames slowly rose, flowing continuously across the broad ruler’s surface, like a small lake gradually deepening into an abyss. The temperature inside the stone chamber also rose sharply, and the cracks in the walls deepened considerably.

The Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler, a top-grade weapon, seemed to possess a spirit. Its multicolored body gradually brightened, and an inexplicable power overflowed, as if resisting the invasion of the bluish-gray flames.

However, under Xiao Yan’s soul-soothing influence, it slowly calmed down and peacefully accepted the baptism of the flames. Time flows by, drop by drop, like fine sand slipping through your fingers, silently and without a trace. Above the stone chamber, Zhenni and the others stood or lay down, some cultivating, some on guard; inside the stone chamber, Xiao Yan was highly focused on controlling the flames.

The originally colorful Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler was now covered in a bluish-gray mist, as if it were wearing a layer of gray-gold armor, bright and mysterious, as clear as a pool of autumn water. Under the tempering of the bluish-gray flames, the splendor of the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler slowly faded, but its aura became even stronger, presenting a semi-transparent bluish-gray tone, as if the Moon Palace had descended, shrouded in moonlight, hazy and ethereal.

The bluish-gray flames, like the moonlight reflected in water, spread across the entire ruler, and like mercury spilling onto the ground, penetrating every nook and cranny, tempering every inch of the ruler. The ruler’s body is enveloped in a bluish-gray glow, slowly refining into a translucent bluish-gray tone, exuding the warmth and crystalline luster of jade, captivating the heart like a bright pearl, possessing unparalleled beauty, while also having a profound feeling, as if a chaotic expanse truly covers the wide ruler’s body.

Under Xiao Yan’s tempering, the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler lost all its splendor, leaving only a flowing, bluish-gray light on its body, shimmering with a hazy luster. Xiao Yan held the heavy ruler and moved it lightly, drawing a beautiful trajectory as he slashed at the wall beside him. The heavy ruler, though without a sharp edge, unleashed streaks of sharp light, easily shattering the hard bluestone and creating a huge hole in the wall.

From its body, a watery, bluish-gray flame surged forth, instantly scorching the bluestone inside the hole into a charred black. The bluestone wall was only scorched with cracks by Xiao Yan’s three fused heavenly flames. Now, with the Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler, its power was so strong that Xiao Yan was very satisfied, and a smile appeared on his handsome face.

“Everyone must have been waiting for a long time. I wonder what they’re doing up there right now.” Silver lightning appeared beneath Xiao Yan’s feet, and before his bone wings could unfold, his figure had already disappeared into the stone chamber.

Outside the entrance to the stone chamber cave, Zhenni’s clothes fluttered in the wind. Watching the stars change and time fly by, she couldn’t help but worry about Xiao Yan. Her bare arm, exposed outside her sleeve, was like a lotus root emerging from the water, warm like white jade, and had a luster like agate.

With a soft sigh, Zhen Ni was somewhat lost in thought. She withdrew her gaze from the distant sky and was about to bend down to check on Xiao Yan’s condition. Just as she turned around, a dark figure gradually solidified and stood in front of Jenny, its delicate face almost touching Jenny’s beautiful face.

“What are you thinking about, so lost in thought?” Hearing that familiar voice whispering almost right next to her ear, Jenny’s heart suddenly pounded.

“You…you’ve finally come out.” Smelling Xiao Yan’s scent and gazing at her beloved whom she had longed for day and night, Zhen Ni’s face flushed red. All the words she had been waiting for Xiao Yan to say were now reduced to this one sentence
