Chapter 69: The Legion of Bones

(“White and red are forever the most beautiful colors in the world. Legion of bones, heed the call of my progenitor of vengeful spirits, offer your loyalty to me, and show your worship with the blood of your enemies!”) The Bone Path winds and twists, its end faintly visible yet seemingly without end.

After turning countless corners and breaking countless bones, the crowd gradually relaxed. Just as everyone was starting to feel irritated by the endless, headless path of white bones, the hazy mist on both sides of the path suddenly reflected a series of images. The scene unfolds like a canvas, undulating with the wind, hidden in the mist yet appearing and disappearing intermittently.

It is somewhat illusory, yet seems extremely real, as if it were a story born on this land, transcending time and space, and unfolding right before our eyes. On the scene, amidst indescribable years and unfathomable eras, countless warriors clash beneath a somber sky on a land shrouded in sandstorms. Warhorses neigh, spears gleam with sharp light, and countless piercing crossbow bolts rip through the air, leaving trails of blood on cold armor, staining the heavens crimson…

Those in front fell, and those behind swiftly filled their places, without the slightest hesitation or retreat; soldiers, their arms slashed by sharp iron blades, would strike again with their other hands; knights, their chests pierced by high-spinning crossbow bolts, would be thrown from their horses, but even in their dying moments, they would hurl their spears to pierce the enemies in front of them…

The clash of swords and the flying carnage transformed this place into a living hell, a landscape of mountains of corpses and seas of blood, littered with severed limbs and mutilated bodies. The air was thick with the pungent stench of blood; the ground was awash in a dark crimson stain. The war continued, and countless years and seasons passed.

The resentment of the dead soldiers accumulated in the sky, slowly coalescing into a yellow river that separated heaven and earth. The ground in the scene was indistinguishable between mud and blood. Xiao Yan and the others seemed to smell the stench of blood from ancient times, so strong it was nauseating.

The scene continued, changing, until night fell… It was a windless, moonless night. Amidst the sounds of battle, a crack silently appeared in the sky, and a blurry figure descended from the heavens, stirring up the resentment that filled the air.

The endless resentment seemed to have found an outlet, and suddenly went berserk, pouring into that blurry figure from the yellow river, from the dead soldiers, and from the hatred between the two sides of the war. The blurry figure seemed to be thoroughly enjoying the endless resentment. Amidst the countless currents of air, only a pair of blood-red eyes stood out conspicuously in the night sky, faintly revealing extreme madness.

The resentment that filled the sky swirled and gathered together, forming a massive tornado between heaven and earth. A blurry figure stood at its center, laughing wildly, the laughter sounding particularly eerie in the dark night. The sudden change caught the attention of both sides in the battle.

The moment they looked up, everyone was shocked. It seemed that everything was beyond their comprehension. Everyone forgot about the battle that was taking place.

The weapons in their hands fell to the ground involuntarily, and the warhorses under their mounts trembled, their front hooves pawing the ground restlessly, showing extreme fear. After absorbing a large amount of resentment, the blurry figure transformed into a ferocious face, which magnified infinitely, obscuring the entire sky, and let out a furious roar downwards. The roar was like thunder, filled with intense dissatisfaction.

Under the roar, the tornado formed by resentment reversed its direction and swept across the entire land. Bloodshot eyes flashed in the air, and resentment, like a ghost possessing its body, swiftly transformed into vengeful spirits, overwhelming everyone on both sides of the battle, whether former comrades or enemies. Instantly, the empty land was filled with wails of agony.

Countless souls in the mist became vaguely ferocious and terrifying, as if struggling in pain, yet unable to escape being driven, and involuntarily rushing towards the ground. Countless vengeful spirits, fearless of swords and blades, rushed into the battlefield. Without any suspense, soldiers quickly withered and died, leaving only bare bones.

Their resentment before death transformed into rising resentment, which flowed into the Legion of Vengeful Spirits. The Legion of Vengeful Spirits grew rapidly at a snowball rate, and despair permeated the entire land. A renowned warrior, recognized throughout the army, witnessed this scene with sorrow in his eyes, yet still drew his sword to meet the attack, hoping for a miracle.

But countless resentful spirits met his blade, burrowing into his body. The warrior’s physique instantly became visibly wrinkled and dull, his youthful face beneath his helmet turning pale and wrinkled, his body hunched over, his vitality drastically diminished, and he died a gruesome death on the spot. Seeing that even the strongest warriors in the army could not escape, the confidence of countless soldiers was completely destroyed.

They could not understand why they did not die in the blood of swords, but instead fell humiliatingly on the bodies of vengeful spirits. No one could explain it all. The vengeful spirits howled and passed through them, and the warriors became hunched over, trembling, and the light in their eyes gradually dimmed.

They grew old one by one, until their lives withered away, and then with a “thud,” they fell off their mounts and died of old age, their resentment rising into the sky. Their mounts were the same; no longer strong and powerful, they were all emaciated, and soon followed their masters away. Before long, only countless corpses remained on the battlefield, a truly horrific sight.

This scene was truly terrifying. Such a bizarre way of dying, so many vibrant lives turning to dust and vanishing completely in the blink of an eye, sent chills down the spines of Xiao Yan and the others. High in the air, the hideous face seemed satisfied.

It opened its mouth and inhaled, swallowing all the endless resentment. Instead of growing larger, its body shrank, slowly condensing into a phantom figure. Its powerful strength caused the space to tremble.

Disdainful of the countless bones below, the illusory figure gazed at the sky and casually waved his hand. To the astonishment of Xiao Yan and the others, all the corpses gathered together, piling up into a massive white bone throne, with drops of still-fresh blood forming streams. The illusory figure casually raised its foot and took a step, creating ripples as it arrived before the throne of bones.

The illusory figure sat down, slowly turned around, and its blood-red eyes seemed to transcend time and space, piercing towards Xiao Yan and the others with divine light. The divine light emanating from the blood-red eyes was so tangible that everyone felt a tightness in their chests and was shocked. When they came to their senses, they found that the path of white bones had come to an end, and the illusory scenes on both sides had disappeared.

Everything had returned to normal, and in the distance, a huge white bone throne stood. On the throne of bones, a figure stood with his back to Xiao Yan and the others, gazing up at the sky. His moon-white robes fluttered in the wind, but both his clothes and his body seemed somewhat illusory and unreal.

The figure wore a black shawl and possessed a calm and refined demeanor, making it impossible to connect him with the ferocious and insane person seen in the previous scene. Some even had the illusion that they were completely different people!

However, the white bone throne flowing with blood clearly told everyone that this person was definitely the one with the ferocious face in the scene. The illusory figure didn’t react to the arrival of Xiao Yan and his group, continuing to gaze into the distance, seemingly waiting for something… Everyone gripped their weapons tightly, their palms sweating, unable to decide what to do next.

A fleeting shadow suddenly appeared in the night sky, gradually approaching from the horizon and piercing the chaotic eternal night. As the afterimage passed, the void shattered, and a crimson moon appeared, hanging high in the sky, as red as blood. The moonlight was no longer as gentle as water; it simply looked down indifferently at the vast human world.

The blood moon is silent, and the ground is covered in frost. Under its light, the vast Gobi Desert stretches for thousands of miles, and the desolate wasteland is shrouded in gray sky, where the leaden desert meets the sky, creating a scene of utter desolation. The illusory figure gazed at the blood moon and sighed softly.

The cold moonlight bathed the earth, yet it seemed unable to penetrate the layers of gauze, unable to dispel the loneliness and solitude that belonged to him.

“Is that the loneliness and emptiness left behind by endless lifespan?” Xiao Yan thought to himself.

“The moon has its phases of waxing and waning, just as people experience joy and sorrow, separation and reunion.” The illusory figure stretched out his slender fingers, filled with boundless sorrow, and wrote a line of words in the night sky, just like a lonely scholar. Then he slowly turned around.

When his gaze fell upon the crowd, the illusory figure changed! His blood-red eyes instantly turned cold and ruthless, flashing with a chilling light. An arrogant and domineering aura suddenly burst forth from his body, making everyone feel suffocated.

The stark contrast in temperament, transformed so quickly within the same person, left everyone speechless and dumbfounded. The illusory figure sat firmly on the throne of bones, as if ruling the world. With a wave of his hand, countless vengeful spirits appeared, surrounding and kneeling in worship.

The air within a radius of a thousand feet almost solidified into a tangible substance. The hideous and terrifying faces of the thousands of vengeful spirits appeared even clearer in the almost tangible air, so distorted that they sent chills down one’s spine.

At this moment, the illusory figure truly deserves the throne of bones, truly resembles the lord of the vengeful spirit army, and is the ruthless character in the scene who can destroy the heavens with a single rage! As for the loneliness and desolation before, perhaps it was a thousand years of solitude, or perhaps ten thousand years of loneliness, who knows?

“Young Master Xiao, do you think this guy has gone insane from being cooped up in this terrible environment for too long?” Xiao Zhan leaned over and said. Xiao Yan turned his head to look at Xiao Zhan, speechless for a moment. The place was extremely eerie, and everyone was on tenterhooks, their hearts pounding and their expressions incredibly solemn.

But when they heard Xiao Zhan’s words, Zi Ying burst out laughing, and the atmosphere immediately eased considerably.

Just then, the illusory figure on the bone throne’s expression darkened. He pointed at the crowd from afar, his deep voice seemingly ringing in their ears.

“After fifty thousand years of solitude, someone has finally come. Welcome to the Land of Resentment.”

“The moon is so beautiful tonight. I wonder if it is a blessing or a curse for you.” The illusory figure’s tone suddenly became urgent, losing its previous composure and becoming somewhat excited.

“Madman!” Xiao Zhan once again uttered a blunt assessment.

“Tonight is the darkest night, with a blood moon hanging in the sky. As the saying goes, things reach their extremes and then reverse, which just happens to suppress my Yin body, making my strength much weaker than usual. This is your good opportunity.”

The illusory figure’s voice became somewhat hysterical.

“However, the favor of the blood moon is also an indestructible night for vengeful spirits and skeletons! This is your tragedy!”

“Flowers blooming in the night sky are always the most beautiful gift under the night sky. My dear guests, are you ready to offer this gift to me? Come, taste the feast of my undead legion!” The illusory figure’s eyes turned blood red, filled with a mad thirst for blood.

“White and red are forever the most beautiful colors in the world. Legion of bones, heed the call of my progenitor of vengeful spirits, offer your loyalty to me, and show your worship with the blood of your enemies!” The excited voice suddenly turned into an extremely serious one, exuding a kingly aura without anger.

“Your assessment is truly accurate,” Xiao Yan said to Xiao Zhan, turning his head. This Ancestor of Wraiths was an absolute madman.

“Thank you for the compliment.” Xiao Zhan accepted Xiao Yan’s praise without hesitation. No one had ever realized that Xiao Zhan’s skin was actually quite thick, and that it seemed to be getting even thicker as his strength increased.

“Why does your face look more and more like a shield?” Zi Ying wanted to pinch Xiao Zhan’s cheek, but found that her foot seemed to be pulled by something. Looking down, Zi Ying’s hair stood on end, her whole body trembled with fear, and she screamed and jumped up.

Countless white skeletons struggled to crawl out of the ground, their bony hands tugging unconsciously around them. Skulls opened and closed, and phosphorescent light rose eerily from the bones, making the whole scene incredibly bizarre and terrifying.

“Leap into the air,” Xiao Yan reminded him, then lowered his waist and raised his knees, his bone wings flapping rapidly, drawing countless incomparably sharp light blades. Wherever the light blades touched, white bones flew like shredded paper, and a large number of them fell down in unison. Immediately afterward, Xiao Yan dodged another batch of skeletons extending their bone hands from the ground.

With a flap of his wings, he was already in mid-air. Looking down, he saw countless white bones swaying and crawling up from the ground. Zi Ying had already lost her composure, and Zhen Ni also reached out her slender hand to grab Xiao Yan’s arm, her hand trembling slightly.

Everyone’s brows furrowed. A whirlwind swept across the desert, whipping up the yellow sand high into the air. Countless white bones emerged from the ground, like countless folds of sand and gravel, or like solidified waves, stretching all the way to the distant black horizon, continuing even at the junction of heaven and earth.

In the darkening night, pale skeletons clad in tattered armor, wielding ancient weapons, gazed up at the crowd above. There was no sign of life; a blood moon shone coldly down, making the skeletons appear as if stained with undried blood, a truly horrifying sight. Xiao Yan and the others watched all this from mid-air, exchanging bewildered glances.

They felt it was all too unreal, and while they were speechless, they also showed expressions of anxiety and worry.

“Damn, there are so many! Are you selling bones instead of meat in a brothel?” Xiao Zhan couldn’t hold back any longer and blurted out a curse, leaving everyone stunned.

“Fortunately, although there are many skeletons below, they are not conscious and there is no sign of life. We should capture the leader first, so it shouldn’t be difficult.” Le Shaolong was the most meticulous and always offered timely suggestions.

Judging from the fluctuations of the Vengeful Spirit Ancestor’s fighting spirit, he should be at the peak of a six-star Dou Di. If we can get close, victory may not be too difficult However, before this suggestion could be implemented, it was like a vow made by a man and a woman under the moonlight—beautiful enough to intoxicate, but also shattered extremely quickly.

“Wraiths, the skeletal bodies are ready for you. Merge, and you will once again forge an invincible legend!” On the throne of bones, the ancestor of vengeful spirits rapidly changed his hand seals, and his battle aura swept out in all directions, quickly condensing into green runes in the air and striking into the bodies of the kneeling vengeful spirits.

The runes entered the vengeful spirits’ bodies, and sharp screams echoed through the night sky as countless vengeful spirits rushed towards the upright, motionless, and unconscious skeleton. Possessed by vengeful spirits, a green light flashed in the empty skulls of each skeleton, and two eerie green flames burned in their eye sockets.

Suddenly, they showed signs of life. Then, all the skeletons pushed off with their legs and shot into the air, slashing countless bone blades at Xiao Yan and the others. The illusory figure on the Bone Throne dimmed, indicating that using this strange fighting technique had consumed a lot of his fighting spirit.

However, a smug sneer appeared in his eyes, as if he had already foreseen the fate of Xiao Yan and the others. Xiao Yan and the others were in mid-air, like fish in water. Countless white skulls leaped up, crisscrossing and intertwining, as if weaving a net that was quickly tightening in mid-air.

Finally, everyone moved, moving amidst this vast white ocean. If Xiao Yan and his companions were fish, then what they were displaying at this moment was the ferocity of sharks. Xiao Yan strode forward through the void like a bolt of lightning, his eyes sharp and fierce.

He dodged a sharp sword beam, reached out and snapped the skeleton’s wrist with a “crack,” snatched the rusty longsword, and then slashed back. The sword light swept across, and with a “crack,” a head rolled off. The purple figure moved unpredictably, the sharp blade in its hand flashing with a cold light, knocking away all the skeleton weapons that surrounded it.

Then, it cleaved a skeleton in two with a powerful vertical slash, and with another forceful shake, the skeleton shattered into several pieces and fell into the air. Le Shaolong brought forth a wisp of starlight, like twinkling stars in the night. He swiftly thrust out his twin daggers, instantly piercing through a group of skeletons.

The powerful battle aura sent the skeletons flying more than ten meters before they fell to the ground. Xiao Zhan’s fighting power was immense and his strength was extremely abnormal. His heavy punches had a force exceeding ten thousand jin, destroying any skeletons that approached him into pieces.

Nanerming’s poisonous gas surged and transformed into a spear. The spearhead burst forth with dazzling light, shattering the void and piercing the chest of a heavily armored skeleton general wielding a sturdy shield. The powerful force propelled the skeleton general, creating a nearly hundred-meter-long blank space behind him, with bone dust flying everywhere.

As the group entered the horde of skeletons, they were like tigers descending a mountain, breaking bones and shattering everything in their path. Skeletons in mid-air were blasted down by the group, and shattered bones covered the ground. The illusory figure in the distance was unmoved by the shattered bones fluttering before him, for he knew perfectly well that in the sea of skeletons, Xiao Yan and his companions had destroyed nothing more than a tiny, insignificant wave in a vast ocean.

Soon, Xiao Yan and the others also noticed this situation and quickly changed their strategy, shifting from individual slaughter to overwhelming the enemy with group combat techniques. The group formed a circle back to back, their movements echoing each other, and their fighting techniques connected to create an all-around fortress, once again shattering the attack of thousands of skeletons.

However, before the storm arrives, the splashing waves are merely the first note of the prelude; the subsequent surge is the true theme song. Countless skeletons surged forward, filling the space where everyone was. The skeletons that had already been broken apart by the crowd, as long as they were not completely destroyed, would drag their broken bodies forward one after another without the slightest hesitation or fear.

It could not be called bravery, but rather numbness.

However, when this emotion was taken to the extreme, it became terrifying. Xiao Yan swung his ruler like the wind, the wind carrying killing intent. The ruler was enveloped in bluish-gray flames, cleaving through the space in front of him.

But Xiao Yan soon realized that before he could retract the ruler’s shadow, the area he had cleaved was filled by a surging mass of skeletons. The skeleton with its left arm severed continues to slash with its right arm; the headless skeleton thrusts its spear forward, creating a flurry of spear shadows; the skeleton, its entire body shattered except for a dull head, opens and closes its jaws, still determined to take one last bite… There is a big difference between being unconscious and being fearless of death.

One describes a state of being, while the other emphasizes courage. When a tidal wave of skeletons is in this state, and they push it to its extreme, that painless charge is a hundred times more terrifying than being fearless of death. Even those who are not afraid of death still have the ability to feel pain.

With pain, their movements inevitably become clumsy. The difference between life and death is just this slight buffer. But without consciousness, even this slight buffer is taken away!

Xiao Zhan shook off the severed arm that was clinging to him, looked down at the sea of skeletons, and gasped.

However, their strength was only between three and four stars, but the sheer number of skeletons managed to build a bridge between heaven and earth. Surrounded by a vast expanse of white bones, the Storm Staff was raised high, and whirlwinds rose up, swirling around the group, knocking away nearby skeletons, and shattering the bones that entered the center of the whirlwinds. The storm swept up countless severed limbs, temporarily isolating a very small space.

Reaching out to brush away the bone fragments, Zi Ying frowned and looked at Xiao Yan, saying, “This can’t go on. We’ll be swallowed up by these skeletons sooner or later.” Everyone nodded. Even the strongest elephant cannot withstand being swallowed by a swarm of ants; everyone knew this principle well.

“To catch the thief, first catch the king; perhaps this is the only solution.” Le Shaolong stretched his aching arms, his gaze seemingly piercing through the vast sea of bones, and saw that lonely yet insane figure.

“Shaolong’s suggestion is worth a try.” Jenny followed Le Shaolong’s gaze and saw nothing but the hideous skeleton.

“Then let’s give it a try.” Xiao Yan glanced at the army of skeletons about to rush in from outside the whirlwind and no longer hesitated. In the short time it took to say those few words, the outer whirlwind had been filled with bones, and the wind force had become sluggish.

A large group of skeletons wielding broken swords and shattered blades rushed in through the gaps in the whirlwind. The blades gleamed as they scraped against the air, producing a metallic hum; the sword energy pierced the whirlwind with a sharp hiss; countless jaws snapped open and closed with a “crackling” sound; and the ant-like skeletons stomped through the void with a low, muffled thud… All of this, in the pitch-black void, was particularly unsettling.

This scene, even if only glimpsed briefly, will never be forgotten, even after ten lifetimes. When the whirlwind of the storm had completely subsided, Xiao Yan led everyone out. With a single swing of Xiao Yan’s Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler, a hazy blue heavenly flame spread along the ruler’s body.

Without any fancy moves, he simply cleaved out a clearing in a sea of skulls. The Heavenly Fire, still possessing immense power, continued its forward slash, shattering hundreds of skeletons before finally stopping after cleaving a skeleton general away. Having advanced to the late stage of a three-star Dou Di and obtained the Spirit Fire Pill, Xiao Yan’s strength has indeed reached a new level.

Such a simple and direct attack has such power. Unfortunately, even the strongest attack was like a boulder hitting water. In just a moment, the group only caught a fleeting glance at the desolate land below.

Before they could even locate the Bone Throne, the entire area was overrun by a swarm of skeletons. The endless, menacing skeleton army completely shattered Xiao Yan and his companions’ dream of quickly forging a path directly to the White Bone Throne. Although the skeletons were only between three and four stars in strength, they were countless and inexhaustible, suppressing everyone to the point of being unable to breathe.

This feeling was so suffocating that Xiao Zhan finally became angry.

“kill!” Xiao Zhan shouted “Kill!” like a thunderclap, shaking the sky. Infected by Xiao Zhan’s rage, the crowd’s suppressed anger exploded completely.

Their weapons gleamed, their killing intent soared, and they slashed at the skeletons around them, their roars carrying for dozens of miles. As the battle qi swept through, the blade shadows of Le Shaolong and Zi Ying, the ruler wind of Xiao Yan, the ring blade of Zhen Ni, the battle spear of Nan Erming, and the whirlwind of the storm intertwined, gathering unimaginable killing intent.

Carrying the ruthlessness honed through countless life-and-death trials, it broke through layers of bone seas and shattered layer upon layer of battle qi, stirring up bone fragments everywhere with an unstoppable momentum. If it were an ordinary magical beast, it would probably have been intimidated by that killing intent long ago. Unfortunately, what the group encountered was an undead legion, a legion without any emotions!

They broke through layer after layer, shattering batch after batch, but the group was still surrounded by skeletons on all sides. The layers upon layers of skeletons obscured the moonlight, turning everything in front of them into darkness, as if they had fallen into an endless hell.

With one kick, he broke the ribs of the skeleton that crawled in; with a turn, he smashed the broken spear that was thrusting up from below with a punch; he dodged the axe blade that was slashing diagonally; and with a lightning-fast strike, he twisted the neck of the attacker… The sweat of the crowd soaked through their clothes, dripping down their trousers and falling into the air, mixing with bone dust and becoming murky. As they fought, they drank bottle after bottle of Cleansing Elixir to replenish their fighting spirit.

They had lost count of how many ribs they had broken, how many heads they had crushed, and how many skeletons they had shattered. Their once overwhelming killing intent had turned into boundless numbness, and they mechanically repeated every action, their arms aching so much that they felt they would be unable to lift them again. Looking blankly at the endless skeletons ahead, the group reluctantly took another deep breath, as each breath carried away bone dust, making them gag countless times.

“Young Master Xiao, these skeletons are all like this because they were possessed by vengeful spirits. Vengeful spirits may also be spiritual entities in the realm of souls, otherwise how could they accept the consciousness of that madman below? Why don’t you try your soul power?”

Jenny pondered this question for a long time. She originally thought these skeletons were immune to soul fighting techniques, but no matter how she thought about it, something didn’t seem right. Finally, she figured it out: these things that looked like skeletons were actually vengeful spirits, just using skeletons’ bodies.

Once she understood this, she immediately offered her advice to Xiao Yan. Jenny’s suggestion immediately snapped Xiao Yan out of his mechanical battle. His eyes lit up.

He had originally thought that skeletons had no souls, but Jenny’s reminder was like a wake-up call, and his mind suddenly became clear. In the instant that Xiao Yan’s mind wandered, several spears suddenly thrust out from the side, coming with great force. Several skeletons beside him also swung their swords at Xiao Yan’s back, the rusty broken swords bursting with four-star Dou Qi fluctuations.

The skeletons’ insensitivity to pain, combined with the cunning of the vengeful spirits, perfectly embodies the essence of the human wave tactic. In a hurry, Xiao Yan twisted and dodged the spear. His bone wings spread out, and he moved with extreme speed, slicing through the rusty blade and turning to slash diagonally at the several skeletons that were attacking him.

Before the skeleton could react, its mangled body was split into four pieces, and Xiao Yan temporarily escaped the encirclement. Jenny also shook off the skeletons around her and rushed to Xiao Yan’s side to protect him. Looking at Zhen Ni, Xiao Yan nodded slightly, his hand seals overlapped, and the Blood Spirit Technique was activated.

A special energy flowed along with the power of his soul and merged into the world. A giant ten thousand feet tall condensed out, surging with powerful soul power, and strode over, casting a large shadow in the sky. The skeletons, suddenly sensing the change, all looked up, their pale heads flickering with green flames, seemingly filled with fear and unease.

With cold eyes looking down, the giant ignored the countless weapons leaping towards him. He unleashed a powerful sonic boom, and then his massive form gradually vanished into nothingness, disappearing into the void. But the sound waves didn’t disappear; they continued to envelop the skeleton army below.

And just then, something unexpected happened to Xiao Yan. The yellow river in the sky suddenly began to flow with rays of light, converging towards the sound waves of “Yellow Springs Wrath.” The light surrounded the sound waves and quickly merged into them, seemingly indistinguishable from each other.

Could the “Wrath of the Yellow Springs” and that yellow river originate from the same source? Xiao Yan looked at this scene with some shock and confusion, unable to guess the reason. In fact, Xiao Yan had already guessed pretty accurately.

The creator of “Yellow Springs Wrath” was inspired by the Yellow Springs and used his soul power to imitate the power of the Yellow Springs River; the two could naturally merge with each other. Although the “River of the Underworld” here does not possess the same pure power as the Underworld beneath the Nine Netherworlds, the accumulation of resentment over the years should not be underestimated. Its light continuously merges with the sound waves, making the power of the sound waves even stronger.

The sound waves traveled incredibly fast, instantly feeding downwards and affecting a range of thousands of miles, causing the void to violently tremble. The skeleton army enveloped by the sound waves froze in place, their weapons poised to strike, their raised feet suspended in mid-air, their jaws held in place as if by invisible supports, unable to move… In that instant, time seemed to stand still.

Among the skeletons below four stars, the green light inside their heads flashed and then disappeared, leaving no trace of life. Those below the peak of four stars had clusters of green light trembling and rushing out from their heads, running around wildly, no longer as strong as before, becoming extremely dim. Those at the peak of four stars were stunned for a moment, the green light flickered for a while, and then they resumed their movements, their weapons striking out with the momentum they had before, but without the previous strength and power.

Purple Shadow parried with her twin blades, the sharp blades indestructible, cutting all the attacking weapons in half. A blush rose on her little face, and feeling the sudden emptiness around her, a sweet smile appeared on her face, a rare sight indeed. Everyone stared in astonishment at this scene, forgetting their joy, clearly still reeling from the shock.

Even Xiao Yan was somewhat bewildered. The power of “Yellow Springs Wrath” had never been so formidable. Perhaps it was due to Xiao Yan’s advancement, the integration of the “Yellow Springs River,” or the elemental advantage…

But whatever the reason, before “Yellow Springs Wrath,” the skeleton army was utterly crushed for the first time with such overwhelming force. The skeleton legion, unaffected by the attack, glanced blankly at the empty space around Xiao Yan and the others before surging forward again. Their synchronized steps trampled the void, countless spears and swords clashed, and killing intent filled the air.

The successful strike boosted Xiao Yan’s confidence. He changed his hand seals one after another and unleashed “Yellow Springs Wrath” repeatedly, connecting with the “Yellow Springs River” above the sky. His soul power was like a raging flood, engulfing all the skeletons within it.

Without any suspense, vast swarms of skeletons fell to the ground like harvested rice. The lower-ranking skeletons were instantly devoid of their sentience; the surviving vengeful spirits scattered and fled, their hateful howls piercing the eardrums, yet they could not escape the encirclement of the group; only the higher-ranking skeletons escaped unscathed, but the dwindling sea of skeletons was slowly losing its power…

Back to contents

Chapter 70: The Fall of the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits

(Sensing the impending death, the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits stared intently at Xiao Yan and the others, then burst into loud laughter in extreme resentment. Amidst his hysterical laughter, a grand feast of fighting spirit unfolded on the White Bone Throne.) The illusory figure seated on the bone throne seemed to sense everything.

He could no longer restrain himself. He stood up abruptly and pressed his hand on the bone throne with a sudden force. With a muffled bang, the armrest of the bone throne snapped, scattering fragments everywhere.

The ancestor of vengeful spirits’ hideous face was filled with even more uncontrollable rage, and a pair of blood-red eyes suddenly materialized, piercing through the void and hanging high above Xiao Yan and the others. Those blood-red eyes possessed a captivating magic; though suspended in mid-air, they seemed to pierce through people’s bodies and intimidate their souls, sending chills down one’s spine. Only Xiao Yan’s soul power was strong enough, so he was not as severely threatened as the others.

He turned around coldly, revealing a mocking smile, and confronted the blood-red eyes. Provoked, the blood-red eyes suddenly darkened, becoming even more bloodthirsty and frenzied. The powerful soul intimidation caused Xiao Yan’s lips to twitch slightly.

Xiao Yan gritted his teeth and secretly circulated the Blood Spirit Technique, immediately feeling the pressure greatly reduced.

“Hahaha, young man, what powerful soul force, what a wide-ranging soul combat technique! Absorbing your soul force would be of great benefit to me!” The hoarse voice of the ancestor of vengeful spirits seemed to echo in everyone’s ears.

Upon hearing the words “What a wide-ranging soul fighting technique!”, Xiao Yan breathed a sigh of relief. These words were tantamount to acknowledging the power of Xiao Yan’s “Yellow Springs Wrath”. In other words, the Ancestor of Wraiths wouldn’t be foolish enough to continue expending excessive soul power to summon countless skeletons!

And this is exactly the result Xiao Yan wanted. Xiao Yan was overjoyed, but he dared not show the slightest expression on his pale face. His soul power was already exhausted; his soul sea felt as if it had been torn into countless pieces, pieced back together only to be torn apart again, pieced back together only to be torn apart once more…

Battle qi can be restored with elixirs, but soul power cannot; it can only be restored slowly over time. Xiao Yan gritted his teeth, took a deep breath, and suppressed the almost uncontrollable cry of pain. His steps felt unsteady, but supported by the Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler, he dared not and did not show the slightest abnormality.

“Hmph! A bunch of useless trash!”

Suddenly, a roar from the progenitor of vengeful spirits rang out, and a green light trembled among the dense skeleton army in the endless desert. Countless vengeful spirits uttered low hisses of submission and pleading. The Ancestor of Wraiths glanced sideways, his hand seals shifted rapidly, and the green light from countless skulls was instantly extracted from their skeletons and flew towards the Ancestor of Wraiths.

The skeleton, devoid of its vengeful spirit, stood blankly in place, posing no threat to Xiao Yan and the others. Everyone breathed a sigh of relief, feeling as if a great weight had been lifted from their shoulders. Their legs went weak, and they almost collapsed.

Xiao Yan persevered with all his might, not allowing himself to show the slightest sign of fatigue, gambling on the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits’ fear of his soul power. Clearly, Xiao Yan made the right bet. This matter is very dangerous.

If the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits had a little more patience and waited even a little longer, everyone’s fate might have been rewritten! Thinking of this, Xiao Yan couldn’t help but feel a chill run down his spine.

“Don’t be too happy yet! Try my Eighteen Blood Spirit Array, forged from vengeful spirits, again!” The ancestor of vengeful spirits suddenly curled up a smug smile.

As the battle aura and vengeful spirits converged, eighteen blood-red banners appeared out of thin air on the Bone Throne, soaring into the sky like pillars supporting the heavens. The ancestor of vengeful spirits, positioned at the center of the throne of bones, swiftly unleashed a series of hand seals, continuously pouring his battle aura and soul power into the formation.

“No one has ever survived under this formation; it was true before, and it will be true again!” The words of the progenitor of vengeful spirits were resounding and filled with a mad confidence. Before Xiao Yan and his companions could even catch their breath, their slightly relaxed nerves tightened again.

In the distance, centered on the ancestor of vengeful spirits, eighteen blood-red banners, each a thousand feet tall, fluttered and swirled, creating a hazy atmosphere that completely enveloped the world.

At this moment, the sky is no longer the same sky, and the earth is no longer the same earth. The crowd stood still, without moving an inch, yet they felt as if they were amidst swirling flags, with only a crimson expanse above and below, like a raging torrent of boiling blood. The overwhelming stench of blood, along with the overwhelming crimson, was so thick it was nauseating.

Xiao Yan stared intently and saw that the blood flag was actually formed by countless vengeful spirits, devouring and tearing at each other, as if they wanted to choose someone to devour, making it extremely ferocious and terrifying. No one dared to make any rash moves; they could only form a tight circle, keeping a watchful eye on their surroundings and ready to defend themselves against any possible situation.

“At the cost of sacrificing the life force of vengeful spirits, we summon the bloodthirsty energy of the earth to create the supreme Blood Spirit Array! It is your honor to enjoy this array! Hahaha.”

The vengeful spirit’s arrogant voice came and went, impossible to trace.

“This is eerie, filled with resentment; we must be careful.” The golden shield had already lit up, enveloping everyone. Xiao Zhan, who was usually impulsive, stared intently at the flickering, illusory figure of the Ancestor of Wraiths in the center of the formation, his brows furrowed, and a rare calm and composure on his face.

As everyone watched the churning Blood Spirit Array, they remained unmoved by the words of the Ancestor of Wraiths, quietly awaiting the impending final battle.

At this moment, vengeful spirits fluttered everywhere around them amidst layers of blood, and the eighteen banners became barely visible, seemingly within reach of a step forward, yet also seemingly unattainable. This strange visual contrast caused Xiao Yan to frown deeply.

“The first flag moves, the blood-stained blade is offered to the heavens!” The roar of the Ancestor of Wraiths echoed from within the Blood Spirit Array. Immediately afterward, the Blood Banner was waved, and countless Wraiths transformed into a giant blade, slashing down from the sky.

A sharp blade of light illuminated the faces of Xiao Yan and the others, striking them like a bolt of lightning. The blades moved fiercely, swift as meteors, and the resulting gusts of wind ruffled everyone’s hair, but could not shake their unwavering resolve.

“The Wrath of the Underworld!” Xiao Yan moved his hands slowly but powerfully, enduring the weakness caused by the depletion of his consciousness, and launched an attack. A colossal figure, ten thousand feet tall, once again stepped out, stirring up the Yellow Springs River and sweeping towards the giant sword.

Xiao Yan sighed inwardly. Although he no longer had the sea of skeletons, he had gained countless vengeful spirits in return. He didn’t know whether to be happy or sad.

But now, he had no choice but to fight to the death.

“Well!” Everyone gasped in surprise.

In the blink of an eye, the once invincible “Wrath of the Yellow Springs” met the giant sword, but it was like a leaf falling into water, only causing a few ripples before its afterimage disappeared into the void, and everything returned to calm. Immediately afterwards, a gust of wind surged in, the knife fell even faster, and the wind became even stronger.

“Why, why?” Everyone was puzzled. Why was “Wrath of the Underworld,” which had just dealt with the skeleton army as easily as an autumn wind sweeping away fallen leaves, now completely powerless against the vengeful spirits?

Not only was it powerless, but it also seemed to be easily defeated. In the midst of the shock, the blade was already close.

“I’ll do it!” Xiao Zhan suddenly moved, taking a step forward into the void. The golden light rapidly contracted, transforming into layers of armor covering his hard fist.

He then unleashed a powerful punch, colliding fiercely with the giant sword, stirring up a sky full of blood-red golden light.

“Bang!” With a heavy thud, the light and shadow vanished in an instant. Xiao Zhan retreated seven steps, and the armor on his arm, following a knife mark, cracked and broke apart, shattering into fragments that vanished into thin air.

At the same time, the blood blade slowly dissipated, turning into a wisp of vengeful spirits that merged into the Blood Spirit Array.

“Hahaha, this is just the beginning!” The ancestor of vengeful spirits roared again, his voice tinged with smug contempt.

“Damn it! You… if you’ve got the guts, come out and fight me one-on-one!” Xiao Zhan roared furiously, his angry voice deafening.

“Young man, even though your soul power is strong enough, it’s still wishful thinking to try and break through the vengeful spirits that have covered my accumulated blood and yin energy over the years!” The ancestor of vengeful spirits ignored Xiao Zhan’s provocation and arrogantly said to Xiao Yan, “Just obediently accept your death!”

“That’s really arrogant.” Jenny couldn’t help but interject, brushing aside the messy hair with her slender hand to reveal her beautiful face. She turned to look worriedly at Xiao Yan, whose face was turning pale and then red.

She understood his worries, but she was at a loss and extremely anxious. Feeling Jenny’s gaze, Xiao Yan slightly curled the corners of his mouth, revealing a forced smile.

“If soul power is useless, then how can we break this Blood Spirit Formation?” Gazing at the blood-red array of vengeful spirits, Xiao Yan fell into deep thought.

“The second flag moves, and the blood-stained spears emerge from the cave!” Without giving them a moment to catch their breath, the Ancestor of Wraiths unleashed his second attack. Eighteen blood-red banners surged violently like a raging flood, while eighteen blood-red spears changed positions swiftly like lightning, appearing and disappearing unpredictably, piercing through layers of blood mist and heading straight for the crowd.

At the same time, amidst the overwhelming blood-red aura, the sharp and mournful cries of countless vengeful spirits suddenly rang out, disturbing everyone’s minds. The blood-red flag waved, the spear tips danced like the forked tongues of venomous snakes, flashing with a sharp light, so agile and swift that it was impossible to tell what was real and what was not.

“call!” A whistling sound suddenly rang out. Before Xiao Yan could react, the blood-red spear tip had already pierced through his clothes and was heading straight for his chest.

“careful!” Le Shaolong and Zi Ying had been waiting for a long time, but were taken aback by the speed of the blood spear. Their weapons were already drawn and their figures had disappeared, flashing in front of Xiao Yan, leaving only two afterimages in their original positions.

His solid figure and sharp weapon just barely blocked the spear’s attack, and then, following the momentum, he swiftly slashed at the eighteen spears with a blade as fast as the wind.

“Bang bang bang!” The eighteen blood-red spears were like eighteen enraged dragons, fiercely striking at Le Shaolong and Zi Ying with a bloodthirsty ferocity, each strike more ferocious and awe-inspiring than the last. Large beads of sweat appeared on Le Shaolong and Zi Ying’s foreheads, their faces became distorted and pale, their hands gripping their weapons tightly with veins bulging, and a violent clanging sound came from between their teeth.

They won’t be able to hold on much longer. The crowd hastily drew their weapons, forming a dazzling series of hand seals, preparing to provide support.

Just then, “Bang!” A loud explosion suddenly erupted in the sky filled with blood mist. The two of them unleashed their powerful battle qi with all their might.

The final blood spear flashed by, and the heat wave surged away and disappeared in a hurry “We finally held on!” Le Shaolong and Zi Ying both breathed a sigh of relief. A numbness spread throughout their bodies, followed by waves of burning pain that pierced their hearts.

Under this intense pain, even their toes felt weak, and they lost their balance, falling to the ground. The others quickly caught them.

“The third flag moves, the blood sword swiftly winds!” Within the blood-red array, swords moved like the wind. Even before the fragments of the spear were still some distance from the ground, eighteen blood-red swords were already upon them.

The sword blades clanged and clashed, intertwining to form an impenetrable net of swords. The sword wind was extremely cold, stirring up a raging storm. The crowd was like a piece of duckweed adrift in a raging sea, swaying helplessly in the sky, unable to maintain their balance, and their formation was thrown into disarray.

A rustling sound rang out as the sword net swiftly swept past the group, splitting into two groups to directly attack Xiao Zhan and Xiao Yan. Xiao Yan wiped the sweat from his forehead, his bone wings trembled slightly, and he quickly adjusted his posture, drew the Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler, and calmly met the attack. Xiao Zhan, who took the hard route, was unsteady on his feet and lost his balance.

He was unable to stand up and defend, and instead became the team’s weak point. It must be said that the Ancestor of Wraiths had an absolutely brilliant grasp of the situation, and in just a few moves, he saw through everyone’s weaknesses. Xiao Yan is the core of the team.

Although his soul power is strong, he is only at the late stage of the three-star level. Xiao Zhan is brave and has extremely strong defense, but his weakness is obvious if he is not steady. Once these two are eliminated, the rest of the team will fall apart like scattered sand and collapse without a fight.

“Let’s see how long you can hold out!”

As soon as the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits finished speaking, the crimson sword net intensified, relentlessly stabbing towards Xiao Yan and Xiao Zhan.

“Ha!” Xiao Zhan shouted in a deep voice, his body enveloped in golden light, and his armor reappeared, with mysterious runes flowing within it. The two forces clashed, producing a series of piercing squeaks.

However, after only a few seconds of confrontation, the golden armor was shattered by the sharp blood sword. At the same time, the crimson sword net was quickly obliterated, leaving only sharp sword energy that relentlessly carved deep, bone-revealing bloodstains on Xiao Zhan’s powerful body.

As soon as the sword passed, Xiao Zhan quickly took out a few pills from his storage ring, swallowed them whole, then sat cross-legged, closed his eyes, and took the time to regulate his breathing. Although Xiao Zhan barely managed to block two rounds of attacks from the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits, he also exhausted his fighting spirit, his body covered in bloody marks, a truly unsightly sight.

Even so, Xiao Zhan was still able to barely protect himself, so everyone immediately retreated to Xiao Yan’s side, their weapons moving at will, instantly engaging in fierce combat with the approaching sword net. The Blood Sword was unpredictable, its sword strikes sometimes hard and sometimes soft, sometimes fast and sometimes slow, sometimes feinting and sometimes real.

However, thanks to the concerted efforts of the well-coordinated group, the Blood Sword Gale, the third banner of the Ancestor of Wraiths, could only return empty-handed.

“The fourth flag moves, and blood-stained spears pierce the clouds!” In the illusion, the Ancestor of Wraiths waved his hand, and a huge blood dragon halberd appeared above the Blood Spirit Array. The halberd has been known as the “king of weapons” since ancient times!

The Blood Dragon Halberd was unleashed with overwhelming power, its blade adorned with two dragons pointing skyward with unparalleled arrogance. The space within the formation instantly began to tremble and collapse, the blade tip pointing directly at the crowd. The power of the Blood Halberd followed immediately after the dissipation of the Blood Sword, combining seamlessly.

Although its approach was very slow, it was as frigid as ice that had been frozen for hundreds of millions of years, approaching inch by inch, chilling people to the bone. Everyone felt a suffocating sensation, as if even their souls were about to be frozen. Slow, incredibly slow.

Time seemed to stand still at that moment. Under the astonished gazes of Xiao Yan and the others, the Blood Dragon Halberd moved through the air at an almost halting speed, but the power it emitted became more and more terrifying. The air within the Blood Spirit Formation seemed to be frozen in place by the power of the Blood Dragon Halberd, growing increasingly dense.

Everyone felt as if they had fallen into a swamp, their movements slowing to a crawl, each gesture feeling as if bound by a thousand pounds of force, causing them unbearable discomfort. Jenny turned her head slightly and looked at the group of people who were extremely tired from the pressure. With the three flags of the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits striking down, Xiao Zhan was seriously injured, and Yue Shaolong and Zi Ying also suffered considerable losses.

Although Xiao Yan was unharmed, his soul power was exhausted. If the Blood Dragon Halberd were allowed to continue its oppressive force, everyone would probably turn into a pile of minced meat. Jenny took a difficult breath of the murky air, her slender, jade-like hands moving with difficulty, her fighting spirit circulating throughout her body, and a surge of powerful energy flowing from her fingertips.

Instantly, the air rippled, and countless vines suddenly appeared on the several-meter-long handle of the slowly sinking Blood Dragon Battle Halberd. The vines twined upwards, extending rapidly, absorbing the blood essence and transforming it into nutrients. The green leaves, as if newly sprouted after the rain, instantly covered the entire Blood Dragon Battle Halberd.

“Buzz!” Bound tightly by the green vines, the Blood Dragon Halberd struggled violently, trying to break free from the endless vitality.

“Bang! Bang!” The vines were constantly shattered by the powerful energy of the Blood Dragon Halberd, and then regenerated again and again, endlessly.

The struggle between life force and blood energy eased the pressure in the space, and everyone finally broke free from the pressure. Xiao Zhan leaped up from the ground, took a big step forward, clenched his fists so tightly they cracked, and channeled his pent-up anger into his right arm, throwing a punch.

Although the Blood Dragon Halberd’s blood energy was extremely corrosive, and although this powerful blood energy caused the vines to vanish into nothingness after only a short time, the Blood Dragon Halberd’s color also dimmed after its struggle with the vines.

“boom!” The Blood Dragon Halberd, which had not yet had time to replenish its energy from the Blood Spirit Array, met Xiao Zhan’s heavy, mountain-like punch! Then came the second punch!

“Boom boom boom!” The sound of metal clashing rang out in mid-air, and the fierce energy of the battle surged through the fists. One punch after another, like a thousand surging waves, crashed wildly and domineeringly against the Blood Dragon Battle Halberd.

“Waaah—” It’s unclear how many punches Xiao Zhan unleashed, but just as he was about to exhaust all his strength, a mournful sound came from the Blood Dragon Halberd. The massive halberd finally cracked open with spiderweb-like fissures, and it seemed on the verge of destruction. Even a centipede with a hundred legs will not fall down when dead.

The Blood Dragon Halberd was certainly not willing to be destroyed. The fragments scattered on Xiao Zhan’s body immediately released a large amount of bloody energy to backfire. The dense aura of blood corroded the surrounding air, like flames igniting, hissing and crackling, but it stopped outside Xiao Zhan’s golden armor, unable to advance an inch.

“Well!” Xiao Zhan let out a muffled groan, lowered his waist and twisted his body, raised his knee and squatted slightly, gathered his energy in his dantian, and sent his fighting spirit from his toes to his thighs, then used the power of his waist to transmit it to his right arm, unleashing his final punch! The fist’s intent was direct and simple.

The Blood Dragon Battle Halberd finally succumbed to this final blow after a series of attacks, shattering and scattering. Xiao Zhan slowly withdrew his fist and landed beside the crowd. A trickle of blood oozed from the corners of his mouth, indicating that he had suffered a serious injury.

However, his face showed surprise: “This Blood Spirit Formation is indeed powerful. This victory is really thanks to the young lady for taking the initiative.”

“Each banner is more powerful than the last, but this is only the fourth banner, and it has already caused Xiao Zhan to be injured repeatedly…” Xiao Yan sighed, his expression still solemn.

“Those who can break into this place are indeed remarkable. It’s just a pity that the Blood Spirit Formation has eighteen layers in total. As you said, you are only at the fourth level now.

If your skills stop here, this show can end here!” The words of the ancestor of vengeful spirits always rang out when everyone was at their most frustrated, igniting their anger.

“You bastard! Come out here if you dare!” Seeing Xiao Zhan severely injured, Zi Ying couldn’t help but roar angrily.

Unable to see the true form of the ancestor of vengeful spirits, yet trapped within the formation and unable to move in or out, this feeling of punching cotton with absolutely no force was driving her almost crazy.

“Haha, you’re not qualified to challenge me. Take my Eighteen Banners first!” The voice of the ancestor of vengeful spirits came from the blood energy.

The voice was deep and cold, and the purple shadow’s mockery did not cause him any emotional fluctuation.

“That sounds nice, but I think he’s just a coward.” Seeing the eighteen banners engulfed in blood-red flames, forcing everyone to move closer together and shrinking the space, Jenny was anxious and joined Zi Ying’s group, using a provocation tactic on the ancestor of vengeful spirits. Unfortunately, their response was— “The fifth flag rises, the blood-stained axe challenges the heavens!”

The ancestor of vengeful spirits gave a concise and clear answer: action was his only option. Blood surged, and a sharp axe swept through the air, with a vengeful spirit struggling in agony faintly visible on its tip. The greatest energy of a vengeful spirit originates from the resentment it harbors after death; as long as this resentment remains, the vengeful spirit will not dissipate.

The Blood Spirit Array is activated by burning the resentment of vengeful spirits, transforming it into energy that condenses into eighteen layers of banners, one after another. When one layer collapses, the vengeful spirits will merge back into the great formation, and the resentment will accumulate once more, making each layer more terrifying than the last. If the great formation were to unleash its final eighteenth banner, even a Seven-Star Dou Di would likely perish there.

They were only at the fifth flag now, and the axe beams they were unleashing were already stinging their skin from a great distance. Not to mention the eighteenth flag, even the sixth, seventh, and eighth flags that followed, they couldn’t guarantee they would be able to withstand them. Although they were all outstanding among their peers, they were not skilled in formations.

When they were in the formation, it was as if their eyes were blinded by fog. All they could see was rolling blood and rampant vengeful spirits. The cries and screams disturbed their minds, making it even more difficult to see the way forward in the confusion.

Blood Axe is approaching The group exchanged glances, their eyes growing even more determined. They moved closer together, forming a tight circle that completely surrounded Xiao Yan. The power of the axe blades came one after another, each stronger than the last, but Xiao Yan remained unmoved, as if everything around him was irrelevant, his mind racing.

As the Blood Axe drew near, blood mist swirled around it. Storm and Nanerming, who had been gathering their strength for a long time, met it head-on, flashing across the sky in an instant. Then, under everyone’s watchful eyes, they collided fiercely with the Blood Axe.

The moment they collided, a thunderous explosion resounded through the sky, and the Blood Axe was destroyed by the combined attack of the storm and Nan Erming. At the same time, muffled groans were heard, indicating that both of them had also suffered some injuries, though not serious, but it meant that the situation was becoming increasingly critical.

“The sixth flag moves, and the blood-red axe sweeps across the sky!”

“The Seventh Flag moves, the Blood Hook reaps lives!”

“The Eighth Flag Moves, the Blood Fork Pierces the Soul!” The continuous sounds, like a soul-reaping melody from the depths of the underworld, rang out coldly and mercilessly. Various vengeful spirits and bloodthirsty energy transformed into divine weapons that broke through the sky and appeared in the world, stirring up the wind and clouds.

Despite everyone’s desperate resistance, the blood soldiers shattered and reappeared one after another, and the Blood Spirit Array resounded like thunder, with dust flying everywhere. The blood flowing from the wounded was instantly absorbed into the bloodthirsty aura, making the whole scene eerie, bloody, and terrifyingly strange.

“The Ninth Flag rises, the Blood Whip lashes forth!” As the Blood Spirit Array unleashed its ninth attack, the group was forced to shrink in size, now huddled shoulder to shoulder, desperately fighting within a tiny space. The blood whip moved strangely and nimbly at times, and at other times it was like a storm, whipping out countless whip shadows that covered the entire area around everyone.

From a distance, it looked as if bloodstains were branded into the void. Everyone leaped into the air, their faces still calm as still water. Powerful battle qi surged from their weapons, and with an indomitable and fierce momentum, they unleashed area-of-effect battle techniques without hesitation.

The battle qi was so solid that it seemed tangible, and together they were about to cut through the whip shadow! Just as the battle aura was about to collide with the whip shadow, the whip shadow changed again, completely shrinking into a simple whip, avoiding a large-scale collision, breaking through the surface with a single point, and striking Xiao Zhan’s golden fist. Blood mist rose from the whip shadow, and Xiao Zhan’s fist immediately made a “hissing” sound.

The battle aura was almost dissolved, and the combined efforts of everyone were instantly broken. From within the blood mist, a chilling aura shot upwards, sending shivers down everyone’s spine. Transforming complexity into simplicity, and flamboyance into practicality, the Blood Whip, imbued with the resentment of the eight Blood Banners, unleashed its strongest power!

The power of the ninth level is incredibly strong! Xiao Zhan’s face was so gloomy it seemed to drip water. He waved his sleeves repeatedly, dispersing the blood mist and forcing back the whip shadows.

The whip flashed and disappeared in an instant, causing unease to rise in everyone’s hearts. They searched everywhere for any trace of the blood whip, their faces grave, not daring to let their guard down.

At this moment, Xiao Yan raised his head, his eyes brightening, and he already had a plan in mind. Xiao Yan mainly relies on three methods: Heavenly Fire, Soul Power, and Bone Wings. The damage from bone wings has little effect on spirits; the success of attacks using soul power depends on “Wrath of the Underworld,” “River of the Underworld,” and Blood Spirit Technique, but “Wrath of the Underworld” and “River of the Underworld” are no longer effective; now, only Blood Spirit Technique and Heavenly Fire remain.

The Blood Spirit Technique has a restraining effect on the soul, while Heavenly Fire has an innate advantage against Yin and evil energy. What would happen if the Blood Spirit Technique were combined with Heavenly Fire? With a thought, Xiao Yan slowly drew out the Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler.

At this moment, the blood whip reappeared, accompanied by a sharp sound, and pounced on Xiao Yan, the wind it generated blowing everyone’s clothes tightly against their bodies. Biting his lower lip, feeling the powerful wind pressure coming his way, Xiao Yan’s expression gradually became solemn. He bent his body slightly, stomped his left foot down, and used the force of the ground to lift himself up.

With a flap of his bone wings, he transformed into a streak of light and rushed forward. At the same time, he activated the Blood Spirit Technique, channeling mysterious power into the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler. Faintly visible blood vessels appeared on the thick ruler covered by bluish-gray flames, imprinted on it like veins.

The speed brought by the bone wings was so fast that even the blood whip couldn’t dodge it. Xiao Yan pursed his lips, his face grim. After the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler completed a near-perfect charge in mid-air, it simply slashed down without any fancy moves.

Xiao Yan’s goal was to test whether his ideas were effective, so the simpler the moves, the more effective they were. The moment the whip and ruler collided, a muffled sound, like a muffled thunderclap, spread out from the point of contact. Fighting spirit surged rapidly on the whip shadow, and it only had time to release a thick bloody aura to attack Xiao Yan before it was struck by the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler.

Xiao Yan, who was hit by the recoil, staggered back a few steps, felt a sweet taste in his throat, and spat out a mouthful of blood. The bloodthirsty aura pressed on, but Xiao Yan steadied himself, covering his body with heavenly fire to resist it. Instead of retreating, he advanced, and the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler slammed down with even more ferocious force.

Upon touching the cyan-gray heavenly fire formed by the fusion of the Blood Spirit Technique’s energy, the Blood Whip’s protective blood-fiend aura ignited instantly, as if doused with gasoline. The flames spread rapidly upwards, engulfing the Blood Whip. Extremely shrill screams emanated from the blood whip.

Countless vengeful spirits that had condensed into the blood whip had extremely distorted faces as they struggled to escape, but were bound by an invisible force and could not break free. The burning blood whip in mid-air became the focus of attention, attracting everyone’s gaze. Everyone’s eyes lit up, and Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up even more.

As he had thought, this method was indeed effective, but he did not expect the effect to be so significant. Just as the vengeful spirits that had condensed into the Blood Whip were about to be completely incinerated by the heavenly fire, the vengeful spirits within the Blood Whip suddenly unleashed unimaginable power, breaking free from their invisible restraints and fleeing in all directions with bluish-gray flames. Wherever the vengeful spirit went, bluish-gray flames followed and spread, like a flame encountering a vast oil field.

The fire became uncontrollable, and large swaths of bloody aura ignited, with swirling flames and bluish-gray flames filling the sky. Under the scorching flames, the eighteen large banners hidden deep within the blood-fiend energy were faintly revealed. The huge banners fluttered in the wind, continuously drawing blood-fiend energy into the formation, trying to replenish the lost energy.

One of the flags was not far from Xiao Yan. Xiao Yan moved his bone wings again and rushed over. His dark eyes suddenly surged with killing intent.

The Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler in his hand brought a sound of wind breaking and slammed down hard on the Blood Flag. The bluish-gray flames struck the flagpole, breaking it in two. The fluttering flags fell to the ground, and the bloodthirsty aura dissipated abruptly.

The dense, impenetrable blood energy of the Blood Spirit Array suddenly thinned out. Xiao Yan’s actions served as a guiding light, prompting everyone to take action and successively sever eight blood flags. The Blood Spirit Formation immediately began to teeter on the brink of collapse, its blood energy rapidly dissipating.

“The tenth flag moves, the blood hammer settles the score!” The Ancestor of Wraiths was enraged, and his voice, which had been filled with arrogance, suddenly lost its usual arrogance. Clearly, the situation had unfolded as he had anticipated, and he hurriedly activated the tenth level of the Blood Spirit Array.

A blood-red hammer hovered in the air, its shaft a thousand feet long, with countless runes swirling around it.

As soon as the Blood Hammer was unleashed, the power of the soul transformed into layers of ripples, the sky full of bluish-gray flames vanished without a trace, the Blood Banner quickly disappeared into the formation, and this place once again became a barren land devoid of life. After displaying its full power, the Blood Hammer dimmed and lost its luster, as if it had used all its strength to maintain the Blood Spirit Array, and then silently crumbled into dust.

Although the Blood Spirit Formation has been stabilized with difficulty, the loss of eight Blood Flags has reduced the surge of Blood Fiend Qi to a level far below what it was before. Seeing the grayish-blue Heavenly Flame, infused with the mysterious energy of the Blood Spirit Technique, achieve such results, Xiao Yan’s confidence soared. Now that the Blood Spirit Formation’s foundation was unstable, it was the perfect opportunity to destroy it in one fell swoop.

Opportunities are fleeting, and Xiao Yan naturally understands this principle. He will never allow the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits to consolidate its power again. Xiao Yan brandished the Heavenly Flame Ancient Ruler, and with the “Wandering Flame Transforms into Three Feet” technique, streaks of fire rippled out, sweeping across the ten directions like waves.

The bluish-gray flames ignited the seemingly calm but nearly boiling blood energy. A storm, seemingly in perfect harmony, surged forth, the staff shifted rapidly, whirlwinds arose, and the fire, fueled by the wind, unstoppably burned across the entire Blood Spirit Array.

“The eleventh flag rises, and blood-stained spears shatter the sky!” The voice of the Ancestor of Wraiths became extremely shrill, as the uncontrollable fire brought the Ancestor of Wraiths’ anger to its limit. The blood spear pierced through the air, and the hurricane it created disrupted the flames.

The heavy pressure caused the ground where everyone was standing to cave in, and the people who were suspended in mid-air lost their footing and fell to the ground. As expected of the ultimate move from the eighth level of the Blood Spirit Formation, its power was extraordinary. Unfortunately, with the Eight Banners destroyed, the Blood Spirit Formation lost its power, and the Blood Spear had barely descended into the air when its color quickly dimmed and its energy rapidly dissipated.

With the pressure on everyone greatly reduced, Xiao Zhan roared, his eyes narrowed, and a cold smile appeared on his lips. He unleashed a series of eighteen punches against the wind, each punch tearing through the air and destroying the Blood Spear. With the Blood Spear destroyed, the Blood Spirit Array became even darker, and the bluish-gray flames burned even more wildly.

Xiao Yan gave the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits no time to recover, and once again used “Wandering Flame Transforms into Three Feet,” where the foot’s shadow multiplied into thousands and then merged back into one, like a fire dragon traversing the Blood Spirit Array, shaking the foundation of the array. After suffering repeated blows, the Blood Spirit Array could no longer be maintained, and the remaining ten Blood Flags appeared. Everyone rushed forward and destroyed the Blood Flags in one fell swoop.

As the blood flag was extinguished, the blood-red aura dissipated, revealing the desolate desert before everyone, where the vast army of white bones still stood blindly in the distance. On the throne of bones, a phantom figure, almost transparent, had his arrogant expression replaced by resentment. He stared viciously at the crowd, and the hand supporting the throne of bones trembled slightly.

It seems that the destruction of the Blood Spirit Array has caused considerable damage to the Ancestor of Wraiths. The survivors, having just escaped danger, did not waste time on endless reflections. Xiao Yan and Yue Shaolong took the lead, using the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler to seal off the sky and their twin daggers to lock onto the area below, closing in on the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits.

Considering the terrifying power of the Blood Spirit Formation, Xiao Yan and the others didn’t want anything to escalate, so finishing off their opponents as quickly as possible was the safest course of action. Faced with the crowd’s swift reaction and merciless attacks, the ancestor of vengeful spirits’ eyes grew even more venomous, and his illusory figure trembled. He raised his translucent arm, causing ripples to spread across the heavens and earth, as energy rapidly gathered.

He seemed to want to do something, but after a moment, his arm fell limply to his side again. It wasn’t that he didn’t want to; he desperately wanted to.

However, he hadn’t recovered yet and couldn’t unleash his powerful fighting skills. The Ancestor of Wraiths could only let out an extremely mournful howl. With this whoosh, the previously bewildered and helpless skeleton army began to stir and charged towards the group.

However, after the great formation was broken, the ancestor of vengeful spirits was unable to control the fusion of vengeful spirits and bones, and the bone army alone did not pose much of a threat. Xiao Yan and Le Shaolong continued their offensive, while the rest of the group, led by Zhen Ni, charged into the skeleton horde like tigers descending a mountain, leaving the skeleton army powerless to stop them. Everywhere there were flashes of swords and shattered bones, a scene of utter devastation resembling a hellish landscape.

In that instant, Xiao Yan and Yue Shaolong had already closed in. The ancestor of vengeful spirits’ blood-red eyes suddenly turned cold, and his soul power was rapidly condensing. A strange rune was about to take shape in the air, and a sharp, piercing force whistled out.

“not good.” Just as Xiao Yan was about to speak up to remind Le Shaolong, the Youjue Mingling, which had been silent in his dantian, suddenly trembled and a powerful pressure emanated from it. The ancestor of vengeful spirits froze as it was about to complete its action, and the runes immediately lost their continuation of fighting spirit and dissipated in mid-air.

The ancestor of vengeful spirits turned deathly pale, an expression of disbelief appearing on his face, and a deep fear flashing in his eyes. He dared not make another move. The two rushed off so fast that Xiao Yan didn’t even have time to think about why the Netherworld Spirit was trembling and about to emerge at this moment, and they had already arrived in front of the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits.

Compared to the heavy Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler, the nimble twin daggers undoubtedly had the advantage in speed. Le Shaolong took the initiative, dodging the barely resisting hands of the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits. With a swift movement, the twin daggers pierced the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits’ chest.

The powerful battle qi slammed the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits hard onto the White Bone Throne. The residual force from his body shattered the White Bone Throne, creating countless cracks. Immediately following, the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler smashed into the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits’ head.

The heavenly fire spread along the Heavenly Fire Ancient Ruler to the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits’ body, instantly scorching the bloodthirsty qi on the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits’ surface. Having landed a successful blow, Xiao Yan and Yue Shaolong subtly shifted their positions, creating a considerable distance to prevent the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits from launching a desperate counterattack.

Having lost the control of the Ancestor of Wraiths, the skeleton legion’s empty eyes glazed over, their broken heads swaying aimlessly from side to side, all standing frozen in place. Everyone breathed a sigh of relief and flew to Xiao Yan’s side. The ancestor of vengeful spirits pointed a trembling finger at Xiao Yan, his expression filled with extreme resentment and fear, yet he was speechless for a moment, leaving everyone utterly puzzled.

Only Xiao Yan, witnessing the scene before him, felt a tidal wave of emotions churning within him! Recalling the sudden dissipation of the Vengeful Spirit Ancestor’s attack and the drastic change in his expression, and feeling the restless stirrings of the Netherworld Spirit within his body, this situation was strikingly similar to the Chaos Sacred Flame’s interaction with the Spirit-Connecting Pill Fire! Could it be…?

Xiao Yan could no longer remain calm; that conjecture kept churning in his mind. Despite their doubts and speculations, the group showed no mercy, nor dared to! The Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits possessed the strength of a peak six-star being; once he recovered, no one could predict what might happen.

With Xiao Yan’s long-range Fire Lotus attack, everyone’s battle qi slammed towards the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits. The combined full force of a six-star Dou Di and five five-star Dou Di, along with a fire lotus condensed from three of the top twenty heavenly flames, would be something even the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits might not have been able to withstand at his peak, let alone now that he is so weak.

Sensing the impending death, the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits stared intently at Xiao Yan and the others, then burst into loud laughter in extreme resentment. Amidst his hysterical laughter, a grand feast of fighting spirit unfolded on the White Bone Throne. The already cracked Bone Throne could no longer withstand the pressure and collapsed with a deafening explosion.

Countless bone fragments scattered across the sky and earth like a heavy snowfall in the dead of winter, interspersed with specks of blood—the blood that had stained the Bone Throne for thousands of years, carrying a poignant beauty—as they drifted onto Xiao Yan and his companions, landing at their feet…
