Chapter 71: Three Wonders in One Burial Site

The once invincible progenitor of vengeful spirits has fallen, leaving everyone feeling somewhat saddened. As the smoke dissipated, the progenitor of vengeful spirits did not vanish, but it also disappeared without a trace. Amidst the fragments of the throne of bones stood a nearly transparent spirit, appearing bewildered and devoid of intelligence.

But as soon as the spirit appeared, the invisible pressure dissipated, and all the vengeful spirits of heaven and earth showed extreme fear and submitted one after another. In this respect, it was exactly the same as the ancestor of vengeful spirits.

“Could this be his true form? What exactly is this thing?” Le Shaolong exclaimed in surprise, his mouth agape. The others were equally puzzled.

Only Xiao Yan was overjoyed, because he recognized it at a glance – it was Gui Ling!

“My guess was right, it really is a ghost! It’s actually a ghost! This illusion not only has heavenly fire and spirit seals, but also a ghost!”

Although he had suspected it beforehand, Xiao Yan was still deeply shocked after it was confirmed. I came to this illusion to collect Spirit Imprint Moss, naturally hoping to obtain the Spirit Imprint as well.

However, before even seeing the Spirit Imprint, I first obtained the sixteenth-ranked Heavenly Fire—the Spirit-Communicating Pill Fire. Now, I’ve even encountered a Ghost Spirit, one of the Three Wonders! In one illusion, there are actually three Wonders—Heavenly Fire, Ghost Spirit, and Spirit Imprint—coexisting simultaneously!

My goodness, what other surprises will this illusion bring!

“It’s actually a ghost spirit!” Zhan Lao’s exclamation suddenly echoed in Xiao Yan’s mind.

“Even I was mistaken. It turns out that this place is not just two wonders in one cave, but three wonders in one cave! This is extremely rare and unheard of!”

Xiao Yan could tell that the usually calm and composed Elder Zhan was definitely in tears. It’s truly remarkable that the usually composed Elder Zhan could be so excited. — Xiao Yan thought to himself.

“Kid, you’ve really struck gold this time! You actually managed to get three rare items at once! I’m even starting to wonder if you’re even human.

How can you have such incredible luck!” Before Elder Zhan could calm down from his excitement, he began to marvel at Xiao Yan’s incredible luck. Surprisingly, his sighs carried a hint of envy and jealousy, as if he were lamenting not for Xiao Yan, but for himself, wondering why he hadn’t had such an opportunity back then.

After a long while, Elder Zhan finally calmed down from his excitement and emotion, and became very gratified—how could a kid with the strength of a three-star Dou Di obtain three rare treasures with the strength of a six-star peak? It was not something that could be done by luck alone. Let alone a three-star Dou Di, even a six-star Dou Di is hard to say whether they’ll have the life to obtain one!

“By the way, before absorbing the ghost spirits, think about how you’re going to explain it to them later,” Elder Zhan suddenly remembered and reminded Xiao Yan. Xiao Yan was immediately taken aback. Indeed, for a human to be able to absorb ghost spirits was something that had never happened before on the Dou Di Continent.

How could this be explained? Xiao Yan frowned, his expression becoming somewhat solemn.

“Hmm?”

Just then, Elder Zhan suddenly sensed a barely perceptible disturbance coming from the top of the tower.

“What, is he about to break through?” — Elder Zhan muttered to himself.

“Why are you so solemn? It’s just two bloodlines, right? You’ll have three bloodlines in the future!

Figure out how to explain these trivial matters yourself.” Elder Zhan hurriedly said this and then fell silent.

“This is a small matter?” Xiao Yan was almost choked by Elder Zhan’s words and didn’t come back to his senses for a long time. Being able to possess both heavenly fire and ghostly spirits at the same time, is that a minor matter?

Having both human and demon blood is a minor matter. Is it a trivial matter that a human can devour a magic core to level up? If this got out, Xiao Yan believed it would immediately cause the biggest sensation in the history of the Dou Di Continent!

He might even be captured and dissected by various powerful forces! Was this a trivial matter? But in Zhan Lao’s mouth, it was just a trivial matter!

Xiao Yan was speechless. Xiao Yan frowned and thought for a long time. Looking at his teammates who had shared life and death with him, the scenes of their life-and-death experiences since entering the illusion flashed through his mind, and his brows slowly relaxed.

Teammates you can entrust with your life, brothers who will follow you for life—what is there to worry about? What can’t you trust? What is there to hide?

You can hide it for a while, but can you hide it forever? Thinking of this, Xiao Yan stepped forward lightly.

At this moment, everyone was circling around the ghost, curiously examining it. Jenny cast a concerned look at Xiao Yan. With her intelligence, Jenny naturally noticed Xiao Yan’s shock and worry after the appearance of the spirit.

Although she didn’t know why, her female intuition told her that Xiao Yan was deeply moved by the spirit in front of him and even had some concerns. Xiao Yan gave Zhen Ni a grateful, reassuring look, then stepped lightly through the crowd and stood before Gui Ling. Sensing Gui Ling’s familiar scent, he cleared his still somewhat confused thoughts, paused for a moment, and slowly said, “This is Gui Ling…”

After speaking, Xiao Yan subconsciously looked at his companions.

“A ghost?!” Upon learning that this object was actually one of the three wondrous items, the Ghost Spirit, just as Xiao Yan had predicted, everyone was stunned, and at the same time, they were in an uproar. Each burning gaze seemed to pierce through the ghost, like spying on a naked, beautiful young woman; naked desire instantly ignited the entire team.

“Goodness, this is a ghost spirit! If you devour it, it will greatly increase your strength! Sigh, why am I not a demon!

This time, Zi Ying and Nan Er Ming are really lucky.” Storm said, turning to look at Zi Ying and Nan Er Ming, making no attempt to hide his envy of them.

“I’m so jealous of you guys!” Xiao Zhan patted Nan Erming on the shoulder, saying this but laughing heartily.

“You two decide for yourselves who will devour it,” Le Shaolong said with a smile. A struggle flashed in Nan Erming’s eyes. He bit his lip, then resolutely turned to the expectant Zi Ying and said, “Zi Ying, you shall devour.”

Upon hearing this, Zi Ying was overjoyed, her expression lighting up with excitement, but it vanished in an instant.

“No, ghost spirits are too rare. It’s more appropriate for you to devour them than for me to do so.”

“Zi Ying, I’m not being polite. In the team, you excel at assassination and sneak attacks. Improving your strength will be of greater significance to the team.”

Nan Erming’s calm and meticulous analysis drew unanimous nods of agreement from the others. Upon hearing this, Zi Ying paused for a moment, then said, “No, devouring ghost spirits can not only increase strength, but also enhance soul power. You need soul power more than I do, so it’s more suitable for you to devour them.”

“Stop arguing, just absorb it. Your assassinations and ambushes also require an increase in soul power,” Nan Erming insisted… Xiao Yan, standing to the side, felt embarrassed as he watched the crowd discussing animatedly, even deciding who would devour the ghost spirit.

“Uh…” Xiao Yan couldn’t help but interrupt, but then he didn’t know how to start the conversation. Zhenni, who had been keeping an eye on Xiao Yan, saw his hesitation and gave him an encouraging glance with her watery eyes.

She thought that Xiao Yan was about to decide whether it was Zi Ying or Nan Erming who would devour the ghost spirit. Men are always braver in front of women. Xiao Yan cleared his throat, made up his mind, and said, “I have something to tell everyone.” Xiao Yan’s words interrupted the discussion.

It was rare to see Xiao Yan so cautious, and all eyes were on him, waiting for him to make a decision.

“Actually… I can devour ghost spirits…” Xiao Yan said softly, continuing to explain, “I once received the bloodline baptism of the demon race, so I possess the bloodlines of both humans and demons.” silence.

A deathly silence. Everyone, including the extremely conscious Jenny, was so astonished they couldn’t close their mouths. Their eyes froze for a moment, staring blankly at Xiao Yan with disbelief on their faces.

What did Young Master Xiao just say? He possesses both human and demon bloodlines? How is this possible?

When has the Dou Di Continent ever heard of anyone possessing the bloodline of another race? We didn’t hear wrong, did we? Although Xiao Yan has done many amazing things, this one is just too unbelievable.

But… but that’s exactly what Young Master Xiao just said, that he possesses the bloodlines of both humans and demons! What does this mean?

It means that he can not only devour heavenly fire and ghost spirits, but also demonic cores! My goodness! If he can devour magic cores, won’t his advancement speed skyrocket?

Hmm, luckily high-level magic cores are rare and expensive… Wait, will price be a problem? Not at all!

My Dou Xian, does this mean our Young Master Xiao could very well become the fastest-rising cultivator in the history of the Dou Di Continent? For a moment, everyone stood frozen in place, no different from the bewildered skeletons in the distance.

At this moment, the crowd’s emotions were beyond description; words simply couldn’t capture them. The scene grew even quieter, so quiet that even breathing seemed to stop, with only the monotonous sound of the wind swirling past. Xiao Yan did not explain why he had obtained the inheritance of the Demon Clan’s bloodline.

Without Elder Zhan’s permission, Xiao Yan did not intend to reveal the Zichen Void Spirit Tower and Elder Zhan for the time being. Moreover, the reactions of the crowd were beyond Xiao Yan’s expectations.

“Well… moreover, the cultivation technique I practice requires a lot of ghost spirits, and a lot of them…” Seeing everyone staring blankly at him, Xiao Yan had no choice but to continue, his expression stiff, a bitter smile on his lips, his hands clenched tightly, his nails digging deep into his flesh.

Xiao Yan’s words snapped everyone out of their shock and daze.

“Young Master Xiao, there’s nothing to say. Since it’s required by your cultivation technique, the ghost spirits will definitely be devoured by you.”

A warm feeling rose in his heart, making his breathing quicken and his eyes well up with tears. Saying anything else at this point would seem hypocritical, so Xiao Yan nodded without thanking him, because that would be superfluous and only invite criticism “Speaking of which, apart from being slightly weaker than us in strength, we can’t catch up with Young Master Xiao in any other aspect.” Zi Ying’s bright eyes sparkled with immense admiration.

“Young Master Xiao, you are my idol.” Xiao Zhan and Le Shaolong sighed softly, shook their heads self-deprecatingly, stepped forward and patted Xiao Yan’s shoulder heavily, their thousand words condensed into a simple gesture of admiration and praise. Jenny gazed at Xiao Yan with a happy expression, a serene smile blooming on her face, like a noble snow lotus, brightening the desert landscape. Feng Bao also stepped forward and placed his hand on Xiao Yan’s shoulder.

“I wonder what the effects will be after Young Master Xiao absorbs the ghost spirit. I’m really looking forward to it.” Zhen Ni smiled sweetly, her beautiful eyes sparkling.

“Why don’t you try and see?” Purple Shadow’s admiring gaze turned into a blazing fire.

“Young Master Xiao, hurry up and absorb the ghost spirit, everyone is getting impatient.” Zi Ying continued, her delicate nose wrinkling slightly, showing her eagerness, which amused everyone.

“Alright.” Xiao Yan couldn’t help but laugh, feeling the eager gazes of everyone, and planned to devour it on the spot. With the ghostly presence as a deterrent, neither the vengeful spirits nor the skeleton army dared to act rashly, so there was no danger to speak of.

With a wave of his sleeve, he swept away the bone fragments scattered on the ground, clearing a clean space where Xiao Yan sat down cross-legged.

“Elder Zhan, Elder Zhan, do you know what rank this ghost is?” Xiao Yan was very excited as he swept up the mindless ghost and couldn’t help but ask Elder Zhan.

“There is only one who can command all the vengeful spirits in the world, and that is the ancestor of vengeful spirits, ranked seventeenth.” Elder Zhan’s voice suddenly rang out, answering Xiao Yan’s question before falling silent again. Seventeen?

Not bad, not bad. Although it can’t compare to the Netherworld Spirit bestowed by the Old Demon Emperor, it’s still a ghost spirit ranked in the top twenty of the Dou Di Continent. Xiao Yan is quite satisfied.

With a focused mind, Xiao Yan activated his Heavenly Fire to protect himself and swallowed the ghost spirit. A chill instantly rushed from his mouth to his internal organs, as if wind had whipped snow into his body, instantly enveloping his entire being. The chill penetrated to the bone, causing Xiao Yan to shiver involuntarily.

The celestial fire shifted slightly, dispelling the chill. The bluish-gray flame formed by the fusion of the three celestial fires still possessed absolute suppression over the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits. Although the previous Netherworld Spirit ranked fifth and was much stronger than the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits, it automatically recognized Xiao Yan as its master and obeyed him completely.

Therefore, there was no difficulty in subduing or absorbing it.

However, he could not be careless when absorbing the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits. Xiao Yan immediately calmed his mind, activated the Heavenly Fire to protect his meridians, and began to absorb the ghost spirit seriously. The pressure of the Netherworld Spirit intimidated the Ancestor of Wraiths, and the power of the three Heavenly Flames forced the Ancestor of Wraiths to submit.

The ghost spirit, which was only ranked seventeenth, was not much of a challenge for Xiao Yan to devour, and the pain he suffered was not even half that of devouring the Spirit Communication Pill Flame. The devouring proceeded smoothly, and Xiao Yan’s body was covered in frost. The spreading chill made this originally scorching space seem to have entered the dead of winter.

Seeing Xiao Yan absorb the three wondrous items for the first time was quite a novel experience for everyone. They gathered around Xiao Yan, but they never expected the ghost spirit to be so powerful. It was chilling, and the ghost spirit pricked their skin like needles, as if they had jumped from a scorching furnace into an icy cellar.

The leaking cold air was so strong that it was unknown how much pain Xiao Yan was enduring. Everyone looked at Xiao Yan with concern. The air was eerie and cold.

Moments later, snowflakes began to fall from the sky. More and more snowflakes fell, and the space was instantly covered in white. Xiao Yan remained motionless, covered in snow and turned into an ice sculpture.

“Is Young Master Xiao in danger?” The usually cheerful Xiao Zhan looked very serious and asked in a helpless tone. He had never dealt with this kind of thing before and had no idea what it was.

“You’re asking me? Who should I ask?” Nan Erming’s words were even more helpless than Xiao Zhan’s, and his cold face was even colder than the wind.

“I can only sense the wind, not ice or fire… I can’t tell what’s wrong with Young Master Xiao.” Storm rubbed his hands together, pacing back and forth anxiously.

“Look closely at Young Master Xiao’s expression; he should be fine.” Le Shaolong’s shrewdness always allowed him to solve problems at crucial moments. Through the transparent ice, one could see Xiao Yan, who looked like an ice sculpture, with his eyes closed and his thin lips pursed together, but his expression was calm, without showing any anxiety or pain.

Only then did everyone’s mood gradually calm down. As the strong winds subsided and the temperature dropped, the snowflakes accumulated and thickened, and everyone’s mood became more and more relaxed. Xiao Yan’s absorption seemed to be going very smoothly, without any setbacks.

“I must say, seeing such a heavy snowfall in this desolate, scorching desert is quite a treat,” Xiao Zhan said contentedly, brushing the snowflakes off his clothes. Nan Erming, who was usually a man of few words, simply crossed his arms, rested his head on his hands, and took a short rest to recover from the fatigue of the days of fierce fighting. Not far away, Zi Ying sat quietly with her arms crossed, waiting.

Her beautiful face was like a delicate rose blooming in the desert. Jenny gazed at Xiao Yan, who was now completely covered in ice and snow, her fingers fidgeting nervously, indicating her unease. A white dress fluttered in the wind, as if it wanted to melt into the heavy snow.

Le Shaolong stood next to Jenny Tseng, his expression calm, seemingly lost in thought. The storm, meanwhile, had already merged into the seemingly tireless wind of the desert, trying to understand the pulse of the wind. The desert was vast and desolate, with the blood moon shining obliquely, casting a dreamlike glow on the ice and snow.

It wasn’t very bright, but it had a unique charm. Time passed day by day, and everyone’s minds gradually became completely immersed in cultivation… One night, after an unknown amount of time, the ice sculpture, which had been calm for a long time, suddenly cracked, and countless cracks spread out.

The energy of heaven and earth trembled, and then the ice sculpture shattered into countless pieces. A black figure soared into the sky, and the aura of a peak three-star Dou Di burst out, making a muffled thunderous sound in the air. Xiao Yan, who devoured the ancestor of vengeful spirits, has finally emerged from seclusion!

Feeling the power, Xiao Yan couldn’t help but roar to the sky, the roar lasting endlessly.

“He advanced again! That’s insane!” The crowd below watched Xiao Yan, already somewhat numb to his monstrous rate of advancement.

“Besides advancing, there should be other gains as well.” Accustomed to constant surprises, the crowd awaited Xiao Yan’s next move after he had landed back on the ground. Looking at the crowd who were eagerly anticipating a new surprise, Xiao Yan was speechless.

Was he really that abnormal?

However, recalling his progress in the short few years since he arrived on the Dou Di Continent, Xiao Yan had to admit that he was indeed not the normal type.

“His soul power has also broken through; he is now at the peak of the Emperor Realm.” Xiao Yan gave everyone a new surprise they had been waiting for, and at the same time released his soul power, which, like a rolling tide, instantly covered a very far area. After his breakthrough in soul power, Xiao Yan’s perception became clearer.

The range of his perception was as if he were seeing it with his own eyes: yellow sand, boundless, without vegetation or water, only emptiness and silence. The power of the soul continued to extend, stretching to an unknown distance. The entire desert was devoid of any oasis, not even a single leaf could be seen, and the original legion of bones and vengeful spirits had vanished without a trace.

The wind in the air became gentler, and even the slightest ripple in the air thousands of miles away could not escape Xiao Yan’s perception. His soul power roamed freely under the sky like a fish leaping in the vast sea and a bird soaring in the high sky, unrestrained and boundless. This feeling was wonderful, which made Xiao Yan extremely gratified.

It meant that his soul fighting skills would become much stronger and his alchemy skills would reach a new level. While Xiao Yan was still reveling in the changes in his soul power, the numb expressions on everyone else’s faces had long since changed. Having witnessed Xiao Yan’s extraordinary advancement of two levels after absorbing the Spirit Pill Fire, the crowd wasn’t particularly shocked when they saw him advance to the peak of the three-star level after absorbing the Ghost Spirit.

However, the advancement of his soul power from the late Emperor Realm to the peak Emperor Realm was a completely different story. Everyone knows that advancing in soul power is far more difficult than advancing in battle qi strength—not just a little more difficult, but much more difficult! Moreover, it gets harder and harder as you go on!

Advancing from the late stage of the Emperor Realm to the peak of the Emperor Realm is extremely difficult! This can be seen from the scarcity of advanced alchemists in the Dou Di Continent throughout history By devouring and absorbing a ghost spirit, one’s soul power increased from the late stage of the Emperor Realm to the peak of the Emperor Realm. Although absorbing ghosts can enhance one’s soul power, the enhancement shouldn’t be this great, because they all know that very little of the energy absorbed from ghosts can be converted into soul power.

The amount of energy absorbed from ghosts and spirits and transformed into soul power depends not only on the strength of the ghosts and spirits, but also on the strength of the absorber’s soul power, and even more so on the richness and nobility of the demon bloodline! How powerful was the ghost spirit that Xiao Yan absorbed? Even a peak six-star ghost spirit, no matter how powerful, would likely have limited strength, probably ranking similarly to the Spirit Connection Flame.

Although Xiao Yan’s soul power was much stronger than everyone else’s, it couldn’t be considered powerful. On the Dou Di Continent, the soul power of an Emperor Realm cultivator was far from being considered powerful; only when one reached the Intent Realm could it be called powerful. As for a human, although he incredibly possessed the bloodline of the Demon Race, his bloodline was probably not very strong, let alone noble.

However, he actually advanced from the late stage of the Emperor Realm to the peak of the Emperor Realm! Is our Young Master Xiao even human? Not only has his Dou Qi level advancement been monstrous, but his soul power advancement is also so heaven-defying!

This is both surprising and incredibly disheartening, is it even trying to kill us? It’s no wonder everyone was so astonished. Even Xiao Yan himself didn’t know how strong his demonic bloodline was, let alone that its nobility surpassed even that of the Demon King’s Blood Demon Clan!

Otherwise, how could he have met the old Demon Emperor? What no one knew was that Xiao Yan was able to convert more ghostly energy into soul power because he possessed a quasi-saint-level soul technique—the Blood Spirit Technique! When Xiao Yan absorbed the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits, it wasn’t just that his Dou Qi advanced from the late stage of the three-star level to the peak of the three-star level, and his soul power advanced from the late stage of the Emperor Realm to the peak of the Emperor Realm.

There was another benefit he didn’t mention: his Blood Spirit Technique also improved! Previously, the Blood Spirit Technique was activated. The soul energy transformed into wisps of silk, almost invisible to the naked eye.

Now, these threads are intertwined and much thicker, taking on a tangible form, faintly tinged with a dark red hue. The powerful energy exudes a chilling, malevolent aura, swirling within the sea of consciousness. The transformation from intangible to tangible signifies a significant enhancement to the Blood Spirit Technique, allowing it to unleash the powerful might of that mysterious energy.

Xiao Yan is now confident that if he were to unleash “Yellow Springs Wrath,” even without relying on the soul power of a peak Emperor Realm cultivator, its power would undoubtedly reach a new level simply with the support of the Blood Spirit Technique.

At this moment, everyone finally recovered from their astonishment. Besides congratulating them, they mostly smiled bitterly and shook their heads. The bitter smiles were filled with genuine joy, and as they shook their heads, they all decided that they would never be surprised by anything Xiao Yan did again, never!

Seeing everyone’s astonishment followed by a wry smile and a shake of the head, Xiao Yan knew that it was the improvement in his soul power that had shocked them. He was still a little confused. It was just advancing from the late stage of the Emperor Realm to the peak of the Emperor Realm.

He had only advanced one level by completely absorbing a ghost spirit ranked seventeenth. What was so shocking about that? He scratched his head in bewilderment.

Fortunately, he just scratched his head in confusion and didn’t ask, otherwise he would have been roasted by everyone! He had only been on the Dou Di Continent for a few years, how could he know how difficult it was to advance one’s soul power on the Dou Di Continent? Not only did he not know, but he had even felt a little melancholy just now, as the improvement in his soul power was not as significant as when he absorbed the Youjue Mingling.

Xiao Yan even felt that it was slightly insufficient. If Elder Zhan knew about Xiao Yan’s idea, he would definitely give him a severe scolding for “not appreciating his good fortune.” It’s important to understand that the previous Youjue Mingling ranked fifth on the Dou Di Continent’s Ghost Spirit Ranking, while the Ancestor of Wraiths was only ranked seventeenth.

Moreover, the Youjue Mingling was completely bound to Xiao Yan, meaning it was essentially converting 100% of Xiao Yan’s own energy!

However, when devouring the Ancestor of Wraiths, the energy conversion wouldn’t be complete. If it weren’t for his rich and noble demon bloodline and the monstrous Blood Spirit Technique, he wouldn’t have had a chance to directly advance to the peak of the Emperor Realm Soul stage! And yet, he’s still not satisfied!

“Young Master Xiao, I’ve made a decision…” Zi Ying was the first to speak, shaking her head with a wry smile.

As soon as she said this, everyone turned to look at her. To announce her decision at this moment, and with such a serious air, made everyone secretly wonder: Could it be that our lovely Ziying has fallen in love with our Young Master Xiao? Jenny’s heart tightened, and Xiao Yan’s heart also skipped a beat.

Zi Ying paid no attention to the gazes of the crowd, walked straight up to Xiao Yan, pursed her lips as if she had made up her mind, and said, “From now on, even if you tell us that you are not only a man, but also a woman, I won’t be surprised!”

As soon as she finished speaking, everyone was stunned for a moment, and then burst into laughter. Zhen Ji laughed so hard that she couldn’t straighten up, and tears welled up in her eyes. Xiao Yan was also amused by Zi Ying’s words.

“Zi Ying, what you said perfectly expresses what we all feel!” everyone exclaimed. Another burst of laughter followed.

“Young Master Xiao, could you tell us more about this ghost spirit?” Le Shaolong was most interested in these things; he had actually wanted to ask for a long time but hadn’t found the opportunity.

“Oh, okay.” Xiao Yan finally snapped out of his embarrassment and immediately told everyone about the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits. The Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits: Ranked seventeenth among the ghost spirits of the Dou Di Continent, it is said to have been formed from the resentment of an ancient battlefield over hundreds of millions of years.

With a single thought, it can command all the vengeful spirits in the world to submit.

“Now that you’ve obtained the Ghost Spirit, how’s your cultivation technique? Has it advanced?” Zhenni was very concerned about this; she cared more about Xiao Yan’s improvement in strength than anyone else.

“Uh, not yet, there’s still one thing missing…”

“What’s still missing?” Jenny pressed.

“Well… I’ll tell you later… Otherwise, I don’t know if Zi Ying will say I’m a woman.”

Xiao Yan also made a joke.

“Oh, Young Master Xiao, just tell us! Whatever you say, we really won’t be surprised anymore. Can’t you see how curious we all are?

Just satisfy us a little bit!” Zi Ying said, sounding slightly coquettish. Actually, not only was she curious, but everyone was looking at Xiao Yan expectantly, wanting to know how unbelievable what Xiao Yan was talking about would be. Feeling embarrassed under everyone’s gaze, Xiao Yan stammered, “I told you, please don’t look at me like I’m a monster…”

“Don’t worry, Young Master Xiao, absolutely not! How could you be like a monster? You are!”

Nan Erming, who was usually taciturn, always spoke in a way that resonated with people.

“Hahahaha!” Another burst of happy laughter erupted, and Xiao Yan couldn’t help but shake his head, chuckling to himself. Zi Ying laughed and rushed up to Nan Erming, playfully punching him with her fists: “Look at you…

Young Master Xiao, tell me quickly, what else do you need?”

“It’s…it’s a spirit seal,” Xiao Yan finally said.

“Ah! A spirit seal? You can even devour spirit seals?” The group, who had just said they wouldn’t look at Xiao Yan like a monster, were now looking at him with eyes that went far beyond what they thought of a monster; they were looking at him as if he were a monster they couldn’t even imagine!

In fact, as soon as they entered the illusion, everyone knew that Xiao Yan wanted the Spirit Seal.

However, before they knew that Xiao Yan could devour the Ghost Spirit, they never thought that Xiao Yan himself wanted to devour the Spirit Seal. They only thought that it was for alchemy. Now that they saw Xiao Yan devour the Ghost Spirit, they naturally wondered if Xiao Yan could also devour the Spirit Seal.

“Not yet,” Xiao Yan replied honestly.

“Not now? You mean…” Everyone was truly shocked.

Possessing all three bloodlines—what did that even mean?

“Three races’ bloodline, capable of devouring heavenly fire, ghost spirits, and spirit seals. My god, Young Master Xiao, you are absolutely the number one person in the entire history of the Dou Di Continent, without exception, only number one.” Le Shaolong also lost his usual composure, and the finger pointing at Xiao Yan was trembling slightly.

“Hehe, actually, the reason we came to find the Spirit Seal Moss is for the sake of the demon race’s bloodline.” Now that things had come to this point, Xiao Yan didn’t intend to hide anything anymore.

“Moreover, my cultivation technique requires one of each of the three rare items to advance, so…”

“My goodness, a cultivation technique that requires three kinds of rare items to advance? What kind of heaven-defying technique is that? It must be a Saint-level technique, right?

Oh my god, a Saint-level technique!” Nan Erming exclaimed.

“What’s all the fuss about! Isn’t it a good thing that Young Master Xiao has a Saint-level cultivation technique? Why are you making such a fuss?”

Xiao Zhan dismissively “reprimanded” Nan Erming, then muttered something that immediately drew a chorus of eye rolls. What he muttered was—“That’s terrifying, a Saint-level cultivation technique! From now on, let Young Master Xiao tank the monsters!”

Le Shaolong remained calmer.

“Young Master Xiao, you said before that your cultivation technique requires a lot of ghost spirits. Does that mean it also requires a lot of heavenly fire and spirit seals?”

“Mm.” Xiao Yan nodded.

“How much exactly do you need?” Jenny asked nervously.

“To advance to the Saint rank, seven are needed for each…” The Eight Extremes Heavenly Techniques, from the Burning Technique of the First Extreme to the Transformation Technique of the Eight Extremes, each requires seven.

“Seven of each…” Everyone gasped.

“And they all have to be in the top twenty?”

“That should be it…” Hearing Xiao Yan’s words, Xiao Zhan patted Xiao Yan’s shoulder apologetically, “Young Master Xiao, I’m sorry about earlier. I’ll take the hits from now on…

Seven of each of the top twenty rare treasures, this Saint-level cultivation technique is just too damn rip-off!” Xiao Yan’s face immediately darkened…

“Let’s go, the Spirit Seal is still waiting for us.” After resting for a while, Xiao Yan stood up and called to the others. Xiao Yan was already extremely anxious as he was about to advance from the Burning Technique to the Cold Technique.

According to the memories of the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits, the White Bone Throne led to another world, which was very likely the place where the Spirit Seal was located. With a wave of his sleeve, Xiao Yan swept away the scattered bones, slightly swirled his Dou Qi, and slammed it into the ground. Under the intense heat of the Heavenly Fire, the collision between the ground and the Dou Qi did not produce a deafening roar.

The surrounding area was reduced to ashes, and a giant hole appeared before everyone, emitting a bright light. The radiance took a long time to fade, and the cave became somewhat dim. Upon closer inspection, the light was revealed to be from a barrier resembling a membrane.

Over countless years, the barrier had lost its former strength, and the loss of energy rendered it unable to impede the group’s progress. With one step, they entered a new world. Everyone was astonished.

What they saw before them was completely different from the emptiness and desolation they had just witnessed: boundless greenery, vibrant life, and ancient trees stretching as far as the eye could see, making them feel refreshed and invigorated. The air contained a large amount of energy from heaven and earth, many times more concentrated than that of Diyuan Prefecture. Cultivating here would surely be twice as effective with half the effort.

In the dense ancient forest, one can vaguely see a huge temple, tall, magnificent, and awe-inspiring. Before the ancient temple, there are statues of various humanoid creatures, solemn and sacred. These are all ancient races that have now disappeared into the long river of history and are hard to find.

“Is this the energy of heaven and earth from ancient times? It’s so concentrated. No wonder so many talented people emerged before the cataclysm.” The group exclaimed, slowly walking towards the temple and surveying their surroundings.

Along the way, apart from the human-shaped statues by the roadside, although the greenery was endless and there was no sense of desolation or emptiness, there was no sign of life in the view. The greenery became monotonous after a while. As time goes by, past glories become historical clouds, ancient lives fade away with the years, and the dust of history is buried.

This is the most realistic portrayal of the ancient catastrophe. Upon reaching the temple, one can finally see its entirety. The temple is ancient and grand, towering over a thousand feet high.

Its walls are bluish-gray, as if carved from a single massive blue stone, forming a seamless whole without any seams, which is truly astonishing. Without excessive decoration, only the unrestrained carving outlines the shape of the temple. The power of heaven and earth contained in the carving is subtly integrated into the surrounding environment, yet distinctly present within it.

The simpler the design, the more chilling the sense of power emanating from the building becomes; the person who built this temple with bluestone back then must have been a powerful being. Amidst gasps of wonder and awe, the crowd turned their gaze to the temple gate. The gate was tightly shut, its color matching the walls, carved from bluestone.

In contrast to the overall understated elegance, the gate is inscribed with numerous runes, with a blank space in the center of each rune shrouded in thick mist, obscuring whether it is a handle or something else entirely. As the group took a few steps forward, within seven feet of the gate, countless runes began to flash. A large amount of mist surged and covered the area, making it impossible to see anything.

Only the runes shone brightly.

Suddenly, a depression appeared in the center of the runes, and several large characters faintly emerged on them: “然”, “物”, and “外”. These three characters, exuding the aura of iron horses and golden spears, began to rotate around the depression.

“What do you mean?” Zi Ying asked curiously.

“These three characters seem incomplete. We need to complete them before we can go in. Perhaps that’s what it means.”

Le Shaolong stared at the three characters for a long time and quickly figured out the reason.

“Isn’t that simple? A real man should have an open mind and disregard all external things. Where the heart goes, there the world is.”

Xiao Zhan strode forward, his fighting spirit shooting out with his finger, transforming into the character “豁” (huò), which landed in the depression. Like the heart, Xiao Zhan’s calligraphy carries a refreshing determination, and there is a world of its own between the characters. But just as the character “豁” was finished, a powerful force rebounded from the depression, sending Xiao Zhan’s massive body flying.

The change happened extremely quickly; before anyone could react, Xiao Zhan had already fallen seven feet away like a whirlwind, stirring up a cloud of dust. At the same time, a halo of light flashed, encircling the area within seven feet. Xiao Zhan planted one foot on the ground, trying to rush in again, but it was as if he had hit a transparent glass wall.

Even the full power of a six-star Dou Di’s Dou Qi could not damage him in the slightest. Xiao Zhan was outside, furious and pacing back and forth in a panic. Seeing that Xiao Zhan was unharmed, everyone breathed a sigh of relief and then turned to look at the three words that were still spinning on their own.

It seems this is a multiple-choice question, and everyone only has one chance to choose. No one dared to write again. The group argued heatedly within seven feet of each other until they believed it to be the correct answer, at which point they cautiously made their attempt.

But one by one, they were blasted out of the circle, leaving only Zhen Ni and Xiao Yan behind.

“The person who built this temple must have been a reclusive sage, concerned with the affairs of the world yet living outside the bustling city. Such a mindset is truly admirable.” Jenny explained in detail, and her analysis was very reasonable: “It should be ’detached from worldly affairs’ that best fits this person’s mentality.” Seemingly certain, Jenny’s delicate hand wrote the character “”, and then, to everyone’s expectant gaze, she was also blasted away, leaving behind a trail of regret.

Within seven feet, what is normally a step is now like an insurmountable chasm. Now that Xiao Yan was alone, everyone outside held their breath, six pairs of eyes and twelve pupils fixed on him, placing all their hopes on him. Feeling the eager gazes of everyone, Xiao Yan suddenly felt immense pressure.

This should be the location of the spirit seal, and now there is only one last chance left. Xiao Yan has never dared to confidently believe that he is better than others in analyzing things, but this arduous task has fallen on his shoulders. Xiao Yan was overwhelmed with anxiety, and before he knew it, he was drenched in sweat.

He struggled to raise his finger, then hesitated and put it down, his mind in turmoil. This might be the last chance to obtain the Spirit Seal, which was related to the first evolution of the Eight Extremes Heavenly Technique. Even someone as resolute as Xiao Yan dared not act rashly.

Time ticked by, and everyone’s eyes grew increasingly anxious. Xiao Yan’s performance clearly lacked confidence, and everyone felt their hearts were about to leap out of their chests. There were no fierce battles, no difficult illusions, only a seemingly simple yet incomprehensible test that stumped everyone.

If they had a choice, everyone would probably prefer to have another grand battle with the Roaring Black Lord, even though it was dangerous, at least it wouldn’t be so frustrating. Slowly exhaling a breath of stale air to ease the tension in his heart, Xiao Yan’s eyes brightened, and he slowly made a decision. He raised his finger again and stopped at the indentation.

Regardless, it’s time to make a decision. As for the odds of winning, Xiao Yan can’t guarantee it, but indecisiveness has never been his style. With swift, swift fingers, Xiao Yan carved the character “Xiao” into the indentation, which is both his surname and his interpretation of the characters.

In fact, after much thought, Xiao Yan and Zhen Ni shared the same view: “Xiao Ran Wu Wai” and “Ran Wu” are not that different in meaning; both describe someone who is extremely detached and not bothered by worldly affairs. Since that’s the case, why not take a gamble? Perhaps the compatibility between this character and my surname is fate.

Although he didn’t know why he had this feeling, with a heart full of trepidation, Xiao Yan still resolutely wrote down the character “Xiao”…

Chapter 72: The Spirit Seal and the Stone Tablet

The mere mention of “opportunity” from a Nine-Star Dou Di indicates that it must be extraordinary, let alone the fact that this Nine-Star Dou Di spoke of a great opportunity! Needless to say, it must be far more important than the spirit seal. Perhaps it is an item, or something else, no one knows, but everyone knows that this is definitely the most precious thing in this illusion.

“What should we do?” Storm asked, addressing everyone, but mainly seeking constructive advice from Le Shaolong.

“I don’t know either.” After a long silence, Le Shaolong finally spoke under everyone’s gaze, his tone filled with helplessness. Le Shaolong was the most astute among them, able to notice more details and make more insightful judgments than the others.

Upon hearing Le Shaolong’s words, everyone felt a chill in their hearts, but upon reflection, they all understood. How could such a great opportunity, which even a nine-star Dou Di would value so highly, be so easily obtained by others? Most importantly, judging from the comments, this opportunity depends entirely on fate, and the person who left the comments does not necessarily intend to pass it on to the world, which makes it even more difficult for others to know where to start.

Having no other choice, the group resorted to the most primitive, but perhaps most effective, method: a thorough search, paying attention to every piece of bluestone and every trace. The hall was extremely quiet, without a single sound. The group searched for several days, using every method they could think of, but still found nothing.

Apart from the traces left by the items placed there years ago and the carvings on the walls, everything seemed to have been sealed away. The crowd exchanged bewildered glances, their hearts heavy with suppressed frustration and their faces etched with disappointment. Could it be that they were truly destined to miss out on that great opportunity?

“Why don’t we destroy this place and see? The opportunity is right here in this temple. If we destroy the temple, the opportunity will appear,” Zi Ying suggested, unable to resist the temptation of such a great opportunity that even a Nine-Star Dou Di would value.

Upon hearing this, Jenny was taken aback and remained silent. Her eyes drifted towards Xiao Yan, a half-smile playing on her lips, as she watched to see what Xiao Yan would decide.

“Young Master Xiao’s decision is my decision,” Nan Erming said coldly. Under everyone’s gaze, Xiao Yan was struggling with extreme inner conflict. The wind that occasionally blew through the empty hall felt even colder, as if it were seeping into his heart.

After a moment, Xiao Yan raised his head and shook his head firmly.

“If we encounter battles and killings here, just like in the other dimensions outside, then it is perfectly justifiable for us to destroy this place. The victor is king, and the loser is a bandit.”

“However, we did not encounter any danger upon entering this place. We simply passed a very peaceful test and received the gift of the spirit seal.”

“It can be said that this was a gift from the owner of this place in good faith, without any additional conditions. Otherwise, with his power as a nine-star Dou Di, he could have easily wiped us out here.” Xiao Yan patiently explained to everyone, “We cannot accept gifts and destroy the place where people live.” Zi Ying looked at Xiao Yan’s somewhat stern and reproachful gaze, stuck out her little tongue, blushed slightly, and lowered her head in shame. Xiao Yan did not continue to blame Zi Ying.

They were teammates and knew each other’s personalities. Zi Ying was just acting on impulse and did not mean to do so.

“Everyone, follow me out of the temple, bow three times to express our gratitude for the kindness we have received, and then we will leave.” Xiao Yan’s words were resounding and full of passionate enthusiasm. As the group walked out of the temple gate, gazing at the ancient ruins, the words of the Nine-Star Dou Emperor seemed to still echo in their ears.

Once a powerful force, now desolate, the weight of history commands silent reverence from all who behold it. After performing the three bows, the group turned around and prepared to leave.

“It’s a pity. After we leave, this place will still self-destruct from the heavens and earth, and the ancient world will lose some of its memories.” Xiao Zhan sighed, following Xiao Yan, and was about to leave.

As soon as Xiao Zhan finished speaking, the temple gates suddenly closed by themselves with a loud bang. Seven halos descended from the sky, enveloping everyone. The ground in front of the temple seemed to have been torn open by a giant hand, creating a huge crack, from which the sound of rushing water came from below.

“It is truly rare to be able to remain true to oneself in the face of immense temptation. Upholding one’s true self is the first step for all those who have great opportunities, and you have passed the first test. In that case, I will open the door to opportunity and bring you closer to it.”

The unexpected words delighted everyone, calming their wary hearts that had been bewildered by the halo. Zi Ying, on the other hand, turned her head away in shame, too embarrassed to meet everyone’s gaze.

“It seems that following in Young Master Xiao’s footsteps was definitely the right thing to do.” Storm looked back at Xiao Yan, making no attempt to hide his admiration for him.

“Just lucky,” Xiao Yan said, feeling embarrassed and slightly shy.

“Let’s go down and see what the opportunity next will bring. Aren’t you all looking forward to seeing what it will be?” Jenny was very satisfied with the personal charm that Xiao Yan had shown during this period.

She was glad that she had not chosen the wrong person. With a slight sigh, she was reluctant to see Xiao Yan fall into an embarrassing situation, so she changed the subject in time to divert everyone’s attention. Beneath the crack, a deep, straight hole leads to the bottom, and the sound of water is coming from there; it must be an underground river.

Looking down, everyone felt the dark river was extremely cold, and the wind blowing out felt like a dull knife scraping flesh, causing a dull ache to run down their skin.

“Will there be any danger down there?” Le Shaolong asked, his mind attentive and somewhat worried.

“With the strength of a nine-star Dou Di, they wouldn’t be deliberately mysterious, so the test shouldn’t be life-threatening. At most, it will only discourage us and cause us to miss the opportunity,” Zhen Ni analyzed, encouraging everyone’s confidence.

“Even if there is danger, when have we ever backed down?” Xiao Yan’s eyes sparkled with courage. One by one, the group jumped in and moved forward in the underground river.

The river water was icy cold, and although everyone had their fighting spirit protecting them, they still felt the chill seep into their bones. After traveling for about ten miles, the cold intensified, penetrating their bodies and reaching their hearts. Everyone had to exert all their strength to barely resist it.

Looking into the distance, the underground river continued its course straight ahead, stretching for hundreds of miles. The group continued on for nearly a hundred miles, the chill penetrating to their very bones, their breath freezing into ice crystals, yet the river showed no sign of freezing, remaining as black as ink. Looking into the distance through their blurred vision, the group finally stopped, sensing a great danger.

Ahead lay a massive, funnel-shaped black hole, into which the underground river flowed, seemingly never to be filled. The confluence of the waters created enormous waves, and the misty vapor seemed capable of swallowing everyone’s minds, sending chills down one’s spine. The group was reluctant to venture into the massive underground cavern, but upon looking around, they found that all paths had been blocked.

The only sound in the empty underground river cavern was the thunderous roar of rushing water. They stopped in their tracks, unsure of what to do.

“What should we do?” Xiao Zhan exhaled and asked the others.

“There is no other way out here except for the funnel.” Zi had the best eyesight and could see the clearest. Her thin body trembled in the cold river water, and she spoke with a shiver.

Upon stopping, the group found the river water even colder, and their fighting spirit seemed to be unable to withstand it. They truly didn’t know what kind of water this was, that it could freeze without solidifying, its mysteries encompassing the changes of heaven and earth.

“The power of the whirlpool is great, but I believe we can withstand it.” Le Shaolong carefully observed the power of the water flow in the funnel, paused, and continued, “What I’m worried about is that we don’t know what unknown place the funnel leads to, and the chilling cold will be much stronger after the water flows swirl and collide with each other.” Upon hearing this, everyone fell silent.

Le Shaolong’s implication was clear: the current chill was already difficult to resist, and once they went down, it might freeze them instantly, turning them into ice blocks and causing them to fall into the bottomless abyss. Death itself is not frightening; what is frightening is being turned into an ice sculpture and falling into an unknown place, with no certainty of life or death, spending a lifetime alone in darkness. That is the most terrifying thing A chill ran down everyone’s spine.

Zi Ying muttered under her breath, “Who said this trial wouldn’t be life-threatening…?” Jenny didn’t refute Zi Ying’s words, staring blankly ahead, then sighed. This situation was completely unexpected, and she couldn’t think of any good solutions.

“Go down and try it out. Those who can’t withstand the cold can stay up here,” Xiao Yan said. He didn’t want to let go of the great opportunity mentioned by the Nine-Star Dou Emperor.

Since ancient times, obtaining an opportunity has always been fraught with danger. He planned to personally delve into it to find out the truth and would never give up until the very last step.

“Young Master Xiao, you stay up here, we’ll go down.” Xiao Zhan was worried about Xiao Yan and didn’t want him to take any risks. As for that unknown opportunity, if it was an item, they would give it to Xiao Yan when they came up, so everyone stopped Xiao Yan from going down.

“So-called opportunities are only for certain people, not something anyone can obtain at will. Those who can go down should go down and try their luck.” Xiao Yan knew what everyone was thinking, but he still insisted on going down himself.

“But this chill…” Everyone knew Xiao Yan’s personality; once he made a decision, he wouldn’t easily change it. They didn’t say anything more, but were worried about Xiao Yan’s strength.

“Have you all forgotten that I possess a ghost spirit? Ghost spirits are the coldest and most malevolent things in the world,” Xiao Yan reassured everyone with a smile. And indeed, it was true; among the group, Xiao Yan seemed to feel little discomfort from the river’s chill.

“I’ll go down with Young Master Xiao,” Zhen Ni said. Her support warmed Xiao Yan’s heart.

“How about this, I’ll go down with Xiao Zhan, Le Shaolong, and Nan Erming, while Zhen Ni, Zi Ying, and Feng Bao stay behind,” Xiao Yan said slowly after thinking it over. Among the group, Zi Ying and Feng Bao were physically weaker and could barely withstand the chill of the river water. With him and Le Shaolong absent, Zhen Ni was the best leader who could calmly handle any unexpected events.

Jenny, being exceptionally intelligent, immediately understood Xiao Yan’s meaning. She sighed softly and did not insist any further. Zi Ying and Feng Bao, biting their lips which were purple from the cold, knew that going down would only be a burden, and nodded sadly.

“You must come back alive. I’ll be waiting for you. If anything happens to you, I won’t live on alone.”

Just as Xiao Yan was warming up, Zhen Ni’s voice transmission gently drifted into Xiao Yan’s ears. Xiao Yan was taken aback, his heart warmed, and he turned to look at Zhen Ni, his eyes filled with tenderness and emotion, and nodded firmly. Then, Xiao Yan and the others held hands and jumped into the funnel.

As soon as they leaped above the vortex, the immense suction from the funnel pulled them down, and the billowing mist instantly swallowed them. The roar of the water was deafening, and the funnel-shaped underground river was pitch black and extremely eerie. Xiao Yan and his three companions held hands, their bodies spinning rapidly in the rushing river.

The centrifugal force of the water was more terrifying than the invisible wind, and the actual pulling force was even more terrifying than the tornado. The four of them were like leaves being swept down a waterfall, unable to maintain their balance and simply falling downwards with the current. The water tore at their muscles and pressed against their chests, making it extremely difficult to breathe.

The four men’s tightly clenched fingers bulged with veins and their knuckles turned white from the force, almost giving way beneath their grip. The biting cold current covered everyone with layers of thick ice, only to have it washed away by the rushing water, only to be covered and washed away again in a never-ending cycle. Each time the thick ice fell, it tore at their skin, as if trying to rip it off alive.

The pain was excruciating, the cold bone-chilling, but everyone was already frozen stiff, barely able to muster their fighting spirit to resist. The descent accelerated rapidly, the water currents squeezed and surged against each other, the density increased, and the chill condensed together, causing the fighting spirit within the four people to become sluggish and its flow to become increasingly obstructed, and it was clear that they would soon be unable to resist.

Xiao Yan quickly circulated his ghostly power, and a chilling energy flowed from his dantian, covering his entire body. It then traveled through their clasped hands to everyone, and their bodies warmed up slightly, regaining some sensation. With the help of the ghostly power, the first thing everyone did after their chill subsided was to hold each other’s hands tightly and form a circle.

The group continued to spin and fall with the direction of the water flow, quickly moving away from the centrifugal force at the center of the whirlpool. Xiao Yan possessed two ghost spirits, so the chill had the least effect on him. He kept secretly measuring the distance in his mind and estimated that everyone had fallen at least three thousand meters, yet they still hadn’t reached the end.

The destination of the underground river seemed to have no end. The deeper they went, the colder it became. But without the swirling current to hold them back, Xiao Yan and his companions gradually regained their footing and looked around at their surroundings.

But apart from the sound of rushing water, there was nothing around them. The group continued to fall. Time is meaningless in the dark river.

After an unknown amount of time, suddenly, a faint light appeared in the distance, and the speed at which everyone fell gradually slowed down. The light grew stronger and stronger, and the speed at which everyone fell slowed down. Finally, a bright light appeared before their eyes, and their feet touched a soft object.

Before they could react, they fell to the ground. Feeling the solid ground beneath their feet, the group stood up. The water had stopped flowing, and the air was very dry.

Looking up, they saw the underground river stopped above a layer of light, which was quite magical. The group looked around, but it was still pitch black and desolate, without a sound.

“Finally we’ve arrived. This is the first time I’ve felt so uncomfortable being suspended in mid-air.” Xiao Zhan said while stretching his muscles.

“This time, we owe a great debt to Young Master Xiao’s ghostly power; otherwise, we might have ended up with just a few ice sculptures,” Le Shaolong joked, and Nan Erming also showed a rare smile.

“It’s good that everyone is safe. Let’s keep going.” Xiao Yan said to the three of them after standing and catching his breath for a moment. After traveling for more than ten miles, the soil suddenly became soft, and the soles of their feet could sink completely into it.

The group didn’t pay much attention to it, but after walking several hundred feet, they felt increasingly cold and biting, as if a north wind was blowing snow all over them.

“Where exactly is this place? It gets colder the further we go,” Xiao Zhan muttered to himself. Xiao Yan remained silent, cautiously observing his surroundings.

After walking several hundred meters further, the group saw a ground covered with dry bones, densely packed everywhere, already rotten, turning to dust with the slightest step. Under the power of time, nothing lasts forever, but this no longer seems like the place built by the temple’s owner, but more like another ancient ruin. Could it be that this illusion is connected to another place?

Upon arriving at this place, the area ahead became vast and empty, like an ice cellar. The biting cold wind felt like knives scraping against the skin, intensifying the unease in everyone’s hearts. Standing still, Xiao Yan carefully observed his surroundings.

This is a valley, and in the center of the valley stands a stone tablet nearly ten meters high. The tablet is very dilapidated, covered with the marks of time, and an ancient aura flows over it. Because it has never seen sunlight for many years, the valley is filled with howling winds and shadowy figures.

Among the piles of withered bones everywhere, there are hazy ghostly figures drifting unconsciously. They are very faint, but no ghostly figures dare to approach the stone tablet, as if the stone tablet has some kind of power to ward off evil.

“It seems another fierce battle is unavoidable,” Le Shaolong sighed, drawing his twin daggers. It seemed the shadow of the Ancestor of Wraiths had not yet faded from his heart “Don’t be nervous. These are just unclaimed vengeful spirits, they haven’t developed consciousness yet, and they won’t attack people.”

Xiao Yan spoke up to stop Le Shaolong’s actions. With the help of the Ancestor of Vengeful Spirits, Xiao Yan’s understanding of vengeful spirits was much deeper than before. There was one thing Xiao Yan couldn’t understand: the vengeful spirits in the ancient ruins had not yet developed their own consciousness after countless years.

Could it be because of the stone tablet in front of him? As Xiao Yan was speculating, a line of words floated up from the stone tablet, imprinted in the void. Judging from the handwriting, it was clearly from the same person as the words outside the temple.

“I had a chance here back then, and now I am about to leave. Perhaps I will never return. So I return these things to their original place and leave them to those who are destined to receive them.”

“If you can see my message, you must have passed the first two tests: the first test of ’gratitude’ and the second test of ’courage.’ Congratulations on having the foundation of a strong heart. You may go even further in the future.”

“Next comes the third and final test. I hope you will all be the ones truly destined to receive this great opportunity.” Jenny was right.

The temple master had no intention of harming anyone; he was merely testing the courage of those destined to be there. After reading this passage, Xiao Yan and the other three felt that they were so close to an opportunity, even within easy reach, that their breathing became rapid and they could no longer maintain their composure. The first two trials were passed unintentionally by Xiao Yan and his companions.

The first trial tested character, which anyone with integrity could easily overcome; the second tested courage, which was also not difficult for them, given their experience in countless battles. It seemed the third trial wouldn’t be too difficult either, and this opportunity appeared to be within their grasp, almost within reach. But when the temple master finished speaking, everyone was dumbfounded.

“Since we are destined to be together, everything depends on fate. Therefore, the third test is about the word ’fate’.”

“Damn it.” Xiao Zhan couldn’t help but curse. What kind of test was this?

In the end, it was just pure luck. If that was the case, weren’t the first two trials just like trying to catch the moon from the riverbank—a complete waste of effort? Seeing Xiao Zhan’s inner turmoil, Le Shaolong spoke up: “It seems that this senior values fate very much.

But when fate brings you together, you must also see through his character and nature. Otherwise, if this opportunity falls into the hands of a villain, it will inevitably cause a bloodbath on the Dou Di Continent. Senior’s thoughts and concerns are not without reason.”

Le Shaolong’s analysis was reasonable. Xiao Zhan opened his mouth but couldn’t refute it, so he turned his head away and sulked. Xiao Yan smiled, knowing that Xiao Zhan was a straightforward person and was just feeling down for a moment, not that he couldn’t understand.

“Let’s take a look at the test first,” Xiao Yan cleverly steered the conversation back on track, giving Xiao Zhan a way out. The words, branded into the void, were bold and powerful, and the last line fell in the void: “Cut your finger and drip blood into the stone tablet. The one whose blood is fused must use all their strength to pull out this tablet and will obtain this opportunity.”

The words vanished into the world, but everyone’s hearts tightened instantly. Their hearts pounded like drums in their chests, as if they were about to burst out of their chests. The excitement in their eyes could no longer be suppressed, and they all stretched out their fingers, eager to try.

“The reason is simple: luck is very important.” Nan Erming, who had been silent until now, suddenly blurted out, summarizing everyone’s feelings at that moment. Everyone was taken aback for a moment, then they all laughed, and their tense mood eased.

“I’ll try first.” Xiao Zhan, who was seething with anger, strode forward, put his finger in his mouth and bit down hard, blood flowing onto the stone tablet. The stone tablet was weathered like sandstone, with an uneven surface.

It seemed impossible that blood wouldn’t see through it. Xiao Zhan waited with full confidence. Blood flowed on the stone tablet, slowly sliding down the tablet and onto the ground, seeping into the soil, yet there wasn’t a single trace of blood on the tablet itself.

Everyone stared at this scene in utter astonishment, finding it quite unbelievable. Xiao Zhan’s eyes widened even more, unwilling to believe the reality before him. He reached out and touched the monument, which was still incredibly rough, and the sweat on his hands seeped into it without any resistance.

Scratching his head, Xiao Zhan refused to believe it and dripped the still-wet blood from his fingers onto the wound again. Xiao Zhan was still a little worried, so he deliberately chose a dented spot and smeared the blood from his finger directly on it, muttering to himself, “I don’t believe it will fall off after I put it on.” Everyone was speechless at Xiao Zhan’s actions, but they had to admit that it was indeed a clever idea.

The pitted stone tablet seemed impossible to allow blood to flow down it. Just when everyone was wondering if the temple master’s test had overlooked the existence of such a shameless fellow as Xiao Zhan, the truth once again overturned everyone’s thoughts. The stone tablet seemed to have a natural repulsion to blood, and the blood that Xiao Zhan smeared on it slowly floated off the stone surface and fell to the ground again.

Xiao Zhan was completely dumbfounded this time. He stood there dumbfounded, unable to come to his senses for a long time.

“I’m telling you, Xiao Zhan, your current misery was destined from the moment you swore.” Nan Erming’s handsome face didn’t show much expression, but his words didn’t miss the chance to strike back at Xiao Zhan.

“I refuse to believe it!” Xiao Zhan turned his face away, too lazy to pay attention to Nan Erming, rolled up his sleeves, and actually tried to pull out the stone tablet directly. He focused his energy in his dantian, unleashed a battle aura that enveloped his body, assumed a steady horse stance, wrapped his arms around the stone tablet, and forcefully pulled it upwards. Xiao Zhan’s strength was undoubtedly the greatest among the crowd, reaching a thousand pounds.

The stone tablet looked to be only a few hundred pounds, so Xiao Zhan felt it should be no problem. But things that seem simple in the world are often not so simple. Xiao Zhan used all his strength, but the stone tablet remained unmoved, as if it were rooted in the ground and connected with the earth.

Xiao Zhan’s face flushed red, his unwillingness turning into even greater power. He gripped the bottom of the stone tablet with both hands, and the golden Howling Tiger appeared behind him. Its massive body contracted rapidly, and veins bulged on its face.

It unleashed the full power of a six-star Dou Di, and its legs shattered the bluestone on the ground into fragments. The stone tablet initially showed no reaction to Xiao Zhan’s antics, but when Xiao Zhan unleashed his power as a six-star Dou Di, a white light suddenly shone on the tablet. A layer of extremely strong yet gentle power overflowed from the tablet’s pits and craters, seemingly slow but actually very fast, instantly enveloping Xiao Zhan.

Xiao Zhan’s power as a six-star initial stage was as powerless as a fist hitting cotton in the face of this power, and his entire body was thrown far away, crashing onto a pile of bones in the distance, scattering a cloud of ashes. Xiao Zhan scrambled to his feet, brushed off the dust, and stood dejectedly to the side, no longer daring to underestimate the stone tablet in front of him.

“Please accept my condolences.” Nan Erming walked over and patted Xiao Zhan on the shoulder to show his sympathy.

“Let me try too.” Le Shaolong stepped forward, dripped his blood into the stone tablet, and amidst the expectant gazes of the crowd, the blood slowly seeped into the stone tablet and disappeared.

“It’s in! It’s finally in!” Le Shaolong’s body stiffened with excitement.

After a moment, he came to his senses and jumped up excitedly, waving his hands in the air. The opportunity to become a Nine-Star Dou Di is about to fall into your lap. It would be worth it even if you went crazy, let alone jumped up and waved your hands.

Everyone watched Le Shaolong jumping and dancing like a child, their faces filled with happy smiles.

“Quickly pull up the stone tablet and see what kind of opportunity it is.” Xiao Zhan urged Le Shaolong to act quickly, somewhat impatiently. Composing himself, Le Shaolong stood before the stone tablet, took a deep breath, unleashed the full power of a five-star Dou Di, inserted his hands into the gap between the stone tablet and the ground, and pulled hard. The wind stopped, and the swirling skeletons seemed to freeze in mid-air.

Everyone held their breath, and four pairs of eyes stared nervously at the stone tablet, waiting for the moment it would be unearthed. The fighting spirit surged forth, continuously pouring into Le Shaolong’s arms. The wind began to move, and the swirling bones crashed into the stone tablet, shattering into pieces and falling down.

The people, who had been holding their breath for too long, could no longer help but let out a long breath. But the stone tablet remained unmoved. Amidst the astonished gazes of the crowd and the flushed face of Le Shaolong from straining, it remained in place, not even an inch pulled out, as stubbornly as if it should have been here thousands of years ago and should still be here thousands of years from now.

What’s going on? Le Shaolong couldn’t believe his eyes. He strained his arms again, and the wind blew even harder, causing more ashes to float away, but the stone tablet still didn’t react at all.

“Isn’t blood recognition enough?” Le Shaolong couldn’t accept this fact. He was almost going crazy.

He exerted force with both hands, pouring wave after wave of fighting spirit into his arms. The cruel reality is always hard to accept. Le Shaolong sat down on the ground in frustration, clenching his fists and helplessly pounding the ground, unable to understand what the reason was.

“Let me give it a try,” Nan Erming said. Since he couldn’t figure out why, why not give it another try? An unexpected thing happened: Nan Erming’s blood seeped into the stone tablet and slowly disappeared!

This scene stunned everyone. Le Shaolong stood up abruptly, staring intently at the dent where the blood had disappeared, utterly shocked. Then, Nan Erming also became nervous, and with a heart full of trepidation, he tried his best to pull out the stone tablet, but the result was the same as Le Shaolong’s.

Now, everyone was stunned.

“Could it be that the stone tablet has deteriorated due to its age?” Xiao Yan stepped forward, pointed his finger like a knife, made a cut, and dripped blood onto it. The crowd nervously surrounded the stone tablet, staring at the pool of bright red blood.

With mixed feelings, they watched the blood disappear on the stone tablet, at a loss for words.

“Time is merciless, and ancient artifacts are bound to suffer accidents. It seems that the stone tablet was damaged,” Xiao Zhan concluded.

“This conclusion is a bit hasty.” Le Shaolong, who had returned to normal, had the strongest analytical ability among the four.

“The blood of me, Nan Erming, and Xiao Shao can all penetrate it. Nan Erming and I tried, and although we couldn’t pull it out, at least there was no backlash. This at least proves one thing: the stone tablet does not reject any of us.”

“But you, your blood cannot penetrate it, and when you try to move the stone tablet, you are thrown back by the force of the impact. This proves another point: the stone tablet is repelling you.”

“Xiao Shao and I are human, Nan Erming is a demon, and you are a monster. Humans are balanced, demons value soul power, and monsters value physical strength. From this, it seems that the stone tablet resists brute force and tends to accept soul power, so the three of us can do it but you cannot.”

“In other words, the temple’s owner must be a human or a demon, and may even have some prejudice against the demon race.” Listening to Le Shaolong’s analysis, Xiao Yan and the other two felt it made perfect sense; it seemed that was the truth. Everyone glanced speechlessly at Xiao Zhan, who turned his head away, looking very aggrieved, not wanting to look at the heartbreaking stone tablet anymore.

“How can I bear this! Out of the four of us, I’m the only one who’s been excluded. I’m heartbroken.”

Xiao Zhan was extremely frustrated
