Chapter 73: A Frustrating Opportunity

“There’s something I don’t understand. Since the instruction is that blood fusion is the condition for activation, and both humans and demons can activate it, what’s the point of talking about a great opportunity?” Xiao Yan asked, puzzled, which was also the source of everyone’s confusion.

“It seems we’ve all been fooled by that senior. Blood fusion is just the foundation; pulling it out is the key.” Le Shaolong looked at the stone tablet with great helplessness and resentment, his expression revealing a mixture of laughter and tears. Le Shaolong’s words completely cleared up everyone’s confusion.

Recalling everything that had happened before, everyone fell silent in unison, with a hint of self-mockery and a cold smile on their lips.

“Only Young Master Xiao is left.” Le Shaolong looked up at Xiao Yan, his eyes filled with expectation, but even more so with worry. If Xiao Yan also failed to pull out the stone tablet, it would be fine if he made a wasted trip, but to miss out on the great opportunity mentioned by the Nine-Star Dou Di would be very unacceptable.

“Give it a try.” Xiao Yan sensed the expectation in Le Shaolong’s eyes, and slowly walked to the stone tablet, gazing at it intently. The stone tablet was still as dilapidated as ever, but Xiao Yan dared not underestimate it.

How could he, a mere three-star Dou Di, easily judge something based on its appearance when it could leave three high-level Dou Di helpless? Xiao Yan steadied himself in a horse stance and silently reached out to support the stone tablet with both hands. The stone tablet was cold to the touch, and the weight in his hands made Xiao Yan feel considerable pressure.

Xiao Yan calmly tried using his Dou Qi, but the stone tablet did not react. His expression remained normal, without the slightest fluctuation. In terms of fighting spirit, Xiao Yan was far inferior to Xiao Zhan, Le Shaolong, and Nan Erming, so it was expected that the stone tablet would not move.

Xiao Yan slowly poured his soul power into the fighting spirit in his hands. With a shout of “Rise!”, his fighting spirit surged like waves, sinking down and then suddenly rising again. The hearts of Le Shaolong and his two companions immediately jumped into their throats, their tense eyes scanning the stone tablet in unison.

As expected, Xiao Yan’s fate was the same as everyone else’s; the stone tablet remained completely still.

“Could it be that this stele is not meant for us?” Xiao Zhan was somewhat disappointed. He leaned on the mountain wall in the valley, his mood plummeting.

Xiao Yan stood there, lost in thought— “So-called fate refers to an opportunity, a specific condition, perhaps due to differences in oneself, or perhaps due to favorable timing and location.”

“If favorable timing and location are no longer suitable in a new environment, then the only solution is to start with oneself. Strength and bloodline have been excluded; what makes one different?” Xiao Yan pondered deeply, his solitary figure appearing somewhat forlorn against the backdrop of the stone tablet.

“Heavenly Fire and Ghost Spirit!” Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up, as if he had caught something.

“Yes, that’s right. In the entire Dou Di Continent, I’m probably the only one who can possess both of these things at the same time!” Xiao Yan stepped forward, placed his hands on the stone tablet again, and slowly released the Heavenly Fire in his left hand and the Ghost Spirit Power in his right.

Xiao Yan’s actions were observed by everyone, and three pairs of fiery and expectant eyes were firmly fixed on him. The blazing heavenly fire and the chilling ghostly power reached the stone tablet almost simultaneously, and after a moment, the long-silent stone tablet lit up.

“There’s hope!” Xiao Yan was overjoyed, and a smile appeared on his face.

However, the path to enlightenment always seems to be winding. The light emanating from the stone tablet flickered only briefly before quickly fading away, and Xiao Yan’s smile froze on his face.

“That’s impossible! There was definitely a reaction just now.” Everyone found it unbelievable.

“Perhaps the order is wrong.” Xiao Yan pondered for a moment, then placed his hand on the stone tablet again. This time, Xiao Yan was much more cautious, only slowly releasing his ghostly power to test the stone tablet’s reaction.

A chilling power spread upwards, covering the entire stele, yet the stele remained even more silent.

“It all depends on the Heavenly Fire.” Xiao Yan’s heart tightened, and he frowned as he stared at his hands pressed against the stone tablet, his face showing an extremely solemn expression. The bluish-gray celestial fire touched the stele, and the stele suddenly lit up with a dazzling light. Eight runes appeared on the stele, guiding the celestial fire to form the character “fire”.

As soon as the character “fire” appeared, the stones on the surface of the stele cracked and the stele shook violently, as if it wanted to break through the soil. Xiao Yan was overjoyed. With a little effort, he pulled out the stone tablet, revealing a dark cave.

As soon as the cave opened, a very soft light flew out and then hovered in the air, swaying uncertainly. Upon closer inspection, everyone realized it was a jade box. The jade box was fiery red, crystal clear, with a misty aura flowing within it, obscuring the view and preventing any discernible contents.

Xiao Yan grasped the jade box in his hand. It was heavy, carved from a single piece of jade, perfectly formed and without a single flaw. The box itself is priceless; what is contained within such a priceless treasure must be of immeasurable value, Xiao Yan thought to himself.

Just then, a message entered Xiao Yan’s mind from the jade box: “I obtained three Heavenly Flames back then, but I was troubled by the inability to devour and fuse them together.”

“I have spent thousands of years researching a technique that can fuse several kinds of heavenly fire, but I have no solution. Until the great calamity was about to come, I could only keep two kinds of heavenly fire coexisting in my body, but they still could not devour and fuse with each other. This is the greatest regret of my life.”

“Therefore, I leave behind a great opportunity to those who are destined to receive it, in the hope that future generations can fulfill my greatest wish in this life.”

“I know this requirement is extremely difficult, so I dare not hope for too much. Anyone who, like me, possesses both heavenly fire powers within their body is a destined person and can obtain my relics…” Sensing the desolate mood of the temple master, Xiao Yan could fully understand.

In the tens of thousands of years of the Dou Di Continent, a technique that could fuse with Heavenly Flame had never appeared. Countless geniuses had exhausted their efforts but still could not solve this problem. Only Xiao Yan was lucky enough to obtain the Burning Flame Technique, which could be considered as fulfilling a great wish of the temple master.

“No wonder it’s called a great opportunity; this is an extremely rare opportunity, one in ten thousand!” Xiao Yan couldn’t help but exclaim, while also secretly rejoicing at his good fortune.

“Congratulations to Young Master Xiao on finding a good match.” Nan Erming walked over slowly, a refined smile on his handsome face.

“Congratulations, Young Master Xiao, on your promising future! Throughout history, every genius has been accompanied by extraordinary luck.” Le Shaolong clasped his hands in a fist salute, his words warm and comforting.

“Congratulations! Young Master Xiao, what is this great opportunity inside? Everyone is very curious.

Let’s open it and take a look.” Xiao Zhan strode over, his previous frustration gone, his eyes fixed on the box in Xiao Yan’s hand, the fervor in his eyes undisguised.

“Look how impatient you are.” Xiao Yan couldn’t help but laugh as he looked at the impatient Xiao Zhan, and held the jade box up high so that everyone could see it clearly. The three licked their lips and quickly surrounded the area, eager to see what kind of great opportunity a nine-star Dou Di was talking about. Their hearts pounded faster and faster, the “thump-thump” sound clearly audible in the silent valley.

Xiao Yan instinctively tried to lift the lid of the box, but found that the jade box had no gaps at all, and he had no idea where to start. He was stunned and stood there dumbfounded. The three people surrounding them quickly realized the problem and were all dumbfounded.

The current situation was incomprehensible to them. What was going on? The jade box they had gone through so much trouble to get couldn’t be opened?

Wasn’t this just playing with them?

“I think this Nine-Star Dou Di must be named Hu You.” Xiao Zhan said indignantly, his previous resentment still lingering.

“With your attitude, it’s no wonder you’re not favored by the stone tablet.” Nan Erming glared at Xiao Zhan.

“You… if you’re so capable, then find out how to open it!” Seeing that Nan Erming was deliberately bringing up the wrong thing, Xiao Zhan glared angrily, looking very unconvinced.

“Young Master Xiao, there seem to be words on the bottom of the jade box.” Although Nan Erming was taciturn, he was extremely meticulous. Taking advantage of the dim light in the valley, he used his actions to give Xiao Zhan a hard time.

Xiao Zhan was so angry that his nose was crooked. Le Shaolong patted Xiao Zhan’s shoulder comfortingly, showing his helplessness. Ignoring their bickering, Xiao Yan turned the jade box over and examined it carefully.

Near a mark on the bottom of the box, three lines of tiny characters were engraved: “Anyone who can have two kinds of Heavenly Flames coexisting in their body is an exceptionally talented individual. If there are no unforeseen circumstances, they must already be at least a Seven-Star Dou Di level.”

“Therefore, I casually set a six-star peak mark on this box. It was just an itchy hand, a bored work after thousands of years of seclusion. I hope you will accept it with a smile.”

“With the strength of a Seven-Star Dou Di, the destined person only needs to inject the power of the Heavenly Fire that has fused two kinds of Heavenly Fire to open this box.” After reading the three lines of small print, Xiao Yan stood speechless, and everyone else was stunned and speechless. The atmosphere was very oppressive, and the silence was so profound that even the wind seemed to breathe heavily.

The temple master’s simple message stirred up a complex mix of emotions in everyone, leaving them speechless with a feeling akin to a tangled web of willow catkins, impossible to unravel, a bittersweet mix of feelings washing over them. From the moment they stepped into the temple, they were constantly surprised and disappointed. Just when they finally succeeded, they thought that the light of good fortune had finally enveloped them, but they were still tricked by the temple master.

Moreover, this move was truly insidious against Xiao Yan! The strength requirement of a six-star peak is simply unattainable for Xiao Yan, who is currently only a three-star peak! Holding the jade box, Xiao Yan had an urge to smash it.

Although this was a highly valued relic of a nine-star Dou Di, and a treasure coveted by everyone on the entire Dou Di continent, damn it, he had to wait until he reached the peak of six stars to open it. When would that be? Xiao Yan felt like crying.

Tell me, you’re a senior who’s lived for countless years, why would you be so bored as to casually put a seal on a jade box? Even if you’re extremely bored, you could at least grow some flowers and tend to some plants, why did you have to mess with the box you left for me? Do you think a six-star peak Dou Di is as common as radishes and cabbages, something you can just grab any old handful of?

The thought that he was only a three-star peak Dou Di made Xiao Yan want to die at this moment. They say curiosity killed the cat, but little do they know that curiosity can also kill a person? Xiao Yan’s frustration surged to his head.

He plopped down on the ground, looking utterly desolate. He grabbed a handful of wild grass, stuffed it into his mouth, and chewed it frantically, letting the faint bitterness spread through his mouth.

“This is outrageous!” Xiao Zhan couldn’t accept this situation for Xiao Yan either “This is a serious mess.” Nan Erming gave a self-deprecating bitter laugh, picked up the box with a somewhat dejected expression, and examined it from all angles, but he had no clue what to do.

He wanted to pry it open by force, but that was obviously not a wise approach. How could you open the relics of a nine-star Dou Di just because you want to? Why bother setting up so many tests?

After studying for a long time without any results, Nan Erming gave an awkward bitter laugh, stood up, walked straight to Xiao Yan’s side, sat down, and kept Xiao Yan company as he watched his lonely figure. Le Shaolong sighed inwardly, hesitated for a moment, and then walked up to sit down next to the two of them.

“Take it easy, Young Master Xiao! With your demonic bloodline, you can devour demonic cores, and reaching the peak of the sixth star will be much faster than ordinary people.” After sitting quietly for a long time, Le Shaolong saw that Xiao Yan’s mood had calmed down a bit, and comforted him, “Moreover, I think the seal set up by the temple master is not just for fun; perhaps it is a prerequisite for controlling the contents of the jade box.

After thinking it over, I always feel that a nine-star Dou Di would not easily joke about a relic that he cares about so much.”

“But it clearly says on the box…” Xiao Yan’s heart skipped a beat. Le Shaolong’s analysis made sense, but then he realized that the words on the box were very clear, and Xiao Yan was a little confused.

“The long years and endless loneliness may make people find amusement in their words, but they will never affect the mind of a nine-star Dou Di. I always believe that although the comments are a bit humorous, they are never unfounded.” Le Shaolong’s expression was gentle, but his words were firm, and his eyes, fixed on Xiao Yan, flashed with confidence.

“If this is considered humor, then it’s no less humorous than the scariest ghost story in the world.” Xiao Yan’s heart stirred slightly. He raised his somewhat pale hand, his eyes peering through his fingers at something, but it was clear that Le Shaolong’s words had greatly affected him.

After a while, Xiao Yan sighed softly, stood up, his eyes somewhat unfocused, but more so resolute. Xiao Yan trusted Le Shaolong’s judgment; there was no other reason than pure intuition. Besides, even if his intuition was wrong, so what?

He still had to reach the peak of the six-star level to unlock this secret. Since that was the case, why bother worrying about it? Having come to terms with it, Xiao Yan patted everyone on the shoulder, put away the jade box, and prepared to leave.

Many years later, Xiao Yan opened the jade box and, recalling Le Shaolong’s judgment today, couldn’t help but admire him! Moreover, every time he thought of Xiao Zhan’s words, “I think this nine-star Dou Di must be named Hu You,” he would smile wryly, feeling a deep sense of awe. Of course, that’s a story for later.

“Young Master Xiao, take this stone tablet with you. It’s not as simple as it seems on the surface,” Le Shaolong hurriedly reminded Xiao Yan as he prepared to leave.

“Hmm?” Upon hearing this, Xiao Yan turned around and looked at the stone tablet lying among the ashes on the ground. After being pulled out, the stone tablet appeared even more dilapidated, with countless cracks appearing on its pitted surface.

“There seems to be more to this stone tablet than meets the eye. I tried to break it before, and even with all my strength, I couldn’t open it after removing the seal. This stone tablet is extraordinary,” Le Shaolong explained.

“Perhaps, like the jade box, it can only be broken with the power of Heavenly Fire after reaching the peak of the sixth star.” Even a full-force attack from a five-star Dou Di couldn’t damage it in the slightest? Xiao Yan was astonished.

He bent down and gently stroked the monument. The monument appears to be no different from ordinary stone, but when you tap it lightly with your fingers, it produces a metallic sound, and a faint light shines through the cracks. Without attempting to break the stone tablet again, Xiao Yan dusted himself off, looked up at Le Shaolong with a half-smile, his eyes like a mother-in-law looking at her son-in-law, growing more and more fond of him, which gave Le Shaolong goosebumps.

“What’s wrong with me?” Le Shaolong asked, puzzled, as Xiao Yan stared at him.

“Could it be that Young Master Xiao has some kind of problem with his sexual orientation?” Xiao Zhan wondered to himself, but didn’t dare to say it aloud.

“Hehe, it’s nothing. Shaolong, you’re really outstanding.” Xiao Yan smiled, his face full of satisfaction. Throughout the journey, with everyone risking their lives to protect him, Le Shaolong repeatedly demonstrated his outstanding analytical and leadership abilities.

Even the message from the Nine-Star Dou Di could not shake Le Shaolong’s calm and meticulous mind.

“He can remain calm and composed amidst great ups and downs; he is a rare talent.” This is Xiao Yan’s assessment of Le Shaolong.

“Yes, yes, you even dare to question the words of a nine-star Dou Di. Shao Long, I admire you more and more.” Nan Erming echoed, unable to hide his genuine admiration for Le Shao Long.

“I was just following the principle of a merchant’s meticulous care for goods,” Le Shaolong said modestly, feeling a little embarrassed by the praise.

“Excessive modesty is just hypocrisy.” Nan Erming’s words were always so insightful and concise, and the smiles on everyone’s faces deepened.

“Alright, it’s time to go out. Jenny and the others must be getting impatient.” Xiao Yan put away the stone tablet and led everyone back.

Having obtained this opportunity, the group walked back with lighter steps.

However, Xiao Yan still seemed somewhat depressed. The attributes of the spirit seal, the jade box, and the stone tablet were all unknown. Although it was only a matter of time, Xiao Yan’s heart was still itching with a hundred claws scratching at him from time to time.

When everyone returned to the light screen, they saw that the water flow had become much calmer, as if the removal of the stone tablet had caused some subtle changes. When they entered the underground river, the chill had lessened considerably, indicating that this place was closely related to the stone tablet. With the resistance greatly reduced, the group easily escaped the whirlpool and spotted Jenny and the other two who had been eagerly waiting in the distance.

Water splashed up, and four figures emerged from the waves. Zhen Ni’s anxious face blossomed into a smile she hadn’t seen for many days. That smile was charming and alluring, making Xiao Yan feel half of his bones go weak.

He almost lost his footing and fell back into the water. Xiao Yan’s disheveled state made Zi Ying laugh so hard she bent over. Xiao Yan, feeling quite embarrassed, jumped up and stood next to Zhen Ni, scratching his head, looking somewhat uneasy.

“It hasn’t been long since we last met, but Miss’s charm is growing stronger and stronger. One smile from her can send a thousand troops packing. We must bring Miss along when we fight in the future; it will save us a lot of effort,” Xiao Zhan joked, drawing laughter from everyone.

After exchanging pleasantries for a while, Xiao Yan recounted his experiences in the vortex to Zhen Ni and the others in detail. When they heard that he had obtained a great opportunity but was unable to activate it, everyone fell silent, and Zi Ying even rolled her eyes in dissatisfaction.

“Although it’s a bit of a regret, being able to obtain the opportunity to become a Nine-Star Dou Di is already a blessing from heaven. What do we have to be dissatisfied with, and what is there to complain about?” Zhen Ni comforted everyone, and her words were like a spring breeze, taking away everyone’s low spirits.

“Well said! Haha, this trip to the illusionary realm has been very fruitful. We should have a celebration banquet when we get back.” Xiao Yan’s mood brightened immediately after Zhen Ni’s encouragement, and he called everyone to leave.

After passing through the underground river and around the temple, the group was in high spirits. They looked at the huge stone gate behind the temple, with runes shimmering in the middle, just like the gate they had entered through. Near the gate, lush greenery abounds, with sycamore trees swaying gently and their abundant leaves rustling softly.

However, specks of dark purple interspersed among the vegetation somewhat spoil the beautiful scene. But when Xiao Yan looked closely, his eyes immediately lit up.

At this moment, all he could see were these dark purple things, and no other beautiful scenery could be seen.

“These are actually Spirit Seal Moss! They’re everywhere! My God!” Xiao Yan exclaimed excitedly Although he had obtained quite a few Spirit Imprint Moss on the outskirts of the Illusionary Realm, Xiao Yan always wanted more of this rare treasure.

So he had been keeping an eye out for it, but he had never found any more. Now that he was about to leave, he was able to get his wish. How could Xiao Yan not be extremely excited?

“Hey Ziying, can you be careful? This place is full of treasures.” Seeing Ziying so happy that she did somersaults in the grass and trampled over a patch of green, Le Shaolong felt extremely distressed.

“Shaolong is still the same as always, so cautious with treasures. Shaolong, this isn’t an auction house. Look at all this Spirit Seal Moss covering the mountain.

Even if you crush a patch, the rest can be used to make soup that will last for months,” Xiaozhan laughed.

“Making soup? Be careful you don’t die from the tonic.” Le Shaolong rolled his eyes at Xiao Zhan.

Although the Spirit Imprint Moss before him had remained undamaged throughout the countless years of its association with the Spirit Imprint, and given that the energy of heaven and earth here was many times more concentrated than outside, resulting in an unknown amount of growth, Le Shaolong still felt a pang of heartache. This was the very reason they had come here—such a rare treasure, once missed, would be hard to find again! How could they not cherish it?

“It’s rare to see Shaolong arguing with someone, haha.” Xiao Yan couldn’t help but laugh.

“Alright, everyone, hurry up and collect the Spirit Seal Moss.” Xiao Yan’s words were naturally obeyed by everyone, and they all began a competition to collect Spirit Seal Moss… Looking at the mountain of Spirit Imprint Moss, Xiao Yan’s face lit up with a smile.

Now he had enough materials to refine the Marrow Cultivation Pill. After collecting the Spirit Imprint Moss, it was already dark. It was time to leave.

The group waved goodbye to the temple one last time and stepped into the portal. A flash of light appeared, and the group found themselves in an open space, looking around to see where they had come in. Looking at Xiao Yan returning safely from afar, Xiao Yao burst into tears.

The Xiao clan has produced such a genius, who is the hope for the rise of the Xiao clan. What would happen if something were to happen to him? During the time that Xiao Yan was in the illusion, Xiao Yao was constantly uneasy and anxious, and could not even sleep peacefully.

Now that he saw Xiao Yan come out safely, his heart that had been hanging in suspense finally settled down. Looking at Xiao Yao’s haggard face, Xiao Yan felt a surge of emotion and suddenly felt an even greater sense of responsibility towards the Xiao clan. He swiftly descended from mid-air and flew to Xiao Yao’s side.

He clasped his hands in a fist salute, bowed deeply, and uttered a thousand words in the simplest of sentences: “Clan leader, Xiao Yan has lived up to your expectations and has returned.”

“It’s good that you’re back, it’s good that you’re back safe and sound.” Xiao Yao’s eyes welled up with tears as she looked at Xiao Yan, who had returned safely. Xiao Yao didn’t expect Xiao Yan to have any extraordinary encounters inside; as long as Xiao Yan could come out alive, nothing else mattered. The simple phrase “It’s good that you’re back safe and sound” carried the weight of countless sleepless nights of worry from the old man.

When Xiao Yan saw Xiao Yao’s outstretched hands to help him up, his nose tingled, and he quickly straightened up.

“Clan Chief Xiao, you can rest assured. Young Master Xiao has obtained a great opportunity inside, and his strength has greatly improved.” Zhen Ni walked over with light steps and said softly to Xiao Yao.

“That’s good, that’s good. The rise of my Xiao clan depends entirely on him.” Xiao Yao was excited again.

Suddenly, Xiao Yao seemed to remember something, and quickly bowed to Zhen Ni with a very respectful attitude, “Oh my young lady, I was only concerned about Xiao Yan and neglected you. This, this is too bad.”

“Hmm?” Xiao Yan looked at Xiao Yao with some confusion, then at Zhen Ni, his heart churning with turmoil. His group called Zhen Ni “Miss Zhen Ni” because she was the niece of the Merchant Alliance’s president, but why did the Xiao Clan’s patriarch also address her that way? Xiao Yan had already guessed that Zhen Ni was very likely the daughter of the Human Emperor.

As the daughter of the Human Emperor, it was understandable that Xiao Yao addressed her as “Young Miss.”

However, what Xiao Yan did not understand was why Xiao Yao, as the head of the clan, would be so respectful to Zhen Ni, even if she was the daughter of the Human Emperor. Xiao Yan could sense that the respect was not polite respect, but the respect a subordinate would show to a superior! It’s no wonder Xiao Yan was so puzzled, because the Xiao clan and the Zhen family had a relationship he was unaware of!

Sensing Xiao Yan’s doubt, Zhen Ni nodded slightly, tacitly agreeing with his guess.

However, this only acknowledged her identity and did not completely dispel Xiao Yan’s doubts. Despite lingering doubts, Xiao Yan couldn’t help but feel dizzy upon receiving confirmation of Zhen Ni’s identity. With a merchant alliance that monopolized the economy of the Dou Di Continent and the power of the entire human race behind her, Zhen Ni’s background was simply too astonishing.

Xiao Yan felt a bitter taste in his mouth. I must become stronger as soon as possible! — A strong desire to become stronger rose in Xiao Yan’s heart.

Seeing Xiao Yan’s constantly changing expression, Zhen Ni assumed he was blaming her for concealing her identity. She bit her lip lightly and looked at Xiao Yan with great guilt. Xiao Yan smiled and gave Zhen Ni an understanding look, without any hint of blame.

Xiao Yan understood that Zhen Ni didn’t want to put too much pressure on him. After all, Zhen Ni’s status was too noble, giving her more reason to be proud than anyone else. But in reality, Zhen Ni’s interactions with Xiao Yan were always genuine exchanges, without a trace of spoiled pampering or arrogance.

She even entrusted him with something as vital as the blood pact. What did such sincere affection have to do with their status? Both were intelligent and understood each other perfectly; a single glance was enough.

Jenny breathed a sigh of relief, her gaze towards Xiao Yan softening even more. Xiao Yao glanced at their expressions, seemingly noticing something. His shock surpassed even Xiao Yan’s; could their relationship have progressed to…?

Xiao Yao quickly shook his head, sweat soaking his back, unable to bear the thought. After all, in his view, although Xiao Yan was young and promising, he was still only a three-star Dou Di, a far cry from being a match for Zhen Ni’s status. Moreover, should Xiao Yan know about the relationship between the Xiao clan and the Zhen family?

Xiao Yao was at a loss… Above the azure sky, clouds drift and gather. On the ancient sacrificial altar in Ghost Valley, broken stones and crumbling walls lie scattered, while ancient trees stand tall in the distance, and majestic mountains stretch endlessly.

After a short rest, Xiao Yan and his group headed to the Xiao residence. Their movements in the valley were accompanied by a series of whooshing sounds as streaks of silver-blue light flashed across the sky like shooting stars, leaving only blurry afterimages. After a rapid flight day and night, they arrived at the Xiao residence in just a few days.

Everyone breathed a long sigh of relief. Stepping into the familiar Xiao residence, they felt extremely at ease, as if they had been transported to another world. Xiao Yan frowned deeply, showing no sign of joy.

He learned from Zhen Ni that they had failed to purchase the 100,000-year-old Purple Heart Turtle shell, a key ingredient for refining the Purple Heart Pill, and he felt indescribably distressed. The Purple Heart Turtle was an ancient magical beast, named for its purple heart. It possessed incredibly powerful abilities, including extremely high defense and the ability to emit a strange energy that weakened the power of lightning.

After the ancient cataclysm, for unknown reasons, this turtle suddenly became extinct overnight. Without the Purple Heart Turtle Shell, it’s impossible to refine the Purple Heart Pill! And the Purple Heart Pill is so important to Xiao Yan right now.

How could he not be distressed? The Purple Heart Pill is a fifth-grade imperial pill. Although it is also a fifth-grade pill like the Rejuvenation Pill, the Purple Heart Pill has a heaven-defying power, unlike the Rejuvenation Pill, which only keeps one young and does not help with cultivation.

It can greatly weaken the power of the fifth thunder tribulation of the Five Star Emperor Tribulation! Four-star Dou Di are as numerous as hairs on a cow on the Dou Di Continent, but five-star Dou Di are very rare, simply because the leap from four-star to five-star is a qualitative one! Below the Five-Star Dou Di level, lifespan increases with advancement, but there is always a limit.

However, above the Five-Star Dou Di level, one can live forever and be as old as heaven and earth!

However, to advance from the peak of four stars to five stars, one must pass through the five-star emperor tribulation, which is like passing through the gates of hell! The fifth thunder tribulation of the Five-Star Emperor Tribulation possesses world-destroying power, testing the physical strength of the person undergoing the tribulation! Ordinary people, without the physical strength of a Five-Star Dou Di, even if they are as strong as demons, will be instantly annihilated by its attack!

But how can a Four-Star Dou Di possess the physical strength of a Five-Star Dou Di? Therefore, those who can generally undergo the tribulation rely on high-grade inner armor—inner armor made of Seven-Star Demonic Beast hide! But how easy is it to kill a Seven-Star Demonic Beast?

Even if it were possible, how easy is it to encounter one? This is why the vast majority of people on the Dou Di Continent stop at the four-star Dou Di level. The Purple Heart Pill can greatly weaken the power of this calamity!

This is equivalent to directly elevating a four-star Dou Di to a five-star Dou Di! If this pill can be refined in large quantities, it is entirely possible to mass-produce five-star Dou Di! Such efficacy is nothing short of defying the laws of nature!

Xiao Yan wants to improve the overall strength of the Xiao Clan in a short period of time, and refining this pill is essential.

However, he cannot find the main medicinal materials. How can he not be troubled?

However, on the other hand, such a miraculous pill would truly be miraculous if the medicinal materials weren’t so hard to find!

“Little liar!” Qing Mu’er’s coquettish yet slightly aggrieved voice rang out, interrupting Xiao Yan’s thoughts. Xiao Yan was taken aback.

He looked up and met Qing Mu’er’s delicate face, which was covered in tears. He was at a loss for words. Seeing Xiao Yan staring at her blankly, Qing Mu’er felt a surge of anger.

She took a few steps forward and shouted, “Little liar, don’t you even recognize me?” Xiao Yan suddenly realized, and gave a wry smile: “No, no, I was just thinking about something and didn’t come back to my senses for a moment.”

“Hmph! So you finally came back?” Qing Mu’er pouted, her voice immediately softening as she softly complained. After Xiao Yan left, she felt like each day was an eternity.

Then Zhen Ni also disappeared. She guessed that Xiao Yan must be in danger, and she counted down the days. No matter how much she missed him, these words didn’t sound like something a young girl would say.

It was clearly the complaint of a wife waiting for her husband to return, which immediately made Xiao Yan extremely embarrassed. Everyone secretly chuckled.

“Mu’er looks so pitiful for a young wife,” Zhenni suddenly uttered a strange compliment, her jealousy filling the entire Xiao family courtyard. Qing Mu’er blushed and secretly glanced at the helpless Xiao Yan, then stopped arguing.

“Brother’s back!” Qing Haoran’s cheerful voice rang out at the right time, easing Xiao Yan’s embarrassment. Xiao Yan quickly stepped forward and cupped his hands in greeting: “Big Brother.”

Seeing the brilliance in Xiao Yan’s eyes, and noticing his increased strength, Qing Haoran smiled and nodded, “It seems you’ve gained quite a bit on this trip, brother.” Xiao Yan didn’t stand on ceremony and nodded in acknowledgment: “We did gain something, it was worth the risk of our lives.”

“Oh? Quickly, tell your brother about it.”

“No rush, elder brother, let me introduce you to someone first.” Xiao Yan stepped aside slightly, revealing Xiao Yao beside him.

“This is Xiao Yao, the patriarch of my Xiao clan.” A hint of surprise flashed in Qing Haoran’s eyes. With his strength, how could he not see that Xiao Yao was only a four-star early-stage Dou Di?

But in an instant, his doubtful gaze turned into admiration. He stepped forward, cupped his hands, and said, “Clan Chief Xiao, I have long admired your reputation back then. The Xiao Clan’s success today is all thanks to you.”

“Qing Haoran? Demon Emperor Qing Haoran?” Xiao Yao was startled.

He looked Qing Haoran up and down and quickly returned the greeting with a cupped hand.

“Your Majesty flatters me. I have heard about Your Majesty’s assistance to the Xiao Clan. I am very grateful to Your Majesty for saving the Xiao Clan.”

“Clan Chief Xiao, you are too kind. Xiao Yan and I are brothers. How can I stand idly by when the Xiao clan is in trouble?”

Qing Haoran’s casual remark impressed Xiao Yao even more. This man was exceptionally chivalrous, and Xiao Yan was fortunate to have such a brother. After exchanging a few pleasantries, Xiao Yan invited everyone inside to sit down and briefly recounted his experiences in Ghost Valley.

Upon learning that Xiao Yan had encountered a series of fortuitous events and advanced several levels in strength, Qing Haoran, Qing Mu’er, and Xiao Yao were all filled with admiration. Qing Haoran was filled with envy upon hearing that Xiao Yan had obtained the seventeenth-ranked Ghost Spirit Ancestor and that his Blood Spirit Technique cultivation had made great progress. Qing Mu’er, upon hearing the thrilling details, immediately scolded Xiao Yan, blaming him for not including her in such a fun activity.

Xiao Yao was even more astonished that Xiao Yan possessed both human and demon bloodlines, and that he might even acquire demon bloodlines in the future. He was first incredulous, then found it unbelievable, and then almost fainted from shock, exclaiming that a monster had appeared in the Xiao Clan! Qing Haoran and Qing Mu’er, however, weren’t particularly surprised that Xiao Yan might also possess demon blood.

Qing Haoran, in particular, had specifically asked their clan’s ancestor after learning of Xiao Yan’s demon bloodline. He learned that the demon race didn’t have a secret method to grant other races demon blood, so he didn’t believe Xiao Yan’s explanation that the old demon emperor had given him demon blood. He believed Xiao Yan had other secrets.

However, Xiao Yan was his brother, and if his brother didn’t want to talk about it, there must be a reason. But since he could possess demon blood, it wasn’t surprising that he also possessed demon blood. Listening to Xiao Yan’s narration, everyone’s tension of the past few days dissipated, and bursts of laughter and chatter lasted until late at night before they finally dispersed.

Xiao Yan looked at the departing figures of the group, then at the bright moon and twinkling stars high in the vast darkness, and sighed heavily.

“Boy, you’ve gained so many benefits in the illusion, why are you still sighing?” Elder Zhan’s voice echoed softly.

“Elder Zhan, as you know, my strength might not be enough to directly refine the Marrow Cultivation Pill, so I want to refine the Purple Heart Pill to improve my alchemy skills. Moreover, the Purple Heart Pill is no less important to me than the Marrow Cultivation Pill! But I don’t know where to find the main ingredient of the Purple Heart Pill, the 100,000-year-old tortoise shell.”

“Hahahaha!” Elder Zhan’s arrogant laughter boomed into Xiao Yan’s ears like thunder.

“I thought it was something big, it’s just a 100,000-year-old Purple Heart Turtle Shell.”

“So what? You make it sound so easy. Do you have any skills?”

Xiao Yan rolled his eyes and retorted to Elder Zhan in an annoyed tone.

“Hehe, a 100,000-year-old tortoise shell, I don’t have one…” Old Zhan wasn’t angry at all, and said with a smile.

“Why are you causing trouble? Can’t you see that people are annoyed?” Xiao Yan was furious.

You didn’t do anything, yet you spoke so dismissively, even saying “So what?” He almost blurted out, “Go away, go away, go somewhere cool and stay there.”

“Hehehe…” Old Zhan laughed even more heartily, “I really don’t have any 100,000-year-old ones, but I have quite a few 200,000-year-old ones.”

“What?!” Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up immediately.

“Say that again! You old man, are you playing me? How can you only say half a sentence like that?”

“Hey, kid, is this my fault? I hadn’t even finished speaking before you started berating me, and now you’re blaming me!” Old Zhan was clearly annoyed—kid, you want it, huh?

Say something nice, I’m waiting! Xiao Yan could easily understand the meaning in Elder Zhan’s tone, so he immediately put on a smile and said, “Hehe, Elder Zhan, look, could you give me some, considering how we spend every day together?” Elder Zhan deliberately cleared his throat, paused for a moment, and whetted Xiao Yan’s appetite.

After waiting for a long time, seeing that Elder Zhan did not respond, Xiao Yan immediately bowed respectfully to the void and said, “Please help me, Elder Zhan.”

“Alright, since you’ve been quite respectful, I’ll give you some! Anyway, this stuff is useless to me.”

As soon as Elder Zhan finished speaking, a transparent, aged hand appeared out of thin air, with several pieces of dark brown tortoise shell lying quietly in its palm. Xiao Yan took them, grinning and saying, “Didn’t you say you had a lot? Anyway, you said these things aren’t of any use to you, so just give them all to me.”

Old Zhan curled his lip: “Kid, you really don’t hold back! I’d say your tone changes pretty quickly, it’s only been a blink of an eye and you’re already using all these ’you’s’.”

“Hehe, isn’t this just showing respect to the elders? Elder Zhan, look, those tortoise shells are just sitting there taking up space anyway. Why don’t you let me use them to make Purple Heart Pills?

It’ll be a way of cleaning up your trash.”

“You…” Elder Zhan was both amused and exasperated. Just a moment ago, someone was lamenting that they couldn’t find a 100,000-year-old tortoise shell, but in the blink of an eye, even 200,000-year-old ones had become trash.

However, these things were originally prepared for Xiao Yan, so Elder Zhan was quite happy that he was able to force Xiao Yan to say all the right things.

“Here, take them all!” With a “whoosh,” a large pile of dark brown tortoise shells of various sizes were thrown out of thin air, hitting Xiao Yan’s feet accurately. With each hit, Xiao Yan’s heart would pound, and his eyes would brighten.

Xiao Yan’s eyes burned with fervor.

“Elder Zhan, are there still such rubbishes? This junior would be happy to clean them up for you.” Old Zhan’s lips twitched violently.

“Go away!” Then, his voice disappeared.

“Thank you, Elder Zhan!” Xiao Yan transmitted his thoughts, slowly suppressing his excitement, but his lips were still twitching, unable to hide his uncontrollable smile—now he could finally refine the Purple Heart Pill. Xiao Yan immediately ordered someone to summon Long Yi.

Shortly after Xiao Yan hurriedly entered the alchemy room, a golden figure, filled with anxiety, rushed in…
