Chapter 74: Xiao Yan’s Fortuitous Encounter

Jenny’s explanation made everyone suddenly understand, and they felt even more awe for this magical world. The Dou Di Continent seems to have concealed countless unknowns over the years. What lies behind the ancient truth remains unknown and impossible to investigate, but Xiao Zhan’s safety is currently on everyone’s mind.

“Is it really impossible to break it from the outside?” Xiao Yan looked at Zhen Ni with a glimmer of hope, or rather, wishful thinking.

“Unless absolute power can break the domain from the outside, it can only be broken from within.” Jenny frowned, her face pale with worry.

“How do we get in?” Everyone was impatient, their feet already floating off the ground, their battle qi circulating.

“Unless Roaring Black Lord volunteers, no one can get in,” Jenny murmured with a wry smile.

“No matter what, brothers, follow me. We can’t watch Xiao Zhan be in danger.” Upon hearing this, Xiao Yan was greatly disappointed.

His expression changed drastically, but he didn’t hesitate to draw his heavy ruler. With a wave of his hand, he took out five “Hun Tian Pills” and swallowed them with everyone. Then he led the group to charge forward.

My brothers are in grave danger inside. No matter what, I have to rush in, even if it costs me my life. This is friendship; it needs no explanation and no reason.

The figure rushing towards the blood shield carried anxiety, a touch of desolate helplessness, and a hint of the sorrow of a hero’s demise. Jenny sighed, sat down cross-legged, and rapidly changed hand seals, using her battle qi as the seals to control the twelve elven warriors within the blood shield. Since there was no hope of breaking through from the outside, she would break through from the inside.

The blood shield wasn’t too far away, and Xiao Yan and his group arrived in an instant. They bombarded the blood shield from the outside, their faces covered in anxious sweat, their battle qi being released almost non-stop, and their hands, which were gripping their weapons tightly, were torn and bleeding from the recoil.

Meanwhile, Xiao Zhan, who was inside the blood shield, was also suffering terribly. Within the blood-red shield, amidst the roaring laughter of the Black Lord, the power of the blood-red light increased dramatically. Each strike seemed to carry the force of wind and thunder, like ten thousand arrows raining down, filling every inch of space within the domain, leaving nowhere to hide or dodge.

Moreover, the wind generated by the whistling blood light sounded like ten thousand drums beating in unison, deafeningly loud, and exploded in Xiao Zhan’s heart, making his battle qi circulation extremely sluggish and difficult, as if he were stuck in mud, unable to do what he wanted. His golden protective shield was struggling to resist, and had shrunk to a range of one meter, barely able to hold on. Blood gushed from Xiao Zhan’s body.

He covered his wound with one hand, and blood continued to seep out from between his fingers, staining the mud beneath his feet a bright red. Having lost too much blood, Xiao Zhan felt weak all over.

“Roaring Black Lord, I will definitely kill you!” Xiao Zhan roared from time to time, but his voice was not as strong as before and sounded weak and powerless in the domain.

Gradually, Xiao Zhan’s consciousness began to blur, and he became disoriented. He squeezed his eyes shut, shook his head violently a few times, and bit his lips tightly, trying his best to stay awake and not pass out. Jenny’s twelve elven warriors faced even more terrifying impacts from the blood-red light as they sped along, but the twelve warriors did not retreat at all.

Holding shields in one hand, they approached the Roaring Black Lord step by step, and were now less than three hundred meters away from him. Within the Blood Domain, the power of the Blood Eye was amplified immeasurably. Strands of blood-red light shot out, densely striking the green shield that was half a person tall.

With each impact, the shield shadow of the twelve elven warriors dimmed a little. At the same time, the terrifying killing intent that penetrated their bodies continuously corroded their life essence, causing the vine armor of the twelve elven warriors to become increasingly dim, almost to the point of being illusory.

“It seems that even the twelve elven warriors couldn’t hold out until they reached Roaring Black Lord,” Xiao Zhan murmured, a sense of disappointment washing over him. Just as Xiao Zhan’s murmurs ended and the Roaring Black Lord’s face lit up with ecstasy, Zhen Ni, far away outside the blood shield, spat out a mouthful of blood, but a smile appeared on her face. The shields of the twelve elven warriors finally gave way, shattering under the crimson light and dissipating into specks of green light that floated in the air.

With the green shields broken, the twelve elven warriors simultaneously bent over, their bodies leaning backward like drawn bows. They channeled their power into their right arms, their entire bodies transforming into specks of green light that merged into their spears. Then, they hurled their spears with tremendous force, the spears emitting an extremely dazzling light that instantly pierced through space.

Concentrating all his energy for this single strike, this strike will surely be earth-shattering, possessing the power to destroy the world, and it will be delivered at a distance of less than three hundred meters. This strike is destined to bring an end to the battle between everyone and the Roaring Black Lord. The outcome will be decided in this one strike.

Ruthless, fast, and accurate, his timing was so perfect that no one could have predicted it, including Roaring Black Lord. Roaring Black Lord’s face immediately darkened, but Xiao Zhan’s eyes lit up. He suddenly snapped out of his daze and watched the progress of the battle.

Concentrating all of Jenny’s power, the twelve spears arrived in an instant, so fast that no one could react. The speed was astonishing, and the entire Blood Domain fell silent. All sounds disappeared.

A fallen leaf swirled up and suddenly stopped abruptly three feet off the ground.

For the first time, the blood light was so slow that Xiao Zhan could see its entire trajectory clearly. The entire space seemed to have paused for a moment, even in time.

“What powerful fighting skills! What terrifying speed! When the speed reaches its limit, one can travel through time.

It’s true!” Xiao Zhan was so shocked that he couldn’t close his mouth for a long time. The Roaring Black Lord, residing in the Blood Domain, exuded an extremely terrifying sense of oppression.

His blood-red eyes were surrounded by a lot of black mist, and his enormous body was faintly visible, looking particularly ferocious.

However, none of this could stop the spear that came flying through the air. Without any surprise, the spear instantly pierced through the black mist and stabbed into the Roaring Black Lord’s huge body. Compared to the Roaring Black Lord’s enormous body, these spears, which were over two meters long, were nothing.

However, the Roaring Black Lord’s expression clearly told Xiao Zhan that the power of these spears was far greater than that of the ten-thousand-foot-tall Howling Tiger. The spear penetrated almost to the very root, and the Roaring Black Lord roared in pain, his entire body writhing in black mist, his veins bulging, and his screams tearing through the sky. The spear pierced his body, bursting forth with an incomparably dazzling green light.

Suddenly, a massive wheel appeared behind the Roaring Black Lord. The wheel was perfectly formed, covered with marks, filled with ancient simplicity and the vicissitudes of history. The aura of life flowed ceaselessly in the hazy mist, pulsating as if it were alive.

This is the life wheel of the Roaring Black Lord. Everyone possesses the wheel of life. Every year, the trajectory of life leaves a mark on the wheel of life until it is covered with scars and completely collapses.

That is when a person’s lifespan ends and they return to death. It’s incredible that the twelve spears could force out the Roaring Black Lord’s Wheel of Life. Moreover, there are twelve new engravings on the Wheel of Life, several inches deep, still green, and spreading wildly.

The twelve spears slowly dissipated, and the twelve marks continued to spread, turning into cracks at an extremely fast speed. The cracks crisscrossed and slowly extended to the entire Wheel of Life. From a distance, the Wheel of Life appears to be cracking, centered around the twelve markings.

As the cracks extended, Roaring Black Lord’s appearance aged visibly. His once elastic skin quickly became dry and withered, like that of a traveler who had walked in the desert for years. It was as if the ruthlessness of time had robbed him of his vitality.

His hunched back, frosty white temples, and faltering, clumsy steps—the drastic change in Roaring Black Lord’s appearance shocked Xiao Zhan to the point of complete disbelief. The Roaring Black Lord never dreamed that the twelve spears possessed such astonishing power. In its astonishment, its life force was already rapidly fading.

For the first time, it felt death so close. Overwhelmed with fear, it pulled its hands across the void, and a blood-red sun appeared out of nowhere, like a raging crimson flame, drawing wisps of green from the Wheel of Life. After extracting some greenery, the Roaring Black Lord’s complexion became rosier, and it was overjoyed.

It raced against time to increase the speed at which it extracted the greenery. Time is cruel and merciless; whether or not the greenery can be destroyed before the wheel of life collapses is a matter of life and death for the Roaring Black Lord. Roaring Black Lord’s distraction provided Xiao Zhan with a chance to catch his breath, and also an excellent opportunity to attack.

Xiao Zhan took a breath, gathered all his battle qi, and was about to unleash a full-force attack on Roaring Black Lord.

Suddenly, a wisp of green drifted to Xiao Zhan’s front, transformed into a line of words, and then dissipated into the air without a trace.

“Tear space apart with all your might before the wheel of life.” The green light manifested as the line of text above. Although Xiao Zhan did not understand its meaning, he had unwavering faith in Zhen Ni.

Dragging his battered body, Xiao Zhan gathered his remaining strength and charged towards the Roaring Black Lord like lightning. He leaped into the air, his fists lashing out like thunder, unleashing an unstoppable momentum! Having mobilized all of Blood Eye’s power to fight against Greenery, Roaring Black Lord’s clones were exhausted.

Left with no other choice, he could only launch a split punch, striking directly at the source. With his life at stake, Roaring Black Lord naturally fought desperately, his fist as fierce as a lion’s, its power still not to be underestimated before the Blood Domain was broken. But Roaring Black Lord never expected that when Xiao Zhan’s two fists were about to collide with his own single fist, he suddenly changed his fist into a claw and tore it to both sides.

The space in front of Roaring Black Lord was immediately torn open with a crack, and black spatial turbulence roared. Within the Blood Domain, the spatial resistance was astonishing; Xiao Zhan exerted all his strength but could only tear a tiny crack. Faced with the roaring Black Lord’s powerful punch, Xiao Zhan was unable to dodge.

However, having completed the task assigned by Zhen Ni, Xiao Zhan was satisfied. He smiled and met the Roaring Black Lord’s punch.

At this moment, all Xiao Zhan could do was to conjure up his battle qi armor to barely protect his body. He was blasted far away by the Roaring Black Lord’s single punch, and as blood gushed from his mouth, he crashed into the blood shield in an arc, bounced back, and fell heavily to the ground. Xiao Zhan’s unusual behavior puzzled the Roaring Black Lord.

Looking at the inconspicuous crack that Xiao Zhan had torn open, a strong sense of unease inexplicably rose in its heart. Although the tiny crack was not enough to break its domain, it couldn’t explain why it felt that the crack held a feeling that made its heart tremble. Just as the Roaring Black Lord vaguely sensed something was wrong, a green light flashed from the crack.

Before the green light, the space that had been torn open suddenly became as fragile as a piece of paper. Without any doubt, the green light burst forth, flashing a chilling light in mid-air. The Roaring Black Lord took a closer look and was immediately terrified.

The green light was none other than Jenny’s “Evergreen Dream Ring”! The green light paused briefly in the air before accelerating abruptly, flashing like lightning, bypassing the Roaring Black Lord’s heavy punch, passing through layers of black mist, and cutting into the enormous blood-red eye. Using the greenery as a guide and Xiao Zhan as a secondary force, Zhen Ni unleashed a deadly attack within the Blood Domain with a green light that pierced through the void!

This deadly move sealed the fate of Roaring Black Lord and determined the outcome of this battle! The bloodshot eye was ruptured, and blood gushed out like a spring. The Roaring Black Lord was severely injured and rolled on the ground, clutching his forehead, his screams echoing through the heavens.

Using two powerful combat techniques consumes a great deal of battle qi and life essence; the greater the power, the greater the price to pay. Now that its Blood Eye is shattered, the Roaring Black Lord is suffering a backlash of battle qi, with a continuous stream of crimson blood corroding every part of its body, leaving it riddled with wounds and oozing disgusting pus. The excruciating pain has rendered the Roaring Black Lord powerless to resist the green of the Wheel of Life.

Without the Roaring Black Lord’s protection, the greenery extended unimpeded. The Wheel of Life hung high in the sky, but it was dim and covered with spiderweb-like cracks, like wind-eroded rock, having reached the end of its life.

In the blink of an eye, the Roaring Black Lord also became shriveled and withered, like dry firewood, with only a layer of old skin covering the bones, looking ferocious and terrifying. With the Blood Eye shattered, the Roaring Black Lord lost a large amount of his life force, and the Blood Domain lost its supporting power. In no time, it vanished like bubbles under the attack of Xiao Yan and his group.

“Rescue Xiao Zhan! Attack the Stone Wheel with all your might! That’s the Roaring Black Lord’s Wheel of Life!”

As soon as the Blood Domain shattered, Zhen Ni hurriedly warned, only to cough up a mouthful of blood as soon as she finished speaking. Utilizing the miraculous effects of the “Hun Tian Dan,” Zhen Ni had finally unleashed the full power of this fighting technique, managing to strike just as the “Hun Tian Dan” was about to weaken. At the same time, her fighting spirit was completely exhausted.

Seeing victory imminent, she could no longer hold on. Upon hearing this, everyone acted without hesitation and immediately rushed towards the Wheel of Life, unleashing their full power. Starlight tore through the sky, flames raged, storms raged relentlessly, poisonous gas filled the air, and lightning destroyed the last glimmer of hope of the Roaring Black Lord.

The Roaring Black Lord barely managed to support his swaying body, his eyes filled with despair and resentment, looking like a gambler who had lost everything. The strong have their dignity. Even in death, Roaring Black Lord was unwilling to fall at the hands of ants.

However, Roaring Black Lord soon sadly discovered that the collapse of the Wheel of Life had ruthlessly stripped him of even the right to self-destruct.

“Snap!” The wheel of life shattered into countless fragments, returning to the void. As the last fragment of the Wheel of Life vanished, the Roaring Black Lord’s massive body collapsed helplessly onto the ground, its back to the yellow earth and its face to the sky.

It waved its arm in the air, seemingly unwilling to be defeated by a few ants, but then its arm fell limply, its eyes lost their light, and its life force was extinguished. The blood gushing from the Blood Eye stained the earth red, flowing into a small river, leaving only the cold, lifeless corpse of the Roaring Black Lord on the entire wilderness. Xiao Yan turned his head, glanced at everyone, and instructed them, “Everyone, recover here and wait for the weakness period of the ’Hun Tian Dan’ to pass.”

Then he turned his attention to Zhen Ni. Zhen Ni was not seriously injured; she was just weak after the effects of the “Hun Tian Dan” wore off due to excessive consumption of her fighting spirit. Xiao Yan was immediately relieved.

Seemingly sensing Xiao Yan’s concerned gaze, Zhen Ni’s face flushed slightly, giving her a unique charm. She gently turned her head away, somewhat embarrassed.

“Sister Zhenni, you are really amazing!” Zi Ying’s eyes were full of admiration.

“I never imagined that Miss would be so capable and brave.” Xiao Zhan lay sprawled on the ground, breathing in the slightly fishy and hot air of the wilderness. He felt exceptionally fresh, and the feeling of surviving the ordeal was still stuck in his heart.

“Young Miss is truly a hidden talent. She doesn’t make a move until she does, and when she does, it’s astonishing.” Storm also praised her endlessly.

The life-or-death battle brought everyone much closer, and they became much more easygoing in their conversations, unlike before when they were still somewhat constrained by their social status.

“What, doesn’t everyone want to see how plentiful the spoils are?” Feeling the admiring and worshipful gazes of everyone, Zhen Ni quickly changed the subject. She didn’t want to show off anything, and she certainly didn’t want to bring up the Zhen family because of the combat weapons.

Upon hearing Jenny’s words, everyone immediately dropped the admiration that had made her uncomfortable. They exchanged glances, then rushed towards the corpse of the Roaring Black Lord. The Roaring Black Lord, a peak six-star magical beast, was a treasure trove from head to toe.

Seeing everyone’s reaction, Jenny Tseng burst into laughter.

“Damn it, I only took a breath and you lost so much blood, what a waste!” Xiao Zhan was the first to arrive, looking heartbroken and frustrated in front of the Roaring Black Lord’s corpse. Xiao Yan followed closely behind, standing beside the corpse of the Roaring Black Lord and examining it carefully, sighing repeatedly.

Due to the passing of the Wheel of Life, the Roaring Black Lord’s essence blood had lost a large amount of life essence and was no longer usable. Xiao Yan was no less heartbroken than Xiao Zhan. There is nothing more heartbreaking than an alchemist facing the waste of a large amount of top-grade medicinal materials.

Everyone expressed their understanding, but why are you, Xiao Zhan, a mere brute, feeling so heartbroken? Everyone rolled their eyes. Fortunately, although it lost its essence and blood, the top-quality materials it possessed were truly worthy of being produced by a six-star peak magical beast.

The hide is tough and elastic. The Roaring Black Lord is known for its abnormal defense. Its hide is an excellent material for making inner armor.

The hide of a six-star peak Roaring Black Lord is comparable to that of a regular seven-star magical beast. The skeleton is indestructible. Adding a little tempering during weapon manufacturing can increase its hardness.

The magic core is at the peak of the sixth star. It is definitely the best among the six-star magic cores. Whether for alchemy or absorption, it is a rare and excellent product.

The heart, the source of the “chaotic demonic heart,” is an excellent material for refining elixirs related to cultivating the mind. These excellent materials made Xiao Yan overjoyed. Under the plundering of the crowd, the Roaring Black Lord was swept away like a locust swarm that had passed through once in a thousand years.

In an instant, only a bloodstain remained on the vast wasteland to prove that the Roaring Black Lord had once existed. Apart from that, there was nothing else. Xiao Yan stood up, dusted himself off, looked at the storage ring on his hand, and still felt a little unsatisfied, as if something was missing.

“Damn, this Roaring Black Lord is the strongest of the three guardians, how come he’s even poorer than the other two?” Xiao Zhan complained. Xiao Yan suddenly realized that he had gotten used to the benefits. Reminded by Xiao Zhan, everyone started searching everywhere, even turning the black fortress where the Roaring Black Lord lived upside down.

The result only proved how true the saying “the greater the hope, the greater the disappointment” was. Apart from the daily necessities, there was only the howling wind passing through the windows inside the fortress, which was as clean as a virgin. Everyone was so frustrated that they rolled their eyes.

“Alright, alright, all the treasures in the world are meant to be possessed by those with the right fate. Our harvest this time has been quite good, so let’s not be dissatisfied.” Xiao Yan couldn’t bear to watch any longer as Xiao Zhan turned over the collapsed castle bricks to examine them. Jenny covered her mouth and laughed, her eyes sparkling with the gentle light of spring water.

Every smile and frown was captivating, but her skin lacked color and appeared pale. Xiao Zhan angrily kicked a nearby rock away. He felt very unbalanced that he had only gained this much after surviving a near-death experience.

Actually, everyone was a little unhappy, but they didn’t show it like Xiao Zhan did.

However, they all knew that sometimes, success required a bit of luck, and it couldn’t be forced.

“It seems we’ve reached the end of this illusion, but why haven’t we sensed the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal?” Xiao Yan frowned slightly. The battlefield had been cleaned up, and the period of weakness had passed. He began to pay attention to the purpose of everyone’s continued exploration of this place.

The next moment, Xiao Yan leaped up, his soul power sweeping out like a wave, covering the entire surrounding area. The towering mountains, the vast earth, all things in heaven and earth, every blade of grass and every tree, were all taken into his heart.

However, the wind was still the same wind, and the sand was still the same sand. The world was no different, and he did not feel any powerful energy fluctuations. Xiao Yan stood in the sky, his brows furrowed.

Meanwhile, in the sky above, the clouds surged, and the Roaring Black Lord rose from the large amount of bloodstains left on the ground, a faint rune disappearing into the clouds. The thick clouds rippled with golden waves like water, as if the faint rune had unlocked some secret. But at this moment, Xiao Yan was focused on finding clues about the Heavenly Flame and the Spirit Seal.

He didn’t notice these things at all and was still racking his brains trying to figure out where the problem was.

“Spirit imprint moss is a byproduct of spirit imprints. The fact that so much spirit imprint moss was harvested before proves that there are definitely spirit imprints here.”

“Elder Zhan said he sensed the existence of the Heavenly Fire, so the Heavenly Fire must exist. But why is there no energy fluctuation at all? There’s no reason for it!

What could be the reason for this?” Xiao Yan hovered high in the sky, letting the breeze caress his face. His thick black hair fluttered gently, and his eyes shone like two stars, filled with confusion and bewilderment.

“Could I have forgotten something?” Xiao Yan’s heart skipped a beat, his thoughts racing as he quickly recalled every detail of his time in the illusion. Scene after scene flashed before his eyes, but Xiao Yan couldn’t remember where he had missed anything.

The Heavenly Flame and the Spirit Seal are crucial to the evolution of the Burning Technique, and Xiao Yan naturally wouldn’t overlook their significance. He explored every place he visited with his soul power. Xiao Yan had considerable confidence in his soul power, so where did the problem lie?

He was completely baffled.

“Instead of racking your brains, we should search more carefully; perhaps we can find something.” Xiao Yan spread his bone wings, calling out to everyone. Like a dazzling rainbow piercing the sky, he launched a carpet search across tens of thousands of miles.

Without the powerful magical beasts guarding them, Xiao Yan and his companions were fearless, and wherever they went, smoke billowed, and their powerful and fierce aura dissipated. The group climbed over cliffs, searched rocks, brushed past withered trees, and trudged through sandstorms, doing everything in their power, but still found nothing. The desolate wilderness was silent, save for the faint whistling of the wind.

The stifling heat in the air amplified the silent oppression in Xiao Yan’s heart. He lost his usual peaceful and serene demeanor, and his heart began to feel restless and uneasy. He risked his life and paid a heavy price in blood, but he still couldn’t find what he wanted.

The dark spiritual moss in his storage ring was a vivid testament to its existence right here, so why couldn’t he find it? Xiao Yan really couldn’t understand. After going around in circles, I returned to the same spot.

Xiao Yan reluctantly descended from mid-air, his long, thick, black hair flowing naturally down his chest and back, swaying gently in the wind, just like his thoughts at this moment, chaotic as the wind and tangled as a net. Seeing Xiao Yan like this, everyone felt bad, but after searching everywhere to no avail, no one had a better idea, and the atmosphere became somewhat gloomy. The oppressive atmosphere on the ground did not affect the mood of the heavens.

Golden light, like ripples on water, had already enveloped the entire cloud layer. An unparalleled power was brewing within it, and dazzling lightning appeared and disappeared, seemingly about to break through the sky. Looking at the disappointment written on Xiao Yan’s pale face, Xiao Zhan felt a sense of powerlessness and suffocation in his heart, with nowhere to vent it.

He was extremely uncomfortable. In his anger, he kicked out casually, and a stone under his feet flipped several times in the air before inadvertently passing by the storm. The gust of wind pulled Storm out of its myriad thoughts.

As a stone whizzed past, Storm paused for a moment, suddenly feeling a sense of familiarity. After a moment of reflection, it finally remembered that this was the very stone that Xiao Zhan had kicked away earlier.

“Hey Xiaozhan, you really have a connection with this rock. You kicked it twice in a row. I think you should pick it up and put it in your house as a souvenir,” Storm joked, trying to distract everyone from their worries and also to adjust his own mood.

“It’s not fate, it’s grudge,” Jenny said with a forced smile. Everyone chuckled, and the atmosphere eased slightly, but Xiao Yan remained frowning. The words had barely left her lips when, seemingly even the heavens themselves bore witness to Zhen Ni’s statement, blaming Xiao Zhan, a dignified six-star Dou Di, for twice incurring the wrath of a rock.

Suddenly, a strange light appeared in the sky; the long-brewing, water-like golden patterns tore through the thick clouds, causing them to part rapidly. A bolt of lightning flashed across the heavens, and golden flames flickered as they surged forth from the sky, followed by a continuous barrage of thunderbolts that struck Xiao Zhan’s footing. The rolling lightning possessed an overwhelming power, and Xiao Zhan’s expression changed drastically.

He hastily retreated, shouting as he went, “What kind of era is this? Even kicking a stone brings divine retribution…!” Actually, none of this had anything to do with the stone; it was all about luck.

He just happened to be standing where the gate to the secret realm opened after the three guardians of the illusion had fallen… Thunder and lightning raged incessantly, striking the spot where Xiao Zhan had just stood. The celestial might bombarded the area for a full quarter of an hour before the sky returned to calm, the wind still and the clouds still, as if nothing had happened.

As the smoke dissipated, everyone stared intently. A huge crack had been blasted open in the ground, and a winding stone staircase could be vaguely seen descending. A faint light swirled around it, like a light mist, creating a hazy, spiritual atmosphere.

At the end of the crack, faint points of light could be seen, the nature of which was unknown.

“Where is this place?” Xiao Yan asked, puzzled. He looked into the depths of the crack with a wary gaze. The previous battle still left a shadow in his mind, and he dared not be careless in the slightest.

“Perhaps the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal are down there. We’ve searched for miles around without finding a trace. This is our only hope.”

Zhenni, being observant, voiced her judgment, her eyes reflecting undisguised joy for Xiao Yan.

“It could also be the exit of the illusion. Look, there’s no exit within this vast area of tens of thousands of miles…” Le Shaolong offered another theory.

Although Le Shaolong didn’t want to spoil Zhen Nixing’s mood, he still spoke his mind based on the facts.

“Of course, I also hope it’s as Miss says,” Le Shaolong added. After the previous search, everyone had lost much hope for the existence of the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal. As Xiao Yan listened to Zhen Ni and Le Shaolong’s words, his gaze remained fixed on the depths of the crack, and he was anxiously making a judgment.

Whether it’s the exit or the Heavenly Fire and Spirit Seal are below, we’ll only know by going down to investigate. The existence of the Heavenly Fire and Spirit Seal within the illusion is almost certain, and I’m determined to obtain them. If this is the exit, it’s not too late to return and continue searching.

The powerful magical beasts have all been eliminated; I can find them eventually by searching slowly. With this in mind, Xiao Yan made a decision.

“Cheer up, be careful, let’s go down and take a look.” Xiao Yan said, then circulated his soul power and went down the steps. The bronze lamps on both sides of the steps are simple and unadorned, with unidentifiable patterns carved on them. They are shrouded in milky white spiritual energy, and the red flames flicker slightly, making them seem to have come alive, as if the patterns on the bronze lamps have come to life.

Xiao Yan and his companions circulated their inner energy to refresh themselves and carefully descended the steps. Having experienced the troublesome situation with the Roaring Black Lord, they were all on tenterhooks, yet they encountered no danger whatsoever, only the echoes of their footsteps on the stone steps reverberating in the empty cave. After descending the steps and turning a slight corner, they found themselves in a vast, spacious, and simple area, devoid of any superfluous decorations.

Directly ahead, streaks of silver light swirled, their nature unclear. Further away lay a portal of light, emanating an extremely powerful aura, its destination unknown. Xiao Yan’s heart leaped into his throat.

Could that gate of light be the exit that Le Shaolong had mentioned? If it was an exit, why did it possess such an extremely powerful aura? And what was this silvery light before him?

Xiao Yan felt a dryness in his throat, and his heart seemed to leap into his throat, the pounding of his heart clearly audible in the quiet space. Jenny gently patted Xiao Yan’s back, calming him down slightly. Then, she led the group cautiously closer to the silver light.

As they got closer to Guanghua, the group’s steps became shorter and shorter, and their vigilance was raised to the highest level. Just as they were about to approach the silver light, Xiao Yan’s bone wings suddenly opened automatically, emitting a series of buzzing sounds, as if they were reacting to the silver light in some way. The bone wings vibrated violently, whipping up gusts of wind, as if trying to carry Xiao Yan forward.

Xiao Yan quickly steadied himself. He could clearly feel the silver light attracting the bone wings, just like breast milk has an instinctive allure for a crying infant. This puzzled Xiao Yan greatly, and everyone else was also surprised as to why Xiao Yan had suddenly unfolded his bone wings at this moment.

The attraction grew stronger, and the bone wings flapped more and more violently. The silver light rippled out like waves, and an overwhelming pressure rose into the sky, making people want to worship. Faced with this oppressive force, everyone felt as if they were carrying a mountain on their backs, making it difficult to move forward.

Their weapons could not advance an inch. Except for Xiao Yan, everyone had blood seeping from the corners of their mouths and cold sweat pouring down their foreheads.

“This is at least the pressure of an eight-star Dou Di! What on earth is this?!” Xiao Zhan asked in surprise. Just when everyone felt the immense pressure, Xiao Yan found himself surrounded by silver light like ripples on water, but he felt no discomfort whatsoever.

Not only did he not feel any discomfort, Xiao Yan also felt a strange change. Streaks of silver light rushed into his body, constantly moving through his flesh and blood, and then entered the bone wings on his back, causing the bone wings to shimmer with light. The light flowed continuously through Xiao Yan’s limbs and bones, and Xiao Yan found that his body’s resilience had been strengthened, which made him overjoyed.

“It seems that this silver light is quite helpful in tempering the body. What exactly is this? Could it be a spirit seal?”

But it doesn’t feel like it. Although I have never seen a spirit seal before, my intuition tells me that it is not.

“However, this silver light doesn’t seem to do me any harm, this…” Just as Xiao Yan was lost in thought, the silver light that poured into the bone wings seemed to find an outlet. Like a breached dam, a torrent of silver light surged forth, making the bone wings even more crystalline and translucent, as if carved from jade.

The light shimmered and flowed with a hazy radiance, and the vibrations became more frequent, causing a dull ache in Xiao Yan’s shoulders, as if they were about to detach from his body. Xiao Yan hurriedly circulated his Dou Qi and activated Heavenly Fire to protect himself, slowly suppressing the urge of the bone wings to take flight. As the radiance diminished, everyone was finally able to catch their breath.

The items within the silver radiance slowly came into view, and upon first glance, everyone froze, their expressions revealing utter disappointment. On the open ground lay a colossal skeleton, its entire body covered by a pair of enormous wings. Judging from the skeleton’s grayish-white color, it must have survived countless ages.

Yet, this skeleton, having endured countless years, still exuded an extremely powerful aura, causing those nearby to feel an unbearable pressure in their chests and difficulty breathing, a testament to its former majesty. This had absolutely no connection to the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal, leaving everyone greatly disappointed.

However, amidst their disappointment, they noticed that the wings of this skeleton were remarkably similar to Xiao Yan’s bone wings; in other words, Xiao Yan’s bone wings were a miniature version of these skeleton wings. The silver light continued to flow across the sky, pouring into Xiao Yan’s body and continuously entering his bone wings. When the light finally dissipated, the oppressive aura vanished, and everyone returned to normal.

“Such a powerful aura!” Xiao Zhan exhaled, saying with lingering resentment.

“If I’m not mistaken, this should be the ancestor of the Heavenly Phoenix Clan on the Dou Di Continent. Because they are of the same bloodline, they have a strong attraction to each other.” Xiao Yan’s eyes began to burn, and his previous disappointment was suddenly replaced by excitement.

Xiao Yan was indeed right. This was indeed an ancestor of the Heavenly Phoenix Clan on the Dou Di Continent, an eight-star Dou Di who had suffered severe injuries in the ancient calamity and sought refuge here to heal, ultimately perishing here. The three guardians, including Roaring Black Lord, were his former guards.

After their master’s fall, the three guardians refused to leave and have remained here to protect his remains.

“Why does Young Master Xiao have such a judgment?” Due to his past professional habits, Le Shaolong always wanted to understand these unknown things.

“Shaolong still has the same old habits,” Jenny teased.

“Sister Zhenni, tell us quickly, what are Boss Le’s old habits?” Zi Ying seemed very interested in this. The others also cast interested glances at Zhenni.

Jenny smiled, glanced at Le Shaolong, who scratched his head, looking somewhat embarrassed. Then Jenny said, “He used to work as an assistant manager at the auction house. He always made sure to ask about anything he hadn’t seen before, it became a habit.”

Seeing everyone’s enlightened expressions, Jenny turned to Xiao Yan and said, “Young Master Xiao, you’d better explain this to Shaolong quickly, otherwise, he’ll probably be thinking about this the whole way.” Le Shaolong chuckled. Xiao Yan then explained the origin of his bone wings to everyone.

After hearing about Xiao Yan’s previous experiences, everyone felt that Xiao Yan’s judgment was correct, and their disappointment turned into excitement.

“Congratulations, Young Master Xiao! Regardless of the circumstances, this is a great thing for you. A skeleton with the strength of an eight-star or higher—this is a stroke of luck from heaven.

Young Master Xiao, first get the skeleton’s wings to match your bone wings. As for the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal, there is still hope ahead,” Xiao Zhan said, his eyebrows smiling.

“Since these are the wings of an ancestor of the Heavenly Phoenix Clan, their compatibility with Young Master Xiao’s bone wings should be very high. They might even greatly enhance the quality of the bone wings.” Zhen Ni’s tone was full of joy for Xiao Yan.

Just as Xiao Zhan said, there is still hope ahead. At least what lies beyond the gate of light is an unknown. It is Xiao Yan’s good fortune to have obtained such an opportunity here.

But will Xiao Yan be able to enjoy this good fortune? The greater the opportunity, the higher the risk…

Back to contents

Chapter 74: The Heaven-Defying Purple Heart Pill

Golden light flashed, and as he watched the figure gradually solidify and felt the increasingly powerful aura, Xiao Yan’s brows furrowed slightly, his joy deepening.

“Not bad, not bad, you’ve improved again,” Xiao Yan said with a smile. After not seeing him for a while, Long Yi seemed to have made great progress; Xiao Yan could no longer fathom his cultivation level.

“You’re finally back,” Long Yi said, a smile blooming on his face as he looked at Xiao Yan. Sensing the concern in Long Yi’s words, Xiao Yan felt a warmth in his heart. He couldn’t help but stroke Long Yi’s head, nodding repeatedly with satisfaction as he watched the lightning coursing across Long Yi’s body grow even stronger.

“Why did it take you so long to come back? I was so worried at home. You must have gained a lot, right?”

The worry that had lingered when Long Yi first arrived was gone from his eyes, replaced by deep affection.

“I encountered a fortuitous opportunity and absorbed Heavenly Fire and Ghost Spirit.”

“Tell me about it!” Long Yi was as excited as a child. Xiao Yan affectionately ruffled Long Yi’s hair and then recounted his experiences in the illusion to him.

Listening to Xiao Yan’s account, Long Yi sometimes pursed his lips, sometimes widened his eyes, sometimes clenched his fists, and sometimes smiled. He tilted his head slightly, his smile growing wider. Long Yi, not good with words, used his expression to tell Xiao Yan that he was happy for him.

“You’re quite good too. What’s your strength now?” Xiao Yan finished recounting his experience in the illusion, patted Long Yi’s shoulder, and asked.

“Early stage four stars.”

“Not bad, Long Yi, even faster than me.” Xiao Yan wasn’t surprised. From the fact that he couldn’t see through Long Yi’s cultivation level, he knew that Long Yi must have already reached four stars.

“Meeting that crazy madman who doesn’t care about his life when refining medicine, absorbing so much pill lightning, it’s hard not to advance.” Long Yi pouted and complained, his eyes filled with fear of Lang Tian.

“Haha, looks like Lang Tian really put you through the wringer.” Xiao Yan laughed heartily. He knew that Lang Tian was far more insane when it came to refining medicine than he was. The pile of herbs Lang Tian had brought out before his team went to search for the Spirit Seal Moss had given him quite a fright.

“Although it’s been tough, the rewards are substantial. Look, you’ve already advanced to a four-star Dou Di,” Xiao Yan comforted him. Long Yi snorted.

Although he knew that Lang Tian’s madness in refining medicine had greatly helped his improvement, the thought of that madman’s relentless, day-and-night refining madness still sent shivers down his spine, his little face filled with grievance. Seeing Long Yi acting like a child, his chubby little face even plumper than before, was incredibly cute, and Xiao Yan couldn’t resist giving him a couple of squeezes.

“What’s Lang Tian doing right now?” Hearing Xiao Yan ask about Lang Tian, Long Yi pouted unhappily and said, “What else could he be doing? Of course, he’s refining pills!

I really don’t understand, with just a few pill formulas, he’s been refining them for so long, how come he’s not tired of it yet? His interest is still so strong!” Xiao Yan smiled wryly upon hearing this, truly unable to believe that the outwardly refined Lang Tian was actually more hardworking than he had imagined.

“Long Yi, I’m going to refine a fifth-grade pill, the Purple Heart Pill. Do you need to rest for a bit?” Seeing Long Yi’s slightly tired appearance, Xiao Yan couldn’t help but ask.

Upon hearing that Xiao Yan was going to refine a fifth-grade pill, Long Yi’s previous tiredness vanished instantly, her eyes sparkling with excitement, and she shook her head like a rattle drum: “No, no, no, come on!” No matter how diligent Lang Tian was, he had always been refining fourth-grade and lower-grade pills, and Long Yi had long been tired of it. Now, with a new challenge, Long Yi immediately perked up.

“Alright, let’s begin.” Xiao Yan smiled and nodded in satisfaction. Then, with a flick of his hand, the materials needed to refine the Purple Heart Pill—a five-star earth-attribute magic core, five-star earth-attribute magical beast essence blood, and a two-hundred-thousand-year-old Purple Heart Turtle shell—were lined up and floated in mid-air. With another wave of his sleeve, the Flame Wood Cauldron appeared, its flames resembling the mane of an enraged lion, eerily strange.

Xiao Yan slowly extended his palm, and with a light pinch of his fingers, a bluish-gray flame shot out, hissing and emitting an eerie light. Instantly, the temperature in the alchemy room rose sharply. The Heavenly Fire, fused with the Spirit-Connecting Pill Fire, was even more powerful.

Xiao Yan calmed his mind, flicked his fingertip, and shot the bluish-gray flame into the Flame Wood Cauldron. The flame spread out within the cauldron with a “poof,” and a cauldron of blazing bluish-gray flames converged and surged, causing the room temperature to rise once more. Once the flames within the Flamewood Cauldron had stabilized and moved smoothly, Xiao Yan waved his hand slightly towards the void, and all the medicinal herbs flew steadily into the cauldron.

With his current peak Emperor Realm soul power, Xiao Yan was quite confident in refining the Purple Heart Pill, but being as cautious as ever, he kept a tense expression, fully focused, his eyes fixed on the Flamewood Cauldron. Under the continuous burning of the bluish-gray flames, the heat waves within the cauldron surged and leaped excitedly, each herb being refined and tempered, then gradually fused together under Xiao Yan’s powerful soul power, constantly rotating and tumbling.

As time passed, the medicinal liquid gradually condensed into a purple pill. Long Yi watched with great interest, his tense nerves finally relaxing, a smile appearing on his lips The prototype of the Purple Heart Pill was complete. Xiao Yan slowly withdrew his Dou Qi, probing with his soul power.

The flames gradually weakened, and the Purple Heart Pill, nurtured by the flames, slowly transformed from its uneven surface into a smooth, round shape, finally floating before Xiao Yan, surrounded by a purple mist and a refreshing, elegant fragrance. With the fifth-grade pill complete, the fifth-grade pill lightning arrived immediately. Instantly, the sky darkened and gloomy, thick with mist, and thunder roared like a tiger’s roar and a lion’s roar, echoing through the heavens and shaking the earth.

Above the alchemy room, a bolt of lightning forcefully tore open the azure sky, containing immense energy and displaying powerful divine might. What a magnificent display! The fifth-grade pill lightning was indeed extraordinary!

Long Yi was already surging with excitement, looking eager to try it out.

“Come on! Fifth-grade pill lightning!” Then, he charged straight towards the lightning!

Muffled thunder rumbled in the sky, like the drums of war beating on a battlefield.

“Sizzle!” A bolt of lightning, like a giant dragon, exploded in the air, illuminating the entire sky for a fleeting moment. The lightning, carrying immense power, struck down fiercely towards Long Yi, who was approaching from mid-air.

“Crack!” A deafening roar echoed as the lightning struck Long Yi, unleashing a dazzling golden light and flashing arcs of electricity. Looking at Long Yi in mid-air, Xiao Yan’s expression was solemn.

Although he had enough confidence in Long Yi, seeing the power of the fifth-grade pill lightning still made him somewhat worried. But in the blink of an eye, the lightning vanished, abruptly disappearing. Only Long Yi stood steadily in mid-air, a smile of excitement on his face.

As he smiled, he actually burped! The fifth-grade pill lightning had been so easily absorbed by Long Yi? Xiao Yan was shocked.

He remembered that when Long Yi was refining the Rejuvenation Pill, he hadn’t absorbed the fifth-grade pill lightning so easily! It seemed that after Long Yi’s strength increased, even his ability to absorb pill lightning had become more formidable. Long Yi leaped down, a slight smile playing on his lips, his joy barely concealed.

“Fifth-grade pill lightning is much better than fourth-grade!” Xiao Yan couldn’t help but laugh heartily. Having successfully refined his first Purple Heart Pill, a gleam of elation rose between Xiao Yan’s brows.

With a flick of his wrist, he tossed another set of medicinal herbs into the Flaming Wood Cauldron. His ten fingers danced nimbly, and with complete control, Xiao Yan gradually closed his eyes, immersing himself entirely in the refining of the Purple Heart Pill. A dozen days later, four small white jade bottles floated in mid-air.

“Long Yi, are you alright?” Xiao Yan looked at Long Yi, who had just returned from receiving the pill lightning. His face, which had been tense, shone with a golden light, and Xiao Yan felt a pang of anxiety, asking with concern. Long Yi followed Xiao Yan from the Dou Qi Continent.

On this Dou Di Continent, where he had no old friends, Xiao Yan initially considered Long Yi his best brother.

However, Long Yi explicitly stated that he followed Xiao Yan as a father. Over time, Xiao Yan unconsciously began to see Long Yi as a son.

“It’s nothing. I just absorbed several Rank 5 Pill Lightnings; the energy is quite strong. I need to digest it while you refine it,” Long Yi hurriedly said.

“That’s good,” Xiao Yan breathed a sigh of relief. Now, thanks to the addition of the Spiritual Flame, Xiao Yan’s soul power had advanced to the peak of the Emperor Realm. Even he himself didn’t realize that his alchemy skills had actually improved significantly.

He just felt that refining the Purple Heart Pill this time was very easy; he hadn’t failed even once and was in high spirits. He felt that, in his current state, refining the Marrow Cultivation Pill shouldn’t be a problem. He needed to strike while the iron was hot and refine one Marrow Cultivation Pill; then things would be easier in the future.

Xiao Yan reached out and stored the four white jade bottles containing the Purple Heart Pills into his storage ring. He then immediately retrieved the materials needed to refine the Marrow Cultivation Pill: Blue Blossom Branch, Ice-Spirit Grass, Illusionary Dream Vine, Origin Grass, Demon Tea Moss, Spirit Seal Moss, one five-star earth-attribute magic core, and twenty drops of five-star earth-attribute magical beast blood essence. The Marrow Cultivation Pill was a peak fifth-grade pill.

Xiao Yan had attempted to refine it when he first advanced to the fifth grade of Emperor, but had failed each time. Having learned from his previous failure, he was much more cautious this time. Based on his previous experience, Xiao Yan refined the Blue Blossom Branch, Ice-Spirit Grass, and Illusionary Dream Vine, then the Origin Grass and Demon Tea Moss, and finally purified the five-star earth-attribute magic core and the five-star earth-attribute magical beast blood essence.

As before, everything went smoothly.

However, there was no joy on Xiao Yan’s face. He knew that the most difficult part of refining the Marrow Cultivation Pill was yet to come: the extraction of the most crucial ingredient, Spirit Seal Moss. Gazing cautiously at the dark, murky Spirit Seal Moss in his hand, Xiao Yan narrowed his eyes and flicked his fingertips, sending the moss floating into the Flaming Wood Cauldron.

He concentrated his peak Emperor Realm soul power, slowly probing into the surging, burning Flaming Wood Cauldron, carefully manipulating the bluish-gray flames. As Xiao Yan’s expression grew increasingly solemn, the Spirit Seal Moss transformed into a bluish-green liquid, slowly becoming clear and pure, before gradually condensing into a crystal-clear, round bluish-green liquid bead.

With a wave of his hand, Xiao Yan poured the previously refined Azure Branch, Ice-Spirit Grass, and Dream Vine, followed by Origin Grass and Demon Tea Moss, and finally the purified five-star earth-attribute magic core and demonic beast essence blood into the Flaming Wood Cauldron. The herbs, upon entering the cauldron, immediately and visibly enveloped the bluish-green liquid bead, then gradually fused together.

Beads of sweat dripped from Xiao Yan’s forehead, instantly evaporated by the intense heat of the room Xiao Yan’s hands moved rapidly, forming intricate hand seals. His vast Dou Qi activated the Heavenly Fire within the cauldron, causing the flames to surge violently and dramatically, instantly raising the temperature inside the cauldron… As time passed day by day, the elixir slowly fused together.

Finally, a round, emerald-green pill floated above the cauldron, enveloped in a faint mist.

“Haha! Success! The Marrow Cultivation Pill has finally been successfully refined!”

Xiao Yan breathed a sigh of relief and couldn’t help but laugh out loud. No wonder he was so excited. The successful refining of the Marrow Cultivation Pill meant that he would have the bloodline of the demon race after possessing the bloodline of the demon race.

It meant that he would be able to devour the spirit seal, and that the Eight Extremes Heavenly Technique would advance!…

“Young Master Xiao, what rare pills have you refined this time? Are they fifth-grade?” Just as Xiao Yan was seated surrounded by a group of people, Lang Tian rushed over from his alchemy room.

Without even a greeting, his first question was to ask how the pills were. In fact, as soon as Xiao Yan went into seclusion, Lang Tian, who was no longer with Long Yi, knew that Xiao Yan had returned. These past few days, seeing the powerful pill lightning appearing in the sky from time to time, he had been impatient to know what kind of fifth-grade pill Xiao Yan was refining this time.

“You rascal, besides pills, what else interests you?” Xiao Yan smiled wryly, then suddenly his gaze shifted to Lang Tian.

“You’ve advanced too? Your aura is similar to Long Yi’s. You haven’t reached the early stage of the four-star level, have you?

My god, how did you get there so fast?” Xiao Yan’s advancement from the early stage of three stars to the peak of three stars was entirely due to his absorption of the Heavenly Flame and the Ghost Spirit; otherwise, he wouldn’t have been able to advance so quickly. But Lang Tian just stayed at home refining medicine, so how could he have advanced so fast as well?

Although Lang Tian was originally at the late stage of three stars, it’s not like he could break through to the early stage of four stars in an instant. Xiao Yan was shocked by the advancement speed of the others for the first time. Actually, this is because Xiao Yan had never witnessed Lang Tian’s madness in alchemy.

If he had, he wouldn’t have been so shocked. Langtian’s alchemy knows no seasons, no day or night; all he does is refine medicine, refine medicine, and refine medicine again! When his battle qi is depleted, he replenishes it by pouring handfuls of Qingling Liquid into his mouth; when he is too tired to continue, he meditates and rests before continuing to refine medicine.

He kept squeezing out the last bit of battle qi, then replenishing it, squeezing it out again, and replenishing it again. This high-intensity cycle made the generation of battle qi faster and faster. Lang Tian actually broke through to the fourth star in the process of almost self-torturing alchemy.

After listening to Long Yi’s whispered explanation, Xiao Yan’s mouth gaped open, his jaw almost dropping—“Is this acceptable?” Xiao Yan had always thought he was quite diligent in his cultivation, but he never expected that Lang Tian’s diligence was beyond description; it was downright monstrous! Xiao Yan was stunned for a long time.

“Hehe…” Lang Tian scratched his head sheepishly, then uttered a classic line that would later be regarded as a motto by all the alchemists in the Dou Di Continent: “Level doesn’t matter, as long as you’re good enough to refine medicine.”

“What kind of top-grade pills have you refined? Take them out and let me see them quickly.” Lang Tian urged Xiao Yan, his tone already impatient.

Everyone looked at Xiao Yan, making no attempt to hide their intense curiosity. Over the past few days, everyone has witnessed several incredibly powerful lightning bolts. Everyone is wondering what kind of incredible pill Xiao Yan will refine after going through so much trouble and even risking his life to find the Spirit Seal Moss.

Xiao Yan snapped out of his daze, but smiled without saying a word. He slowly picked up his tea, took a sip, and whetted the appetites of Lang Tian and the others. Setting down his teacup, Xiao Yan smiled faintly, then slowly reached out and flipped his hand, revealing a perfectly round purple pill in his palm.

The pill shimmered with purple light, surrounded by mist, and exuded a fragrant aroma.

“This is…” A greedy light suddenly surged in Lang Tian’s eyes. As far as he knew, this pill must be a fifth-grade precious pill, otherwise, it would not have condensed light and the fragrance of the medicine would not have lingered for so long.

Xiao Yan flicked his finger, and the Purple Heart Pill transformed into a beam of purple light that shot towards Lang Tian, hovering half a foot in front of Lang Tian’s eyes.

“This pill is called the Purple Heart Pill, a fifth-grade Emperor-level pill,” Xiao Yan said calmly, but couldn’t help but reveal a hint of pride.

“Its effect is to greatly weaken the power of the fifth thunder tribulation of the Five-Star Emperor Tribulation.” Xiao Yan’s words exploded in the room like a bomb, causing everyone to gasp and their heads to spin. Almost all of them had experienced the Five-Star Emperor Tribulation and knew very well the power of that fifth lightning tribulation.

If it weren’t for the inner armor made from the hide of a seven-star demonic beast, none of them would have been able to become a Five-Star Dou Di! Yet, the pill Xiao Yan had refined could actually weaken the power of that lightning tribulation! For a moment, they couldn’t believe it, thinking Xiao Yan was joking.

“Xiao Yan, are you telling the truth? This pill really works…” Of all the people present, Xiao Yao reacted the most strongly.

His eyes widened, and he swallowed hard, seemingly unable to believe what Xiao Yan had said. Back then, Xiao Yao left the Imperial State and his family to strive for a breakthrough to the Five-Star Dou Di realm, aiming to lead the Xiao Clan onto a path of prosperity. Unfortunately, he failed to achieve his goal and instead contracted a strange illness.

Therefore, he understands best the hardships of a Four-Star Dou Di’s path to becoming a Five-Star Dou Di, and he also understands best what weakening the Fifth Thunder Tribulation of the Five-Star Emperor Tribulation means for a Four-Star Dou Di!

At this moment, hearing Xiao Yan say that such a single pill could weaken the Fifth Thunder Tribulation of the Five-Star level, which was considered a death calamity by all four-star Dou Di, how could he not be shocked? How could he believe it?

“Yes, that’s what the old Demon Emperor said on the pill formula he left me,” Xiao Yan nodded seriously. Upon receiving Xiao Yan’s affirmative answer, everyone was truly stunned. Didn’t that mean that such a Purple Heart Pill was equivalent to a five-star Dou Di?

“This Purple Heart Pill is truly priceless,” Zhen Ni couldn’t help but exclaim in admiration. Everyone immediately echoed, nodding vigorously. If the Rejuvenation Pill had sparked a frenzy of buying among the women of the Dou Di Continent, then this Purple Heart Pill would surely make all the four-star Dou Di of the Dou Di Continent willing to go bankrupt and die for it.

“This pill’s effects are so powerful. If it were auctioned off and publicized, it would probably bring Brother Xiao Yan a great deal of trouble, even… even death,” Qing Haoran, worthy of being the Demon Emperor, was the first to recover from his astonishment.

Xiao Yan hadn’t planned to auction off the Purple Heart Pill anyway, so he nodded in agreement and waved his hand, the pill landing steadily in his palm.

“Big brother is right.”

“Brother, I see you used more than one pill lightning bolt this time. How many Purple Heart Pills did you refine in total?” Qing Haoran asked.

“Four.”

“Little liar, your pills can only help a peak four-star Dou Di advance to five stars. They’re useless to us. These four are perfect for the four of you.

Now you four won’t have to worry about not reaching five stars.” The four people Qing Mu’er was referring to were, of course, Xiao Yan, Lang Tian, Long Yi, and Xiao Yao, who hadn’t yet reached five stars.

“Mu’er, don’t cause trouble!” Qing Haoran scolded, then suddenly asked Xiao Yan very seriously, “Brother, I’m asking you, are the medicinal herbs for refining this Purple Heart Pill hard to find?”

As soon as Qing Haoran asked this, the shrewd Zhen Ni and Le Shaolong immediately understood his intention. Xiao Yao also seemed to have thought of something, and they all looked at Xiao Yan. Xiao Yan was perfectly aware of the meaning behind their gazes, as he had already planned how to use the Purple Heart Pill.

He glanced apologetically at Zhen Ni and said, “The medicinal herbs are extremely difficult to find. Even with the combined efforts of the Merchant Alliance, we haven’t found a single piece. I heard that the Purple Heart Turtle is a magical beast that existed before the ancient calamity and became extinct after it…” He looked apologetically at Zhen Ni because he had immediately begun refining the pill after receiving the Purple Heart Turtle shell from Elder Zhan, and hadn’t had time to tell Zhen Ni that he already had the shell.

It seemed that Zhen Ni had spent a lot of time and effort searching for the almost extinct Purple Heart Turtle shell.

“Alas, what a pity…” Before Xiao Yan could finish speaking, everyone sighed.

“Little liar! What did you use to refine these four pills?” Qing Mu’er shouted impatiently. As everyone was sighing to themselves, Qing Mu’er’s question suddenly brought them to their senses—“That’s right!

How did you refine it? We didn’t encounter any Purple Heart Turtles in the illusion this time.” Then, looking at Xiao Yan’s still unfinished expression, they couldn’t help but chuckle—“He’s really too shocked by this Purple Heart Pill to keep his composure.”

The reason Xiao Yan only spoke halfway through was that he was thinking about how to tell everyone the origin of the Purple Heart Turtle shell. He really couldn’t tell Zhan Lao and Zi Chen’s Spirit Void Tower yet, at least not now. After hesitating for a while, he had to apologize to everyone, “I’m sorry everyone, there are some things I can’t say right now…

However, I have indeed found the Purple Heart Turtle shell.” As he said this, he deliberately looked at Zhen Ni.

“Don’t say that, everyone has their own secrets, right? It’s good that the shell was found, it saves me from searching endlessly. Are there many?” Zhen Ni smiled without minding, showing concern for the number of Purple Heart Turtle shells.

“Quite a lot, I estimate it can refine several hundred pills without any problem,” Xiao Yan replied. Several hundred pills! This number truly shocked the few people who genuinely understood the value of the Purple Heart Pill!

“I think everyone understands what several hundred Purple Heart Pills mean!” After careful consideration, Qing Haoran raised his eyebrows and slowly spoke, his expression becoming extremely solemn. He uttered these earth-shattering words, “These Purple Heart Pills can create a powerful force in a short time!

As long as a few more experts are in charge, it will soon become a first-class force on the Dou Di Continent! A large quantity of Purple Heart Pills is enough to defy the heavens!” Apart from Xiao Yan, everyone present, including Zhen Ni and Le Shaolong, was stunned upon hearing this.

They held their breath, imagining the grand scene of several hundred five-star Dou Di, imagining a powerful force that could mass-produce five-star Dou Di. While their blood was boiling, a chill ran from their feet to their heads. They all secretly rejoiced that the person who possessed this pill formula was Xiao Yan, and not an enemy force.

After a long while, they slowly came back to their senses and let out a long sigh.

“Xiao Yan, this…this is a golden opportunity to revitalize our Xiao clan!” Xiao Yao couldn’t contain himself any longer. He sprang from his chair, almost unsteady on his feet, his wrinkled face taut with excitement.

Xiao Yan quickly steadied him.

“Ancestor, please slow down. I also want our Xiao clan to become strong quickly, but, Ancestor, you know that besides you, the entire Xiao clan doesn’t even have a four-star Dou Di…” Upon hearing this, Xiao Yao deflated like a burst balloon “Young Master Xiao, I have an idea, but I don’t know if it’s feasible.”

A faint smile appeared in Le Shaolong’s eyes. Xiao Yan nodded, signaling him to continue. Le Shaolong stared intently at the Purple Heart Pill radiating an alluring purple light in Xiao Yan’s hand, and slowly said, “The Purple Heart Pill definitely cannot be auctioned off.

Right now, no one in the Xiao Clan needs it, and since Young Master Xiao wants to revitalize the Xiao Clan as soon as possible, why not find some four-star peak Dou Di with excellent aptitude and character, and relatively clean backgrounds?”

“That’s right, Young Master Xiao, as long as they hand over the blood pact, they will actually be members of the Xiao clan.” Jenny leaned back in her chair, her curvaceous figure in agreement with Le Shaolong’s suggestion.

“Hmm, not bad, Shaolong’s suggestion is good.” Qing Haoran also supported the suggestion.

“As long as it can really help those peak four-star Dou Di advance to five-star, there are plenty of outstanding talents on the Dou Di Continent who are willing to serve the Xiao Clan. Clan Chief Xiao, what do you think?”

“Great! Of course that’s good!” Xiao Yao nodded repeatedly, so happy he didn’t know what to say. Xiao Yan also agreed with Le Shaolong’s suggestion, but he still had one last uncertainty: the effects of the Purple Heart Pill were only described in the formula, and no one knew whether it could actually achieve those effects.

When he voiced his concern, Le Shaolong laughed.

“Young Master Xiao, don’t we trust your alchemy skills? As long as you refine it properly, I think the efficacy of the Purple Heart Pill recorded by the Old Demon Emperor should also be fine. If there’s any unexpected situation, there’s only one possibility.”

At this point, Le Shaolong pondered.

“Hey, why are you hesitating? Speak up, what’s possible?” Qing Mu’er was impatient after all, and immediately urged Le Shaolong to speak.

“Unless the Purple Heart Turtle shell that Young Master Xiao is looking for is less than 100,000 years old,” Le Shaolong said, pointing out that possibility.

As soon as he spoke, everyone looked at Xiao Yan. Upon hearing this, Xiao Yan immediately laughed, “What Shaolong said is not entirely wrong. The tortoise shell I found is indeed not 100,000 years old, but 200,000 years old.”

As soon as Xiao Yan finished speaking, everyone looked at each other, wishing they could slap him immediately. Seeing that Xiao Yan was about to be attacked by Qing Mu’er, Zhen Ni immediately spoke up: “Young Master Xiao is really a lucky man. We couldn’t find one even after searching for 100,000 years, but he found one after 200,000 years without anyone noticing.

I estimate that the Purple Heart Pill’s effects are probably even stronger. Young Master Xiao, why don’t we try it on someone at the peak of the fourth star?” Yes, we need to find someone to try it out.

But who can we find? Xiao Yan doesn’t know anyone except the people in front of him. Where can he find a suitable person?

Besides, people on the Dou Di Continent are probably unheard of of pills with such effects. Who would believe it? Who would dare to take it?

Moreover, a blood pact must be signed first.

“Young Master Xiao, I have a few suitable candidates in mind. They are all nearly 50,000 years old, yet they are still hovering at the peak of the four-star level, without a certainty of breaking through to the five-star Dou Di level. They are all good in all aspects, and their backgrounds are also clean,” said Le Shaolong.

“Since Shaolong thinks it’s suitable, then of course there’s no problem. Let’s call one over to try it out first.” Xiao Yan agreed without hesitation. Xiao Yan’s trust in his team members went far beyond the typical superior-subordinate relationship.

Since it was Le Shaolong who introduced them, Xiao Yan naturally felt at ease.

“Yes, Young Master Xiao, please rest assured. These men are my brothers of many years, and I can guarantee their loyalty.” In response to Xiao Yan’s sidelong glance, Le Shaolong bowed slightly to express his respect “I trust you.” Xiao Yan smiled calmly, then handed over the Purple Heart Pill in his hand.

“Young Master Xiao, there’s no need for the pills. I’ll bring the person here, and you can make your decision later.” Le Shaolong dared not touch the Purple Heart Pill, hastily bowed, and rushed out the door.

． “Little liar, this Purple Heart Pill is useless to me. Next time, refine one that can directly advance me from five stars to six stars.” Qing Mu’er tilted her head slightly, glancing at Xiao Yan with an air of smugness.

Xiao Yan was both amused and exasperated.

“I want a pill with the effects you described more than anyone else. If it really existed, you could all advance to the sixth star level overnight.” Everyone burst into laughter.

“I’m just making a reservation with you in advance, just in case I get pregnant later?” Qing Mu’er smiled sweetly, and she was genuinely happy to see Xiao Yan helplessly teased by her. Before long, two illusory figures appeared outside the door. With a creak, Le Shaolong gently pushed open the door, and the person following him eagerly stepped into the room.

“Demon Emperor?” Upon seeing the room full of people, the man first recognized Qing Haoran, his eyes widening slightly before he clasped his hands in a salute.

“Young Master Xiao, this is my friend, Zhan Yi,” Le Shaolong introduced the man to Xiao Yan. Xiao Yan nodded slightly and carefully examined the person. The person’s battle aura was very solid, indicating that he had been at the peak of the four-star level for a long time.

His eyes gleamed with excitement, and his face, which was faintly dark blue, was flushed with hope. As Xiao Yan sized up Zhan Yi, a great wave of emotion surged within Zhan Yi. Le Shaolong hadn’t introduced him to the Demon Emperor, but rather to this black-robed youth who was now scrutinizing him.

Could it be that he was the true possessor of the Purple Heart Pill? Zhan Yi quickly clasped his hands in greeting, “May I ask your name, young master?” Xiao Yan smiled slightly, not directly answering Zhan Yi, “Brother Zhan is Shaolong’s friend, and I am extremely grateful for your willingness to test the pill’s effects.

However, this pill is being refined for the first time. Although I am confident, it has not yet been verified, and I dare not guarantee that it will be foolproof. Have you thought it through?”

Upon hearing this, Zhan Yi was slightly taken aback, but quickly understood the implications.

“I was being presumptuous, please forgive me, young master. I trust Shao Long, and since you have been so frank with me, I also trust you. I hope you will bestow upon me a pill to help me break through.

I will remember this kindness forever.” Xiao Yan nodded slightly, his eyes showing appreciation for Zhan Yi, but his brows furrowed, and he remained silent for a long time. Zhan Yi’s heart jumped into his throat. Could it be that his words just now were not enough to show his determination?

He paused for a moment, then slapped the back of his head.

“Look at me, I’m so happy I’ve lost my mind. I’ve forgotten everything Shaolong told me.” Then he slapped his forehead and was about to offer his blood pact to Xiao Yan.

Xiao Yan’s furrowed brows immediately relaxed, but he stretched out his hand to stop Zhan Yi.

“I am very grateful for your loyalty to me, Brother Zhan, but you can give your blood oath to Shaolong.” These words shocked everyone present, and Zhan Yi was at a loss for what to do. Qing Haoran’s expression hardened, and he turned to look at Xiao Yan’s extremely serious face, just watching silently.

Jenny clenched her fist, then relaxed it. Whatever decision Xiao Yan made, she would support him unconditionally. Xiao Zhan, Nan Erming, Feng Bao, Zi Ying and the others were stunned for a moment, but felt very warm in their hearts.

It was rare for Xiao Yan to trust everyone so much. With such a wise leader, what more could they ask for? Qing Mu’er pursed her lips, but didn’t dare to say anything.

Only Le Shaolong’s expression changed drastically, and he said urgently, “Young Master Xiao, you absolutely cannot, Shaolong cannot bear this responsibility.” Xiao Yan waved his hand and said, “Shaolong, after spending so much time together, you should know me. I’m not good at management. Apart from alchemy and cultivation, I’m far inferior to you in other areas.”

“This matter concerns the rise of the Xiao clan and is of great importance. I hope Young Master Xiao will rescind his order and choose someone else from within the Xiao clan.” Le Shaolong repeatedly declined, not daring to take on this heavy responsibility.

“No one in the Xiao Clan can shoulder this heavy responsibility! Shaolong, I know your concerns, but I must tell you, although we are nominally master and servant, we are in reality brothers. Could it be that you, Shaolong, are unwilling to share my burden?”

Xiao Yan pursed his lips, seemingly somewhat displeased.

“But…” Le Shaolong opened his mouth to speak.

“Jennie has to manage the household affairs, and among everyone, only you are calm, cautious, and capable of making strategic decisions and have leadership qualities. Tell me, if you don’t help me, who will?” Xiao Yan’s tone was very firm and left no room for doubt.

Seeing Xiao Yan’s unprecedentedly serious expression, Le Shaolong was choked up and unable to speak, while Xiao Zhan and the others were already in tears
