Chapter 75: The Wings of the Eight-Star Heavenly Demon Phoenix

Feeling the changes brought about by the bone wings, the sensation of almost being able to soar through the air with a single flap of the wings made the heat in Xiao Yan’s eyes momentarily surpass the temperature of the Heavenly Fire. A sudden happiness filled his chest, even his breathing carried a burning desire. And this was merely the effect of the silver radiance condensed from the life essence of the bone wings.

If he were to refine the Heavenly Demon Phoenix Wings of an eight-star Dou Di, who knew how much stronger he would become? Xiao Yan’s Adam’s apple bobbed involuntarily, his lips became dry, and his eyes couldn’t hide his impatience.

“Young Master Xiao, refine them here. We’ll protect you, and everyone can also search this place carefully to see if there are any clues about the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal,” Le Shaolong said gently, seeing Xiao Yan’s eagerness. The crowd immediately scattered, protecting Xiao Yan while also tapping on the walls, hoping for the existence of a hidden door or a new discovery.

This was clearly a sign of their unwavering determination. Although the space was vast, with everyone’s strength, at such close range, virtually no fluctuation could escape their sensitive nerves.

However, being at the site of the Eight-Star Dou Di’s fall, they all harbored a sliver of hope, clinging to the possibility of a miracle. As for the gate of light, perhaps it was the exit from the illusion, or perhaps it held the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal—who knew? They could explore it after Xiao Yan finished refining it.

In any case, Xiao Yan had already obtained the Eight-Star Dou Di’s relics here, a tremendous unexpected gain from this trip, and everyone was genuinely happy for him. Xiao Yan, however, had no time to pay attention to any of this. His eyes were fixed solely on the enormous wings.

Forcing himself to calm down, Xiao Yan regulated his breathing, circulating his Dou Qi within his body several times. He slowly exhaled a breath of stale air, his eyes growing even brighter. With a flick of his left hand, dark cyan heavenly fire surged forth, completely enveloping the skeleton.

Xiao Yan spared no effort, pouring in a continuous stream of Dou Qi, making the flames blaze fiercely, like a raging torrent. Fortunately, Xiao Yan possessed extremely strong soul power, suppressing the heavenly fire to just cover the skeleton. Even so, the temperature within the space still rose sharply, even the walls faintly turning a dark red.

Xiao Yan seemed to have entered a state of alchemy, calm and focused, concentrating on tempering the skeleton. His expression was solemn and serious, his handsome face highlighted by the blazing heavenly fire, exuding a focused and handsome charm. It is said that a man is most handsome when focused, and Zhen Ni, looking at Xiao Yan at this moment, was somewhat captivated.

As the saying goes, beauty is best appreciated under lamplight; under the flickering firelight, Zhen Ni, looking infatuated, possessed a unique and alluring charm. Unfortunately, Xiao Yan was so engrossed in tempering that he had no time to be distracted and missed the beautiful sight. Refining the Heavenly Phoenix Wings was a massive undertaking, allowing no room for distraction.

Xiao Yan dared not relax for a moment, his mind highly focused as he manipulated the Heavenly Fire, enveloping the massive skeleton in dark cyan flames and repeatedly scorching and tempering it. The diffused light spilled outwards, distorting under the intense heat, casting a hazy glow over the entire stone chamber. Time passed relentlessly; though spring flowers hadn’t withered and autumn hadn’t arrived, the years flowed by like water.

The relentless tempering day and night consumed a tremendous amount of Dou Qi. With Xiao Yan’s current strength as a three-star Dou Di in the early stages, sustaining it all in one go was undoubtedly a pipe dream. Fortunately, the abundance of pills allowed the tempering to continue uninterrupted.

In fact, without the prepared pills, no matter how excited or impatient Xiao Yan was, he wouldn’t dare begin refining at this point, because interruption would mean all his previous efforts were in vain. For a full forty-nine days, only a cyan flame burned fiercely in the dim room, its dark cyan light flickering incessantly. His skeletal structure finally began to change; bubbles continuously welled from his bones, bursting to form a hazy, colorful mist that shrouded the area above.

This was a sign that the refining process was nearing success, and also the most crucial stage. Xiao Yan’s face was deathly pale, glistening with sweat; clearly, he was on the verge of collapse. He freed one hand and easily picked up a bottle of Cleansing Elixir from the ground, gulping it down.

He felt slightly better and then focused intently on the refining process again. The Cleansing Elixir was placed there by Zhen Ni. For the past forty-nine days, she had stayed by Xiao Yan’s side without leaving, wiping his sweat and always keeping a bottle within easy reach.

All of this brought knowing smiles to the faces of those watching. Each time the group shared a knowing smile, Jenny’s cheeks would flush with a rosy blush, like the sunset, a mixture of shyness and, more importantly, happiness. Seven more days passed in a flash.

The Heavenly Flame enveloping the skeleton shrank, until, from the outside, only a small, flickering flame could be seen. The crowd gathered around Xiao Yan, their eyes fixed on his Heavenly Flame, anticipating the moment of successful refining. When Xiao Yan’s face turned almost translucent with paleness, the Heavenly Phoenix’s skeleton finally dissolved into a wisp of gray mist within the Heavenly Flame, swirling like winter snowflakes and settling at everyone’s feet.

Above the stone chamber floated seven drops of essence blood, the very essence of the Heavenly Phoenix. Upon closer inspection, a radiant life force flowed within the blood, captivating the senses, like jade refined from the finest stone, shimmering with a soft, translucent, and emerald-green light. Having previous experience refining bone wings, Xiao Yan wasn’t overly surprised, only a deep joy flickering in his eyes.

The others, however, were seeing the essence blood of an eight-star Dou Di for the first time, and their excitement was palpable.

“A massive skeleton, its essence fully condensed—this is top-grade essence blood! The tempering power of the Heavenly Fire is truly extraordinary,” they exclaimed. Xiao Yan waved his hand, gathering the seven drops of essence blood in mid-air to his side, preparing to store them in a white jade bottle for future alchemy.

As soon as the essence blood approached them, its vigorous life force quickly spread through their limbs and meridians, leaving them feeling refreshed and almost groaning. Truly worthy of being the essence blood of an eight-star Dou Di, its effects were astonishing.

However, while seemingly gentle on the surface, its inner violent power could cause the person who consumed it to explode instantly, unless refined by an alchemist to neutralize its ferocity—a fact everyone was well aware of. Having carefully collected the essence blood, Xiao Yan laughed heartily, his heart filled with anticipation. His emotions surged, his gaze sweeping across the sky above the stone chamber—that was where he was most concerned.

At the very top of the chamber, a pair of wings, several meters long, floated silently, as lustrous as white jade and emerald, shimmering with an eerie luster. The wings fluttered slightly, as if stirring up a thousand turbulent waves in the space, causing the stone chamber to tremble continuously.

“Such powerful wings!” Xiao Yan’s emotions were hard to contain; he couldn’t help but feel a surge of pride, his excitement reaching an unparalleled level—he had finally succeeded in tempering them; now all that remained was fusion. Biting his tongue, he forced out a mouthful of essence blood.

Xiao Yan formed a hand seal, pointed diagonally at the half-suspended wings, and uttered the word “Go!” A blood arrow flew towards the bone wings. The blood spread, transforming into a cloud of blood mist that swiftly merged into the bone wings.

The bone wings roared, displaying a myriad of strange phenomena, before transforming into a beam of white light that shot into the wings on Xiao Yan’s back like lightning. As the bone wings entered his body, the wings on Xiao Yan’s back trembled as if a humble subject had met an emperor, surrendering without hesitation and offering no resistance as the white light entered. When the white light overlapped with the wings, Xiao Yan clearly heard the sound of the bone wings breaking.

It seemed as if all his bones were being shattered and then reformed. The relic of the eight-star Dou Di was undergoing this fusion process with overwhelming force. Sweat poured down Xiao Yan’s forehead, as if he were facing a vast, raging sea, with lightning flashing and thunder shaking the heavens.

He was merely a small boat on the waves, completely at the mercy of fate. His fingers were connected to his heart; the bone wings had long been fused with Xiao Yan, and now, with all his bones shattered, the excruciating pain pierced his very soul. Xiao Yan gripped the ground desperately, his veins throbbing like a drawn bowstring, his face contorted almost unrecognizable.

All traces of civility were gone from Xiao Yan; only the roar of a savage remained. Suppressing the boundless pain, Xiao Yan barely managed to stop the others from helping him. He circulated his Heavenly Flame, desperately protecting his last vestige of consciousness to prevent himself from fainting.

This was a hurdle he had to overcome himself; outsiders couldn’t help him, a fact Xiao Yan was acutely aware of. Faced with the power of an eight-star Dou Di, he could do nothing but protect his true self and endure this boundless torment. Fortunately, the wings on Xiao Yan’s back were of low grade and offered no resistance; otherwise, the combined force of the two sides would have easily torn him apart.

The fusion lasted for a full three hours before gradually subsiding, and the stone chamber slowly returned to calm. Xiao Yan slowly opened his eyes, his gaze radiant with divine light, his body brimming with vitality. The wound on his back had completely disappeared, leaving no scar.

At this moment, Xiao Yan felt that although his flesh, organs, and bones had only been eroded by a very small portion of the fusion’s power, they had already benefited immensely. A powerful energy flowed slowly within him, and his skin shimmered with a crystalline luster. With a thought, Xiao Yan unfurled a pair of wings several meters long.

Now, after its evolution, the bone wings are completely different from the previous milky-white skeleton. The bones are now crystal clear, almost translucent, shrouded in a light mist. Countless runes shimmer and appear in the dim light, like stars in the night sky, a beauty that is both dreamlike and ethereal.

With a gentle flap, Xiao Yan felt as if the air offered him no resistance. With a light step, Xiao Yan’s figure vanished instantly. Xiao Zhan only felt a light tap on his shoulder, but before he could see anyone, Xiao Yan’s afterimage solidified again.

Seeing Xiao Yan smiling at him, Xiao Zhan suddenly realized that it was Xiao Yan who had tapped his shoulder. Before even a fleeting afterimage could dissipate, he had already flown back and forth. Everyone gasped in astonishment; this speed was simply monstrous.

“Congratulations, Young Master Xiao.” Xiao Zhan looked at Xiao Yan with extreme envy.

“Congratulations to you.” Zhen Ni smiled lightly, her eyes brimming with undisguised joy. With these bone wings, Xiao Yan’s survival ability would be greatly enhanced, and Zhen Ni was naturally overjoyed. Everyone came forward to offer their congratulations.

Zi Ying circled around the bone wings, constantly stroking the dreamlike wings, unable to put them down. In Zi Ying’s eyes, these wings were simply a beautiful work of art crafted from white jade—too perfect.

“Young Master Xiao, besides the increased speed, what other remarkable features do these wings possess?” Feng Bao asked curiously, feeling that the relics of an eight-star Dou Di should be more than just simple. Feng Bao’s question sparked the curiosity of everyone, and they all focused their gazes on Xiao Yan, scrutinizing him from head to toe, as if his face and body were covered in flowers.

“I don’t know either.” Xiao Yan was uncomfortable with the crowd’s attention. He scratched the back of his head slightly embarrassed.

“The fusion process only has the effect of tempering the body and strengthening the physique; I haven’t noticed anything else yet.” As he spoke, Xiao Yan casually took a few steps forward. The edge of his bone wings lightly grazed the thick wall, producing a soft cutting sound.

Xiao Yan turned around, his expression slightly startled. Xiao Yan’s reaction aroused the crowd’s curiosity. In a flash, a deep scratch remained on the wall, leaving them looking at each other in shock.

No one knew better than them just how hard the wall was. When Xiao Yan was tempering his wings, they had searched for hidden compartments and even tried to scratch the wall with weapons, leaving only a faint mark. At the time, they were amazed by the wall’s seemingly stone-like material, its incredible toughness.

They never expected that a light scratch from Xiao Yan’s wings would penetrate several inches. Xiao Yan was initially slightly startled, not expecting the edges of the bone wings to be so sharp. Seeing everyone’s astonishment and hearing their explanations, he realized it wasn’t just sharp, it was terrifyingly sharp.

Overjoyed, a bold idea arose in his mind.

“What would be the effect of extreme speed combined with extreme sharpness?” Xiao Yan proposed the idea. Zhen Ni was the first to agree, and everyone else was also intrigued, urging Xiao Yan to demonstrate. With a thought, Xiao Yan channeled his Dou Qi into the bone wings.

The wings emitted a blinding white light, radiating a dazzling brilliance that made it impossible to open one’s eyes. He turned, unleashing the “Three Thousand Thunderbolts,” and the entire underground space saw only a flash of silver light and a figure impossible to catch. Swift as lightning, fast as thunder, without making a sound, like a fish swimming in the sea or an eagle soaring in the sky, Xiao Yan’s exhilarating emotions rose from his dantian, passed through his throat, and transformed into a roar that echoed throughout the stone chamber.

Having briefly satisfied his craving for flight, Xiao Yan abruptly turned in mid-air. A sharp light emanated from the edges of his bone wings, seemingly sharper than a divine blade, their dazzling brilliance blinding. In an instant, he approached the wall, slashing through it with a resounding clang.

In the blink of an eye, deep, crisscrossing scratches crisscrossed the wall, leaving it appearing shattered and shocking. Xiao Yan gracefully folded his wings and stood in mid-air, only to find that there was absolutely no reaction below. The silence was so profound that even breathing was clearly audible.

Looking around, everyone below seemed petrified, staring at Xiao Yan in disbelief, unable to process what had happened for a long time.

“Heavens, this is too amazing! I only saw a beam of light, like the moon piercing the dark sky, and this impenetrable wall is destroyed just like that? Unbelievable!

Zi Ying, pinch me!” Xiao Zhan stared intently at Xiao Yan, still wondering if he was dreaming.

“Impressive! Its sharpness is comparable to my Evergreen Dream Ring, but its speed far surpasses it. Young Master Xiao, you’ve truly struck gold this time!”

Zhen Ni’s radiant smile brightened the entire space. Everyone touched the scratches on the wall, marveling at it. With Xiao Yan’s current speed and the sharp edge of his blade, even a five-star Dou Di would likely perish under his bone wings if underestimated.

This journey through the illusion had indeed yielded substantial gains. A faint smile played on Xiao Yan’s lips, his heart filled with joy as his gaze turned to the portal of light.

“Heaven has granted us such an opportunity, and the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal will certainly not disappoint us.” Gazing at the portal of light behind the space for a long time, Xiao Yan spoke slowly. His voice, though tinged with the weariness of days of bloody battle, still couldn’t conceal his fervent heart and unwavering confidence.

A cool breeze blew, a chill wafting through the long staircase. The sky outside was still shrouded in dust, still carrying a hint of blood. This place holds the memories of everyone’s life-and-death experiences.

Now, everything has settled, and it’s time to leave. There’s a sense of parting, a bittersweet feeling, and everyone is inevitably moved. But their steps don’t falter.

Slowly turning back, they follow Xiao Yan’s footsteps, preparing to step through the gate of light and head towards another world—perhaps the exit, perhaps the location of the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal. The unknown—what an exciting word! The unknown represents hope; it represents trepidation; it represents passion and risk.

Led by Xiao Yan, everyone stepped into the gate of light. The light flashed, and in a distorted beam, their figures appeared in mid-air. Xiao Yan stood high in the sky, the mountains and rivers below him.

His bone wings slowly unfurled, a sense of control over “degree” welling up within him, as if his thoughts could reach anywhere. High above, the view was expansive. Everyone gazed into the distance, the sky high and the clouds light, everything within sight, filling them with a sense of peace and inspiring a surge of heroic ambition.

They exhaled a long breath, their gazes sweeping across the ground. Their expressions froze instantly, like dried rocks, their faces a mixture of joy and shock. Joy because there was no sign of Xiao Yao, no familiar scenery; this was definitely not the exit, meaning it was highly likely the location of the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal.

Shock because the scenery below exceeded their expectations, challenging their very senses. Beneath the clouds lay nine stone mountains, their peaks soaring into the sky, majestic and imposing. The mountains rose and fell, rugged and craggy like dragons, ancient trees reaching for the heavens, their canopies dense and their trunks ancient and gnarled—a truly magnificent sight.

But this was far from the reason for their astonishment. They were horrified because the vast forest below lacked endless green waves, the sound of rustling pines, the red of maple leaves, the pale yellow, the pink, the deep emerald, the light green—not even a single vibrant color. There was only a boundless expanse of gray!

Yes, a gray hue. The entire endless mountains, forests, vegetation, and even the flowers below were all gray, varying shades of gray, much like a traditional Chinese ink painting. The crowd could hardly believe their eyes, but after rubbing them and looking again, it was still a vast expanse of gray.

A clear spring flowed gently, and the dew on the branches, glistening and clear, was reflected in the gray of the leaves; the violets blooming were also a pale gray—perhaps they should be called gray violets; the strawberries growing in the vast thicket were also a dark gray… There were no vibrant colors, no lush greenery, only the gray that should have been deathly, yet everything was so serene and fragrant, full of life. It was simply unbelievable, but it was so vividly presented before their eyes.

“Ink wash landscape, a freehand depiction of life. Scenery that has always only appeared in paintings is now so realistically presented before us. Nature’s creations are truly wondrous.”

Xiao Yan gazed down, regaining his composure, and spoke leisurely. The cold wind in the high sky made his black robes flutter, as if a drop of thick ink had been casually splashed into space, leaving a trace that was so conspicuous.

“When you reach the summit, you can look down on all the mountains below. I wonder what it would feel like to lean on the railing, wine in hand, facing a landscape of gray tones.” Xiao Zhan glanced lightly at the mountaintop, his hearty laughter dispelling the astonishment on everyone’s faces.

“A unique kind of beauty.” Zhen Ni landed, stroking a palm-sized jade leaf. It shimmered with gray light, the naturally formed veins of the leaf were very smooth.

“All of this is truly magical.” Zi Ying also curiously picked a peony flower. Its surface shimmered with light, neither flower nor jade, not dazzling, but full of ancient charm, giving people an unusual mood.

Away from the bloodshed, relaxing and strolling through the novel gray-toned forest, the soft sunlight filtered through the treetops and shone down the trunks, making everyone feel much more comfortable and exuding a sense of ease.

“There seems to be something in the distance.” Even while relaxing, Xiao Yan’s soul power was still constantly probing, as it was always better to be cautious in an unknown environment. Soaring into the air, following the direction Xiao Yan pointed, they saw, nestled among the mountains, towering gray-black stone peaks and pillars pointing straight to the sky, like a vast, dark forest.

In the middle of the stone forest, a pool of clear water held a colossal creature. This colossal creature was over a thousand meters long, covered in heavy armor, resembling an infinitely enlarged version of a pangolin. A row of sharp spikes several meters long ran along its back, ferocious and terrifying.

With a casual flick of its massive tail, rocks crumbled and dust billowed endlessly. The oppressive aura of a peak six-star expert was chilling.

“If I’m not mistaken, this is a Mountain-Breaking War Beast from the ancient savage beasts. How could it appear here?” Le Shaolong frowned and asked, his eyes twitching involuntarily.

“I think it’s best not to provoke these ancient savage beasts without the connection of the Heavenly Fire and the Spirit Seal,” Storm suggested from the side. After experiencing the power of a six-star peak beast from Roaring Black Lord, everyone still felt a lingering fear in their hearts

Chapter 75: Soul-Devouring Mark

However, Le Shaolong knew that Xiao Yan would never accept his blood pact; otherwise, he wouldn’t have returned it to everyone in the illusion. It was precisely because he knew Xiao Yan wouldn’t accept it that he insisted Xiao Yan agree first! Will Xiao Yan agree?

Of course not! Xiao Yan shook his head and said, “Young Dragon, I know what your request is, but I will not agree to it. If the Purple Heart Pill is confirmed to be effective, you and I both know what kind of power it will be.

That will be the foundation for the rise of my Xiao Clan.

However, the path of cultivation is full of crises and dangers, and my own strength is still weak. I cannot guarantee when I, Xiao Yan, will perish. I absolutely do not want this power to be destroyed because of me!

The reason I entrust this power to you is precisely because of this consideration.” When Xiao Yan finished speaking, everyone realized that Le Shaolong’s request was to donate blood again. They looked at Le Shaolong with new respect and admired Xiao Yan even more—this was a true leader who treated them like brothers!

Le Shaolong had to admit that Xiao Yan was right, but wouldn’t there be no risk in entrusting everything to him? Thinking of this, a flash of inspiration struck him, and he came up with another idea that could put his mind at ease and also avoid such risks to some extent.

“Young Master Xiao, it’s not that I’m shirking my responsibility, but it’s really that I can’t shoulder such a heavy burden on my own. I have a suggestion that you might agree with.”

“Oh? Quick, tell me quickly.” At this point, Xiao Yan also felt that he had placed too heavy a burden on Le Shaolong.

It wasn’t that Le Shaolong lacked the ability, but rather that possessing such a powerful force without the restraint of a blood pact would make anyone uneasy. Therefore, upon hearing that Le Shaolong had a suitable suggestion, he hurriedly urged Le Shaolong to speak quickly. Le Shaolong turned around and pulled out Xiao Zhan, Zi Ying, Nan Erming, and Feng Bao one by one, and said, “Young Master Xiao, if the Purple Heart Pill is effective, it will be the power of hundreds of five-star Dou Di.

Do you think we can divide it into five teams, with each of us leading one team, under my command? But each team member should hand over their blood pact to their respective team leader. How about that?”

When Le Shaolong made this suggestion, everyone’s eyes lit up! Especially Xiao Yan, who praised it as the best solution to the problem!

“Excellent! Excellent! This method is excellent!” Qing Haoran exclaimed three times, still feeling unsatisfied, and continued to praise, “Brother, I admire your vision.

Shaolong is truly a brilliant strategist.” Xiao Yan laughed heartily, “Shaolong, it’s settled then. You can arrange the details.” After saying that, he turned to look at Zhan Yi, who was standing to the side, looking somewhat bewildered.

Standing to the side, Zhan Yi was truly stunned by what he saw. Noticing Xiao Yan looking at him, he immediately snapped back to reality. Having found such a wise lord, he had no regrets even in death.

Zhan Yi no longer had any concerns. He clenched his fingers, and a bloodline instantly pierced through Le Shaolong’s brow, demonstrating his unparalleled loyalty to Xiao Yan through his actions. Xiao Yan looked at Zhan Yi with satisfaction, flicked his finger, and a Purple Heart Pill flew towards Zhan Yi, instantly filling the room with a rich medicinal fragrance.

Holding the tiny pill, Zhan Yi felt as if he were carrying the heaviest mountain in the world. Tears welled up in his eyes, and his heart was filled with mixed emotions. Tens of thousands of years of struggle and fighting, just when despair is about to be shattered, suddenly seeing hope—this feeling is undoubtedly wonderful, but it can also easily make one lose control.

“You can stay here in the manor. If you want to advance, you can do so here,” Xiao Yan said after Zhan Yi calmed down a bit. The Xiao family mansion had long been well-organized under Zhenni’s management.

Many hand seals were set up in the mansion, all of which were arranged by Qing Haoran at Zhenni’s request. The hand seals were set up by seven- or eight-star Dou Di, so that the Emperor Tribulation below the eight-star level could not cause any damage to the Xiao family mansion. Soul-Eating Mark(3) Zhan Yi nodded and agreed with everyone that they would undergo the tribulation in seven days…

Seven days passed in the blink of an eye. Everyone got up early and gathered in an open space in the Xiao residence, all wanting to witness the birth of a miracle. Zhan Yi sat cross-legged in the open space, keeping a great distance from the others to prevent them from being affected by the lightning tribulation.

Around Zhan Yi, Qing Haoran and the others had already jointly set up a battle qi barrier, forming a double layer of protection with the original hand seals in the Xiao residence, ensuring absolute safety. Zhan Yi smiled and nodded to Xiao Yan, then his fighting spirit soared into the sky, unleashing his full power as a peak four-star expert without reservation. His fighting spirit soared like a rainbow; once the energy accumulated over many years exploded, its power was unimaginable, instantly breaking through the bottleneck of the five-star level.

As soon as the bottleneck was broken, a strange change occurred in the sky. Lightning flashed everywhere, and churning dark clouds covered the entire sky above the Xiao residence. The muffled thunder grew louder and louder, and the four thunder demons of heaven, earth, black, and yellow descended from the sky with indifferent expressions, as if they were going to eliminate the challenger on behalf of heaven and earth.

Facing the Thunder Demon, Zhan Yi stood up and fearlessly met it head-on, easily destroying the Thunder Demon that descended from the sky amidst a series of “booms”. Having easily passed the first tribulation of the Emperor’s Tribulation, Zhan Yi gazed at the darkening sky, but instead of feeling relieved, his expression grew increasingly solemn. For the second tribulation, the Tribulation of Ten Thousand Thunderbolts, was about to begin—that would be the true test!

Above the sky, the darkness grew thicker, like ink being poured onto the heavens, flowing down and covering the entire world. The surroundings were deathly silent, so oppressive that it was almost impossible to breathe.

Suddenly, a huge crack appeared in the sky, and silver snakes appeared and disappeared in the darkness, as thousands of thunderbolts descended! Lightning illuminated the entire Xiao residence, followed by a deafening roar of thunder, and countless bolts of lightning crackled and clattered down. One calamity after another struck.

The dazzling blue light of lightning flashed past rapidly, followed by the deafening roar of thunder. Amidst the thunder and lightning, Zhan Yi made a series of attacks, shattering one bolt of lightning after another. His fighting spirit collided with the lightning in the air, creating countless sparks.

Blood seeped from the corner of Zhan Yi’s mouth, flowing down his cheek and soaking his chest. Zhan Yi is no ordinary four-star peak Dou Di. It can be said that he has accumulated enough to reach the peak of four-star that there is no way to improve further.

Nearly 50,000 years of condensation has made him stand at the absolute peak of four-star! Even such a peak four-star Dou Di was seriously injured by just the first four lightning bolts in the face of the five-star Emperor Tribulation’s myriad lightning bolts! Zhan Yi hurriedly stuffed various healing and recovery pills into his mouth, trying to quickly restore his fighting spirit.

However, the Emperor’s Tribulation is merciless, and the Five-Star Emperor’s Tribulation is especially merciless! Zhan Yi had just recovered less than 50% of his injuries and fighting spirit when the dark night sky solidified into something almost tangible. A dazzling and astonishing flash of light pierced through the darkness, shattering the sky into nothingness.

The fifth lightning tribulation, known as the Tribulation of Death, had descended! A devastating thunderstorm blanketed the entire sky, and the whole world was filled with the power of thunder. Before the lightning even arrived, the powerful aftershocks of energy had already shattered the ground beneath everyone’s feet, creating countless cracks.

The surrounding barrier swayed precariously like a typhoon, and Zhan Yi could barely stand in the wind, his body already struggling to withstand the onslaught, his face turning deathly pale. That’s incredible! The aftershocks of that energy alone are so powerful!

If lightning were to strike, one can imagine the energy—far beyond what a four-star Dou Di could withstand! How could they possibly withstand such divine might? The crowd standing on the periphery couldn’t help but worry for Zhan Yi.

“Zi Ying, what would happen if you were to withstand this lightning bolt now?” Xiao Yan asked Zi Ying, who was standing to the side, because Zi Ying was the weakest in terms of physical strength among the five-star Dou Di. Zi Ying shook her head.

“He won’t die, but he’ll definitely be seriously injured, very seriously injured.”

“Xiao Zhan, what about you?” Xiao Yan asked Xiao Zhan, who was the strongest among them. With Xiao Zhan’s six-star demon race Dou Di status, it should be no problem for him.

“It’s definitely not difficult, but you’ll likely get some minor injuries.” Upon hearing Xiao Zhan’s words, Xiao Yan gasped. Even a six-star Dou Di from the demon race would suffer minor injuries under this fifth lightning tribulation.

This fifth lightning was indeed too abnormal, completely an insurmountable obstacle deliberately set by Heaven to prevent the advancement to a high-level Dou Di! Now, it’s clear that Zhan Yi can’t survive this ordeal on his own; it all depends on the Purple Heart Pill. Xiao Yan is both hopeful and worried for Zhan Yi.

Just as the fifth bolt of lightning was about to strike, Zhan Yi took out the Purple Heart Pill. He looked at the small pill in his hand, then looked up at the fifth bolt of lightning, which was filled with the wrath of the heavens and was about to strike. Without further hesitation, he flipped his hand and swallowed the Purple Heart Pill.

The Purple Heart Pill melted instantly upon entering his mouth, but Zhan Yi’s power did not surge at all, and even his aura remained unchanged. Could it be that the Purple Heart Pill is ineffective? Xiao Yan’s eyes widened in disbelief.

It’s a fifth-grade pill after all; the real pill lightning can’t be faked. How could there be no reaction at all? Xiao Yan roared inwardly, staring intently at Zhan Yi in the arena.

Le Shaolong clenched his hands, biting his lips so hard that blood trickled down his lips, feeling immense self-reproach. Zhan Yi was introduced by Le Shaolong and is Le Shaolong’s close friend. If Zhan Yi dies because the Purple Heart Pill is ineffective, Le Shaolong will never forgive himself for the rest of his life.

However, a pleasant surprise awaited them. Just as everyone was filled with doubt and the fifth bolt of lightning was about to strike Zhan Yi’s head, purple ripples spread out from Zhan Yi’s body and quickly merged into the sky. A miraculous scene unfolded.

Wherever the purple ripples passed, the energy of the fifth lightning bolt quickly dissipated into the sky, as if some mysterious force was absorbing the lightning or transferring its power. The lightning weakened at a visible rate, and its overwhelming power was constantly being eroded. Zhan Yi broke out in a cold sweat, and his despair turned into joy.

He roared to the sky, and his enormous battle qi energy surged in the air like a fountain, turning into a battle blade that swirled and intertwined in the air to form a blue sea of blades, slashing at the lightning. Although the lightning had weakened, it was still not to be underestimated. The powerful energy struck Zhan Yi, causing his head to stand on end and his entire body to be charred black, but it was no longer fatal.

Zhan Yi was overjoyed. He hadn’t expected that the Purple Heart Pill would have such unpredictable power, and that it could really weaken the fifth lightning bolt. His battle blade suddenly intensified, attacking fiercely like a storm.

He unleashed almost all of his fighting techniques, carrying all the remaining fighting energy in his body, and slammed it down on the lightning.

“Boom!” The deafening roar lasted only a moment before the sky returned to its usual tranquility, as if nothing had ever happened. Only the rich energy of heaven and earth enveloped Zhan Yi’s body, rapidly repairing his damaged form, proving that the fifth bolt of lightning had indeed struck.

Zhan Yi’s Five Star Emperor Tribulation is over! With the help of a Purple Heart Pill, Zhan Yi survived the ordeal of death! The Xiao family was in an uproar.

Xiao Yan’s eyes were filled with joy; Le Shaolong breathed a sigh of relief “Thank you for your life-saving grace, Young Master Xiao.” Zhan Yi descended from mid-air and immediately knelt before Xiao Yan. His shoulders trembled slightly with excitement, and the aura of a five-star Dou Di stirred the emotions of everyone present.

“Get up, get up quickly.” Xiao Yan hurriedly helped Zhan Yi up.

“Quickly check if he feels unwell.” Xiao Yan was worried that this miraculous pill might have some side effects, so he hurriedly asked Zhan Yi.

“No. If I had to say something, it would be that my whole body is filled with power.” Zhan Yi carefully sensed every part of his body, and after confirming that there was no discomfort, he suppressed his barely suppressed excitement and answered respectfully.

“That’s fantastic! The Purple Heart Pill is so miraculous, and it doesn’t even have any side effects!” Xiao Yan couldn’t help but burst into laughter, his laughter infectious to everyone present.

“Brother, congratulations. You’ve shown me a miracle. I’m so proud to have a brother like you.

Hahahaha.” Qing Haoran laughed heartily, even playfully giving Xiao Yan a thumbs up. Jenny’s eyes sparkled as she gazed at Xiao Yan, seemingly unable to get enough of him. Her face was full of satisfaction, and upon closer inspection, it was happiness.

Xiao Yao was already in tears, unable to utter a single word.

“Young Master Xiao, I think if I were to suddenly die one day, it wouldn’t be because I was killed, but because I was shocked to death by you.” Xiao Zhan ran over and patted Xiao Yan’s shoulder jokingly.

“Impossible, that will only thicken your skin.” Nan Erming smiled and continued to tease Xiao Zhan, eliciting laughter from the crowd.

“Little liar, your pills are really amazing! Remember to help me make one that can directly advance from five-star to six-star!” Qing Mu’er’s eyes curved into crescent moons with laughter, but she still didn’t forget to tease Xiao Yan.

Xiao Yan was speechless and had a headache looking at Qing Mu’er.

“Such a joyous occasion, shouldn’t we offer our congratulations?” Zi Ying exclaimed, trying to help Xiao Yan out of his predicament.

“Yes, yes, it’s rare for the Xiao clan to rise in my lifetime. We should not only celebrate, but I’m going to get drunk today.” Xiao Yao’s face was still wet with tears, but his wrinkles turned into a blooming peony when he smiled. Xiao Yao’s greatest wish in life was to revitalize the Xiao clan.

He had already given up hope, but in a short time he saw hope again. No, not hope, but something that was about to become a reality. How could he not be overjoyed?

After saying that, he pulled Xiao Yan up, called to the others, and left. He hummed a little tune as he walked, gesturing wildly like a child. Everyone wanted to laugh, but they held back; after all, he was Xiao Yan’s ancestor, the patriarch of the Xiao clan.

． ． No one knows how much they drank, but everyone was a little tipsy. Xiao Yan, his eyes blurry with drunkenness, leaned back in his chair. Looking around, he saw Xiao Yao slumped over the table.

“I can’t even remember the last time I was this happy. The future of the Xiao Clan is promising. I may be powerless, but I am still gratified.”

Xiao Yao’s low murmur reached Xiao Yan’s ears. Upon hearing this, Xiao Yan shook his head slightly, rested his hands on his head, and looked somewhat dazed. It seems that Xiao Yao has had a very difficult time these past few years.

He is trying to escape, but his heart is with the Xiao clan. I don’t know how Xiao Yao has managed to get through this double torment. All of this was due to one damned reason: Xiao Yao’s strength was inexplicably declining day by day.

Thinking of this, Xiao Yan stood up with a start. Although Xiao Yao no longer cared as much about his strength as before, since Xiao Yan had made a promise, he had to help Xiao Yao resolve this matter so that the clan leader, who had been wandering abroad, could return to the Xiao clan in high spirits.

“Brother, I have a question I’d like to ask you.” Xiao Yan asked Qing Haoran, hoping that with the Demon Clan’s extensive knowledge over the years and Qing Haoran’s status as the Demon Emperor, he might have some understanding of this situation.

However, after listening to Xiao Yan’s detailed explanation, Qing Haoran’s brows furrowed even more tightly than Xiao Yan’s, and his lips appeared somewhat ashen. Xiao Yan’s heart leaped into his throat. Could it be that even his elder brother was unaware of this strange situation?

When Xiao Yan introduced Xiao Yao’s situation to Qing Haoran, Xiao Yao, who had been lying down, raised his head. The sad things he had been suppressing in his heart were mentioned, and Xiao Yao sobered up a lot. He gently stroked the wine bottle with his fingers.

“Clan Chief Xiao, did this start happening after you fought with someone?” Qing Haoran asked Xiao Yao with a solemn expression. Qing Haoran’s solemn expression made Xiao Yao, who had been looking forward to something, feel extremely heavy-hearted. He nodded sadly “Anyone in our clan who practices the Soul-Devouring Technique will be killed without mercy!” the brother and sister said in unison.

“I understand, I understand! This Soul-Devouring Mark was planted by the Soul-Devouring Technique! No wonder our ancestor ordered us to kill those who cultivate this technique without mercy; this technique is indeed too insidious,” Qing Haoran said with a hint of realization.

“Brother, you said the old Demon Emperor left you some last instructions?” Xiao Yan asked, somewhat puzzled. He remembered that when the old Demon Emperor entrusted him to deliver the Blood Demon Token to the Blood Demon Clan, there were no last instructions.

“Hehe, brother, it’s like this. Didn’t you bring me a Blood Demon Ring back then? The old Demon Emperor attached his divine sense to the Blood Demon Ring, and only those with the bloodline of the Blood Demon Clan can hear it,” Qing Haoran explained to Xiao Yan.

“Oh, I see. Since the old Demon Emperor specifically mentioned this Soul Devouring Technique, did he say there’s any way to counter it?” Xiao Yan asked unwillingly.

“No, not at all. Apart from the Blood Demon Token and the Blood Demon Ring, all the medicine prescriptions and other things are with you.” Upon hearing Qing Haoran mention the prescription left by the old Demon Emperor, Xiao Yan suddenly remembered something, and with a thought, he reached into his storage ring.

“Hahahaha! The Soul-Devouring Mark has been removed! The Soul-Devouring Mark has been removed!”

Xiao Yan suddenly burst into laughter, appearing almost manic. In the quiet night, the wind suddenly picked up, swirling fallen leaves into a flurry of motion. Everyone’s eyes lit up as if a fire had been lit in a pan, and Xiao Yao even jumped off his chair, staring blankly at Xiao Yan.

“Brother, you can solve something that seems unsolvable?” Qing Haoran seemed a little unsettled, anticipating Xiao Yan’s next explanation. Xiao Yan stopped laughing, raised his hand, and an ancient-looking prescription floated in mid-air.

Xiao Yan read the prescription word by word: “Soul Devouring Pill, can remove the Soul Devouring Mark. Fourth Grade Emperor.”

“The pill formula was given by the Old Demon Emperor.” After reading it aloud, Xiao Yan added, and then said excitedly, “I didn’t know what the Soul Devouring Mark was before. I glanced at this formula and then threw it aside.

I almost forgot about it. Fortunately, my brother mentioned the Old Demon Emperor, otherwise I really wouldn’t have remembered it for a moment.”

“Oh, right, this recipe also has the old Demon Emperor’s annotation— ‘Release it after unifying the continent, hmph!’” Xiao Yan said to Qing Haoran.

“No wonder they say this technique is unsolvable; it turns out our ancestor Emperor controlled the antidote.” Qing Haoran suddenly understood.

“Hahaha, it’s like finding a needle in a haystack!” Xiao Zhan laughed loudly. Knowing his personality, he had long been bored out of his mind in this stifling atmosphere. Jenny stood behind Xiao Yan, her faint fragrance spreading in the wind, making everyone feel happy.

Xiao Yao was stunned. This dramatic rise and fall in the blink of an eye would have driven him crazy if he hadn’t spent so many years in the martial world.

“I knew those little liars had a lot of tricks up their sleeves,” Qing Mu’er muttered, her delicate nose wrinkling up and down, looking quite adorable.

“Are the materials difficult to obtain?” Only Le Shaolong remained calm and asked the question.

“The ingredients for this pill are not rare. With the Merchant Alliance’s capabilities, it shouldn’t be difficult to acquire them on the market. Once Zhenni has purchased the materials, we can start refining it as soon as possible.” Xiao Yan shook off his previous dejection and became all smiles, looking radiant.

“No rush, no rush, come on, let’s drink first.” Xiao Yao grabbed the wine jug and gulped down several mouthfuls before his trembling hands finally steadied.

“Brother, you can rest assured now,” Qing Haoran laughed loudly.

“My wish has been fulfilled, so naturally I’m happy.” In high spirits, Xiao Yan toyed with his wine glass, his brows slightly furrowed “You look so worried, you don’t seem happy at all. Haha.

Is there something you’re worried about?” Qing Haoran asked.

“Big brother, do you remember what the War Demon Clan said about their ancestor?”

“I remember, so what?” Qing Haoran didn’t understand why Xiao Yan would bring up the matter of the War Demon Clan’s ancestor at this moment.

“Don’t you think he also looks like he’s been afflicted by the Soul-Devouring Mark, brother?”

“Yes, it is very similar.

However, his decline in strength is mainly due to a decrease in his soul power, so it’s different.” Qing Haoran said with some doubt. Upon hearing Qing Haoran’s words, Xiao Yan turned to Xiao Yao and asked, “Ancestor, after you were struck by this Soul Devouring Mark, was your soul power damaged?”

“Of course it was damaged, and it was my soul power that was damaged first. At first, I just felt that I couldn’t concentrate, and later I realized that there was a problem with my soul power, but I couldn’t find the reason, and my strength also declined day by day.” Xiao Yao knew that his “strange illness” could be cured, and his mind was at ease.

He talked about the details of his injury. After receiving confirmation from Xiao Yao, Xiao Yan and Qing Haoran exchanged a glance, basically confirming that the War Demon Clan’s ancestor had also been afflicted by the Soul Devouring Mark. Xiao Yan spoke softly, “In the coming period, I need to prepare more Purple Heart Pills.

I’ll also need to trouble you, elder brother, to look after the Xiao family. After all, Shaolong and the others don’t have much experience.”

“Brother, you’re too kind. Your business is my business, isn’t it?” Qing Haoran downed a cup, a glint of light flashing in his eyes, clearly still pondering the matter of the Soul Devouring Technique.

The Soul Devouring Technique is a demonic art. As the Demon King, Qing Haoran knew from the three words “no mercy” left by the old Demon King that this matter was of great importance.

However, he did not know where to start, so he could only put it aside for the time being and think about it later.

“Young Master Xiao, there’s no need to worry about trivial matters. I’ve got you covered,” Zhen Ni said with a smile.

“There’s something I haven’t had a chance to tell you yet. While you were refining the medicine, the Demon Emperor sent someone to deliver the Fruits of Life. The Demon Emperor is really generous, as if he was afraid you would fail, he actually sent six.

I’ve already prepared the other materials for you, but the blood of the dragon and phoenix is really hard to find, so I only prepared one set.” After saying that, she took out six Fruits of Life and a set of Rejuvenation Pill ingredients and handed them to Xiao Yan.

“Hehe, this Demon Emperor is quick to collect debts. Just one set of medicinal herbs? I have to be careful.

It would be troublesome if it were ruined, and I’d also have to waste a Life Fruit.” Xiao Yan joked, and after putting the items into his storage ring, he said to Zhen Ji, “Oh, right, there are a few more things I need to trouble you with, sister. You need to prepare the medicinal herbs for two sets of Soul Devouring Pills in the near future, and also purchase a large quantity of four-star to five-star magic cores.” As Xiao Yan spoke, he poured out all the demon cores from his storage ring.

The colorful, glittering demon cores filled the corner of the room, reflecting the faint moonlight and flowing with a dreamlike beauty. Qing Mu’er’s beautiful eyes drifted over, already shining brightly. The allure of the demon cores to the demon race was instantly revealed.

“These were all obtained in the illusion, but it’s not enough,” Xiao Yan explained, “because there are still Lang Tian, Zi Ying, and Nan Er Ming.” Upon hearing this, Zi Ying, Nan Erming, and Lang Tian, who were enjoying their drinks, simultaneously stopped mid-air with their raised glasses. The three looked at Xiao Yan, and faced with this affection that was even closer than that of blood brothers, their throats choked with emotion, and the contents of their cups seemed to weigh a ton, making them unable to move them.

Jenny’s slightly confused brows finally relaxed—so that’s how it is, no wonder Xiao Yan still felt that so many magic cores weren’t enough.

“Is there a problem with the funding?” Xiao Yan asked, sounding slightly uneasy.

“Don’t worry, with the revenue sharing from the merchant alliance, funding won’t be a problem. Not to mention four- or five-star magic cores, even six- or seven-star magic cores are no problem.” Zhenni smiled and continued, “Four-star ones are no problem, we have as many as you want, and they’re very cheap. It’s just that I’m afraid we don’t have enough five-star ones for you to buy.”

Hearing Jenny’s words, Xiao Yan nodded in relief, while Xiao Yao, who was standing next to him, almost fell off his chair. Did I hear that right? A single five-star magic core is already incredibly valuable, and this kid Xiao Yan wants to buy them in bulk?

And he’s even worried he won’t have enough to buy? How many does he want to buy? Just how much wealth does this kid have?

“Luckily, this kid is from my Xiao clan.” Xiao Yao thought to himself with relief, but he didn’t ask any more questions. He slowly became numb to it, just like everyone else.

“Then I’ll have to trouble you, sister,” Xiao Yan said.

“Why are you being so polite with me…” Zhen Ni moved gracefully, and as she passed by Xiao Yan, she suddenly whispered, her breath sweet as orchids, and Xiao Yan’s bones immediately went weak.

“I need to refine the Purple Heart Pill during this time, so you can divide these demon cores among yourselves,” Xiao Yan said to Zi Ying, Nan Erming, and Lang Tian, pointing to the pile of demon cores in the corner of the room.

“I’ll say it again, Zi Ying needs it more than I do, and Lang Tian needs to improve to help Young Master Xiao refine medicine. You two can split it.” Nan Erming leaned back in his chair and directly chose to abstain.

“Let’s divide it as Nan Erming suggested. Nan Erming, please bear with it for now.” Nan Erming’s words made a lot of sense, and Xiao Yan spoke up when he saw that Zi Ying and Lang Tian wanted to refuse.

“Hehe, what’s there to feel aggrieved about? They’ll let me win next time. I’m just a step behind,” Nan Erming laughed.

“Nan Erming, thank you.” Lang Tian and Zi Ying didn’t refuse, and put away the magic core in the corner of the room, their eyes shining with a fiery light. With enough wealth, it turns out that it’s that simple for the demon race to increase their strength!

Le Shaolong, Xiao Zhan, Feng Bao, and Zhan Yi were extremely envious, regretting that they weren’t demons. They all sighed and complained, which made everyone laugh.

“You little liar, why wasn’t there any for me?” Qing Mu’er protested, pouting her little lips.

“Mu’er, stop making a scene,” Qing Haoran shouted, a flash of anger crossing his face.

“Mu’er, you don’t need to go out for a bloody battle and adventure for the time being, so you should take care of the others first. After Zhenni buys the magic cores, you’ll definitely get your share,” Xiao Yan said with a wry smile.

“Then you didn’t include me just now.” After Qing Haoran spoke, Qing Mu’er’s voice lowered considerably, but she was still somewhat indignant, her little face full of grievance, making her look pitiful to everyone. In fact, now that the Blood Demon Clan has participated in the refining of the Blood Qi Pill, their wealth is far greater than before.

Qing Mu’er doesn’t covet the demon core; she’s just upset that Xiao Yan miscalculated her, that’s all. Jenny, seeing all this, knew exactly what Qing Mu’er was thinking. She sighed inwardly and walked over, patting her forehead in self-reproach: “Look at my memory!

Young Master Xiao told me before that I must prepare some magic cores for Mu’er after we leave the illusion. Look, I’ve been so busy that I forgot.”

“Really? Did the little liar really say that?” Qing Mu’er turned to look at Xiao Yan, her face full of anticipation. Xiao Yan’s eyes were full of confusion.

He was about to say something when Zhen Ni gave him a look that stopped him. Xiao Yan immediately understood and nodded awkwardly.

“Well, since you still have some conscience towards me, I won’t pursue this matter this time,” Qing Mu’er smiled sweetly.

“I think Xiao Shao’s expression is a bit too forced,” Xiao Zhan whispered to Feng Bao. Everyone was discerning; how could they not see it? Only Qing Mu’er, having received affirmation from her beloved, had long since overlooked everything else.

Xiao Yan secretly wiped away a cold sweat; the feelings between men and women!

“As for the candidates to take the Purple Heart Pill, you each go and find them yourselves, with Le Shaolong in full charge,” Xiao Yan said, coming back to his senses.

“Young Master Xiao, please rest assured, we will certainly live up to your expectations.” Le Shaolong and his group clasped their hands in a slight fist salute and replied solemnly… The next morning, as the first rays of sunlight filtered through the lush trees of the Xiao residence and fell upon the ground, Xiao Yan was already up and heading towards the alchemy room.

With Le Shaolong and the others already preparing for the selection of new members for the Xiao clan’s new power, the refining of the Purple Heart Pill needed to be expedited, and Xiao Yan naturally wouldn’t delay. As for the Soul-Devouring Pill, it’s only a fourth-grade pill. Once Zhenni has procured the materials, refining it will be a piece of cake.

As for the Rejuvenating Pill, which only requires one set of ingredients, Xiao Yan is confident that with his current alchemy skills and soul power, he can succeed on his first try without any problem. Long Yi followed Xiao Yan into the alchemy room, and as the door closed tightly, the two began a long period of secluded alchemy training.

Jenny had to handle many trivial matters between the Merchant Alliance and the Xiao family; Le Shaolong, Xiao Zhan, Nan Erming, and Feng Bao were busy looking for suitable candidates; Zi Ying and Lang Tian had long since gone into seclusion to advance their cultivation levels with a bunch of magic cores… The Xiao family was quiet
