Chapter 76: The Girl in Red and the Jade Pendant

Feng Bao, Xiao Zhan, and Nan Erming were lounging on the railing of the small pavilion in the Xiao family garden, idly playing with their teacups. A cool breeze occasionally swept across the pavilion, carrying away the warmth from the cup and leaving behind a touch of loneliness.

“Shaolong took Zhanyi to look for candidates, and the Demon Emperor and Miss Mu’er are also busy investigating the Soul Devouring Mark. There are only a few of us idle in this huge Xiao Mansion. Sigh.”

Feng Bao’s gaze fell on the swaying branches in the garden, looking somewhat bored.

“I’d like to look for someone, but I can’t think of anyone suitable,” Nan Erming said, staring blankly at the blue-and-white rim of the teacup.

“Who says otherwise? I’ve been racking my brains these past two days. As for suitable candidates, they shouldn’t be below the peak of four stars.

Even at the peak of four stars, I’m not entirely confident myself, so I dare not easily select anyone,” Storm sighed.

“Having gotten used to the life of constant danger and fighting, suddenly having so much free time is really hard to adjust to,” Xiao Zhan grumbled.

“How about we go out for a stroll?” Xiao Zhan couldn’t stand the leisurely days any longer and looked at the two of them with questioning eyes.

“I’d better go cultivate, or they’ll catch up with me.” Storm glanced in the direction where Purple Shadow and Wave Sky were in seclusion, his competitive spirit rising.

“You just escaped the illusion and went straight to cultivation? Aren’t you tired?” Nan Erming put down his teacup and said with a smile.

“I don’t have your pretty face that makes women jealous. Going out with you is less appealing than cultivating.” Storm rolled his eyes.

“I’m not afraid, I’m thick-skinned.” Xiao Zhan could tell that Feng Bao had no intention of going out for a stroll, and fearing that Nan Erming wouldn’t accompany him either, he quickly stuck to Nan Erming.

“It’s not my fault that I’m handsome,” Nan Erming said with a wry smile.

“You can go out together, but it’s wrong of you to try and attack us.” Storm stood up and laughed loudly.

“I’m going to cultivate. You guys have fun.” The storm departed without a trace, leaving only two people in the pavilion.

Nan Erming gazed at the peach blossoms and willows in the garden, slowly sipping his tea, feeling quite content. Xiao Zhan, being an active person, couldn’t sit still and paced back and forth in the pavilion, occasionally blocking Nan Erming’s view. Nan Erming’s brows furrowed slightly.

“Can’t you be quiet for a second?” Nan Erming’s expression was somewhat impatient.

“You’re a man, why are you sitting here like a woman, sipping tea and admiring the scenery? Come with me for a walk.” Xiao Zhan couldn’t hold back any longer and grabbed Nan Erming’s arm, pulling him outside.

Looking at Xiao Zhan’s almost pleading expression, Nan Erming hesitated for a moment, then touched his nose and nodded… The Demon District of Juxi City is a very prosperous and prime location. The roads are bustling with carriages and people, and all kinds of shops line both sides of the street, offering a wide variety of goods.

The two wandered aimlessly, looking around.

“It’s kind of weird that the two of us men are out shopping without any particular purpose,” Xiao Zhan muttered, looking at Nan Erming who seemed indifferent to everything.

“It was your terrible idea that led to this.” Nan’er understood and glanced at Xiao Zhan.

“How about we go to the auction house?” Xiao Zhan suggested.

“Why go there? The things you want to buy you can’t afford, and the things you can afford you don’t need.”

“Then let’s check out Taobao Marketplace, maybe we’ll find a pleasant surprise.”

“Do you think we’re discerning people? We’d be lucky if we weren’t fooled.”

“Then where should we go?” Nan Erming’s words made perfect sense, but wandering around aimlessly seemed even more boring than drinking tea at home.

However, Xiao Zhan really didn’t know where to go, which made him very frustrated.

“How should I know? You were the one who suggested going shopping.”

“I just wanted to get out and clear my head.”

“Now that you’ve calmed down, let’s go back.”

“Go back and keep staring at those flowers and plants?” Xiao Zhan asked with a bitter expression.

“It’s better than just wandering around here aimlessly, right?”… When the two stopped arguing, they found themselves out of the bustling city center and into a small alley. The alley was deserted, and the walls, which had been in disrepair for years, were even a bit dilapidated, a complete contrast to the bustling scene just moments before.

The doors of each house were tightly shut, with only one small shop struggling to stay afloat. On the old counter were some strange and unusual items, and an old cloth curtain had faded to a grayish-white color. Such an inconspicuous little shop should not have attracted their attention, but a woman in red standing in front of the counter caught their eye.

The woman was very young, with a strength of only a three-star Dou Di. Her long hair was casually draped over her back, and her red dress fluttered in the wind. A pink ribbon was used to tie her waist.

Although her appearance was not stunning, her skin was as white as snow, and she had a special temperament that made people feel friendly. She stood there, like a floating red cloud. The woman’s clothes were brand new, creating a stark contrast with the dilapidated state of the small shop.

Her delicate hands lightly touched the items on the counter, her movements so natural and casual that they didn’t seem out of place at all. Behind the woman stood two middle-aged men, who appeared to be at the early stage of the Five-Star Dou Di realm. They had indifferent expressions and stood closely guarding the girl, presumably as her bodyguards.

There was only one old man in the small shop. His sickly pale face had a sparse beard, and his triangular eyes were fixed on the girl.

However, his eyes would occasionally dart behind her, as if he was somewhat wary.

“This isn’t a place for this girl.” A look of surprise flashed across Xiao Zhan’s face. Not far away, the area was bustling with activity, offering all sorts of goods—a true shopping paradise.

Why would this girl appear in this dilapidated alley? This puzzled Xiao Zhan greatly.

“Perhaps she enjoys seeking tranquility amidst the city’s hustle and bustle,” Nan Erming smiled faintly.

“However, the fact that she has two five-star Dou Di as her bodyguards suggests that this young woman’s identity is not simple.”

“They seem to be members of the demon race.” Nan Erming was extremely observant; even from such a distance, he noticed a layer of tiny scales beneath the foreheads of several people.

“But I’ve never seen their clothing before. It seems they are of demon lineage, but not from a nearby force.” As the two were speculating, the girl in red picked up half of a jade pendant from the table and examined it closely, then held it up to the sunlight.

Under the sunlight, a hazy purple glow seemed to flow within the jade pendant, giving it a dreamlike quality. The girl in red fixed her gaze on the half of the jade pendant, examining it carefully. The old man glanced at the girl, then at the guards behind her, his eyes flickering slightly.

“Young lady, you have a good eye. You spotted the best item in our shop at a glance,” the old man said with a chuckle. As he spoke, his gaze subtly swept over the extremely expensive silk clothes on the girl’s body.

He looked around, his eyes lingering on Xiao Zhan and Nan Erming for a moment, as if he had some reservations.

“Let’s go.” Xiao Zhan pulled Nan Erming forward and turned into a side road.

“There’s something wrong with this old man.” Nan Erming frowned, but didn’t say anything more.

“Yeah, I think so too. Why don’t we take a look?” Xiao Zhan’s curiosity was piqued.

“What’s so interesting about this? It’s better to mind your own business.” Since it didn’t concern him, Nan Erming didn’t intend to get involved.

“Since I’m just killing time anyway, it’s better than wandering around aimlessly, right?” Whether out of excessive curiosity or boredom, Xiao Zhan was determined to find out what was going on. Unable to resist Xiao Zhan’s persistent pleading, Nan Erming was pulled to a secluded corner on the rooftop by Xiao Zhan.

He held his breath and secretly glanced towards the small shop. Seeing that no one was around, the old man casually asked, “Your attire is quite unique; I’ve never seen anything like it before. Is this your first time visiting Juxi City?”

“Yes, this is my first time here.” The girl in red seemed to have no guile; she casually answered while examining the jade pendant. The girl in red examined the jade pendant against the light, her eyes darting around as she thought for a moment. Then, she held the pendant in her hand, stroking it gently.

A thought struck her, and she secretly channeled a wisp of battle qi into it The jade pendant trembled slightly in the girl’s palm before returning to calm. But that slight tremor stirred up a storm in the girl’s heart! Because at the moment the jade pendant trembled, she felt that the power of her bloodline had weakened somewhat, as if it was somewhat afraid of the trembling aura of the jade pendant.

This is simply unbelievable! You must know that my bloodline is exceptionally noble, yet this unremarkable jade pendant has caused my bloodline power to weaken somewhat. This jade pendant is definitely not simple; it might even be something else entirely.

The girl in red recalled a legend from her tribe, and her heart immediately filled with excitement. If the legend was true, then the value of this jade pendant would be immeasurable, far beyond what money could buy. The girl in red was extremely excited inside, but on the surface she remained calm and composed, still so elegant and serene.

However, Nan Erming could still sense her excitement from the slight trembling of her jade-like hands.

“Make an offer.” The girl in red smiled slightly, revealing adorable dimples on her small face. She seemed a little impatient, as her tone when asking the price carried a hint of determination.

“To be honest, I really can’t bear to sell this. It’s an incredible opportunity. Alas, if it weren’t for the fact that I’m nearing the end of my lifespan and in a hurry to prepare for the Five-Star Emperor Tribulation, I would never have sold this.”

“Are you going to sell or not?” The girl in red blinked her long eyelashes, pretending to be nonchalant.

“Sell, sell, of course I’ll sell. How can I not sell when I’m in business?” the old man quickly replied.

“I’m getting old, so I’m bound to be a bit long-winded. I hope you won’t mind, Miss.”

“Just tell me if you’re selling, how much?” The girl in red was very straightforward. The old man held up five fingers and waved them in front of the group.

“Five hundred thousand?”

“Yes, five hundred thousand. Miss, that’s already very cheap. Opportunities can’t be bought with money.

If it weren’t for my own…”

“You’ve already said that, no need to repeat yourself.” Seeing that the old man was about to ramble on again, the girl in red interrupted him sharply, secretly pleased, and continued to ask expressionlessly, “Five hundred thousand for just this half a piece?”

“Yes, yes, this is a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity, not everyone can encounter it, young lady is truly blessed.”

“Never mind then, you can keep it yourself.” Upon hearing that there was only half a piece, the girl in red’s initial excitement immediately cooled down.

“Don’t be impatient, young lady. Although it’s only half a dollar, it means you already have a 50% chance, right? How about this, I’ll give you a 20% discount, 400,000, this is the lowest price I can offer, absolutely no lower,” the old man said hurriedly.

“It’s not about the price. What use would I have for half a jade pendant? Where am I supposed to find the other half?”

Holding half of the jade pendant, the girl in red looked somewhat dejected. It turned out she wasn’t trying to haggle, but simply felt the jade pendant was incomplete. The old man chuckled twice and lowered his voice, saying, “Young lady, you want to buy the whole thing?

Hehe, to be honest, this jade pendant was obtained by a friend and me during an adventure. It’s said to be the key to unlocking a secret realm, but my strength is too weak to risk it easily. So, I divided the pendant in two, with my friend keeping one half each, intending to find a good buyer to sell it for a good price to prepare for the Five-Star Emperor Tribulation.

Therefore, I only have this half here; the other half is with my friend.”

“Then please have your friend bring the other half, and I’ll buy it together. We can calculate the price together.” Upon hearing that there was another half, and that it was in the old man’s friend’s hands, the girl in red’s heart, which had just cooled down, immediately warmed up again.

Seeing that the girl in red didn’t care about the price at all, the old man swallowed hard a few times and hurriedly said, “Well, how about this, Miss, you can buy my half first, and then I’ll take you to my friend’s place to buy the other half. What do you think?”

“Why should I go with you to your friend’s place? Why don’t you just ask him to bring the other half of the jade pendant here? I’ll wait here.

Or do you only have half and are trying to deceive me?” The girl in red’s face darkened, her expression one of disbelief. Upon hearing the girl in red’s words, a glint of light flashed in the old man’s eyes, but his expression appeared extremely troubled, as if he were struggling internally.

After a long pause, he finally spoke: “Miss, how could I dare to lie to you? It’s just that my friend has an extremely eccentric temper and is very stubborn. When I was discussing selling this jade pendant with him, I spent more than half a month persuading him before he finally agreed.

However, he also made a request: he would only sell it to someone who was destined to be with him.”

“Then how can I tell if I’m destined to be with him?” the girl in red asked, half believing and half doubting. Upon hearing this, the old man’s expression immediately became quite complex, seemingly both amused and exasperated. He shook his head and said, “In my friend’s view, ’fate’ means someone who catches his eye.

He also emphasized that if someone isn’t meant to be, even if mountains of gold and silver were piled up in front of him, he would absolutely not sell! Many people have asked about my half piece, but like the young lady, they all want to buy the whole piece. I’ve gone to my friend’s place, but none of them have caught his eye, and he simply won’t sell to them!

Alas, I’m anxious too, but what can I do?” The old man sighed and complained, as if blaming his friend for making it impossible for him to sell his half of the jade pendant. The girl in red had initially been somewhat skeptical, but after hearing what the old man said, she began to believe him. She was getting anxious; who knew how to please the old man’s friend?

“What should we do?” Anxiety breeds confusion, and the girl in red began to worry about not being able to buy the complete jade pendant. She couldn’t help but ask, “Where does your friend live?”

“It’s located not far outside the city; it only takes the time it takes for an incense stick to burn to get there.”

“Outside the city? Not far? Then why don’t you call him over so we can trade here?” the girl in red asked doubtfully.

“Alas, Miss, you may not know this, but my friend was once publicly humiliated in this city. He couldn’t afford to offend the person, so he has been living in seclusion outside the city ever since. Moreover, he swore a solemn oath never to enter this city until he avenged that day.

Otherwise, I would probably have sold this half of the jade pendant long ago.” The old man’s eyes welled up with tears as he spoke of his friend’s sad story.

“I have told you the whole story, Miss. Whether you want to try your luck with my friend is up to you.”

“Miss, you mustn’t!” One of the guards who had been standing behind the girl in red hurriedly spoke up to dissuade her “Miss, please think it over,” another person advised. They seemed very cautious and extremely unwilling to let the girl in red leave the city and risk her life.

“It’s always good to be wary of others, I know that too.” Seeing the red-clad girl hesitate slightly after the two guards tried to dissuade her, the old man interjected, “But if anyone should be worried, it should be me. You have two high-level Dou Di guards, while I’m only a peak four-star.

If we leave the city, I’m worried you’ll rob me! If I didn’t desperately need the money, I wouldn’t be willing to take that risk.”

“Moreover, I saw that Miss has an extraordinary temperament and excellent judgment, so I must come from a wealthy family. That’s why I dared to invite Miss to meet my friend. If it were anyone else, I would rather not do this business than put myself and my friend in danger,” the old man said, sounding slightly aggrieved.

The girl in red looked at the old man’s sincere, even somewhat aggrieved, expression, then looked down at the half-jade pendant in her hand. She froze on the spot, her hesitation tinged with deep reluctance.

“Forget it, you’re not willing to buy half a piece; you’re not willing to come with me to see my friend; and you’re worried about the danger. Let’s not do this deal.” The old man waited for a while, and seeing that the girl in red still hadn’t decided, he said impatiently, his tone full of disdain, and reached out to take back the jade pendant from the girl in red.

“I’ll go!” That disdain seemed to have pierced the heart of the girl in red, and the jade pendant was too tempting for her, so the girl made a decision immediately.

“Miss!” The two guards spoke up to stop her at the same time. As they were responsible for protecting her, they naturally did not want to let the young lady take such a risk.

A sinister smile crept across the old man’s lips.

“Miss, I think we should postpone this business deal to avoid putting you in a difficult position.”

“Who’s in charge here?” The girl in red was still too young to bear being looked down upon, and since it concerned the integrity of the jade pendant, she couldn’t let it go. Her tone suddenly became more forceful. The two guards opened their mouths, as if to say something, but ultimately swallowed their words in resignation.

“Since Miss is determined to go, I will take you there. First of all, congratulations on your upcoming great opportunity. It will surely be a great achievement for your family.”

The old man was adept at understanding the other party’s psychology and seized the opportunity to flatter the girl in red.

“Let’s go,” the girl in red urged. The old man closed the shop and led the girl in red out of the city. ． “Judging from the demeanor of this girl in red, she must be from some powerful force, but she’s just too naive,” Xiao Zhan said, patting Nan Erming on the shoulder.

“That jade pendant must be extraordinary. Although the young girl is naive, she definitely has a discerning eye, otherwise she wouldn’t have insisted on it so much.

However, the idea of buying such a rare opportunity for a few hundred thousand seems suspicious no matter how you look at it,” Nan Erming said.

“Let’s follow them and see.” Xiao Zhan pulled Nan Erming along and followed the group. The old man and the girl in red quickly left the city and flew towards the forest in the southwest.

Xiao Zhan and Nan Erming followed closely, controlling their auras to prevent the other party from noticing. After flying only a few thousand miles, Nan Erming noticed something was wrong: although they were heading in one direction, the route the old man was leading them took was winding left and right, sometimes forward and sometimes backward, and it took them a full half day to finally enter a small valley in the dense forest with great care. There was a small thatched hut in the valley.

The grass on the roof was dry and yellowish, but upon closer inspection, there was a lot of green mixed in with the grass. It was obviously newly built and the grass was dried with heat to make it look like an old thatched hut that looked quite old.

“The girl in red and the others are in danger.” Nan Erming and Xiao Zhan are both Dou Di who have fought their way to the top. How could they not see such an obvious flaw?

“Those two guards are complete idiots! Five-star Dou Di, huh? They must have grown up under the protection of their families!

Damn it, sending them to protect this girl is no different from sending them to their deaths.” Nan Erming couldn’t help but curse inwardly.

“Judging from your words, it seems you’re somewhat interested in this girl in red,” Xiao Zhan teased, rarely seeing Nan Erming’s emotions fluctuate.

“Nonsense,” Nan Erming hissed, but his eyes inexplicably became unfocused. ． “Miss, this is it,” the old man said to the girl in red, pointing to the thatched hut ahead. The girl in red nodded slightly, a fiery glint in her eyes, and led the two guards toward the thatched hut.

The door of the thatched hut suddenly opened, and two old women walked out. The old women looked at the group of people and paused for a moment, then looked at the little old man with some confusion.

“This young lady is here to buy a jade pendant,” the old man enthusiastically explained to the two elderly women. The two elderly women’s expressions softened after hearing the old man’s introduction, and they kept looking at the girl in red.

“What a beautiful young lady! Our humble abode is simple, please come in.” The two old women looked kind and gentle, giving people a very amiable feeling. Moreover, judging from the fluctuations of their fighting spirit, they were only at the early stage of the fifth star.

The two guards of the girl in red had the confidence to be fearless against any opponent of the same level. Their minds relaxed slightly, and their wary eyes softened a little Upon entering the thatched hut, an old woman asked, “Miss, are you here to buy a jade pendant?” The girl in red nodded and replied, “Yes, I wonder if Grandma would be willing to part with it?”

“Alas, it seems I can’t keep this half of the jade pendant today. Over the years, many people have come to see it, but you, young lady, are the one I find most pleasing. Alright, take a look, just don’t overcharge me.”

With that, the old woman took out half of the jade pendant from her bosom and carefully handed it over. The girl in red took the half of the jade pendant from the old woman and the other half from the old man. She pieced the two halves together, and a soft light emanated from the jade pendant, revealing a ghost face within it.

As soon as the ghostly face appeared, the red-clad girl and the two guards were immediately drawn to it.

Just then, the two old women’s fighting spirit surged, and they simultaneously lunged forward, their four palms striking the unsuspecting guards squarely. The two guards were sent flying backward, coughing up blood. The two old women chased after them, their fists enveloped in powerful fighting spirit, and they pounded the guards’ chests repeatedly.

At the same time, the old man beside him grinned maliciously, a dagger flashing in his hand, gleaming with a ghostly blue light, clearly coated with a strong poison. He lunged forward in a few swift steps, plunging the dagger into the backs of the two guards.

In the blink of an eye, the two guards were covered in wounds, lying on the grass, spitting out mouthfuls of blood. Their faces were ashen, and the poison spread up their injured bodies, corroding their internal fighting spirit. Although they could still fight, their combat strength could no longer be maintained at the level of a five-star Dou Di.

The girl in red finally reacted. She raised her hand in the air, about to fire a signal flare.

However, with the strength of a mere three-star Dou Di, she was utterly powerless against two five-star and one peak four-star Dou Di.

As soon as the old man raised his hand, the girl in red groaned, and the metal tube in her hand fell to the ground.

“You’d better be smart, or don’t blame me for being ruthless,” the old man said with a sinister smile. The two elderly women stood guard closely beside the guard, their faces expressionless; their previous kind and gentle expressions had vanished.

“What are you going to do?” The girl in red leaned against a large tree, biting her lower lip, staring at the other party in terror. Her composure had long since vanished, and her striking red dress looked extremely out of place in the surrounding environment.

． “This girl has a very peculiar temperament. Before she loses her composure, no matter what clothes she wears, she blends in perfectly with her surroundings,” Nan Erming remarked.

“You still have the mind to say these things at a time like this?” Nan Erming’s sudden remark annoyed Xiao Zhan.

“Should we go up and help?” Xiao Zhan asked Nan Erming.

“If this girl cultivates a technique that matches her temperament, her future achievements will be limitless,” Nan Erming continued to exclaim.

“You seem to really admire that girl in red. But no matter how much you admire her, if you don’t go up and help her, she’s doomed.” Xiao Zhan, hiding in the tree, urged Nan Erming with a surge of righteousness.

“Bad guys usually give a speech after their plot succeeds, so they’re not in danger for now,” Nan Erming said calmly, staring intently at the girl in red.

“You’re talking nonsense! That’s written in the book,” Xiao Zhan said angrily, already on the verge of rushing out.

“Don’t be impulsive, I was just joking. Even if a Dou Di of the same level is seriously injured, his dying blow is still terrifying.” Nan Erming pulled Xiao Zhan back from rushing out and continued, “If it were me, if the other party was poisoned, I would do my best to delay for a while and let the poison intensify. That would save me a lot of trouble.”

Nan Erming’s mastery of poisons was unparalleled. Upon hearing this, Xiao Zhan gradually calmed down and observed the scene below. ． The two guards of the girl in red struggled to get up, blood dripping from their mouths and covering the ground.

They kept urging their fighting spirit, preparing to fight to the death.

Just then, the old man spoke.

“If you cooperate obediently, I will give you the antidote and let you go.” The girl in red glared coldly at the old man, her eyes filled with no anger, only icy chill. The two guards also showed suspicion in their eyes.

“I was just trying to rob some money because I was forced to do so while undergoing the Five-Star Emperor Tribulation. We have no grudges against each other, so there’s no need to risk our lives.” The old man glanced at the two guards, his expression turning bitter, as if he truly had a difficult reason.

The three girls in red felt a slight sense of relief upon hearing this. If the other party was truly only after money, they would give it to them. As long as they could return alive, with their background and influence, crushing the other party would be as easy as crushing three ants.

Seeing that his words had an effect, the old man, seemingly very sincerely, began to tell the three why he had chosen this path, rambling on and on, sobbing and crying, trying to erode their resolve to die. ． “Could it be that these three people really have their own difficulties and are not heinous criminals?” Even Xiao Zhan was somewhat puzzled “It’s just a delaying tactic.” Nan Erming looked at Xiao Zhan as if he were an idiot.

“The fact that he was accompanied by two five-star Dou Di guards shows that the other side is quite powerful. Do you still dare to let them leave? Unless they have a death wish.”

Xiao Zhan reluctantly withdrew the sliver of sympathy that had just arisen within him. ． “well.” As the old man sighed, the two old women suddenly threw punches, the fighting spirit generated by their punches was extremely fierce, and they slammed into the two guards on the ground.

“You’ve gone back on your word!” Enraged, the two guards rose to their feet, their hands suddenly covered in dense, shimmering silver scales, brimming with power, like venomous snakes emerging from their holes, producing a sound that could shatter stone and split metal, as they blocked the attack. The clash of fists directly triggered an explosion of poison within the two guards, causing their fighting spirit to falter and leaving them powerless to continue, their arms hanging limply.

As soon as the two guards lost their defenses, they were completely overwhelmed by the two old women’s fierce attacks and lost their fighting ability. The girl in red watched helplessly as her two guards were beaten to the ground, filled with regret for her earlier disobedience. She glared angrily at the old man, a hint of horror rising in her eyes.

“Hehe, we had no choice. It’s obvious that the power behind you is extraordinary. How could we dare let you leave?”

The old man was secretly alarmed when he saw the scales covering the arms of the two guards, and his eyes turned cold.

“We can give you everything, just don’t hurt the young lady,” the guard said, struggling to his feet.

“You’re all so naive! After you destroy the evidence, won’t everything you own still be mine?” The old man approached the three of them step by step, his expression terrifying.

The three of them were in despair. The girl in red clenched her fists, biting her lips until they bled red, and stared stubbornly at the old man. That look made Nan Erming feel a pang of heartache for no reason.

Nan Erming couldn’t explain why he suddenly had this feeling. He shook his head and said to Xiao Zhan, “It’s time for us to make our move.” Xiao Zhan took a deep breath of the cool air, his hands already clenched tightly.

The old man strode in, standing only a few feet away from the girl in red, and raised his hand amidst a ferocious laugh. Just as the girl in red slowly closed her eyes, resigned to her fate, Nan Erming activated his battle qi, lightly flicked his toe against the tree trunk, and transformed into a black shadow, rushing towards the old man. With a muffled thud, the girl in red turned deathly pale and shuddered, only to find that she was unharmed.

She suddenly opened her eyes and saw the old man’s body sweep through the air and plunge headfirst into the mud, leaving only his feet struggling outside. She was stunned, not knowing what had happened. She turned her head and saw a tall, slender young man with a dashing air standing not far away, slowly turning towards her.

Xiao Zhan had already rushed out. The Golden Howling Tiger, carrying the force of wind, slashed out at the two old women’s palms, slamming heavily onto them. Then, it stomped heavily on the ground, its body arching up like an arrow, catching up with the two old women who were still being knocked into the air.

Its fists carried sharp energy, and it threw at least a hundred punches in the blink of an eye. As Xiao Zhan landed safely and struck a pose that he felt good about himself, the two old women had already been blasted into the cliff face of the valley, their blood staining the flying dust.

“Who are you?” The two guards, who had survived the ordeal, stared at Xiao Zhan, too excited to react. Before Xiao Zhan could answer, Nan Erming’s face, which he had turned around to face, came into view of the three of them.

The girl in red’s gaze shifted from stunned to blank; she was taken aback by Nan Erming’s appearance, completely forgetting that she had just been in mortal danger.

“What a handsome man!” After a long while, the girl in red finally murmured these words, forgetting even to say thank you. Xiao Zhan was initially stunned, then immediately burst into laughter, bending over with tears welling up in his eyes.

Knowing he was a bit too handsome, Nan Erming still felt embarrassed to be praised like that by a beautiful young woman to his face, and beads of cold sweat appeared on his forehead.

“Beautiful?” Nan Erming scoffed, his face full of self-mockery.

“Girl, is that how you describe a man?”

“Ah! I’m so sorry, I’m so sorry, I really…” The girl in red panicked and became incoherent, a blush rising on her face.

“Brother, thank you for saving my life. Our young lady has never seen anyone as handsome as you, so she is understandably a little shocked.” The two guards stood up, still slightly unsteady on their feet, and clasped their hands in apology to Nan Erming Nan Erming felt a little better.

“It’s nothing.” He waved his hand, suppressing his displeasure. But when he saw Xiao Zhan leaning against a tree, laughing triumphantly, his anger flared up again.

He glared at Xiao Zhan fiercely. Xiao Zhan quickly suppressed his laughter, his face turning bright red. He turned his back to Nan Erming, but his shoulders were still twitching uncontrollably.

Nan Erming then asked the girl in red, “Are you alright?” The girl in red had recovered from her shock at Nan Erming’s “beauty,” but she still couldn’t help glancing at him a few times out of the corner of her eye, thinking to herself, how could there be such a handsome man in the world? She parted her cherry lips slightly and said, “Fortunately, you two came to my aid.

Otherwise, the consequences would have been unimaginable. I beg you two benefactors to tell me your names. I will surely repay you in the future.”

“It was nothing, please don’t mind.” Before Nan Erming could even politely decline, Xiao Zhan quickly replied, “My name is Xiao Zhan, and his name is Nan Erming.”

“Given our young lady’s status, how could we not repay such a great favor? Please leave your address, we will certainly come to express our gratitude in person in the future,” a guard said, clasping his hands in a gesture of respect, his tone brimming with obvious pride.

“No need. We just couldn’t stand what those people were doing.” Nan Erming’s tone was gentle, and his handsome face, which made women jealous, made people feel like they were bathed in a spring breeze.

“Alright, you are no longer in danger. Those three have completely lost their fighting ability. You can deal with them yourselves.

Until we meet again.” For some reason, Nan Erming felt a strange tension when facing the girl in red. He quickly gave a few instructions and wanted to leave as soon as possible.

“Wait!” Seeing Nan Erming about to leave, the girl in red suddenly called out. Looking at Nan Erming’s face, as bright as a star and with gentle eyes, her heart pounded.

A blush spread from her cheeks to her neck. She lowered her head and asked softly, “You…you really can’t leave your address?” Watching the two strands of hair on the red-clad girl’s cheeks flutter in the wind, adding a touch of alluring charm to her small face, Nan Erming felt a warmth rise in his cold heart.

He paused for a moment, then finally sighed softly.

“We were just strangers who met by chance, and now we’re gone.” Nan Erming waved his hand, pulled Xiao Zhan who was about to speak, and took a step to leave. In truth, Nan Erming’s mind was in turmoil at this moment.

He clearly felt a feeling he had never experienced before, an indescribable feeling, but this feeling made him, who was used to being indifferent, very uncomfortable. It was as if something had suddenly appeared in his own world, and he instinctively wanted to resist it. Was it just a chance encounter?

Would we never see each other again? The girl in red felt a pang of pain in her heart, a sense of loss and sadness. The girl in red nervously twisted her fingers together.

Suddenly, as if making a decision, she gritted her teeth and said, “Please stop, gentlemen. I came to Juxi City on business in a hurry and didn’t bring anything of value with me. This matter arose because of the jade pendant.

Since you two are unwilling to leave your address, I will use this jade pendant to express my gratitude for saving my life. Please do not refuse.” The girl in red tossed the jade pendant with her delicate hand, and it transformed into a streak of light, heading towards Nan Erming.

Nan Erming froze, caught the pendant, and stared blankly at the girl in red, speechless, his eyes filled with complex emotions.

“You risked your lives for this jade pendant, how can we take it away? What difference would there be between us and those three?” Xiao Zhan frowned.

“Without you, we would be dead. Perhaps we will never see each other again. If you don’t accept this, do you want me to feel guilty for the rest of my life?”

The girl in red’s words struck a chord with Xiao Zhan, leaving him speechless.

“Don’t give in anymore.” The girl in red looked at Nan Erming with a pleading gaze Nan Erming’s heart softened, and he could only nod slightly, “Then I’ll accept it without further ado.” He then clasped his hands in a fist salute, turned around, and left. After walking about a hundred meters, Nan Erming seemed to remember something, turned around and waved to the two guards. Several wisps of green energy rose from the guards and dissipated into the world.

“Thank you for expelling the poison.” The two guards’ expressions immediately improved, and they bowed repeatedly in gratitude, clasping their hands in greeting as the two departed. Watching Nan Erming’s departing figure, a trace of sadness slowly spread from the red-clad girl’s brow.

． Along the way, Nan Erming’s speed increased, almost becoming a green afterimage, as if he wanted to use speed to shake off the troubling feeling in his heart.

“Hey Nan Erming, are you interested in that girl? If you are, why didn’t you leave your address?” Xiao Zhan wasn’t good at speed and was too lazy to use the power of his gauntlets, so he was a little out of breath from chasing her.

“Don’t talk nonsense! With my icy personality, how could I easily fall for a woman I just met?” Nan Erming said with a straight face, but even he himself felt that this statement was a bit far-fetched.

“Even icebergs melt one day,” Xiao Zhan retorted.

“Say it again, and I won’t mind fighting you.” Nan Erming felt an even stronger sense of pent-up frustration, and the red figure in his mind wouldn’t go away.

“Fine, fine, I give up. Then why don’t you leave your address?” Xiao Zhan said sullenly.

“Didn’t you realize that the girl’s background is far from simple? Did you notice? Those two guards have scales growing on their arms!”

Nan Erming stopped in his tracks.

“Only a few demons can grow scales on their arms, but I suspect they aren’t demons, but rather a family of magical beasts!”

“A demon beast clan? I’ve heard they’re a terrifying and mysterious force, rarely venturing into the world. How could they possibly be here?”

“Yes, that’s just my guess, but in any case, Young Master Xiao’s power is just rising, and we don’t know their background, so we should try not to cause trouble for Young Master Xiao.”

“But didn’t we save their lives?” Xiao Zhan asked, puzzled.

“The relationships between major powers are not as simple as we imagine. To earn their respect, the most important thing is to have strength that matches theirs. Therefore, before Young Master Xiao has fully developed his power, we should try to keep a low profile,” Nan Erming said.

“Especially if it is indeed a magical beast family that can command thousands of magical beasts, their foundation is deeper than we can imagine, so we need to be even more careful.”

“So you’re just going to let this opportunity slip away? Don’t you think it’s a pity?” Xiao Zhan persisted.

“That young lady was really quite nice.”

“You really are asking for a beating, aren’t you?” Nan Erming’s eyes turned cold, making Xiao Zhan’s skin crawl.

“Alright, alright, let’s not talk about this anymore. Just don’t regret it later.” Xiao Zhan said listlessly, “Take a look at that jade pendant, I’m a little curious.” Nan Erming took out the jade pendant, put the two pieces together, and a soft light reappeared.

A ghost face clearly emerged from inside the jade pendant. Apart from a chilling, inexplicable power emanating from it, there was no other reaction.

“What happened? It’s already complete.” Xiao Zhan scratched his head, looking very disappointed.

“I don’t know either. Maybe, as they say, it’s just a key that needs to be found to open.” Nan Erming wasn’t sure either.

“The Dou Di Continent is so big, where are we going to find it? If we can’t find that place, isn’t this just a piece of useless jade?”

“It depends on luck. If it were that easy to find, wouldn’t those people have gone to find it themselves already?

However, one thing is certain: the material of this jade pendant is from ancient times. It should be a relic from before the ancient catastrophe, and it is very valuable.” Nan Erming stroked the jade pendant, which seemed to still have the warmth of the red-clothed girl’s body, and it was slightly warm.

“Take it back and let Young Master Xiao study it. Maybe it will yield some great discoveries.” Xiao Zhan’s eyes lit up as he looked at the jade pendant after Nan Erming confirmed that it was an ancient relic.

“Well, let’s go back first.” Nan Erming stared blankly behind him, a trace of regret flashing through his heart—perhaps it really was as the girl in red said, there would be no more days to meet. The two figures slowly disappeared into the forest, growing ever more distant.

Neither of them could have imagined how much the jade pendant would later help Xiao Yan. At the same time, Nan Erming never expected that he would have another encounter with the girl in red, and that second encounter would completely change Nan Erming’s life

Chapter 77: Removing the Soul-Devouring Mark

Back at the Xiao residence, the two studied the ghost-face jade pendant day and night for several days, but still made no progress.

“Perhaps only someone as lucky as Young Master Xiao can figure this out,” Xiao Zhan said helplessly, glancing at Xiao Yan’s still-closed alchemy room. Nan Erming stood by the window, gazing southwest with a hint of melancholy, his figure appearing somewhat forlorn. With nothing to do, Xiao Zhan and Nan Erming also felt bored, and followed Feng Bao’s example, throwing themselves into cultivation.

Throughout the entire Xiao residence, only Xiao Yao spent his days walking his birds and enjoying tea, seemingly living a very comfortable life.

However, he was actually quite anxious because Zhen Ni had already sent the ingredients for the Soul Devouring Pill to Xiao Yan, and he was waiting for Xiao Yan’s Soul Devouring Pill. Time flies, and spring turns to winter in the blink of an eye. The Xiao family mansion remains as peaceful as ever.

Only the occasional bursts of thunder in the sky can give people a jolt, but after a while, everyone becomes numb to it. On this day, the door to Xiao Yan’s alchemy room opened, and Xiao Yan stepped out. Long Yi had absorbed a considerable amount of fifth-grade pill lightning this time, and needed to absorb it quickly, so he directly entered the Meridian Spirit Void Tower.

At this rate of absorption, given enough time, he might break through to the mid-stage of a four-star Dou Di! This speed is terrifying! Entering the main hall, Xiao Yan leaned back in the armchair and stretched out a big yawn.

He was quite tired from refining medicine one after another. Before Xiao Yan could even take a few sips of the fragrant tea brought by the servants, news of his emergence from seclusion spread throughout the Xiao residence. Apart from Zi Ying and Lang Tian, who had just broken through and hadn’t had time to arrive yet, almost everyone rushed over, breaking the tranquility of the Xiao residence.

Xiao Yao was the first to arrive, as the Soul Devouring Pill was crucial to his recovery of strength, so he was naturally the most anxious. Before long, the main hall of the Xiao residence was filled with people. After taking their seats, they exchanged pleasantries, but all their eyes were on Xiao Yan, wanting to see if the Soul Devouring Pill was really effective against the Soul Devouring Mark.

“Brother, how was the harvest?” Qing Haoran asked. Since it concerned the antidote to the demon race’s forbidden techniques, Qing Haoran was naturally very concerned.

“As expected of our ancestors and elder brother, the Soul Devouring Pill has been successfully refined, and there are also twenty Purple Heart Pills.” Everyone was already familiar with the Purple Heart Pills, and Xiao Yan handed them directly to Le Shaolong. Then, with a flick of his wrist, a black pill appeared.

The pill was dark and had no luster. It looked very ordinary and was inconspicuous compared to other pills.

“This is the Soul-Devouring Pill?” A hint of doubt flashed in Xiao Yao’s and Qing Haoran’s eyes. They weren’t doubting whether it was a Soul Devouring Pill or not, but whether it was truly effective against the Soul Devouring Mark.

The pill was far too ordinary, so ordinary that it didn’t seem like a fourth-grade pill at all. Could such an ordinary pill really remove the supposedly incurable Soul Devouring Mark? Seeing Xiao Yao’s initial excitement and flushed face turn into slight disappointment, Xiao Yan scratched his head uneasily and said, “Some things can’t be judged by appearances alone.

When this pill was first successfully refined, I was also startled because it looked so ordinary. But upon closer inspection, I found that the pill’s radiance was restrained, containing an indescribable mysterious power. Perhaps it will be effective.”

“But we’ll have to try to find out; I’m not sure either,” Xiao Yan added. Xiao Yan’s words gave Xiao Yao a shot of adrenaline, and Xiao Yao’s breathing became rapid again. He stared intently at the Soul-Devouring Pill in Xiao Yan’s hand, his throat moving constantly as he swallowed several mouthfuls of saliva.

His cloudy old eyes shone with youthful brilliance due to excitement, tension, and impatience. Qing Haoran, on the other hand, appeared solemn and calm. In fact, it wasn’t that he wasn’t excited or nervous; he also wanted to know whether the Soul Devouring Pill would have any effect on the Soul Devouring Mark.

However, as the Demon Emperor, he didn’t show it like Xiao Yao. During Xiao Yan’s seclusion to refine pills, Qing Haoran mobilized a large number of people to investigate the Soul Devouring Mark, but in the end, they could not follow up because all the clues vaguely pointed to the Soul Demon Clan. The Soul Demon Clan and the Blood Demon Clan have always been at odds.

In recent years, due to Qing Haoran’s identity as the Demon Emperor, he has openly opposed the Blood Demon Clan, forming a considerable opposition force within the Demon Clan. Without concrete evidence, it is difficult to continue the investigation, and the trail has ended. Although the Soul Demon Clan dared not openly cultivate the Soul Devouring Technique against the world’s condemnation, their clandestine cultivation was far more difficult to guard against.

Therefore, Qing Haoran’s anticipation regarding the effectiveness of the Soul Devouring Pill on the Soul Devouring Mark was no less than Xiao Yao’s.

“Ancestor, why don’t you take the Soul Devouring Pill now? We’re all here, so we can see what’s going on, okay?” Xiao Yan looked at Xiao Yao’s burning gaze and then at Qing Haoran’s solemn expression, seemingly understanding Qing Haoran’s worries.

“Alright, I’ll take it right away!” Upon hearing Xiao Yan’s words, Xiao Yao reached out and took the pill from Xiao Yan, then popped it into his mouth. Everyone held their breath.

“Little liar, I’m a little nervous,” Qing Mu’er said softly, snuggling up next to Xiao Yan.

“Why are you so nervous?” Xiao Yan asked.

“Brother has been very worried about the Soul Devouring Mark lately. If the Soul Devouring Pill is successful, brother will be much more at ease,” Qing Mu’er said softly. Upon hearing this, Xiao Yan turned around, a thought striking him.

It seemed that Qing Mu’er, aside from being a bit mischievous, was actually quite considerate. Xiao Yao reacted at this moment, attracting everyone’s attention. Xiao Yao clenched his fists, and a faint white battle aura gradually covered his body.

Under the battle aura, black veins were faintly visible on his skin, like a large net spread out, covering his entire body and greedily absorbing the battle aura on his skin.

In the blink of an eye, it absorbed all the battle aura that Xiao Yao had just exuded from his skin, and the blackness of the veins deepened. Everyone could see that the black veins were the manifestation of the Soul Devouring Mark, which could absorb battle qi and transform it into its own source to continuously strengthen itself. What a vicious technique!

Everyone gasped in shock. As his fighting spirit vanished, Xiao Yao’s face contorted in extreme pain, veins bulging and spreading across his face, tinged with a dark hue, making him appear ferocious and terrifying. The Soul-Devouring Mark had actually seeped into Xiao Yao’s meridians and blood vessels!

The viciousness of this Soul-Devouring Mark far exceeded his imagination. Xiao Yan was startled, and his expression immediately became serious. He was vaguely worried about the effects of the Soul Devouring Pill.

Just as Xiao Yao’s face grew increasingly pale and the black veins became denser, suddenly, a mysterious power emanated from Xiao Yao’s body, and wherever the power reached, Xiao Yao’s skin turned completely black!

“What’s going on? Could it be that the Soul-Devouring Pill isn’t the antidote, but rather exacerbates the effects of the Soul-Devouring Mark?” Zhenni stood up abruptly, her eyes filled with anxiety and unease Xiao Yan’s body suddenly stiffened, his nails were clenched and embedded in his palm, and his fighting spirit burst out.

He took two steps forward, but stood still, at a loss. Xiao Yan was also somewhat bewildered at this point—“How could this happen? What should we do?”

However, no one could provide an answer. There was never any record of the Soul-Devouring Pill on the Dou Di Continent. Everyone was anxiously pacing around, calling out Xiao Yao’s name.

But Xiao Yao seemed to have fallen into a deep sleep, showing no reaction whatsoever.

“I can’t worry about that now. I’ll channel my Dou Qi into my ancestors to temporarily slow the spread of the blackness.” In a moment of inspiration, Xiao Yan decided to try using Dou Qi to see if he could force out the medicinal power of the Soul Devouring Pill.

“Young Master Xiao, wait a moment, don’t be rash.” Le Shaolong was worried that the Soul Devouring Mark would harm Xiao Yan, so he quickly spoke up to stop him.

“If the Soul Devouring Pill and the Soul Devouring Mark are from the same lineage, it’s impossible to force them out. But look closely, do you notice that the black color of the Soul Devouring Pill is somewhat different from the black color of the Soul Devouring Mark? It seems that they are not from the same origin.”

Le Shaolong was always very observant, and he promptly pointed out the anomalies he noticed. Upon hearing Le Shaolong’s suggestion, everyone looked closely and, sure enough, the black of the Soul Devouring Mark was slightly gray and mixed with a few specks of blood, while the black of the Soul Devouring Pill was very pure. The difference between the two was like the difference between water mixed with ink and the flawless night.

The latter was like the darkness before dawn, which made people feel very at ease and without a trace of evil. Xiao Yan plopped back into his chair, finally letting out a slight sigh of relief.

“Little liar, look! There’s a reaction!” Qing Mu’er’s delicate body trembled slightly as she exclaimed urgently. Wherever the pure black passed, the mixed black veins seemed to encounter fire meeting eternal ice, hissing as they quickly dodged, as if they were quite wary.

“It works! It really works!” Xiao Yan exclaimed excitedly.

“Grandpa, hang in there! You’ll definitely get better!” Seeing Xiao Yan happy, Qing Mu’er was overjoyed and cheered him on.

Before long, pure black covered Xiao Yao’s entire body, and from a distance, Xiao Yao looked like a statue carved from ebony. When pure black covered Xiao Yao’s body, the Soul Devouring Mark had nowhere to hide. Countless black veins turned into wisps of black mist and drifted in the air.

Xiao Yan’s bluish-gray heavenly fire immediately rose up, enveloping all the black energy and burning it into ashes. As a large amount of black energy dissipated, Xiao Yao’s skin gradually returned to its original color, and his declining aura began to strengthen slowly. He quickly surpassed the early stage of the fourth star and continued to rise.

“Our ancestor is finally saved! If our ancestor had perished because of the pills I, Xiao Yan, refined, I would have become a sinner for the Xiao clan for all eternity.” Xiao Yan stared intently at Xiao Yao, who was recovering, and breathed a sigh of relief.

Jenny patted her chest, her pink tongue gently licking her red lips, adding a touch of allure “Great! With the help of this divine pill, even if the Soul-Devouring Demon spreads across the Dou Di Continent, it won’t be a problem.” Qing Haoran witnessed all of this and was deeply moved.

The last trace of worry in his eyes completely disappeared, and he no longer needed to worry about the vicious Soul-Devouring Demon causing bloodshed on the Dou Di Continent.

“However, according to the late emperor’s instructions, all those who cultivate this skill must be killed without mercy.” Qing Haoran suppressed his anger and said firmly in a deep voice.

“Brother, count me in! Such a vicious technique must not exist in this world!” Xiao Yan stood up, took out another Soul Devouring Pill, and handed it to Qing Haoran.

“Brother, take this one and give it to the War Demon Clan.”

“Mm.” Qing Haoran patted Xiao Yan’s shoulder, expressing his gratitude.

“Hoo…” With a long breath, Xiao Yao slowly opened his eyes.

“I feel that weakness from before is gone, and the lost battle energy is slowly returning. This feeling is wonderful!” Xiao Yao’s body trembled violently, and his excitement was uncontrollable.

He almost wanted to shout it out! The repression of thousands of years was simply too heavy. Seeing the old man’s cloudy eyes regain their luster, still covered with a thin layer of mist, Le Shaolong, Xiao Zhan, and Nan Erming exchanged glances, stepped forward at the same time, clasped their hands in congratulations: “Congratulations to the clan leader for escaping the sea of suffering and regaining his former glory.”

“I wonder how Chief Xiao is feeling now? Has he regained his former strength?” Zhenni asked softly as she moved slightly.

“Why don’t we try and see?” Xiao Yan said, pointing to a set of tables placed by the door. The table is made of ironwood, a type of wood that grows in the deep mountains of the southern part of the Dou Di Continent. It is rare, black in color like iron, and as hard as refined steel.

It is extremely difficult to damage and is generally used to make scabbards for high-level weapons, making it very precious.

“This?” Xiao Yao hesitated, as the table looked quite valuable.

“Ancestor, let’s try it! Compared to your recovery, what’s a mere table? Even if we sacrifice the entire Xiao family, what’s the harm?”

Xiao Yan encouraged Xiao Yao, warming Xiao Yao’s heart. Without saying anything more, Xiao Yao took a deep breath, pressed his palm down in the air, and his fighting spirit exploded with a suffocating wave. The table was hit by Xiao Yao and wood chips flew everywhere, turning into dust that drifted and enveloped the entire doorway.

Just then, a sharp, piercing sound came from outside the door, followed by a piercing scream that drew everyone’s attention to the doorway. Lang Tian, who arrived just in time, was covered from head to toe in a thick layer of wood dust, his mouth wide open and filled with wood chips. Lang Tian was stunned and dumbfounded.

He had no idea what had happened. He was like a mummy that had been sealed in a warehouse for thousands of years.

Meanwhile, the purple figure behind Lang Tian, taking advantage of his agility, managed to pull himself back more than ten feet. With a wave of his hands, purple light spread out, keeping all the wood chips out. He was secretly relieved, clutching his chest.

“Oh dear, I’m so sorry, I just couldn’t resist the urge. I really feel bad about it.” Xiao Yao was the first to react.

With a wave of his sleeve, a gentle breeze arose, sweeping away the wood dust on Lang Tian’s body.

“Cough cough cough.” Lang Tian hurriedly brushed the wood dust off his body, spat out the wood chips in his mouth, and looked up, still somewhat confused, “Young Master Xiao, was there an earthquake?”

Upon hearing this, the purple-clad figure behind her swayed her slender waist with a smile, exuding endless charm, and everyone else covered their mouths, unable to suppress their laughter “Oh, it’s like this, the ancestor has recovered his power, so I asked the ancestor to practice on the spot, but I didn’t expect…” Xiao Yan suppressed his laughter and explained to Lang Tian.

“Is the Soul Devouring Pill really effective?” Lang Tian and Zi Ying asked a ridiculous question in unison. Xiao Yao was standing right there; did they even need to ask about his increased strength?

Even Xiao Yan couldn’t help but roll his eyes this time before nodding heavily after a long while.

“Congratulations!” The two hurriedly stepped over the threshold to congratulate Xiao Yao.

“Thank you, thank you, all thanks to Xiao Yan.” Xiao Yao was so happy that her teeth were chattering, but when she saw the wood chips left on Lang Tian’s collar, her expression suddenly froze.

“I’m sorry about what happened earlier.”

“Compared to the clan leader’s recovery, what does it matter if I were buried alive with a thousand catties of sawdust, let alone some mere sawdust?” Lang Tian said, bowing repeatedly.

“Why does this sound so familiar?” Xiao Zhan pondered for a moment, then suddenly slapped his forehead.

“Damn, isn’t this the knock-off version of what Young Master Xiao just said?”

“It’s like the upper beam is crooked, so the lower beam will follow suit,” Storm couldn’t help but mutter. Upon hearing this, the entire room fell silent, then erupted in a burst of laughter…

“Ancestor, has your power fully recovered?” Xiao Yan asked with concern, taking a sip of tea.

“Almost there. Although I haven’t recovered to the peak of four stars yet, I’m confident that if I have another month or so, I can return to my former peak,” Xiao Yao said confidently.

“That’s wonderful! After our ancestor’s strength is restored, he will break through to the fifth star in one fell swoop. The first five-star Dou Di in tens of thousands of years of our Xiao clan will be born.”

Xiao Yan was extremely excited, his tone full of joy.

“When our ancestor breaks through to the fifth star Dou Di, we will return to the clan with him in glory.”

“Good, good, that’s great!” Xiao Yao was so happy that his white beard fluttered, as if the rise of the Xiao clan was within reach, and the wrinkles on his face smoothed out.

“Today is truly a day of double celebration! Zi Ying and Lang Tian have both broken through to new heights.” Xiao Yan then turned his gaze to Zi Ying and Lang Tian, “Tell everyone, what are their current strengths?” Everyone turned their gaze to the two, sensing the surge of energy emanating from them, their eyes gleaming with joy.

“As expected, I am now at the late stage of the fifth star, while Lang Tian is at the mid stage of the fourth star,” Zi Ying reported.

“Excellent, excellent, very excellent.” Xiao Yan said “excellent” three times in a row, in high spirits.

“Zi Ying, do you still have any five-star magic cores?” Upon hearing Xiao Yan’s question, Zi Ying’s face immediately showed regret. She said, “Reaching the fifth star level, advancing is indeed difficult.

Just advancing one level has already exhausted the five-star magic cores that Young Master Xiao gave me.” Zi Ying’s answer was not unexpected for Xiao Yan. Advancement above five stars is more difficult with each level, otherwise high-level Dou Di would have been flying all over the Dou Di Continent long ago.

Xiao Yan pondered for a moment, then turned his gaze to Zhen Ni. Jenny immediately became a little embarrassed, “We have acquired a lot of four-star magic cores, enough for Young Master Xiao and Lang Tian to advance to the peak of four-star, but five-star magic cores… there are not many five-star magic cores on the market, and they are very difficult to obtain.

So far, we have only acquired about a hundred.” She paused for a moment and continued, “But Young Master Xiao, don’t worry, I have ordered the entire merchant alliance to do its best to acquire them, and we will continue to acquire them later.” Xiao Yan was well aware of the difficulty of acquiring five-star magic cores. He was silent for a while, looked at Zi Ying, Nan Erming and Qing Mu’er, and seemed a little troubled.

There were only a hundred or so, not many, so how should they be distributed? Nan Erming hadn’t given it yet; Qing Mu’er had promised it last time; it would be best if Zi Ying could be promoted to a six-star Dou Di as soon as possible, so that there would be two six-star Dou Di, and the team’s strength would take a qualitative leap. Xiao Yan fell into thought.

After considering it for a long time, Xiao Yan finally made a decision. As the saying goes, a man keeps his promise. Since he had promised Qing Mu’er, he must keep it.

“Mu’er, take these hundred magic cores to improve your strength first. Nan Erming, you’ll have to make do with them for now. We’ll give them to you once Zhenni has acquired them.

Lang Tian, use the four-star magic cores you’ve collected to continue improving your strength.” After speaking, Xiao Yan looked at Nan Erming and Zi Ying with a slightly apologetic expression, appearing somewhat helpless.

“Mu’er’s advancement first will be a great help to the Demon Emperor, which is a good thing. As for us, we’re free now and have plenty of time, so there’s no rush,” Nan Erming said, his meaning clear: to reassure Xiao Yan that they didn’t need to rush. Zi Ying nodded vigorously in agreement.

Hearing Nan Erming’s understanding words, Xiao Yan’s guilt towards Nan Erming only increased. Both times, Nan Erming had offered to share, and Xiao Yan hadn’t shared with him either time, making him feel very bad. When Qing Mu’er heard that Xiao Yan still remembered his promise from last time, she immediately jumped for joy, her little face full of satisfaction.

“Mu’er!” Qing Haoran could see Xiao Yan’s confusion.

“Brother, no! My little sister was just joking last time. Please don’t take it seriously.

The Xiao Clan is about to rise and urgently needs experts to oversee it. This is the time when you need magic cores the most.”

“Brother, a man’s promise is worth a thousand pieces of gold. Since I have promised, how can I go back on my word? Besides, Mu’er does need the magic core.” Xiao Yan said with a faint smile.

“Little liar, I’m happy that you remember.” Qing Mu’er’s eyes sparkled with satisfaction.

“But brother is right, you need these magic cores more than I do, so I don’t need them.”

“This?” Xiao Yan was at a loss.

“Brother, stop refusing. My Blood Demon Clan has already received too many favors from you, and you need these demon cores more than Mu’er does right now. Stop arguing, it’s settled.” Qing Haoran was very insistent, and what he said made perfect sense, but Xiao Yan had already spoken and could not take it back.

Xiao Yan was stunned for a moment, and the atmosphere became somewhat awkward. Looking at Qing Mu’er’s happy, crescent-shaped eyes, Zhen Ni sighed softly and gently parted her cherry lips to help Xiao Yan out of the predicament.

“The Demon Emperor’s words make perfect sense. I think it would be better if Young Master Xiao took back the Demon Core, but in return, he would owe Miss Mu’er a favor. How about that?”

“Great! It’s settled then. Sister Zhenni, you’re so kind!”

Before Xiao Yan could react, Qing Mu’er’s eyes lit up. She was a hundred times happier than when Xiao Yan remembered to give her the magic core. As if afraid that Xiao Yan would change his mind, she hurriedly agreed.

Everyone knew what Qing Mu’er was thinking and covered their mouths to chuckle. Xiao Yan was somewhat taken aback, lost in thought but not quite understanding.

However, seeing that Qing Mu’er had agreed, he nodded in agreement Isn’t it just owing someone a favor? Xiao Yan muttered to himself, but he always felt a little uneasy looking at Qing Mu’er’s eyes.

“Young Master Xiao, how should these magic cores be distributed?” Seeing that Xiao Yan hadn’t reacted yet, Zhen Ni shook her head and hurriedly asked.

“I still think we should focus on significantly improving Zi Ying’s strength first.” Nan Erming clasped his hands behind his back, leaning back in his chair, looking completely matter-of-fact. Xiao Yan looked at Nan Erming, his eyes filled with increasing admiration, and said, “Zi Ying is already at the late stage of the fifth star level, so let’s give it to Nan Erming this time.”

“Don’t refuse anymore, it’s settled then.” Xiao Yan reached out to stop Nan Erming from declining and made the decision.

“Lang Tian, can you refine fifth-grade pills now?” Xiao Yan turned to ask Lang Tian.

“Although my Danmo clan is naturally stronger in soul power than other races and is skilled in alchemy, the threshold for fifth-grade pills is too high. I estimate that one must reach the peak of the fourth star to refine fifth-grade pills,” Lang Tian said with a wry smile, his initial joy gone.

“I see.” Xiao Yan rubbed his temples and pondered for a long time. He had originally planned to hand over the refining of the Purple Heart Pill to Lang Tian, but in the current situation, there was no use in being impatient.

Although there were hundreds of materials for the Purple Heart Pill, the 200,000-year-old Purple Heart Turtle Shells were becoming increasingly rare, and it would probably be difficult to find them again on the Dou Di Continent. In order to avoid wasting materials due to failure in refining, Xiao Yan would rather delay the process slightly. After all, Lang Tian was a member of the Demon Clan, and as long as there was a sufficient supply of Demon Cores, advancing to the peak of the four-star Dou Di or even the five-star Dou Di would not be too difficult.

He could afford to wait this long.

“Then you should continue to devour four-star magic cores during this period until you reach the peak of four-star, and it would be best if you could break through to five-star before you start refining the Purple Heart Pill.” Lang Tian nodded with a bitter face. He fully understood Xiao Yan’s good intentions.

Giving up alchemy to improve his Dou Qi strength might be something others would be eager for, but for Lang Tian, a medicine fanatic, it was an unspeakable torment. Xiao Zhan patted Lang Tian on the shoulder, understanding his thoughts, and felt rather speechless.

“By the way, Young Master Xiao, there’s something I need to report to you. While you were in seclusion, Xiao Zhan and I went out for a stroll, and some things happened…” Nan Erming then recounted the events of that day to everyone in detail.

“What? You’re saying they might be from a magical beast family?” Qing Haoran’s expression changed drastically upon hearing this.

Seeing Nan Erming and Xiao Zhan nod, Qing Haoran said with a solemn expression, “So, this girl in red is definitely not simple. It’s fortunate that you saved her. If something had happened to her here, with the power of the magical beast family, they would definitely not let it go.

They might even have turned this place upside down.” Qing Haoran’s words sent chills down everyone’s spines. Xiao Zhan and Nan Erming were especially shocked.

The fact that the Beast Clan could cause such a ruckus in Juxi City meant that their background was far more powerful than they had imagined. Seeing everyone’s confusion, Qing Haoran explained, “The Beast Clan is a very mysterious force on the Dou Di Continent. It is mysterious because it rarely participates in the power struggles on the Dou Di Continent, and it rarely even appears in the world.”

“But being mysterious does not mean it is low-key! There was once a force on the Dou Di Continent that relied on a Seven-Star Dou Di to severely injure the members of the Demon Beast Clan. As a result, overnight, only the roars of tigers and the cries of dragons could be heard in the area, and all the beasts dared not go out.

That force was razed to the ground, and not a blade of grass grew.” Everyone felt a chill run down their spines – the power of a Seven-Star Dou Di had vanished overnight.

“The Beast Clan is a bloodline preserved from ancient peak-level beasts, and it is exceptionally powerful. Although countless years have passed, the Beast Clan has always forbidden intermarriage with other races to ensure the purity of its bloodline. Over the years, generation after generation, many beasts have awakened a portion of the power of their ancient bloodline.

Therefore, this force is far more terrifying than we superficially understand,” Qing Haoran continued.

“Intermarriage with outsiders is forbidden?” Nan Erming’s heart skipped a beat, and a sense of loss washed over him for no apparent reason. The image of the girl in red reappeared before his eyes.

He shook his head vigorously to suppress the inexplicable emotion.

“Does the Beast Clan rule over the vast majority of magical beasts on the continent? Then why don’t we see any reaction from the Beast Clan when we hunt and kill magical beasts?” Xiao Yan asked, puzzled.

“The bloodline of the magical beast family is noble, and they consider themselves to be of royal blood. They can all transform into human form and disdain to have any interaction with other magical beasts. Otherwise, their power would be far greater than it is now.” Qing Haoran explained and sighed at the same time.

Just thinking about ruling the vast majority of magical beasts on the entire Dou Di Continent was an extremely terrifying thing. After hearing Qing Haoran’s words, everyone sighed in admiration, realizing that the Warcraft Clan was truly a first-rate power!

“I’m really looking forward to seeing Young Master Xiao lead hundreds of five-star Dou Di. That’s when we’ll truly shine.” Despite the shock brought by the Beast Clan, Storm was still optimistic about the future.

He turned to Lang Tian and said, “Lang Tian, are you feeling the weight of responsibility now?” Lang Tian remained silent, his fists clenched, his dark eyes burning with a desperate light. Long Yi, standing beside him, couldn’t help but shiver, because he was all too familiar with that look in Lang Tian’s eyes.

“I’m going to advance right now!” Lang Tian kept his word, grabbed a bunch of four-star magic cores and rushed out the door, leaving everyone sweating profusely.

“What a daredevil, my idol!” Storm was startled by Langtian’s actions and silently admired him as he looked at Langtian’s back.

“Lang Tian is truly admirable,” Xiao Yan thought to himself.

“Nan Erming, are you regretting it now? You didn’t hold on to such a powerful figure.” After Lang Tian left, Xiao Zhan suddenly remembered something and whispered to Nan Erming.

“Go away, go somewhere cool and stay somewhere else.” Nan Erming waved his hand to shoo Xiao Zhan away.

“What’s wrong?” Xiao Yan turned around and saw that Nan Erming’s expression was a little off, so he asked with concern.

“It’s nothing. I lost my composure for a moment, I’m sorry.” Nan Erming came to his senses and raised his slightly lowered head.

“This is the jade pendant. Xiao Zhan and I studied it for a long time, but we didn’t find anything.” Nan Erming took out the jade pendant and handed it to Xiao Yan.

Touching the slightly cold jade, Xiao Yan slowly pieced the two jade pendants together. A soft light flashed, and a ghost face clearly appeared inside the jade pendant, but apart from that, there was nothing else unusual. Xiao Yan frowned slightly, and everyone’s curiosity turned into confusion.

“Judging from the quality of this jade, it should be an ancient artifact,” Qing Haoran said after examining the jade pendant.

“However, the specific function of this jade pendant is unknown. Even if it is a key to a secret realm, the Dou Di Continent is vast and boundless. Finding a secret realm would be like finding a needle in a haystack.”

“I wonder how many treasures were buried in the long river of history because of the ancient catastrophe.” Storm said with some heartache.

“Since we have the chance to obtain the jade pendant, we may also have the chance to obtain the map. In any case, it’s a good thing to get it.” Jenny and Lok Siu-lung held the jade pendant in their hands and examined it for a long time.

With their shrewdness, they tried many methods, but in the end, they still came up empty-handed and could only console themselves. Even the two most skilled appraisers in the team were at a loss. A hint of disappointment flashed in Xiao Yan’s eyes, but his never-give-up spirit prevented him from giving up.

Xiao Yan let out a soft breath, carefully recalling the key moments when he obtained various opportunities, hoping for some enlightenment. But after thinking it over and over, there was no opportunity that could be connected with this jade pendant. Xiao Yan finally pursed his lips and smiled bitterly—could it be that he would have to rely on the Heavenly Fire and the Ghost Spirit again?

But how many good things in the world come one after another? First the jade box in the illusion, and then this jade pendant? Xiao Yan himself felt that this was like dreaming of marrying a wife—thinking only of good things, it was almost impossible.

“Whatever, let’s give it a try. It’s not like it’s any trouble.” Xiao Yan slightly circulated his Heavenly Fire and enveloped the jade pendant inside.

Seeing the bluish-gray flames rising from Xiao Yan’s hand, everyone broke out in a cold sweat. Who knows if this jade pendant can withstand the heavenly fire? If it gets burned, that would be no laughing matter.

After all, it’s an ancient relic, and the jade itself is priceless, not to mention the secrets it contains. The jade pendant turned slightly red as it was burned by the flames, and its warm, smooth texture became even stronger, as if it were softening. The entire place was silent, so quiet that only the pounding of hearts could be heard.

Cold sweat beaded on Xiao Yan’s forehead and slid down his cheeks.

“Should we burn it or not?” Xiao Yan was unsure and hesitantly looked at the crowd. The group looked at each other, their throats bobbing involuntarily, unsure of what to do.

“I finally understand why that old man dared to use such a precious ancient relic to swindle people.” Xiao Zhan’s lips curled into a self-deprecating smile.

“This is a complete hot potato.”

“Rather than keeping it as a useless thing, let’s take a gamble. I think that since ancient artifacts have survived to this day, they are at least not so easily damaged,” Qing Haoran said resolutely after a moment of contemplation.

“Brother is right.” Seeing that Qing Haoran thought the same way he did, Xiao Yan no longer hesitated and activated the power of the Heavenly Fire. The flames roared and the temperature in the air suddenly rose.

The fire, a mixture of three kinds of heavenly fire, was incredibly powerful. The jade pendant seemed unable to withstand it and made a “crack” sound. Cracks immediately appeared all over its surface, making it look like it was ruined.

“It’s over.” The same thought flashed through everyone’s minds at the same time, even Xiao Yan was no exception. Looking at the charred jade pendant in his hand, Xiao Yan felt like crying. What did this mean?

An ancient artifact destroyed in a single burn? How had it survived for countless ages? Qing Haoran was also stunned.

He opened his mouth, but didn’t know what to say
