Chapter 78: Organizing Combat Techniques and Items

Xiao Zhan was dumbfounded when he saw that the jade pendant had become useless. He was heartbroken and beat his chest in despair.

“Since it’s not meant for us, let’s just burn it thoroughly and let it return to the dust of antiquity.” Xiao Yan smiled bitterly. Amidst the regretful gazes of the crowd, the cracks on the surface of the ghost-face jade pendant spread rapidly under the burning flames, and then peeled off piece by piece.

“Such a beautiful ancient jade pendant, ruined just like that.” Beautiful jade has a fatal attraction for women, and even Jenny felt a little sorry to see such a beautiful piece of jade about to be burned to ashes.

“It’s not meant to be our destiny, and we can’t have it anyway. Let’s all try to be more open-minded.” Xiao Yan comforted everyone, but his heart was clenching with pain.

After all, it’s hard not to feel heartbroken to lose such a treasure. Xiao Yan said this only so that Xiao Zhan and Nan Erming wouldn’t feel bad. Xiao Zhan was fine and gradually calmed down, but Nan Erming’s eyes trembled slightly.

The jade pendant was still burning, and the falling fragments, in Nan Erming’s eyes, were like the beautiful figure of the girl in red, tearing apart bit by bit, and then blurring and fading away.

“Could this really be fate? With the jade shattered, our fate is over. Perhaps we will never meet again in this life.”

Nan Erming was heartbroken and lowered his head slightly. He didn’t want to see the moment when the jade pendant was completely destroyed, or perhaps he wanted to keep the last memory of that red figure in his heart. Just as the already heavy atmosphere became even more somber, suddenly, beams of silver light shot out from Xiao Yan’s hands, shimmering and sparkling like diamonds that could not be hidden in the dust.

Xiao Yan’s heart trembled, and the heavenly fire immediately went out without a trace; everyone was stunned and stared at Xiao Yan’s palm; while Nan Erming was overjoyed and his eyes lit up.

“Could it be…” Xiao Yan was pleasantly surprised. He gently brushed away the dust from the surface of the jade pendant with his hand, and his usually calm fingers trembled slightly.

As Xiao Yan moved, a piece of silvery-white, shimmering jade was revealed before everyone’s eyes.

“So there really was more to it than meets the eye! The Demon Emperor was right, ancient relics aren’t so easily damaged.” Xiao Zhan’s sorrow turned to joy, and in his excitement, he slammed his hand on the table, instantly creating five deep dents.

“Damaging public property will result in a fine! Xiaozhan, are you paying in full or in installments?” Zhenni put on a stern face, but her eyes were full of smiles.

“Hahahaha, the jade pendant is fine. If I can obtain the opportunity within, I’ll accept any punishment. Hahaha.”

Xiao Zhan grinned, but his eyes never left the jade pendant.

“Hehe, you guys still have time to bicker.” Xiao Yan swept away his previous displeasure and examined the jade pendant with everyone. The jade pendant, with its outer layer peeled off, doesn’t resemble a piece of jade.

Instead, it looks like a translucent silver metal, with two ancient characters engraved on it—“Ghost Mansion.” The strokes are powerful and vigorous, the characters are profound and solemn, and the strokes are like silver hooks and iron lines, like a dragon coiling around, leaving the reader wanting more.

“What material is this? I’ve never seen such a special jade before.” Even the well-informed Yue Shaolong couldn’t tell what it was.

Xiao Yan shook his head, then gently channeled his Dou Qi into the jade pendant, causing it to shimmer with an unusual brilliance, which made everyone’s expressions grow increasingly solemn. The colorful projections in front resembled twinkling stars, shining brightly and slowly forming a map.

However, the mountainous terrain depicted on the map was somewhat peculiar, with numerous silver lines connecting the various landforms, appearing chaotic yet orderly. The group studied the map for a long time, finding it enigmatic and extremely difficult to understand “The stars correspond to the heavens above, and the map below is presented. Heaven and earth are created, and the hexagrams are stable.

Could it be that this place can only be found by comparing the star map and the terrain?” Le Shaolong pondered for a long time, then pointed to the silver line and guessed, “This line should be the star map, and the others are the terrain map.”

“Shaolong’s observation is indeed meticulous. The silver threads are connected like stars in the sky. It seems that finding this place will not be easy.” Zhenni said, her delicate body trembling slightly.

“This place is outside our area of knowledge. It will probably take a lot of effort to find it.” Qing Haoran took out all the maps to compare, and sure enough, there was no corresponding place on the map.

Upon hearing this, Xiao Yan felt as if he had been doused with cold water, chilled to the bone. He had finally discovered the secret of the jade pendant, only to be told that there was no place on the current map of the Dou Di Continent that matched it. This was too discouraging!

“Perhaps this place, like the illusion, belongs to a different space?” Zhenni comforted Xiao Yan.

“As long as we have a map, things will be easier. There are many extraordinary people in the Merchant Alliance who are proficient in astronomy, geography, and star chart calculations. I’ll go ask them.”

“Yes, Zhen Ji is right. When it comes to familiarity with the terrain of the continent, the Merchant Alliance is definitely the strongest.” Qing Haoran said with a bright look in his eyes. Xiao Yan also felt that Zhen Ni’s words made sense, so he handed the jade pendant to Zhen Ni.

Jenny didn’t take the jade pendant. She smiled, shook her head, and said, “I only need to make a rubbing.” Seeing Xiao Yan’s confusion, Zhen Ni took out a crystal ball from her storage ring, aimed it at the projection on the jade pendant, and slightly channeled her fighting spirit into it.

Surprisingly, a map exactly the same as the projection appeared on the crystal ball.

“This?” Xiao Yan had never seen anything so amazing before.

“This is called a mirror crystal. It’s not uncommon in the Dou Di Continent. It’s used specifically to make rubbings of images,” Zhen Ni explained.

“However, it’s very expensive, so it’s usually only available to powerful forces.”

“I see. This thing is really good, quite convenient.” Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up.

Seeing Xiao Yan’s eyes light up, Zhen Ni smiled and said, “If you like it, little brother, I’ll get you one later.”

“No need, no need, you have enough of this. Haha.” Xiao Yan was very happy after the jade pendant was revealed.

Nan Erming was overjoyed. The rebirth of the jade pendant allowed the blurry, distant red fragments in Nan Erming’s heart to be pieced back together and become clearer. Seeing that the jade pendant had been deciphered and there was nothing for him to do, Xiao Yao hesitated for a long time before finally saying, “I have nothing to do here, so I’ll go cultivate first.

I need to consolidate my newly recovered strength.” Seeing Xiao Yao’s restless state after regaining his strength, Xiao Yan understood Xiao Yao’s thoughts perfectly. Having regained his power, the dream of advancing to a five-star Dou Di was within reach; anyone would be excited and eager to face the five-star Emperor Tribulation immediately.

However, Xiao Yao’s body had been corroded by the Soul Devouring Mark over the years, and his body was no longer as strong as it had been at its peak. Relying solely on the Purple Heart Pill was not very safe. Looking at Xiao Yao’s somewhat thin face and slightly weak body, Xiao Yan rubbed his forehead.

“Ancestor, please wait.” Xiao Yan spoke up, frowning as he said, “I understand your feelings, Ancestor. I believe that recovering your Dou Qi should not be a problem.

However, I am worried about your health. I am afraid that you will not be able to reach your peak in a short period of time.” Xiao Yao was taken aback for a moment upon hearing this, but he did not refute it.

Everyone suddenly understood Xiao Yan’s meaning, and the atmosphere became somewhat heavy. Despite his confident demeanor after regaining his fighting spirit, Xiao Yao was actually quite anxious. He had witnessed Zhan Yi’s successful Five-Star Emperor Tribulation and seen how powerful the fifth lightning tribulation remained even after using the Purple Heart Pill.

Given his current physical condition, without a long period of recovery, he feared that even with the Purple Heart Pill, he might not be able to guarantee success in his tribulation. Therefore, although he was still incredibly happy, he couldn’t help feeling slightly dejected and wanted to cultivate alone to avoid affecting everyone’s mood “With the Purple Heart Pill here, I think it’s worth taking a gamble,” Xiao Yao said with a smile, reassuring everyone.

“No! I can’t let our ancestor take such a risk.” Xiao Yan stubbornly shook his head. What a joke!

Xiao Yao is his ancestor, the patriarch of the Xiao clan, and the spiritual pillar of the Xiao clan. Even if there is the slightest risk, Xiao Yan would not let Xiao Yao take any risks.

“Why don’t you recover your fighting spirit first, Ancestor? As for the tribulation, let’s put it off for now and think of a solution.” Xiao Yan thought of the Marrow Cultivation Pill at this moment, but he had never tried its effects before, so he planned to try it himself before making a decision. He looked up and stared into Xiao Yao’s eyes, hoping that Xiao Yao would give him a guarantee.

“My lifespan is running out, and who knows when my body will recover from the wear and tear of the past few years,” Xiao Yao thought to himself, a troubled expression on his face.

“Young Master Xiao, I just remembered something,” Le Shaolong said suddenly. Le Shaolong’s words drew everyone’s attention.

“Young Master Xiao, do you remember the Roaring Black Lord we encountered in the illusion? Its skin…” Le Shaolong asked, looking at Xiao Yan. Xiao Yan’s eyes suddenly lit up, and he and Le Shaolong exchanged a smile.

That Roaring Black Lord is a peak six-star magical beast that is infinitely close to seven stars, and its defense is extremely strong. Its hide is probably only slightly weaker than that of a seven-star magical beast. With such a hide as inner armor, and with the Purple Heart Pill, there will be no chance of failure!

Xiao Yan hurriedly took out the hide of the Roaring Black Lord and handed it to Qing Haoran and the others to see. Qing Haoran was naturally aware of the Seven Star Inner Armor’s toughness. He stroked the dark leather surface, and his battle qi swept across it, leaving only a shallow white mark.

He couldn’t help but marvel at it. Xiao Yao was very nervous, staring intently at Qing Haoran’s hands. He had never seen the Seven-Star Inner Armor before.

“Excellent, excellent. The toughness of this hide is only slightly inferior to that of a seven-star magical beast. Using this hide to make inner armor will be perfectly fine.

Congratulations, Clan Chief Xiao.” Qing Haoran laughed heartily.

“That’s great!” Xiao Yao was so excited that he was almost absent-minded, his face beaming with joy, and even his shoulders were trembling slightly.

“Leave the inner armor to me. My Blood Demon clan has experts in making inner armor. I guarantee we’ll have three pieces made in two months.”

Qing Haoran took the initiative to accept the task of making the inner armor.

“You can make three?” Everyone was shocked.

“Of course, you don’t even look at how big this hide is,” Qing Haoran replied matter-of-factly.

“If we didn’t have to remove some of the parts that were damaged in battle, this hide could make four pieces.”

“Look at you guys, if you had been a little gentler back then, you would have had an extra inner armor. This inner armor is comparable to that of a seven-star magical beast,” Zi Ying teased the group.

“If we had been gentler back then, it would have been the Roaring Black Lord who used our skins to make inner armor.” Everyone gave Zi Ying a wry smile.

“What a bunch of little money-grubbers,” Jenny shook her head with a smile. Standing to the side, Xiao Yao stared at the Roaring Black Lord’s skin, swallowing hard a few times.

“A beast’s inner armor plus a Purple Heart Pill, that’s too extravagant.”

“Clan Chief Xiao, it’s rare for your Xiao clan to have such an outstanding and filial junior. You should just enjoy your good fortune,” Qing Haoran said, half-jokingly and half-seriously. Xiao Yao nodded, filled with excitement.

“Young Master Xiao, what are our next steps?” With Xiao Yao’s tribulation now completely resolved, Le Shaolong took a sip of tea and asked “What’s next?” Xiao Yan glanced at Le Shaolong and the others.

“Shaolong, continue looking for candidates for the Purple Heart Pill. Zhenni, keep an eye out for any high-level fighting techniques up for auction.”

“Advanced combat skills?” Jenny asked, puzzled.

“Yes. Now that Xiao Zhan has advanced to six stars, everyone’s strength will continue to improve, and their fighting techniques are starting to fall behind, so I’m thinking of replacing them with higher-level fighting techniques.” Xiao Yan had been concerned about this matter while in the illusion, and now that the trivial matters had been dealt with, it was time to address this issue.

“Except for Shao Long’s fighting technique, which is at the mid-level of the World Rank, Xiao Zhan and Feng Bao’s are only at the early-level of the World Rank, while Nan Er Ming and Zi Ying are both at the high-level of the Intent Rank.”

“World-level beginner and Intent-level advanced are considered low?” Looking at Xiao Yan’s serious face, Xiao Yao’s eyes widened in disbelief.

“Xiao Yan, do you know how many Intent-level fighting techniques my Xiao clan has? Three! And all of them are low-level!

You brat, you’re quite arrogant, even considering World-level beginner and Intent-level advanced techniques low?” Everyone was speechless. How could they compare to the Xiao Clan?

The entire Xiao Clan only has one four-star Dou Di, while everyone here is a five-star Dou Di. How could they be compared?

However, everyone was speechless at Xiao Yan’s words. Judging from his tone, he really thought that high-level combat skills of the Intent Rank were too low, and it seemed that he didn’t even care about low-level World Rank skills. This left everyone speechless.

Qing Haoran shook his head with a wry smile—“My brother’s starting point is too high.” Listening to Xiao Yao’s tirade and seeing everyone staring at him as if he were a monster, Xiao Yan finally realized it was because they didn’t understand the situation on the Dou Di Continent. A little embarrassed, he scratched his head and chuckled, “I was just provoked by Zhen Ni, wasn’t I?

She used a high-level world-class Dou Technique right off the bat, so…”

“You compare yourself to her? Go ask around the entire Dou Di Continent, how many people can compare to her?” Xiao Yao almost roared. Xiao Yao’s loud shout made Zhen Ni very embarrassed.

“Uh…” This time, Xiao Yan was speechless. After a pause, he still said somewhat unwillingly, “I’m only at the peak of three stars.

My fighting skills are probably all at the mid-level of the World Rank.” This time, no one roared. Instead, Xiao Zhan said in a low voice, “Comparing myself to you?

You’re not even human.” Everyone burst into laughter, even Jenny couldn’t help but smile broadly, and Qing Mu’er laughed so hard she threw herself at Qing Haoran. Xiao Yan, rendered speechless by everyone’s laughter, joined in the commotion and then asked Qing Haoran, “Brother, what about that mid-level world-class fighting technique you gave to Xiao Qi back then?” Before Xiao Yan could finish his question, Qing Haoran raised his hand to stop him.

“Brother, I know what you’re going to say. Is a mid-level world-class fighting skill comparable to the Blood Demon Token and Blood Demon Ring that I risked my life to give to my elder brother? Don’t ever mention this again.

I feel ashamed just thinking about it. If my demon clan hadn’t fallen, how could my elder brother have only been able to give his sister-in-law a mid-level world-class fighting skill? Sigh.”

“Sister-in-law? Xiao Qi? Xiao Li’s beloved granddaughter?” Xiao Yao asked Xiao Yan and Qing Haoran with great confusion.

“Oh, Ancestor, I forgot to tell you this. Back then, the Soul Demon Clan was hunting me down because of the Blood Demon Token. The Second Elder and Third Elder arranged for Xiao Qi to marry me, and then covered our escape.

We found Brother Qing, and he gave Xiao Qi a mid-level World-Rank Battle Technique.” Xiao Yan quickly explained to Xiao Yao.

“Xiao Li and Xiao Long did the right thing! Sigh, I’ve been gone for so many years, they’ve really suffered. Oh, so Xiao Li’s dear granddaughter is your wife now?” Xiao Yao asked, glancing at Zhen Ni as he spoke.

“Mmm,” Xiao Yan replied somewhat shyly.

“I understand,” Xiao Yao said thoughtfully. As for what he understood, no one knew When Zhenni was glanced at by Xiao Yao, her heart fluttered. She quickly changed the subject and gently asked Xiao Yan, “Do you know the general situation of fighting techniques now?”

“Well, I have a general idea. Can you give me some advice? After all, I’m not very familiar with the market situation,” Xiao Yan said, feeling a little nervous.

“High-level World-Rank Battle Techniques are extremely rare, and even if they exist, they are monopolized by major powers and rarely appear on the market, making them a rare sight. Mid-level World-Rank Battle Techniques occasionally appear, but their numbers are extremely limited, like phoenix feathers and unicorn horns. Low-level World-Rank Battle Techniques are slightly easier to obtain, but they are still relatively rare compared to the former two.

In the Dou Di Continent, World-Rank Battle Techniques are high-end Battle Techniques, and it is very difficult to find one that suits everyone in a short period of time.”

“Is it that difficult?” Although Xiao Yan was mentally prepared, hearing Zhen Ni say this still felt like being doused with a bucket of cold water, and he exclaimed in surprise.

“I was originally thinking at least a mid-level World Rank skill, but now it seems that a low-level World Rank skill would be good enough. What do you guys think?” Unexpectedly, World Rank fighting skills are so rare that Xiao Yan had to lower his requirements and turned to look at Zi Ying and the others.

The group had been standing behind Xiao Yan for a long time, remaining silent, but their hearts were filled with warmth, and their eyes were slightly moist. It was difficult to express in words how kind Xiao Yan had been to them. Only when they heard Xiao Yan’s question did they react and speak up without hesitation: “We will do as Young Master Xiao says.” Xiao Yan nodded, then asked Zhen Ni with some concern, “Has there been any information about combat techniques at auctions recently?”

“Let me check, but the chances are slim.” After retrieving recent auction information through the communication crystal ball, Jenny’s face turned bitter.

“Sure enough, only a few combat techniques of the Intent Rank were auctioned recently.”

“Obtaining world-class combat skills has always depended on luck, because they are extremely rare. If you try to find them by chance at every auction, it’s like looking for a needle in a haystack,” Le Shaolong said slowly. Years of auction experience gave Le Shaolong’s words considerable authority, and coupled with Jenny’s previous assessment, it was enough to make people despair.

“For a long time, people have relied on auctions to find treasures, but with so many items up for auction, the chances of finding something you specifically want are extremely low. If we want to buy world-class fighting techniques, and not just one or two, we probably need to find another way,” Le Shaolong continued.

“Find another way?” Xiao Yan muttered to himself, scratching his head in frustration.

“How about organizing a special auction for combat skills?” Le Shaolong proposed a very creative idea.

“Great idea! A special auction of combat skills! That way we can gather a large number of combat skills, satisfying our needs in terms of quantity, grade, and range of choices!

Great idea! Shaolong, no wonder you want to follow Young Master Xiao. My uncle was so reluctant to let you go.

You really are a genius!” Zhenni clapped her hands and praised Le Shaolong’s idea immediately.

“Not bad, that’s a good idea. Haha, a special auction of fighting techniques!” Xiao Yan suppressed his excitement and tried to calm his voice.

“However, can we gather that many fighting techniques? That’s the key.” Xiao Yan expressed his concern.

“With the capabilities of the Merchant Alliance, there’s nothing we can’t do if we don’t want to.” Zhenni had enough confidence in this regard, which put Xiao Yan at ease.

“Indeed, with the Merchant Alliance’s influence, it can probably be prepared in a short time. And since this is the first time such a special auction has been held, it will probably be very exciting, with many high-level combat skills appearing. Brother’s problem should be solved easily.

Shaolong is truly a genius.” Qing Haoran’s eyes gleamed. As the Demon Emperor, he immediately saw the brilliance of this idea, and his appreciation for Le Shaolong was evident.

“This is a win-win situation. It will solve our need for world-class combat skills and boost the business of the Merchant Alliance. I’ll take care of it right away and make it a big deal.” Zhenni smiled and stood up, preparing to take her leave.

“Sister, wait a minute.” Seeing Zhen Ni get up to leave, Xiao Yan quickly called out to her, “Sister, this is the Rejuvenation Pill. Fortunately, it worked on the first try.

Please give it to the Demon Emperor for me. Also, I was thinking, if there are really many high-level fighting techniques appearing, shouldn’t we buy more? After all, we will have a lot of new blood in the future, and the demand for high-level fighting techniques will be great.”

“Hmm, you’ve thought this through very well. Let me put it this way, any force has a great need for fighting techniques, especially high-level ones. Your idea is very correct.”

Zhen Ni affirmed, her eyes shining with satisfaction for Xiao Yan.

“If that’s the case, do we have enough funds? High-level fighting techniques are definitely not cheap, and buying a lot of them will definitely require a lot of funds. I’m afraid we don’t have enough funds,” Xiao Yan expressed his concerns.

“You want to use your entire fortune to buy fighting techniques? This is not what you meant by buying more!” Jenny exclaimed in surprise. Not only Jenny was surprised, but everyone was.

Xiao Yan’s spending was far too extravagant. He was actually worried that he didn’t have enough funds? He was now a wealthy man in the Dou Di Continent.

Not to mention that he had sold over 20 million Rejuvenation Pills, the commission from the daily Cleansing Elixir and Blood Restoration Pills alone was an astronomical figure. How could he be worried that he wouldn’t have enough?

“Ahem, brother, don’t scare your big brother. How much are you planning to buy?” Qing Haoran asked.

“Uh, I’m not sure either, but I want to buy as many as possible. It’s a rare opportunity, and you’ll always need them later,” Xiao Yan replied. Buy as much as you can?

If you try your best, doesn’t that mean you’ll buy out the entire event?

“Oh, I was thinking, what Big Brother said earlier makes sense. The first special auction like this is bound to be crazy. But have you all thought about why someone would put such a rare high-level fighting technique up for auction?” Xiao Yan asked.

When Xiao Yan raised this question, everyone looked at each other, puzzled as to why he would ask such a question.

“There are only a few reasons why high-level combat techniques are put up for auction: First, there’s an urgent need for money, either to prepare for the Five-Star Emperor Tribulation and then use the money to buy inner armor (or a six-star if you don’t have a seven-star one, which is better than nothing), or there’s another urgent need, such as buying high-level pills to save a life. Second, they’re extras that you don’t need or that your family doesn’t need.

Third, the combat techniques are high-level but have very low applicability or too high requirements, like the group combat technique that the Demon Emperor gave to Young Master Xiao. That’s roughly it.” Being a professional auctioneer, Zhen Ni quickly analyzed all the possibilities.

After Jenny finished her analysis, no one understood what Xiao Yan meant by asking that question.

However, Le Shaolong finally understood: “I know what Young Master Xiao means. I must say that Young Master Xiao has considered everything very thoroughly and his judgment is very accurate.”

“Oh dear, why are you being so mysterious? Tell me quickly, what do you mean by ’little liar’?” Qing Mu’er couldn’t help but ask.

She was very curious about Xiao Yan’s question and really wanted to know why. Le Shaolong chuckled, glanced at Xiao Yan, and only after receiving Xiao Yan’s supportive look did he speak: “The first scenario shouldn’t be very common. After all, a four-star Dou Di who can use advanced Dou Techniques is generally not without a background.

Even if the idea of exchanging Dou Techniques for money to buy pills exists, it should be extremely rare.” He paused, glanced around at everyone, which made Qing Mu’er anxious.

“Go on, stop looking! We all agree with your analysis, please continue!” Le Shaolong smiled sheepishly and continued, “Then it’s mostly the second and third scenarios. Think about it, what kind of fighting techniques are rarely used?

Fighting techniques can’t be unused, there must be someone who can use them, so who would be the person who can use them? They certainly aren’t in these forces or families, right?” At this point, Le Shaolong stopped completely and looked at everyone.

If you still don’t understand after all this, you’re just being idiotic. The people who need this skill are likely to be from very small factions or families, or even not from any faction or family at all! Can small factions or families afford such expensive high-level fighting techniques?

Most likely not. If Young Master Xiao buys them back, wouldn’t it be much easier for him to recruit talent? Qing Haoran was the first to show a knowing smile.

Xiao Yao was the second to understand.

As soon as he realized, he patted Xiao Yan on the shoulder and said, “Good lad, not bad at all, haha! It seems I have to pass the position of clan leader to you. You are much stronger than me!”

“Ancestor, you can’t say things like that carelessly,” Xiao Yan said in a trembling voice, which made Xiao Yao and Qing Haoran burst into laughter. Jenny remained silent, her mind racing with calculations. She was assessing Xiao Yan’s current funds.

After Xiao Yao and Qing Haoran finished laughing, she spoke up, “If that’s Xiao Shao’s plan, the funds might not be enough. If they’re all low-level World-Rank items, it’ll definitely be sufficient. But if high-level or mid-level items appear, requiring a large acquisition of five-star magic cores, it will certainly be insufficient.

How about this, I’ll borrow some funds from the Merchant Alliance. Since the Merchant Alliance gives you a commission every month, consider it an advance payment. That should definitely be enough.”

“Well then, thank you for your trouble, sister. Please give my and Shaolong’s regards to Chairman Zhen. Oh, right, there’s one more thing I need to trouble you with, sister,” Xiao Yan said, handing over a prescription.

“Prepare the other ingredients for this Soul Love, and try to prepare several doses.” Zhenni nodded and took it.

“You guys are so lucky, you have so much time to cultivate. Sigh, I’m so busy with trivial matters.” As she left, Zhenni thought about the numerous trivial matters of the Xiao family and the merchant alliance, and couldn’t help but rub her forehead and sigh.

“Although you have a shorter training period, you have an advantage in combat skills, so you won’t fall behind us by much,” Le Shaolong said to Zhen Ni with understanding.

“Look at what you’re saying. The more you improve, the more it will help Young Master Xiao. Do you think I’d be jealous of you?” Zhen Ni smiled and waved to everyone before going about her business.

“Mu’er and I also have to deal with clan affairs. If we have time, we should also try to break through to a higher level, otherwise we’ll be overtaken by you younger generations.” Qing Haoran laughed heartily and pulled Qing Mu’er away.

“By the way, Shaolong, how’s the selection process going?” After Qing Haoran and Qing Mu’er left, Xiao Yan asked with concern. It wasn’t that he was worried about not finding anyone, but rather that it concerned whether he should focus on improving his physical strength to prepare for the demon race’s bloodline, or continue refining the Purple Heart Pill.

“I currently have a total of twenty-three Purple Heart Pills, but as for candidates, I only have eight so far,” Le Shaolong said.

“Only eight?” Xiao Yan was somewhat surprised. Looking at Xiao Yan’s slightly furrowed brows, Le Shaolong explained, “Although it is difficult for a four-star Dou Di to resist the temptation of the Purple Heart Pill, my requirements are that they must not only be loyal, of good character, and have a clean background, but also have excellent talent and other aspects.

After all, they will be our elites in the future. If a group of six- or seven-star experts can emerge from among them, the power of the Xiao Clan can be truly consolidated. But in this way, the room for selection will be much smaller.”

“Yes, Shaolong is right to do it.” Xiao Yao agreed wholeheartedly Better to have nothing than something bad; Xiao Yan understood this principle. It seems that the Purple Heart Pill can last for a while, so let’s prepare for the demon bloodline first, and at the same time improve our own strength, otherwise the gap between us and them will only widen.

“Then I’ll have to trouble you, Shaolong.” Xiao Yan nodded with great satisfaction, then looked at everyone and said, “If you all have nothing else to do, go and cultivate.”… Watching the departing figures of the crowd, Xiao Yan smiled and flashed into the Zichen Spirit Void Tower. Xiao Yan’s purpose this time was very clear: he wanted to ask Elder Zhan how to use the Marrow Refining Pill, which was related to whether he could refine the bloodline of the demon race and was the foundation for advancing in the Burning Technique.

It was of extraordinary significance to Xiao Yan. Just as Xiao Yan’s footsteps landed firmly inside the tower, a golden lightning bolt streaked across the sky, and Long Yi appeared before him with a smile on his face. Looking at Long Yi’s cheerful smile, Xiao Yan couldn’t help but affectionately rub Long Yi’s head.

“Long Yi, your aura seems to have become even stronger?” Xiao Yan paused for a moment to sense it, then exclaimed in surprise. How long has it been? Has Long Yi broken through again?

“The fifth-grade pill lightning is really good, much better than the fourth-grade pill lightning. I’m already close to the bottleneck of the mid-stage of the fourth star, and I’ll be able to break through soon,” Long Yi said happily, looking at Xiao Yan. It turned out he was only close to a breakthrough; otherwise, that incredible advancement would have truly terrified Xiao Yan.

Even so, Xiao Yan still felt ashamed. Now, there was no one in the team weaker than him. Even Lang Tian, who had always been refining medicine, was stronger than him.

Where could he put his face? It seemed he really needed to spend more time cultivating, otherwise, being the leader would be too pathetic.

“Father, aren’t you happy for me?” Long Yi looked up when Xiao Yan remained silent for a long time, his eyes darting around.

“How could that be? I was just shocked by Long Yi’s rapid advancement. Hehe, Long Yi, can I talk to you about something?” Xiao Yan pinched Long Yi’s increasingly chubby little face and said affectionately.

“What’s wrong? You’re not going out again without me, are you?” Long Yi asked, tilting his head and pouting.

“No, no. I wanted to discuss something with you. Could you please stop calling me ’Father’ from now on?”

“If I don’t call you Father, what should I call you?” Long Yi asked, puzzled.

“For example, you could call me Young Master Xiao like everyone else, or call me Big Brother?” Xiao Yan suggested tentatively.

“No! I’ll call you Father! You’re the one who gathered my spirit, so you’re my father!”

Long Yi pouted, resolutely refusing to accept Xiao Yan’s suggestion. Seeing Long Yi’s resolute expression, Xiao Yan shook his head helplessly.

“I really can’t do anything with you. Fine, you absorb it first, I’ll go find Elder Zhan. Oh, by the way, where’s Elder Zhan?”

Seeing that Xiao Yan didn’t insist, Long Yi’s smile suddenly blossomed into a bright smile, “Him? He’s been staring at the top of the tower all this time.”

“Stunned?” Xiao Yan blinked in confusion, then pulled Long Yi toward the center of the tower.

“Elder Zhan.” Xiao Yan looked up at Elder Zhan, who was suspended in mid-air, completely puzzled.

“People usually raise their heads to gaze at the bright moon and lower their heads to think of their hometown, but what are you doing, sir…”

“You’ve arrived?” Elder Zhan stared blankly at the tower top, which was faintly visible in the mist, without turning around, leaving Xiao Yan stunned for a moment.

Just then, a loud rumble shook the entire tower, and the space suddenly began to tremble violently. The runes on the top of the tower shone brightly, and the green lights converged, as if it had been subjected to an extremely violent impact, as if something was about to break out of the tower Xiao Yan and Long Yi were completely unsteady on the ground, every muscle in their bodies trembling with the countless tremors in an instant. Xiao Yan hurriedly spread his bone wings, pulling Long Yi into the air.

They stared at the huge and illusory snake shadow that kept appearing between the stone walls at the top of the tower, speechless with shock. Just as the snake shadow became increasingly solid, the golden pagoda shone brightly, and countless runes rapidly extended upwards along the stone wall, forming a mysterious net that firmly enveloped the churning snake shadow to Xiao Yan’s astonishment.

The serpentine shadow struggled desperately, its azure energy surging, letting out a series of desperate “whooshing” sounds, and unleashing countless rays of light in an attempt to break free of the net. But the net was incredibly strong, and the serpentine shadow let out sharp hisses, ultimately forced to slowly and unwillingly disappear into the stone wall.

“What’s going on?” Xiao Yan asked in surprise.

“Even if one’s strength is only slightly lacking, one still cannot break through the limitations of the rules of heaven and earth. What a pity.” Elder Zhan shook his head helplessly and turned around.

“Elder Zhan, is there someone else in this tower? Why haven’t you mentioned it before?” Xiao Yan was full of questions about this, and he really wanted to know what that snake shadow was.

“Hehe, that’s because it’s not the time for you to know yet,” Elder Zhan replied calmly.

“Don’t ask anymore. When it’s your turn to know, you will know. In any case, it’s not a bad thing for you.

Be patient and wait. The answer will be revealed soon.”

“Judging from your words, this illusory snake shadow seems to be related to me?” Xiao Yan asked tentatively, unwilling to give up. Old Master Zhan nodded, a half-smile playing on his lips, which was his reply. Xiao Yan was quite annoyed by Zhan Lao’s suspense and rolled his eyes.

Since it concerned him, Xiao Yan was naturally unwilling to give up, but it seemed that Elder Zhan was not planning to tell him for the time being. In any case, Elder Zhan would not harm him, and would have to tell him the truth sooner or later. With this in mind, Xiao Yan stopped asking.

“I say, kid, you’ve obtained the Spirit Seal Moss, absorbed the Heavenly Fire and Ghost Spirit, and now you have the Spirit Seal. Shouldn’t you be refining the Marrow Cultivation Pill? With your current strength and physical condition, it’s still too weak to forge the demon bloodline.

How can this be?” Elder Zhan looked at the top of the tower, then stared at Xiao Yan, and continued to speak.

“Elder Zhan, stop looking at the top of the tower and then at me. It makes me feel uneasy.” Xiao Yan was a little uneasy and, without getting an explanation, was extremely frustrated.

“I came here today to ask you, how exactly do you use the Marrow Cultivation Pill?”

“You’ve refined the Marrow Refining Pill? That’s great!” He pointed to the pool in the tower.

“Go, dissolve the Marrow Refining Pill in the pool, immerse yourself in it to absorb its medicinal power. Oh, it will hurt a little, but that shouldn’t be a problem for you.” After Zhan finished speaking, he raised his eyes and was about to look at the top of the tower again when he suddenly seemed to remember something.

He paused for a moment and continued, “Also, you need to improve your strength. If you don’t have anything urgent to do during this time, you can temper your body and improve your strength here. The conditions are almost ripe, so you need to hurry up.”

“The conditions are almost ripe? What do you mean?” Xiao Yan rubbed his forehead, feeling uneasy as he sensed that something rather unreliable was about to happen.

“Why ask so many questions? It’s no use knowing now, it will only distract you. Go do what you’re supposed to do.”

Old Zhan stroked his beard and scolded Xiao Yan. Hearing Elder Zhan’s reprimand, Xiao Yan gritted his teeth, took a slow breath, and tried his best to suppress his almost bursting curiosity, not knowing what Elder Zhan was up to. Xiao Yan walked towards the stone pool with a slight sullen expression, while Long Yi obediently continued to digest and devour the pill lightning under Xiao Yan’s instructions, preparing to break through to the mid-stage of the fourth star.

After dissolving the Marrow Cultivation Pill, Xiao Yan immersed himself in the pill pool. The continuous flow of medicinal power seeped into Xiao Yan’s body through his skin, tempering every muscle in his body. It made cracking sounds, as if every cell was being crushed and then reassembled.

The intense pain caused Xiao Yan to tremble violently all over and turn pale.

However, Xiao Yan’s body had been tempered by the Heavenly Fire and the Ghost Spirit, so he quickly adapted to the pain. Every inch of his skin and bones became vibrant, vigorous, and full of vitality under the tempering power of the Marrow Refining Pill. It took three days for the excruciating pain to gradually subside.

It has to be said that the medicinal power of the Marrow Refining Pill is indeed powerful. Xiao Yan felt the power about to burst forth from his body. The feeling of being reborn was simply wonderful.

At this moment, Xiao Yan really wanted to compete with Xiao Zhan in terms of pure muscle strength. After his body had adjusted to the effects of the Marrow Refining Pill for three days, and seeing the clear, watery pool that had lost its medicinal properties, Xiao Yan tossed in another Marrow Refining Pill. He understood that this required repeated soaking and refining.

After getting used to the intense pain, he lay in the pool, feeling somewhat bored.

“How about we organize our combat techniques?” The Great Creation Palm, the Annihilation Fire Lotus, the Body of the Flame Emperor, the Fiery Blazing Slash Ruler, the Eight Extremes Collapse, the Three Thousand Thunderclaps, the Three Mysterious Transformations of Heavenly Fire, the Yellow Spring Palm, the Yellow Spring Finger, the Wrath of the Yellow Springs, the Wandering Flame Transformation into Three Feet, and so on—a whole host of fighting techniques flashed through Xiao Yan’s mind. Xiao Yan shook his head, somewhat amused and exasperated.

Xiao Yan has quite a few fighting techniques, but most of them are brought from the Dou Qi Continent. Although they have been improved by Elder Zhan and are now at the Intent level, Xiao Yan has found in a series of fierce battles that his offensive fighting techniques are no longer sufficient and are even somewhat powerless. Only “Wandering Flame Transforms into Three Feet” is still quite powerful.

As for movement techniques and combat skills, “Three Thousand Thunderclaps” can only be used to a limited extent. Without the support of bone wings, “Three Thousand Thunderclaps” is extremely difficult to use. As for soul combat skills, although “Yellow Springs Wrath” has repeatedly achieved great feats, it is only effective against magical beasts that are not good at using their souls.

If it encounters an evenly matched opponent, it will have no advantage whatsoever. Regarding sonic fighting techniques, Xiao Yan found that in the Dou Di Continent where soul power is generally strong, the effect of sonic attacks is negligible, even though he doesn’t have any good sonic fighting techniques. Numerous but not refined – that’s how Xiao Yan summarizes his current fighting skills.

“It seems I really need to improve my fighting skills.” Xiao Yan paused for a moment, then frowned deeply.

“There are two more pillars of light in the cultivation level of the tower. What kind of fighting skills are inside? Otherwise, I can only hope for the fighting skills auction that Zhen Ni is preparing.”

Thinking of this, Xiao Yan had an urge to rush into the pillar of light and grab a fighting technique.

“I’ll go as soon as I run out of the Marrow Cultivation Pills!” Xiao Yan felt like he was being torn apart.

“Let’s try to distract ourselves by thinking about something else.” Xiao Yan was about to lose his patience, so he quickly shifted his attention.

“The Demon Core is needed for advancement; the stone tablet’s effects are unknown; the jade box is an unknown item; the jade pendant awaits Zhenni’s information; the spirit seal cannot be absorbed…” Xiao Yan examined the various items in his storage ring and found that, apart from the Demon Core, none of them could be used immediately, which made him feel somewhat frustrated.

“Huh, these fragments of maps?” Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up as he looked at three fragments of maps. They were maps given to him by Qing Haoran, won in a medicine-refining competition, and acquired at an auction house. They had been stored at the bottom of his storage ring for a long time.

Xiao Yan casually pieced them together and was pleasantly surprised to find that the joints fit together perfectly.

“A map, a treasure map?” Xiao Yan’s eyes lit up as he looked at the three fragments of the map in his hand
